PASCUAL ORTIZ RUBIO, Constitutional President of the United Mexican States, to its inhabitants, know ye:

That in exercise of the powers granted to him by Decree of January 2, 1931, he has seen fit to issue the following

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

Name of the Code reformed: DOF 23-12-1974 (formerly "Penal Code for the Federal District and Territories in Matters of Common Jurisdiction, and for the entire Republic in Matters of Federal Jurisdiction"), DOF 31-12-1974, 18-05-1999 (formerly "Penal Code for the Federal District in matters of Common Jurisdiction, and for the entire Republic in matters of Federal Jurisdiction")

## FIRST BOOK

PRELIMINARY TITLE

###### Article 1.

This Code shall apply throughout the Republic for crimes of federal jurisdiction.

Article reformed DOF 23-12-1974, 18-05-1999

###### Article 2.- It shall also apply:

I. For crimes that are initiated, prepared or committed abroad, when they produce or are intended to have effects in the territory of the Republic; or, for crimes that are initiated, prepared or committed abroad, provided that a treaty binding on Mexico provides for the obligation to extradite or prosecute, the requirements provided in Article 4 of this Code are met and the probable perpetrator is not extradited to the State that has requested it, and Section reformed DOF 28-06-2007

II.- For crimes committed in Mexican consulates or against their personnel, when they have not been tried in the country where they were committed.

###### Article 3.- Continuing crimes committed abroad, which continue to be committed in the

Republic, shall be prosecuted according to the laws of the latter, whether the offenders are Mexican or foreign.

The same rule shall apply in the case of continued crimes.

Paragraph added DOF 14-01-1985

1 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

###### Article 4.- Crimes committed in foreign territory by a Mexican against Mexicans or against

foreigners, or by a foreigner against Mexicans, shall be punished in the Republic, according to federal laws, if the following requirements are met:

I.- That the accused is found in the Republic;

II.- That the offender has not been definitively tried in the country where the crime was committed, and

III.- That the infraction with which he is charged has the character of a crime in the country where it was committed and in the Republic.

###### Article 5.- The following shall be considered as executed in the territory of the Republic:

I.- Crimes committed by Mexicans or by foreigners on the high seas, aboard national vessels; II.- Those executed aboard a national warship anchored in port or in territorial waters of another nation. This extends to the case where the vessel is merchant, if the offender has not been tried in the nation to which the port belongs;

III.- Those committed aboard a foreign vessel anchored in a national port or in territorial waters of the Republic, if the public tranquility is disturbed or if the offender or the victim are not part of the crew. Otherwise, reciprocal law shall apply;

IV.- Those committed aboard national or foreign aircraft that are in territory or in atmosphere or national or foreign territorial waters, in cases analogous to those indicated for vessels in the preceding sections, and

V.- Those committed in Mexican embassies and legations.

###### Article 6.- When a crime not provided for in this Code is committed, but is provided for in a special law or in

an international treaty of mandatory observance in Mexico, these shall be applied, taking into account the provisions of the First Book of this Code and, where applicable, the pertinent provisions of the Second Book. When the same matter appears to be regulated by different provisions, the special shall prevail over the general.

In case of crimes committed against girls, boys and adolescents, the best interest of children shall always be sought, which must prevail in every application of law.

Paragraph added DOF 19-08-2010

Article reformed DOF 14-01-1985

#### FIRST TITLE

Penal Responsibility

##### CHAPTER I

General rules on crimes and responsibility

###### Article 7.- Crime is the act or omission sanctioned by penal laws.

In crimes of material result, the typical result produced shall also be attributable to the one who fails to prevent it, if such person had the legal duty to avoid it. In these cases the result shall be considered to be 2 of 337

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services the consequence of an omissive conduct, when it is determined that the one who fails to prevent it had the duty to act to that effect, derived from a law, from a contract or from his own preceding act. Paragraph added DOF 10-01-1994

The crime is:

- I. Instantaneous, when the consummation is exhausted at the same moment in which all the elements of the penal description have been realized;

Section reformed DOF 17-06-2016

II.- Permanent or continuous, when the consummation is prolonged over time, and

III.- Continued, when with unity of criminal purpose, plurality of conducts and unity of passive subject, the same legal precept is violated.

Section reformed DOF 13-05-1996

Article reformed DOF 13-01-1984

###### Article 8.- Criminal actions or omissions can only be committed intentionally or

negligently.

Article reformed DOF 13-01-1984, 10-01-1994

###### Article 9.- He who, knowing the elements of the criminal type, or foreseeing as

possible the typical result, wants or accepts the realization of the act described by law, acts intentionally, and Erratum to the paragraph DOF 01-02-1994

He who produces the typical result, which he did not foresee being foreseeable or foresaw trusting that it would not occur, by virtue of the violation of a duty of care, which he should and could observe according to the circumstances and personal conditions, acts negligently.

Erratum to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Reformed DOF 13-01-1984, 10-01-1994

###### Article 10.- Penal responsibility does not pass from the person and property of the offenders, except

in cases specified by law.

###### Article 11.

When any member or representative of a legal person, or of a partnership, corporation or company of any kind, with the exception of State institutions, commits a crime with the means that such entities provide for such purpose, so that it is committed in the name or under the protection of the social representation or for its benefit, the judge may, in cases exclusively specified by law, decree in the sentence the suspension of the group or its dissolution, when he deems it necessary for public safety.

###### Article 11 Bis.- For the purposes of the provisions of Title X, Chapter II, of the National Code of

Criminal Proceedings, legal entities may be imposed one or several legal consequences when they have intervened in the commission of the following offenses:

- A. Those provided for in this Code:
- I. Terrorism, provided for in articles 139 to 139 Ter and international terrorism provided for in articles 148 Bis to 148 Quáter;
  - II. Unlawful use of facilities intended for air traffic, provided for in article 172 Bis;
  - III. Against health, provided for in articles 194 and 195, first paragraph;

XXII. In other cases expressly provided for in the applicable legislation.

For the purposes of article 422 of the National Code of Criminal Proceedings, the following limits of punishability shall apply for the legal consequences for legal entities:

- a) Suspension of activities, for a period of between six months to six years.

b) Closure of premises and establishments, for a period of between six months to six years.

c) Prohibition from carrying out in the future the activities in the exercise of which the offense was committed or participated in its commission, for a period of between six months to ten years.

d) Temporary disqualification consisting of the suspension of rights to participate directly or through an intermediary person in contracting procedures or to enter into contracts regulated by the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector, as well as by the Law on Public Works and Services related thereto, for a period of between six months to six years.

e) Judicial intervention to safeguard the rights of workers or creditors for a period of between six months to six years.

The judicial intervention may affect the entirety of the organization or be limited to any of its facilities, sections or business units. The exact scope of the intervention and who will be in charge of it shall be determined, as well as the deadlines in which follow-up reports must be made for the judicial body. The judicial intervention may be modified or suspended at any time following a report from the intervener and the Public Prosecutor. The intervener shall have the right to access all facilities and premises of the company or legal entity, as well as to receive whatever information he deems necessary for the exercise of his functions. The applicable legislation shall determine the aspects related to the functions of the intervener and his respective remuneration.

In all cases provided for in article 422 of the National Code of Criminal Proceedings, the sanctions may be reduced by up to one quarter, if prior to the act imputed to them, the legal entities had a permanent control body, in charge of verifying compliance with the applicable legal provisions to follow up on internal crime prevention policies and have carried out before or after the act imputed to them, the reduction of the damage caused by the typical act.

Artículo adicionado DOF 17-06-2016

##### CHAPTER II

Attempt

###### Article 12.- A punishable attempt exists when the resolution to commit a crime is externalized

by partially or totally carrying out the executive acts that should produce the result, or by omitting those that should prevent it, if it is not consummated due to causes beyond the will of the agent. Paragraph amended DOF 14-01-1985, 10-01-1994

To impose the penalty for the attempt, the judge shall take into account, in addition to what is provided in article 52, the greater or lesser degree of approximation to the consummate moment of the crime.

Paragraph amended DOF 10-01-1994

If the subject spontaneously desists from the execution or prevents the consummation of the crime, no penalty or security measure shall be imposed with respect to it, without prejudice to applying the one corresponding to acts executed or omitted that in themselves constitute crimes.

Paragraph added DOF 14-01-1985

##### CHAPTER III

Persons responsible for crimes

###### Article 13.- The following are authors or participants in the crime:

Paragraph amended DOF 10-01-1994

I.- Those who agree to or prepare its commission.

II.- Those who carry it out themselves;

III.- Those who carry it out jointly;

IV.- Those who carry it out by using another;

V.- Those who intentionally determine another to commit it;

Fraction amended DOF 10-01-1994

VI.- Those who intentionally provide aid or assist another for its commission;

Fraction amended DOF 10-01-1994

VII.- Those who after its execution assist the offender, in fulfillment of a promise made prior to the crime; and

VIII.- Those who without prior agreement, intervene with others in its commission, when the result that each one produced cannot be determined.

Fraction amended DOF 10-01-1994

The authors or participants referred to in this article shall each be liable to the extent of their own culpability.

Paragraph added DOF 10-01-1994

For the subjects referred to in fractions VI, VII and VIII, the punishability provided by article 64 bis of this Code shall apply.

Paragraph added DOF 10-01-1994

Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 09-03-1946, 13-01-1984

###### Article 14.- If several offenders take part in the commission of a determined crime and one

of them commits a different crime, without prior agreement with the others, all shall be responsible for the commission of the new crime, unless the following requirements are met:

I.- That the new crime does not serve as an adequate means to commit the principal one;

II.- That it is not a necessary or natural consequence of the latter, or of the agreed means;

III.- That they had not known beforehand that the new crime was going to be committed; and

IV.- That they had not been present at the execution of the new crime, or that having been present, they had done everything in their power to prevent it.

##### CHAPTER IV

Causes of exclusion of the crime

Denomination of the Chapter amended DOF 10-01-1994

###### Article 15.- The crime is excluded when:

I.- The act is carried out without intervention of the agent's will;

7 de 337

CÓDIGO PENAL FEDERAL

CÁMARA DE DIPUTADOS DEL H. CONGRESO DE LA UNIÓN Última Reforma DOF 08-05-2023

Secretaría General

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios

II.- The nonexistence of any of the elements that make up the typical description of the crime in question is demonstrated;

Fraction amended DOF 18-05-1999

III.- One acts with the consent of the holder of the affected legal interest, provided that the following requirements are met:

- a) That the legal interest is available;
- b) That the holder of the interest has the legal capacity to freely dispose of it; and
- c) That the consent is express or tacit and without any vice; or that the act is carried out in such circumstances that allow a well-founded presumption that, had the holder been consulted, he would have granted the same;

IV.- A real, current or imminent aggression, and without right, is repelled in protection of one's own or others' legal interests, provided that there is a need for defense and rationality of the means employed and there is no sufficient and immediate intentional provocation by the aggressed party or the person being defended.

It shall be presumed as legitimate defense, unless proven otherwise, the act of causing harm to anyone who by any means attempts to enter, without right, the home of the agent, that of his family, his dependencies, or those of any person he has the obligation to defend, the place where his own or others' property is located with respect to which the same obligation exists; or finds him in any of those places in such circumstances as to reveal the probability of an aggression;

V.- One acts out of the need to safeguard one's own or another's legal interest from a real, current or imminent danger, not intentionally caused by the agent, injuring another interest of lesser or equal value than the one safeguarded, provided that the danger is not avoidable by other means and the agent does not have the legal duty to face it;

VI.- The action or omission is carried out in fulfillment of a legal duty or in exercise of a right, provided that there is rational need for the means employed to fulfill the duty or exercise the right, and that the latter is not carried out with the sole purpose of harming another;

VII.- At the time of carrying out the typical act, the agent does not have the capacity to understand the illicit character of it or to conduct himself in accordance with that understanding, by virtue of suffering from mental disorder or retarded intellectual development, unless the agent had intentionally or negligently caused his mental disorder, in which case he shall be liable for the typical result provided that he had foreseen it or it was foreseeable to him.

When the capacity referred to in the preceding paragraph is only considerably diminished, the provisions of article 69 bis of this Code shall apply.

VIII.- The action or omission is carried out under an invincible error;

A) Regarding any of the essential elements that make up the criminal type; or

B) Regarding the unlawfulness of the conduct, either because the subject is unaware of the existence of the law or its scope, or because he believes that his conduct is justified.

If the errors referred to in the preceding subparagraphs are vincible, the provisions of article 66 of this Code shall apply;

IX.- Given the circumstances that concur in the performance of an unlawful conduct, a conduct different from the one performed is not rationally demandable from the agent, by virtue of not having been able to determine himself to act in accordance with law; or

X.- The typical result is produced by fortuitous case.

###### Article 16.- In cases of excess of legitimate defense or excess in any other cause of

justification, one quarter of the sanction corresponding to the crime in question shall be imposed, the imputation on an intentional basis remaining subsistent.

Article amended DOF 13-01-1984, 10-01-1994, 17-06-2016

###### Article 17.- The causes of exclusion of the crime shall be investigated and resolved ex officio or at the request of

a party, at any stage of the proceedings.

Article amended DOF 10-01-1994

##### CHAPTER V

Concurrence of Crimes

Denomination of the Chapter amended DOF 13-01-1984

###### Article 18.

An ideal concurrence exists when several crimes are committed with a single conduct.
A real concurrence exists when several crimes are committed with a plurality of conducts.
Article amended DOF 13-01-1984

###### Article 19.- There is no concurrence when the conducts constitute a continued crime.

Article amended DOF 13-01-1984

##### CHAPTER VI

Recidivism

###### Artículo 20.- There is recidivism: whenever a person convicted by final judgment issued by

any court of the Republic or from abroad commits a new offense, if a term equal to that of the prescription of the penalty has not elapsed since the completion of the sentence or since the pardon thereof, except for the exceptions established by law.

A conviction suffered abroad shall be taken into account if it derives from an offense that has this character in this Code or special laws.

###### Artículo 21.- If the recidivist in the same type of offenses commits a new crime arising

from the same passion or vicious inclination, they shall be considered a habitual offender, provided that the three offenses have been committed within a period not exceeding ten years.

###### Artículo 22.- The provisions of the preceding articles include cases in which only one

of the offenses, or all of them, remain at any stage of attempt, whatever the capacity in which the responsible party intervenes.

###### Artículo 23.- The preceding articles shall not apply in cases of political crimes and when the agent

has been pardoned for being innocent.

TITULO SEGUNDO

##### CAPITULO I

Penalties and security measures

###### Artículo 24.- The penalties and security measures are:

1.- Imprisonment.

2.- Treatment at liberty, semi-liberty and work in favor of the community.

Numeral derogado DOF 12-05-1938. Adicionado DOF 24-03-1944. Derogado DOF 05-01-1948. Adicionado DOF 13-01-1984

Numeral reformado DOF 31-12-1974, 13-01-1984

4.- Confinement.

5.- Prohibition from going to a specific place.

6.- Pecuniary sanction.

7.- (Repealed).

Numeral derogado DOF 13-01-1984

8.- Confiscation of instruments, objects and products of the crime

10.- Warning.

11.- Bond not to offend.

12.- Suspension or deprivation of rights.

13.- Disqualification, removal or suspension from duties or positions.

14.- Special publication of sentence.

15.- Surveillance by the authority.

16.- Suspension or dissolution of corporations.

17.- Guardianship measures for minors.

18.- Confiscation of assets corresponding to illicit enrichment.

Numeral adicionado DOF 05-01-1983

19. The placement of location and surveillance devices.

Numeral adicionado DOF 30-11-2010

And any others established by the laws.

##### CAPITULO II

Imprisonment

###### Artículo 25.

Imprisonment consists of the penalty depriving personal liberty. Its duration shall be from three days to sixty years, and an additional penalty to the maximum limit may only be imposed when a new crime is committed while in confinement. It shall be served in penitentiary centers, in accordance with the legislation on the matter and in compliance with the respective judicial resolution.

The precautionary measure of pretrial detention shall be credited toward the fulfillment of the imposed penalty as well as those that may be imposed in other proceedings, even if they concerned acts prior to entry into prison. In this case, the penalties shall be served simultaneously.

The maximum limit of the duration of the penalty of deprivation of liberty up to 60 years contemplated in this article is not applicable to offenses that are sanctioned in accordance with what is stipulated in other laws.

Párrafo reformado DOF 17-11-2017

Artículo reformado DOF 12-05-1938, 05-01-1948, 05-01-1955, 03-01-1989, 17-05-1999, 26-05-2004, 03-06-2014, 17-06-2016

###### Artículo 26.- Defendants subject to pretrial detention and political prisoners shall be confined in

special establishments or departments.

##### CAPITULO III

Treatment at liberty, semi-release and work in favor of the community

Denominación del Capítulo reformada DOF 13-01-1984

###### Artículo 27.- Treatment at liberty of criminally liable persons consists of the application of labor,

educational and curative measures, as appropriate, authorized by law and conducive to the social reintegration of the convicted person, under the guidance and care of the executing authority. Its duration may not exceed that corresponding to the substituted prison sentence.

Párrafo reformado DOF 17-06-2016

Semi-liberty implies alternation of periods of deprivation of liberty and treatment at liberty. It shall be applied, according to the circumstances of the case, in the following manner: release during the work or educational week, with weekend confinement, weekend release, with confinement during the rest thereof; or daytime release, with nighttime confinement. The duration of semi-liberty may not exceed that corresponding to the substituted prison sentence.

Work in favor of the community consists of the provision of unremunerated services, in public educational or social assistance institutions or in private charitable institutions. This work shall be carried out in shifts during periods different from the hours of labor that represent the source of income for the subsistence of the subject and their family, without exceeding the overtime shift determined by labor law and under the guidance and supervision of the executing authority.

Work in favor of the community may be an autonomous penalty or a substitute for imprisonment or fine. Párrafo adicionado DOF 10-01-1994

Each day of imprisonment shall be substituted by one shift of work in favor of the community.

The length of the work shift shall be determined by the judge taking into account the circumstances of the case.

Under no circumstances shall this work be carried out in a manner that is degrading or humiliating to the convicted person.

Párrafo reformado DOF 17-06-2016

##### CAPITULO IV

Confinement

###### Artículo 28.- Confinement consists of the obligation to reside in a specific place and not leave

it. The Executive shall designate the place, reconciling the requirements of public tranquility with the health and needs of the convicted person. In the case of political crimes, the designation shall be made by the judge who issues the sentence.

##### CAPITULO V

Pecuniary sanction

###### Artículo 29.- The pecuniary sanction comprises the fine and the reparation of damages.

The fine consists of the payment of an amount of money to the State, which shall be set by day-fines, which may not exceed one thousand, except in cases indicated by the law itself. The day-fine is equivalent to the daily net income of the convicted person at the time of committing the crime, taking into account all their income.

Paragraph amended DOF 10-01-1994, 23-08-2005

For the purposes of this Code, the lower limit of the day-fine shall be the equivalent of the daily minimum wage in force in the place where the crime was committed. With regard to the continuing crime, the minimum wage in force at the consummative moment of the last conduct shall be applied. For the permanent crime, the minimum wage in force at the moment the commission ceased shall be considered.

When it is proven that the convicted person cannot pay the fine or can only cover part of it, the judicial authority may substitute it, totally or partially, with community service.

Each workday shall discharge one day-fine. When substitution of the fine with service provision is not possible or convenient, the judicial authority may place the convicted person under supervised release, which shall not exceed the number of day-fines substituted.

If the convicted person refuses without justified cause to cover the amount of the fine, the State shall demand it through the coercive economic procedure.

At any time, the amount of the fine may be covered, deducting from it the proportional part corresponding to the workdays provided in favor of the community, or the time of imprisonment that the convicted person has served in the case of a fine substituting for a custodial sentence, in which case the equivalence shall be at the rate of one day-fine per one day of imprisonment.

Paragraph amended DOF 17-06-2016

Article amended DOF 13-01-1984

###### Artículo 30.

Reparation of damages must be comprehensive, adequate, efficacious, effective, proportional to the severity of the damage caused and to the harm suffered, and shall comprise at least:

- I. The restitution of the thing obtained through the crime and if not possible, the payment of its price, at its updated value;

12 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Amendment DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

II. The indemnification for material and moral damages caused, including medical and psychological care, social services and rehabilitation or curative treatments necessary for the recovery of health, that the victim has required or requires, as a consequence of the crime. In cases of crimes against the free development of personality, freedom and normal psychosexual development and mental health, as well as family violence, it shall also comprise payment for psychotherapeutic treatments that are necessary for the victim;

III. The compensation for damages occasioned;

IV. The payment for loss of economic income and loss of profits, for which the salary that the victim had at the time of suffering the crime shall be taken as a basis and in case of not having that information, it shall be according to the minimum wage in force in the place where the act occurred;

V. The cost of the loss of opportunities, particularly employment, education and social benefits, according to their circumstances;

VI. The declaration that restores the dignity and reputation of the victim, through electronic or written means;

VII. The public apology, the acceptance of responsibility, as well as the guarantee of non-repetition, when the crime is committed by public servants.

The means for rehabilitation must be as complete as possible, and must allow the victim to participate fully in public, private and social life.

Article amended DOF 05-01-1983, 13-01-1984, 10-01-1994, 30-12-1997, 19-08-2010, 14-06-2012

###### Artículo 30

Bis.- The following have the right to reparation of damages in the following order: 1o. The offended party; 2o.

In case of death of the offended party, the surviving spouse or cohabitant, and minor children; in their absence, the other descendants and ascendants who were economically dependent on him at the time of death.

Article added DOF 21-01-1991

###### Artículo 31. The reparation shall be set by the judges, according to the damage that must be repaired, based

on the evidence obtained in the proceeding and the harm caused to the victim or offended party of the crime.

Paragraph amended DOF 13-01-1984, 14-06-2012

For cases of reparation of damage caused by crimes of negligence, the Federal Executive shall regulate, without prejudice to the resolution issued by the judicial authority, the manner in which, administratively, such reparation must be guaranteed through special insurance.

###### Artículo 31

Bis. In every criminal proceeding, the Public Prosecutor shall be obligated to request, ex officio, the sentence regarding the reparation of damages and the judge is obligated to rule accordingly. Failure to comply with this provision shall be sanctioned in accordance with the provisions of section VII and the second paragraph of Article 225 of this Code.

At all times, the victim must be informed about the reparation of damages.

Article added DOF 10-01-1994. Amended DOF 14-06-2012

###### Artículo 32.- The following are obligated to repair the damages in the terms of Article 29:

I.- Ascendants, for the crimes of their descendants who are under their parental authority:

II.- Guardians and custodians, for the crimes of incapacitated persons under their authority;

13 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Amendment DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

III.- Directors of boarding schools or workshops, who receive in their establishment students or apprentices under 16 years of age, for the crimes they commit during the time they are under the care of the former;

IV.- Owners, companies or managers of businesses or commercial establishments of any kind, for the crimes committed by their workers, day laborers, employees, domestic servants and artisans, by reason of and in the performance of their service;

V.- Partnerships or associations, for the crimes of their partners or managing directors, under the same terms in which, according to the laws, they are liable for the other obligations that the latter contract.

This rule does not apply to the marital community, since, in any case, each spouse shall be liable with their own property for the reparation of the damage they cause, and

VI. Any institution, association, organization or group of a religious, cultural, sports, educational, recreational or any other nature, whose employees, members, participants, auxiliaries or assistants carry out their activities on a voluntary or paid basis, and

Section added DOF 19-08-2010

VII.- The State, jointly and severally, for the intentional crimes of its public servants committed by reason of the exercise of their functions, and subsidiarily when those are negligent.

Section amended DOF 10-01-1994. Renumbered DOF 19-08-2010

###### Artículo 33.- The obligation to pay the pecuniary sanction has priority over any

others contracted after the crime, with the exception of those referring to alimony and labor relations.

Article amended DOF 13-01-1984

###### Artículo 34.

The reparation of damage arising from the crime that must be made by the accused, defendant and sentenced person, has the character of public penalty and shall be required ex officio by the Public Prosecutor's Office. The victim, the legal advisor and the offended party or their beneficiaries may contribute to the Public Prosecutor's Office or to the jurisdictional body as appropriate, the evidentiary data they have to demonstrate the origin and amount of said reparation, in the terms provided by the National Code of Criminal Procedures. Paragraph amended DOF 10-01-1994, 17-06-2016

The failure by the authorities of the obligation referred to in the preceding paragraph, shall be sanctioned with a fine of thirty to forty days of minimum wage.

Paragraph added DOF 10-01-1994

When said reparation must be required from a third party, it shall have the character of civil liability and shall be processed in the form of an incident, in the terms set by the Criminal Procedure Code itself. Whoever considers themselves entitled to reparation of damage, which cannot be obtained before the jurisdictional body, by virtue of the non-exercise of the action or the abstention from investigating by the Public Prosecutor's Office, dismissal or acquittal, may resort to the civil or administrative route in the terms of the corresponding legislation.

Paragraph amended DOF 17-06-2016

Article amended DOF 13-01-1984

###### Artículo 35.

The amount of the pecuniary sanction shall be distributed: between the State and the offended party; to the first shall be applied the amount of the fine, and to the second that of the reparation.

14 de 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

If it is not possible to make effective the entire amount of the pecuniary sanction, the reparation of the damage shall be covered preferentially, and where appropriate, pro rata among the offended parties.

If the offended party waives the reparation, the amount thereof shall be applied to the State.

In the event that a precautionary measure has been imposed to guarantee the reparation of the damage, this shall be made effective in favor of the victim or offended party when the sentence that condemns to repair the damage becomes final.

Paragraph amended DOF 10-01-1994, 17-06-2016

Reform DOF 17-06-2016: Repealed from the article the then fifth paragraph (previously added DOF 10-01-1994)

###### Artículo 36.- When several persons commit the crime, the judge shall set the fine for each of the

offenders, according to their participation in the criminal act and their economic conditions; and as for the reparation of the damage, the debt shall be considered as joint and several.

###### Artículo 37.

The reparation of the damage shall be ordered to be made effective, in the same manner as the fine. Once the sentence imposing such reparation becomes final, the court that has pronounced it shall immediately send a certified copy thereof to the competent tax authority and the latter, within three days following receipt of said copy, shall initiate the economic-coercive procedure, notifying thereof the person in whose favor it has been decreed, or their legal representative.

Article amended DOF 10-01-1994

###### Artículo 38.- If the pecuniary liability cannot be covered with the property of the responsible party or

with the product of their work in prison, the released sentenced person shall continue to be subject to the obligation to pay the missing part.

Article amended DOF 17-06-2016

###### Artículo 39.

The judge, taking into account the amount of the damage and the economic situation of the obligated party, may set deadlines for the payment of the reparation thereof, which as a whole shall not exceed one year, and may require a guarantee if deemed appropriate.

The authority to whom the collection of the fine corresponds may set deadlines for the payment thereof, taking into account the circumstances of the case.

Article amended DOF 29-12-1981. Errata DOF 13-01-1982. Amended DOF 13-01-1984

##### CAPITULO VI

Forfeiture of Instruments, Objects and Products of the Crime

###### Artículo 40.- The jurisdictional body by means of a sentence in the corresponding criminal proceeding, may

decree the forfeiture of property that is instruments, objects or products of the crime, with the exception of those that have caused abandonment in the terms of the applicable provisions or with respect to those on which the declaration of extinction of domain has been resolved.

In the event that the product, the instruments or objects of the criminal act have disappeared or are not located for reasons attributable to the accused or sentenced person, the forfeiture of property owned by the accused or sentenced person(s) may be decreed, as well as those with respect to which they act as owners or beneficial owners or controlling beneficiary, whose value is equivalent to said product, without prejudice to the applicable provisions on extinction of domain.

If they belong to a third party, they shall only be forfeited when the third party who has them in their possession or has acquired them under any title, is in any of the cases referred to in articles

139 Quáter, 400 or 400 bis of this Code, regardless of the legal nature of said third party owner or possessor and of the relationship that they have with the accused or sentenced person, as the case may be. The

15 de 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services competent authorities shall proceed to the immediate seizure of the property that could be subject to forfeiture, during the proceeding. Action shall be taken in the terms provided by this paragraph whatever the nature of the instruments, objects or products of the crime.

Errata to article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 08-05-1945, 15-01-1951, 13-01-1984, 23-12-1985, 08-02-1999, 17-05-1999,

###### Artículo 41.- The objects or assets that are at the disposal of the investigating authorities

or the judicial authorities, that have not been forfeited and that are not collected by whoever has the right to do so, within a period of ninety calendar days, counted from the notification to the interested party, shall be sold at public auction and the product of the sale shall be applied to whoever has the right to receive it. If notified, they do not appear within six months following the date of notification, the product of the sale shall be allocated to the improvement of the administration of justice, after deductions for the expenses incurred.

In the case of property that is at the disposal of the authority, that should not be destroyed and that cannot be preserved or is of costly maintenance, its immediate sale shall be proceeded at public auction, and the product shall be left at the disposal of whoever has the right to it for a period of six months from the notification made to them, after which it shall be applied to the improvement of the administration of justice.

Article amended DOF 15-01-1951, 13-01-1965, 23-12-1974, 31-12-1974, 13-01-1984

##### CAPITULO VII

Admonition

###### Artículo 42.- The admonition consists: in the warning that the judge addresses to the accused, making

them see the consequences of the crime they committed, urging them to amendment and warning them that a greater sanction will be imposed if they recidivate.

This admonition shall be made in public or in private, as the judge deems prudent.

##### CAPITULO VIII

Warning and Bond of No Offense

###### Artículo 43.- The warning consists in the admonition that the judge makes to a person, when they

have committed a crime and there is founded fear that they are disposed to commit a new crime, either by their attitude or by threats, that in the event of committing it, they will be considered as a recidivist. Article amended DOF 13-01-1984


This excerpt sits under: FEDERAL PENAL CODE > FIRST BOOK > FIRST TITLE > CHAPTER V

###### Artículo 44.- When the judge deems that the warning is not sufficient, he shall also require from the accused

a bond not to offend, or another adequate guarantee, in the judge's own judgment.

Article amended DOF 13-01-1984

##### CAPITULO IX

Suspension of rights

###### Artículo 45.- The suspension of rights is of two kinds:

I.- That which by operation of law results from a sanction as a necessary consequence thereof, and II.- That which by formal judgment is imposed as a sanction.

In the first case, the suspension begins and ends with the sanction of which it is a consequence. 16 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

In the second case, if the suspension is imposed with another custodial sanction, it shall begin upon the termination of the latter and its duration shall be that indicated in the judgment.

###### Artículo 46.

The penalty of imprisonment produces the suspension of political rights and those of guardianship, curatorship, being an attorney-in-fact, defender, executor, expert, depositary or judicial receiver, trustee or receiver in bankruptcy, arbitrator, arbitrator or representative of absentees. The suspension shall begin from the time the respective judgment becomes final and shall last for the entire duration of the sentence.

##### CAPITULO X

Special publication of judgment

###### Artículo 47.- The special publication of judgment consists of the total or partial insertion thereof in

one or two newspapers circulating in the locality. The judge shall choose the newspapers and shall decide the manner in which the publication must be made.

The publication of the judgment shall be made at the expense of the offender, of the injured party if the latter requests it, or of the State if the judge deems it necessary.

###### Artículo 48.- The judge may, at the request and expense of the injured party, order the publication of the judgment in a

different entity or in some other newspaper.

###### Artículo 49.- The publication of judgment shall likewise be ordered by way of reparation and at the request

of the interested party, when the latter is acquitted, the imputed act does not constitute a crime, or he did not commit it.

###### Artículo 50.- If the crime for which the publication of judgment was imposed was committed by means of

the press, in addition to the publication referred to in the preceding articles, it shall also be made in the newspaper used to commit the crime, with the same type of font, the same color of ink, and in the same place.

##### CAPITULO XI

Surveillance by the authority

Chapter added DOF 13-01-1984

###### Artículo 50 Bis.- When the judgment determines restriction of liberty or rights, or conditional suspension

of the execution of the judgment, the judge shall provide for surveillance by the authority over the sentenced person, which shall have the same duration as that corresponding to the sanction imposed.

The surveillance shall consist of exercising over the sentenced person observation and guidance of his conduct by specialized personnel dependent on the executing authority, for social reinsertion. Paragraph amended DOF 17-06-2016

Article added DOF 13-01-1984

TITLE THREE

Application of Sanctions

##### CAPITULO I

General rules

###### Artículo 51.- Within the limits established by law, judges and courts shall apply the sanctions

established for each crime, taking into account the external circumstances of execution and the 17 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services particular characteristics of the offender; particularly when it concerns indigenous persons, the uses and customs of the peoples and communities to which they belong shall be considered.

Paragraph amended DOF 30-12-1991, 18-12-2002

In the cases of articles 60, fraction VI, 61, 63, 64, 64 bis and 65 and in any others in which this Code provides penalties in proportion to those provided for the intentional consummated crime, the applicable punishment is, for all legal effects, that which results from the increase or decrease, as appropriate, of the minimum and maximum terms of the penalty provided for the latter. When it concerns imprisonment, the minimum penalty shall never be less than three days.

Paragraph added DOF 14-01-1985

When an intentional crime is committed against any journalist, person or facility with the intention of affecting, limiting or impairing the right to information or the freedoms of expression or of the press, the penalty established for such crime shall be increased by up to one third.

Paragraph added DOF 03-05-2013

In the previous case, the penalty shall be increased by up to one half when in addition the crime is committed by a public servant in the exercise of his functions or the victim is a woman and gender reasons concur in the commission of the crime, in accordance with what the laws on the matter establish. Paragraph added DOF 03-05-2013

###### Artículo 52.- The judge shall establish the penalties and security measures that he deems just and appropriate within

the limits indicated for each crime, based on the gravity of the unlawful act, the quality and specific condition of the victim or injured party and the degree of culpability of the agent, taking into account: Paragraph amended DOF 19-08-2010

I.- The magnitude of the damage caused to the legal interest or of the danger to which it may have been exposed; II.- The nature of the action or omission and of the means employed to execute it;

III.- The circumstances of time, place, manner or occasion of the act committed;

IV.- The form and degree of intervention of the agent in the commission of the crime;

Fraction amended DOF 19-08-2010

V.- The age, education, learning, customs, social and economic conditions of the subject, as well as the motives that impelled or determined him to commit the crime. When the accused belongs to any indigenous people or community, their uses and customs shall also be taken into account;

Fraction amended DOF 18-12-2002

VI.- The subsequent behavior of the accused in relation to the crime committed; and

VII.- The other special and personal conditions in which the agent found himself at the moment of the commission of the crime, provided they are relevant to determine the possibility of having adjusted his conduct to the requirements of the norm.

Errata notice to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 05-01-1983, 13-01-1984, 10-01-1994

###### Artículo 53.- The increase in gravity arising from particular circumstances of the injured party is not imputable to the

accused if he was blamelessly unaware of them when committing the crime.

###### Artículo 54.- The increase or decrease of the penalty, based on the qualities, personal relationships or subjective

circumstances of the author of a crime, are not applicable to the other subjects who intervened in it.

18 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

Those based on objective circumstances are applicable if the other subjects have knowledge of them.

Article amended DOF 13-01-1984

###### Artículo 55.- In the event that the accused is a person over seventy years of age or

affected by a serious or terminal illness, the jurisdictional Body may order that pretrial detention be executed at the domicile of the accused person or, as the case may be, in a medical or geriatric center, under the applicable precautionary measures, in all cases the assessment by the judge shall be supported by expert opinions. The review of the precautionary measure may be promoted by the parties who shall also offer evidence for said purpose.

Paragraph amended DOF 17-06-2016

Likewise, the provisions of the preceding paragraph shall apply when dealing with pregnant women or mothers during lactation.

Paragraph added DOF 17-06-2016

Those who are accused of crimes that merit mandatory pretrial detention or who, in the opinion of the Control Judge, may evade the action of justice or manifest conduct that makes their social risk presumable, nor those accused of the conducts provided for in the General Law to Prevent and Punish Crimes in matters of Kidnapping, Regulatory of section XXI of article 73 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States, shall not enjoy the prerogative provided in the two preceding paragraphs.

Paragraph amended DOF 30-11-2010, 17-06-2016

Once the final judgment has been issued, the penalty may be substituted by a security measure, at the discretion of the judge or court that imposes it ex officio or at the request of a party, when, due to the active subject having suffered serious consequences to their person, or due to their senility or precarious state of health, it would be notoriously unnecessary to serve said penalty, with the exception of those sentenced for the conducts provided in article 9 of the General Law to Prevent and Punish Crimes in Matters of Kidnapping, Regulatory of section XXI of article 73 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States, who in all cases must serve the imposed penalty.

Paragraph amended DOF 30-11-2010

Amendment DOF 17-06-2016: Repealed from the article the then fourth and sixth paragraphs

Article amended DOF 13-01-1984, 30-12-1991, 26-05-2004

###### Artículo 56.

The competent jurisdictional authority shall apply ex officio the most favorable law. When the subject has been sentenced to the minimum term or the maximum term of the penalty provided and the reform decreases said term, the most favorable law shall apply. When the subject has been sentenced to a penalty between the minimum term and the maximum term, the reduction resulting in the arithmetic mean term according to the new rule shall apply.

Article amended DOF 23-12-1985, 17-06-2016

##### CAPITULO II

Aplicación de sanciones a los delitos culposos

Chapter title amended DOF 13-01-1984, 10-01-1994

###### Artículo 60.- In cases of negligent crimes, up to one-fourth of the penalties and

security measures assigned by law to the basic type of intentional crime shall be imposed, with the exception of those for which the law establishes a specific penalty. In addition, suspension of up to three years of rights to exercise a profession, trade, authorization, license or permit shall be imposed, as applicable.

Paragraph amended DOF 13-01-1984, 10-01-1994. Errata DOF 01-02-1994. Amended DOF 26-12-2013

Sanctions for negligent crimes shall only be imposed in relation to the crimes provided in the following articles: 150, 167, fracción VI, 169, 199 Bis, 289, parte segunda, 290, 291, 292, 293, 302, 307, 323, 397, 399, 414, primer párrafo y tercero en su hipótesis de resultado, 415, fracciones I y II y último párrafo en su hipótesis de resultado, 416, 420, fracciones I, II, III y V, y 420 Bis, fracciones I, II y IV de este Código.

Paragraph added DOF 10-01-1994. Errata DOF 01-02-1994. Amended DOF 06-02-2002

When, as a consequence of negligent acts or omissions, classified as serious, that are attributable to personnel providing services in a railway, aeronautical, shipping or any other federal or local public service transportation company, homicides of two or more persons are caused, the penalty shall be five to twenty years of imprisonment, removal from employment, position or commission and disqualification from obtaining others of the same nature. The same penalty shall be imposed when it involves school service transportation.

Paragraph added DOF 10-01-1994. Errata DOF 01-02-1994

The classification of the gravity of the negligence is left to the prudent discretion of the judge, who must take into consideration the general circumstances indicated in article 52, and the following special ones:

Paragraph amended DOF 10-01-1994

I.- The greater or lesser ease of foreseeing and avoiding the resulting damage;

II.- The duty of care of the accused that is required of them by the circumstances and personal conditions that the trade or activity they perform impose on them;

Section amended DOF 10-01-1994

III.- Whether the accused has previously committed a crime under similar circumstances;

IV.- Whether they had time to act with the necessary reflection and care, and

V.- The state of the equipment, roadways and other mechanical operating conditions, in the case of infractions committed in the services of transportation companies, and in general, by vehicle drivers.

VI.- Se deroga.

Section added DOF 13-01-1984. Repealed DOF 10-01-1994

Article amended DOF 10-02-1945, 05-01-1955

###### Artículo 61.- In the cases referred to in the first part of the first paragraph of the preceding article se

exceptúa la reparación del daño. Whenever an intentional crime corresponds to an alternative sanction that includes a non-custodial penalty, that situation shall benefit the person responsible for the negligent crime.

Article amended DOF 10-02-1945, 15-01-1951, 10-01-1994

###### Artículo 62.- When damage to another's property not greater than the equivalent of one hundred times the salario mínimo is caused by negligence, it shall be sanctioned with a fine up to the value of the damage caused,

plus the reparation thereof. The same sanction shall apply when the negligent crime is caused on the occasion of vehicle traffic whatever the value of the damage.

When injuries are caused by negligence and on the occasion of vehicle traffic, whatever their nature, proceedings shall only be initiated at the request of the victim or their lawful representative, provided that the driver was not in a state of intoxication or under the influence of narcotics, psychotropics or any other substance that produces similar effects and has not left the victim abandoned.

Article amended DOF 24-03-1944, 15-01-1951, 05-01-1955, 19-03-1971, 13-01-1984, 19-11-1986, 10-01-1994

##### CAPITULO III

Aplicación de sanciones en caso de tentativa

###### Article 63.- The person responsible for punishable attempt shall be subject, at the judge's discretion and taking into consideration the provisions of articles 12 and 52, to up to two-thirds of the sanction that should have been imposed had the crime they intended to commit been consummated, except where otherwise provided. Paragraph errata Official Gazette 01-02-1994

In cases of attempt where it is not possible to determine the damage that was intended to be caused, when such damage is determinant for proper typical adequacy, up to half of the sanction indicated in the preceding paragraph shall be applied.

In cases of punishable attempt of serious crime so classified by law, the judicial authority shall impose a prison sentence that shall not be less than the minimum sentence and may reach up to two-thirds of the maximum sanction provided for the consummated crime.

Paragraph added Official Gazette 13-05-1996

Article reformed Official Gazette 15-01-1951, 10-01-1994

##### CHAPTER IV

Application of sanctions in case of concurrence, continued crime, complicity, recidivism and vincible error

Chapter heading reformed Official Gazette 13-01-1984, 10-01-1994

###### Article 64.- In case of ideal concurrence, the sanctions corresponding to the crime that merits the greatest penalty shall be imposed, which may be increased without exceeding half of the maximum duration of the penalties corresponding to the remaining crimes, provided that the applicable sanctions are of the same nature; when they are of different nature, the legal consequences indicated for the remaining crimes may be imposed, except in cases where one of the crimes for which there is ideal concurrence is among those contemplated in the General Law to Prevent and Punish Crimes Related to Kidnapping, and the General Law to Prevent, Punish and Eradicate Crimes Related to Trafficking in Persons and for the Protection and Assistance to Victims of these Crimes, both regulatory of section XXI of article 73 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States, in which cases the rules of real concurrence shall apply.

In case of real concurrence, the sanction of the most serious crime shall be imposed, which may be increased with the penalties contemplated by law for each of the remaining crimes, without exceeding the maximums indicated in Title Two of Book One. If the penalties were imposed in the same proceeding or in different ones, but if the facts are connected or similar, or one derived from the other, in all cases the penalties must be counted from the moment liberty was deprived for the first crime.

In case of continued crime, the criminal sanction shall be increased by up to one half of that corresponding to the maximum of the crime committed, without exceeding the maximum indicated in Title

Two of Book One.

21 of 337

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

Article reformed Official Gazette 19-12-1964, 13-01-1984, 10-01-1994, 13-05-1996, 17-05-1999, 26-05-2004, 30-11-2010, 17-06-2016

###### Article 64 Bis.- In the cases provided by sections VI, VII and VIII of article 13, a penalty of up to three-quarters of that corresponding to the crime in question shall be imposed and, where applicable, according to the respective modality.

Article added Official Gazette 13-01-1984. Reformed Official Gazette 10-01-1994

###### Article 65.- The recidivism referred to in article 20 shall be taken into account for judicial individualization of the sentence, as well as for granting or not the benefits or criminal substitutes that the law provides.

In the event that the accused for any intentional crime classified by law as serious or that merits official pretrial detention, as applicable, is a recidivist for crimes of such nature, the applicable sanction for the new crime committed shall be increased by two-thirds and up to one time more than the maximum penalty provided for it, without exceeding the maximum indicated in Title Two of

## Book One.

Reform Official Gazette 17-05-1999: Repealed from the article the then third paragraph

###### Article 66.- In the event that the error referred to in subsection a) of section VIII of article 15 is vincible, the punishability of the culpable crime shall be imposed if the act in question admits such form of realization. If the vincible error is that provided in subsection b) of said section, the penalty shall be up to one-third of the crime in question.

Article errata Official Gazette 31-08-1931. Reformed Official Gazette 12-05-1938, 24-03-1944, 05-01-1948, 10-01-1994

##### CHAPTER V

Treatment of unimputables and of those who have the habit or need to consume narcotics or psychotropics, in confinement or at liberty

Chapter heading reformed Official Gazette 13-01-1984, 10-01-1986

###### Article 67.- In the case of unimputables, the judge shall order the applicable treatment measure in confinement or at liberty, following the corresponding procedure.

If it involves confinement, the unimputable subject shall be confined in the corresponding institution for treatment.

In the event that the sentenced person has the habit or need to consume narcotics or psychotropics, the judge shall also order the appropriate treatment, by the competent health authority or another medical service under its supervision, independently of the execution of the penalty imposed for the crime committed.

Paragraph added Official Gazette 10-01-1986

Article reformed Official Gazette 13-01-1984

###### Article 68.

Unimputable persons may be delivered by the judicial or executing authority, as applicable, to those to whom it legally corresponds to take charge of them, provided they undertake to take appropriate measures for their treatment and supervision, guaranteeing, by any means and to the satisfaction of the aforementioned authorities, compliance with the obligations undertaken. The executing authority may decide on the modification or conclusion of the measure, provisionally or definitively, considering the needs of the treatment, which shall be verified through periodic reviews, with the frequency and characteristics of the case.

Article reformed Official Gazette 13-01-1984

22 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform Official Gazette 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

###### Article 69.- In no case shall the treatment measure imposed by the criminal judge exceed the duration corresponding to the maximum penalty applicable to the crime. If, upon conclusion of this time, the executing authority considers that the subject continues to need treatment, it shall place them at the disposal of the health authorities to proceed in accordance with applicable laws.

Article reformed Official Gazette 13-01-1984

###### Article 69 Bis.- If the capacity of the author to understand the unlawful character of the act or to determine themselves in accordance with that understanding is only diminished by the causes indicated in section VII of article 15 of this Code, in the judge's judgment, as appropriate, up to two-thirds of the penalty that would correspond to the crime committed shall be imposed, or the security measure referred to in article 67, or both, if necessary, taking into account the degree of affectation of the imputability of the author.

Article added Official Gazette 10-01-1994

##### CHAPTER VI

Substitution and commutation of sanctions

###### Artículo 70.- La prisión podrá ser sustituida, a juicio del juzgador, apreciando lo dispuesto en los

artículos 51 y 52 en los términos siguientes:

###### Article 70.- Imprisonment may be substituted, at the discretion of the judge, considering the provisions of

articles 51 and 52 under the following terms:

- I. Por trabajo en favor de la comunidad o semilibertad, cuando la pena impuesta no exceda de cuatro años;

- I. By community service or semi-liberty, when the imposed penalty does not exceed four years;

Fracción reformada DOF 13-05-1996

Section amended DOF 13-05-1996

II. Por tratamiento en libertad, si la prisión no excede de tres años, o

II. By treatment in liberty, if the imprisonment does not exceed three years, or

Fracción reformada DOF 13-05-1996

Section amended DOF 13-05-1996

III. Por multa, si la prisión no excede de dos años.

III. By fine, if the imprisonment does not exceed two years.

Fracción reformada DOF 13-05-1996

Section amended DOF 13-05-1996

La sustitución no podrá aplicarse a quien anteriormente hubiere sido condenado en sentencia ejecutoriada por delito doloso que se persiga de oficio. Tampoco se aplicará a quien sea condenado por algún delito de los señalados en la fracción I del artículo 85 de este Código.

The substitution may not be applied to those who have previously been convicted by final judgment for an intentional crime prosecuted ex officio. Neither shall it apply to those convicted of any crime specified in section I of article 85 of this Code.

Párrafo adicionado DOF 13-05-1996. Reformado DOF 31-12-1998, 17-05-1999

Paragraph added DOF 13-05-1996. Amended DOF 31-12-1998, 17-05-1999

###### Artículo 71.- El juez dejará sin efecto la sustitución y ordenará que se ejecute la pena de prisión

impuesta, cuando el sentenciado no cumpla con las condiciones que le fueran señaladas para tal efecto, salvo que el juzgador estime conveniente apercibirlo de que si se incurre en nueva falta, se hará efectiva la sanción sustituida o cuando al sentenciado se le condene por otro delito. Si el nuevo delito es culposo, el juez resolverá si se debe aplicar la pena sustituida.

###### Article 71.- The judge shall revoke the substitution and order the execution of the imposed prison sentence,

when the convicted person fails to comply with the conditions that were set for such purpose, unless the judge deems it appropriate to warn them that if a new violation is incurred, the substituted penalty will be enforced, or when the convicted person is sentenced for another crime. If the new crime is negligent, the judge shall decide whether the substituted penalty should be applied.

Párrafo reformado DOF 10-01-1994. Fe de erratas DOF 01-02-1994

Paragraph amended DOF 10-01-1994. Errata DOF 01-02-1994

En caso de hacerse efectiva la pena de prisión sustituida, se tomará en cuenta el tiempo durante el cual el sentenciado hubiera cumplido la sanción sustitutiva.

In case the substituted prison sentence is enforced, the time during which the convicted person has served the substitute sanction shall be taken into account.

Párrafo reformado DOF 17-06-2016

Paragraph amended DOF 17-06-2016

###### Artículo 72.- En caso de haberse nombrado fiador para el cumplimiento de los deberes inherentes a

la sustitución de sanciones, la obligación de aquél concluirá al extinguirse la pena impuesta. Cuando el fiador tenga motivos fundados para no continuar en su desempeño, los expondrá al juez, a fin de que éste, si los estima justos, prevenga al sentenciado que presente nuevo fiador dentro del plazo que prudentemente deberá fijarle, apercibido de que se hará efectiva la sanción si no lo hace. En caso de 23 de 337

###### Article 72.- In case a guarantor has been appointed for the fulfillment of the duties inherent to

the substitution of penalties, the obligation of such guarantor shall conclude when the imposed penalty is extinguished. When the guarantor has founded reasons not to continue in their performance, they shall present them to the judge, so that the judge, if considering them just, warns the convicted person to present a new guarantor within the term that shall be prudently set, with notice that the penalty will be enforced if they fail to do so. In case of 23 of 337

###### Artículo 73.

###### Article 73.

El Ejecutivo, tratándose de delitos políticos, podrá hacer la conmutación de sanciones, después de impuestas en sentencia irrevocable, conforme a las siguientes reglas:

The Executive, in the case of political crimes, may commute penalties, after they have been imposed in a final judgment, in accordance with the following rules:

I.- Cuando la sanción impuesta sea la de prisión, se conmutará en confinamiento por un término igual al de los dos tercios del que debía durar la prisión, y

I.- When the imposed penalty is imprisonment, it shall be commuted to confinement for a term equal to two-thirds of what the imprisonment should last, and

II.- Si fuere la de confinamiento, se conmutará por multa, a razón de un día de aquél por un día de multa.

II.- If it is confinement, it shall be commuted by fine, at a rate of one day of the former for one day of fine.

Fracción reformada DOF 13-01-1984

Section amended DOF 13-01-1984

###### Artículo 74.- El sentenciado que considere que al dictarse sentencia reunía las condiciones para el

disfrute de la sustitución o conmutación de la sanción y que por inadvertencia de su parte o del juzgador no le hubiera sido otorgada, podrá promover ante éste que se le conceda, abriéndose el incidente respectivo en los términos de la fracción X del artículo 90.

###### Article 74.- The convicted person who considers that at the time of sentencing they met the conditions for the

benefit of substitution or commutation of the penalty and that through inadvertence on their part or the judge's it was not granted, may request before the latter that it be granted, opening the respective incident under the terms of section X of article 90.

Párrafo reformado DOF 17-06-2016

Paragraph amended DOF 17-06-2016

En todo caso en que proceda la substitución o la conmutación de la pena, al hacerse el cálculo de la sanción substitutiva se disminuirá además de lo establecido en el último párrafo del artículo 29 de este Código, el tiempo durante el cual el sentenciado sufrió prisión preventiva.

In all cases where substitution or commutation of the penalty is appropriate, when calculating the substitute penalty, in addition to what is established in the last paragraph of article 29 of this Code, the time during which the convicted person suffered pretrial detention shall be deducted.

Párrafo adicionado DOF 30-12-1991

Paragraph added DOF 30-12-1991

###### Artículo 75.- Cuando el sentenciado acredite plenamente que no puede cumplir alguna de las

modalidades de la sanción que le fue impuesta por ser incompatible con su edad, sexo, salud o constitución física, el Juez de Ejecución podrá modificar aquélla, siempre que la modificación no sea esencial.

###### Article 75.- When the convicted person fully proves that they cannot comply with any of the

modalities of the penalty that was imposed on them because it is incompatible with their age, sex, health or physical constitution, the Execution Judge may modify it, provided that the modification is not essential.

Artículo reformado DOF 19-03-1971, 17-06-2016

Article amended DOF 19-03-1971, 17-06-2016

###### Artículo 76.- Para la procedencia de la sustitución y la conmutación, se exigirá al sentenciado la

reparación del daño o la garantía que señale el Órgano jurisdiccional para asegurar su pago, en el plazo que se le fije.

###### Article 76.- For the substitution and commutation to proceed, the convicted person shall be required to make

reparation of the damage or provide the guarantee indicated by the jurisdictional body to ensure its payment, within the term set for them.

Artículo reformado DOF 19-03-1971, 17-06-2016

###### Artículo 77.- Corresponde a la autoridad jurisdiccional la imposición de las penas, su modificación y

duración; asimismo, al Ejecutivo Federal la administración penitenciaria.

###### Article 77.- The imposition of penalties, their modification and

duration corresponds to the jurisdictional authority; likewise, penitentiary administration corresponds to the Federal Executive.

##### CAPITULO III

##### CHAPTER III

Libertad preparatoria y retención

Preparatory liberty and retention

###### Artículo 84.- Se concederá libertad preparatoria al condenado, previo el informe a que se refiere el

Código de Procedimientos Penales, que hubiere cumplido las tres quintas partes de su condena, si se trata de delitos intencionales, o la mitad de la misma en caso de delitos imprudenciales, siempre y cuando cumpla con los siguientes requisitos:

###### Article 84.- Preparatory liberty shall be granted to the convicted person, following the report referred to in the

Code of Criminal Procedures, who has served three-fifths of their sentence, in the case of intentional crimes, or half of it in the case of negligent crimes, provided they meet the following requirements:

I.- Que haya observado buena conducta durante la ejecución de su sentencia;

I.- That they have observed good conduct during the execution of their sentence;

II.- Que del examen de su personalidad se presuma que está socialmente readaptado y en condiciones de no volver a delinquir, y

II.- That from the examination of their personality it is presumed that they are socially rehabilitated and in a condition not to reoffend, and

III.- Que haya reparado o se comprometa a reparar el daño causado, sujetándose a la forma, medidas y términos que se le fijen para dicho objeto, si no puede cubrirlo desde luego. Llenados los requisitos anteriores, la autoridad competente tendrá un plazo no mayor a 30 días hábiles para conceder la libertad preparatoria o en su caso informar al interesado el resultado de su trámite, dicha libertad preparatoria estará sujeta a las siguientes condiciones:

III.- That they have made reparation or commit to making reparation for the damage caused, subject to the form, measures and terms set for such purpose, if they cannot cover it immediately. Once the above requirements are met, the competent authority shall have a period not exceeding 30 business days to grant preparatory liberty or, as the case may be, inform the interested party of the result of their proceeding; such preparatory liberty shall be subject to the following conditions:

Párrafo reformado DOF 30-06-2006

Paragraph amended DOF 30-06-2006

a).- Residir o, en su caso, no residir en lugar determinado, e informe a la autoridad de los cambios de su domicilio. La designación del lugar de residencia se hará conciliando la circunstancia de que el reo pueda proporcionarse trabajo en el lugar que se fije, con el hecho de que su permanencia en él no sea un obstáculo para su enmienda;

a).- To reside or, as the case may be, not to reside in a determined place, and to inform the authority of changes to their domicile. The designation of the place of residence shall be made reconciling the circumstance that the convict can obtain work in the place that is set, with the fact that their permanence in it is not an obstacle to their reform;

b).- Desempeñar en el plazo que la resolución determine, oficio, arte, industria o profesión lícitos, si no tuviere medios propios de subsistencia;

b).- To perform, within the term determined by the resolution, a lawful trade, art, industry or profession, if they do not have their own means of subsistence;

c).- Abstenerse del abuso de bebidas embriagantes y del empleo de estupefacientes: psicotrópicos o sustancias que produzcan efectos similares, salvo por prescripción médica;

c).- To abstain from the abuse of intoxicating beverages and from the use of narcotics: psychotropic drugs or substances that produce similar effects, except by medical prescription;

Inciso reformado DOF 31-12-1974

Subparagraph amended DOF 31-12-1974

d).- Sujetarse a las medidas de orientación y supervisión que se le dicten y a la vigilancia de alguna persona honrada y de arraigo, que se obligue a informar sobre su conducta, presentándolo siempre que para ello fuere requerida.

d).- To submit to the guidance and supervision measures that are dictated to them and to the surveillance of some honest and established person, who undertakes to report on their conduct, presenting them whenever required to do so.

Fe de erratas al artículo DOF 31-08-1931. Reformado DOF 19-03-1971

Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 19-03-1971

###### Artículo 85. No se concederá la libertad preparatoria a:

I. Los sentenciados por alguno de los delitos previstos en este Código que a continuación se señalan:

- a) Uso ilícito de instalaciones destinadas al tránsito aéreo, previsto en el artículo 172 bis, párrafo tercero;
- b) Contra la salud, previsto en el artículo 194, salvo que se trate de individuos en los que concurran evidente atraso cultural, aislamiento social y extrema necesidad económica; y para la modalidad de transportación, si cumplen con los requisitos establecidos en los artículos 84 y 90, fracción I, inciso c), para lo cual deberán ser primodelincuentes, a pesar de no hallarse en los tres supuestos señalados en la excepción general de este inciso;

Inciso reformado DOF 12-06-2003

c) Corrupción de personas menores de dieciocho años de edad o de personas que no tienen capacidad para comprender el significado del hecho o de personas que no tienen capacidad para resistirlo previsto en el artículo 201; Pornografía de personas menores de dieciocho años de edad o de personas que no tienen capacidad para comprender el significado del hecho o de personas que no tienen capacidad para resistirlo, previsto en el artículo 202; Turismo sexual en contra de personas menores de dieciocho años de edad o de personas que no tienen capacidad para comprender el significado del hecho o de personas que no tienen capacidad para resistirlo, previsto en el artículo 203 y 203 bis; Lenocinio de personas menores de dieciocho años de edad o de personas que no tienen capacidad para comprender el significado del hecho o de personas que no tienen capacidad para resistirlo, previsto en el artículo 204; Pederastia, previsto en el artículo 209 Bis;

d) Violación, previsto en los artículos 265, 266 y 266 bis;

e) Homicidio, previsto en los artículos 315, 315 Bis y 320; y feminicidio previsto en el artículo Inciso reformado DOF 14-06-2012

f) Tráfico de menores, previsto en el artículo 366 Ter.

g) Comercialización de objetos robados, previsto en el artículo 368 ter;

h) Robo de vehículo, previsto en el artículo 376 bis;

i) Robo, previsto en los artículos 371, último párrafo; 372; 381, fracciones VII, VIII, IX, X, XI y XV; y 381 Bis;

Inciso reformado DOF 26-06-2008

j) Operaciones con recursos de procedencia ilícita, previsto en el artículo 400 Bis;

Inciso reformado DOF 26-06-2008

k) Los previstos y sancionados en los artículos 112 Bis, 112 Ter, 112 Quáter y 112 Quintus de la Ley de Instituciones de Crédito, cuando quien lo cometa forme parte de una asociación, banda o pandilla en los términos del artículo 164, o 164 Bis, o

Inciso adicionado DOF 26-06-2008

l) Los previstos y sancionados en los artículos 432, 433, 434 y 435 de la Ley General de Títulos y Operaciones de Crédito, cuando quien lo cometa forme parte de una asociación, banda o pandilla en los términos del artículo 164 o 164 Bis.

II. Delitos en Materia de Trata de Personas contenidos en el Título Segundo de la Ley General para Prevenir, Sancionar y Erradicar los Delitos en Materia de Trata de Personas y para la Protección y Asistencia a las Víctimas de estos Delitos;

Fracción adicionada DOF 27-11-2007. Reformada DOF 14-06-2012

III. Los que incurran en segunda reincidencia de delito doloso o sean considerados delincuentes habituales.

Fracción recorrida DOF 27-11-2007

IV. Los sentenciados por las conductas previstas en la Ley General para Prevenir y Sancionar los Delitos en Materia de Secuestro, Reglamentaria de la fracción XXI del artículo 73 de la Constitución Política de los Estados Unidos Mexicanos, salvo las previstas en los artículos 9, 10, 11, 17 y 18.

V. Los sentenciados por el delito de Tortura.

Fracción adicionada DOF 26-06-2017

Tratándose de los delitos comprendidos en el Titulo Décimo de este Código, la libertad preparatoria solo se concederá cuando se satisfaga la reparación del daño a que se refiere la fracción III del artículo 30 o se otorgue caución que la garantice.

Artículo reformado DOF 09-03-1946, 14-01-1966, 08-03-1968, 19-03-1971, 08-12-1978, 05-01-1983, 28-12-1992, 10-01-1994. Fe de erratas

DOF 01-02-1994. Reformado DOF 17-05-1999

###### Artículo 86.- La autoridad competente revocará la libertad preparatoria cuando:

I. El liberado incumpla injustificadamente con las condiciones impuestas para otorgarle el beneficio. La autoridad podrá, en caso de un primer incumplimiento, amonestar al sentenciado y apercibirlo de revocar el beneficio en caso de un segundo incumplimiento. Cuando el liberado infrinja medidas que establezcan presentaciones frecuentes para tratamiento, la revocación sólo procederá al tercer incumplimiento, o

II. El liberado sea condenado por nuevo delito doloso, mediante sentencia ejecutoriada, en cuyo caso la revocación operará de oficio. Si el nuevo delito fuere culposo, la autoridad podrá, motivadamente y según la gravedad del hecho, revocar o mantener la libertad preparatoria.

El condenado cuya libertad preparatoria sea revocada deberá cumplir el resto de la pena en prisión, para lo cual la autoridad considerará el tiempo de cumplimiento en libertad. Los hechos que originen los nuevos procesos a que se refiere la fracción II de este artículo interrumpen los plazos para extinguir la sanción.

Artículo reformado DOF 19-03-1971, 10-01-1994, 17-05-1999

###### Artículo 87.- Los sentenciados que disfruten de libertad preparatoria, concedida por la autoridad

jurisdiccional, quedarán bajo la supervisión y vigilancia de la autoridad que al efecto determine la legislación en la materia aplicable.

Artículo reformado DOF 19-03-1971, 23-01-2009, 17-06-2016
