##### CAPITULO IV

Condena condicional

###### Artículo 90.- The granting and enjoyment of the benefits of conditional sentencing shall be subject to the

following rules:

I.- The judge or Court, as applicable, upon issuing a conviction sentence or in the scenario established by section X of this article, shall suspend with reasoning the execution of the sentences, upon request of a party or ex officio, if these conditions concur: a).- That the sentence refers to a prison term not exceeding four years;

Subsection amended DOF 30-12-1991

b) That the sentenced person is not a repeat offender for an intentional crime, has shown good conduct before and after the punishable act, and that the sentence does not refer to any of the crimes indicated in section I of article 85 of this Code, and

Subsection amended DOF 10-01-1994, 17-05-1999

c) That based on their personal background or honest way of living, as well as the nature, modalities and motives of the crime, it is presumed that the sentenced person will not commit crimes again.

Subsection amended DOF 17-05-1999

d) Repealed.

Subsection added as subsection e) DOF 05-01-1983. Repealed as subsection e) DOF 10-01-1994. Repealed as subsection d) DOF 17-05-1999

II.- To enjoy this benefit the sentenced person must: a).- Provide the guarantee or submit to the measures established for them, to ensure their appearance before the authority whenever required; b).- Commit to reside in a specific place, from which they may not be absent without permission from the authority exercising care and supervision over them; c).- Perform within the established time period a lawful profession, art, trade or occupation; d).- Refrain from the abuse of intoxicating beverages and from the use of narcotics, psychotropic drugs or other substances that produce similar effects, except by medical prescription; and

Errata to subsection DOF 07-05-1971. Amended DOF 31-12-1974 e).- Repair the damage caused.

When due to their personal circumstances they cannot immediately repair the damage caused, they shall provide a bond or submit to the measures that in the judgment of the judge or court are sufficient to ensure that they will fulfill this obligation within the established time period.

III.- The suspension shall include the prison sentence and the fine, and as for the other imposed sanctions, the judge or court shall decide at their discretion according to the circumstances of the case.

28 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Amendment DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

IV.- Offenders for whom the execution of the sentence has been suspended shall be informed of the provisions of this article, which shall be recorded in a formal proceeding, without the lack thereof preventing, as applicable, the application of what is provided therein.

V.- Sentenced persons who enjoy the benefits of conditional sentencing shall be subject to the care and supervision of the General Directorate of Coordinated Services for Prevention and Social

Rehabilitation.

VI.- In case a guarantor has been appointed for the fulfillment of the obligations undertaken under the terms of this article, the guarantor's obligation shall conclude six months after the term referred to in section VII has elapsed, provided that the offender does not give rise to a new proceeding or when an acquittal sentence is pronounced therein. When the guarantor has well-founded reasons for not continuing to perform the duty, they shall present them to the judge so that the judge, if deeming them just, may warn the sentenced person to present a new guarantor within the time period that shall be prudently established, with the warning that the sanction shall be enforced if they do not comply. In case of death or insolvency of the guarantor, the sentenced person shall be obligated to bring the fact to the judge's knowledge for such purpose and under the warning expressed in the preceding paragraph.

Section amended DOF 30-12-1991

VII.- If during the term of duration of the sentence, from the date of the sentence that becomes final the convicted person does not give rise to a new proceeding for an intentional crime that concludes with a conviction sentence, the sanction established therein shall be considered extinguished. Otherwise, the first sentence shall be enforced, in addition to the second, in which the defendant shall be recorded as a repeat offender without prejudice to what is established in article 20 of this Code.

In the case of a negligent crime, the competent authority shall decide with reasoning whether or not the suspended sanction should be applied;

Section amended DOF 30-12-1991, 10-01-1994

VIII.- The facts that give rise to the new proceeding interrupt the term referred to in section VII whether it involves an intentional or negligent crime, until a final sentence is issued;

Section amended DOF 30-12-1991, 10-01-1994

IX.- In case of failure to fulfill the obligations undertaken by the convicted person, the judge may enforce the suspended sanction or admonish them, with the warning that, if they fail again to comply with any of the established conditions, said sanction shall be enforced.

X.- The defendant who considers that when the sentence was issued they met the conditions established in this provision and is able to fulfill the other requirements that are established, if it is due to inadvertence on their part or on the part of the courts that they did not obtain in the sentence the granting of conditional sentencing, may promote that it be granted to them, opening the respective incidental proceeding before the judge of the case.

Article amended DOF 19-03-1971

##### CHAPTER I

Death of the accused or sentenced person

Chapter denomination amended DOF 17-06-2016

###### Article 91.- The death of the accused extinguishes the criminal action, as well as the sanctions that may

have been imposed on them, with the exception of the reparation of damage, precautionary measures, seizure and the forfeiture of the instruments, objects or proceeds of the crime as well as the property whose value is equivalent to said proceeds in accordance with the provisions of article 40 of this Code.

Article amended DOF 17-06-2016

##### CHAPTER II

Amnesty

###### Article 92.- Amnesty extinguishes the criminal action and the imposed sanctions, except the reparation

of damage, under the terms of the law that may be issued granting it, and if they are not expressed, it shall be understood that the criminal action and the imposed sanctions are extinguished with all their effects, in relation to all those responsible for the crime.

##### CHAPTER III

Pardon of the Offended Party or Legitimized Person to Grant It

Chapter denomination amended DOF 13-01-1984

###### Artículo 93. The forgiveness of the offended party or of the person legitimized to grant it may only be granted when

the totality of the damages and harm caused by the commission of the crime have been repaired, this extinguishes the criminal action with respect to crimes that are prosecuted by complaint, provided that it is granted before the Public Prosecutor's Office if the latter has not exercised the same or before the jurisdictional body before a second instance sentence is issued. Once forgiveness is granted, it may not be revoked.

Párrafo reformado DOF 14-06-2012

The provisions in the preceding paragraph are equally applicable to crimes that can only be prosecuted by declaration of prejudice or by some other act equivalent to a complaint, being sufficient for the extinction of the criminal action the manifestation of the person who is authorized to do so that the affected interest has been satisfied.

When there are several offended parties and each one may separately exercise the power to forgive the person responsible for the crime and the concealer, the forgiveness shall only have effects with respect to the person who grants it. Forgiveness only benefits the accused in whose favor it is granted, unless the offended party or the person legitimized to grant it has obtained the satisfaction of their interests or rights, in which case it shall benefit all the accused and the concealer.

Párrafo reformado DOF 17-06-2016

Fe de erratas al artículo DOF 31-08-1931. Reformado DOF 13-01-1984, 10-01-1994, 04-12-2002

##### CAPITULO IV

Reconocimiento de Inocencia e Indulto

Denominación del Capítulo reformada DOF 13-01-1984

###### Artículo 94.

Pardon may not be granted, except for a sanction imposed in an irrevocable sentence.

###### Artículo 95.- It may not be granted for disqualification from practicing a profession or any of the

civil or political rights, or to perform a certain office or employment, as these sanctions shall only be extinguished by amnesty or rehabilitation.

CÁMARA DE DIPUTADOS DEL H. CONGRESO DE LA UNIÓN Última Reforma DOF 08-05-2023

Secretaría General

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios

###### Artículo 96.- When it appears that the sentenced person is innocent, recognition of their

innocence shall proceed, in the terms provided by the applicable Code of Criminal Procedure and the provisions of Article 49 of this Code shall be followed.

Artículo reformado DOF 13-01-1984

###### Artículo 97.- When the conduct observed by the sentenced person reflects a high degree of social

reintegration and their release does not represent a risk to public tranquility and safety, according to the opinion of the executing body of the sanction and it is not a person sentenced for treason to the Nation, espionage, terrorism, sabotage, genocide, crimes against health, rape, intentional crime against life and kidnapping, forced disappearance, torture and trafficking in persons, nor a repeat offender for intentional crime, pardon may be granted by the Federal Executive, in the exercise of discretionary powers, expressing their reasons and grounds in the following cases:

Párrafo reformado DOF 17-06-2016

I.- For crimes of a political nature referred to in Article 144 of this Code;

II.- For other crimes when the conduct of those responsible has been determined by motivations of a political or social nature, and

III.- For crimes of federal jurisdiction or common in the Federal District, when the sentenced person has rendered important services to the Nation, and upon request.

Artículo reformado DOF 31-10-1989

###### Artículo 97 Bis.- Exceptionally, on their own initiative or at the request of the Plenary of either of the Chambers of the

Congress of the Union, the Head of the Federal Executive Power may grant pardon, for any crime of federal jurisdiction or common in the Federal District, and with prior opinion from the executing body of the sanction demonstrating that the sentenced person does not represent a danger to public tranquility and safety, expressing their reasons and grounds, when there are consistent indications of serious violations of the human rights of the sentenced person.

The Federal Executive must ensure that the sentenced person has previously exhausted all national legal remedies.

Artículo adicionado DOF 30-10-2013

###### Artículo 98.

Pardon shall in no case extinguish the obligation to repair the damage caused. Recognition of the innocence of the sentenced person extinguishes the obligation to repair the damage. Artículo reformado DOF 13-01-1984, 31-10-1989

##### CAPITULO V

Rehabilitación

###### Artículo 99.

Rehabilitation has the purpose of reintegrating the sentenced person in the civil, political or family rights that they had lost by virtue of a sentence issued in a proceeding or in whose exercise they were in suspension.

Artículo reformado DOF 17-06-2016

##### CAPITULO VI

Prescripción

###### Artículo 100.- By statute of limitations, the criminal action and sanctions are extinguished, in accordance with the

following articles.

31 de 337

CÓDIGO PENAL FEDERAL

CÁMARA DE DIPUTADOS DEL H. CONGRESO DE LA UNIÓN Última Reforma DOF 08-05-2023

Secretaría General

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios

###### Artículo 101.- The statute of limitations is personal and for it the simple passage of the time

established by law shall suffice.

The periods for the statute of limitations shall be doubled with respect to those who are outside the national territory, if due to this circumstance it is not possible to conduct an investigation, conclude a proceeding or execute a sanction.

Párrafo adicionado DOF 13-01-1984. Reformado DOF 17-06-2016

The statute of limitations shall produce its effect, even if not alleged as a defense by the accused, defendant and sentenced person. The jurisdictional body shall supply it ex officio in all cases, as soon as they become aware of it, whatever the stage of the proceeding.

Párrafo reformado DOF 17-06-2016

###### Artículo 102.

The periods for the statute of limitations of criminal action shall be continuous; in them the crime shall be considered with its modalities, and they shall be counted:

Párrafo reformado DOF 23-12-1985

I.- From the moment the crime was consummated, if it were instantaneous;

II.- From the day the last act of execution was performed or the due conduct was omitted, if the crime were in the degree of attempt;

III.- From the day the last conduct was performed, in the case of a continuing crime; and IV.- From the cessation of consummation in the permanent crime.

Artículo reformado DOF 13-01-1984

###### Artículo 103.- The periods for the statute of limitations of sanctions shall be equally continuous and shall run

from the day following that on which the convicted person evades the action of justice, if the sanctions are deprivations or restrictions of liberty, and if they are not, from the date of the enforceable sentence.

Artículo reformado DOF 23-12-1985

###### Artículo 104.

Criminal action prescribes in one year, if the crime only merits a fine; if the crime merits, in addition to this sanction, a custodial or alternative sentence, the statute of limitations of the action to prosecute the custodial sentence shall apply; the same shall be observed when any other accessory sanction should be imposed.

Artículo reformado DOF 23-12-1985

###### Artículo 105.- Criminal action shall prescribe in a period equal to the arithmetic mean term of the

custodial sentence that the law establishes for the crime in question, but in no case shall it be less than three years.

Artículo reformado DOF 23-12-1985

###### Artículo 106.

Criminal action shall prescribe in two years, if the crime only merits dismissal, suspension, deprivation of right or disqualification, except as provided in other regulations. Artículo reformado DOF 23-12-1985

###### Artículo 107.- Cuando la ley no prevenga otra cosa, la acción penal que nazca de un delito que sólo

puede perseguirse por querella del ofendido o algún otro acto equivalente, prescribirá en un año, contado desde el día en que quienes puedan formular la querella o el acto equivalente, tengan conocimiento del delito y del delincuente, y en tres, fuera de esta circunstancia.

Fe de erratas al párrafo DOF 31-08-1931. Reformado DOF 23-12-1985

Pero una vez llenado el requisito de procedibilidad dentro del plazo antes mencionado, la prescripción seguirá corriendo según las reglas para los delitos perseguibles de oficio.

Fe de erratas al párrafo DOF 31-08-1931. Reformado DOF 10-01-1994

###### Artículo 107 Bis.- El término de prescripción de los delitos previstos en el Título Octavo del Libro

Segundo de este Código cometidos en contra de una víctima menor de edad, comenzará a correr a partir de que ésta cumpla la mayoría de edad.

En el caso de aquellas personas que no tengan la capacidad de comprender el significado del hecho o de personas que no tienen capacidad para resistirlo, correrá a partir del momento en que exista evidencia de la comisión de esos delitos ante el Ministerio Público.

En los casos de los delitos contra la libertad y el normal desarrollo psicosexual, así como los previstos en la Ley para prevenir y sancionar la Trata de Personas, que hubiesen sido cometidos en contra de una persona menor de dieciocho años de edad, se observarán las reglas para la prescripción de la acción penal contenidas en este capítulo, pero el inicio del cómputo de los plazos comenzará a partir del día en que la víctima cumpla la mayoría de edad.

Párrafo adicionado DOF 14-06-2012

Artículo adicionado DOF 19-08-2010

###### Artículo 108.

En los casos de concurso de delitos, las acciones penales que de ellos resulten, prescribirán cuando prescriba la del delito que merezca pena mayor.

Fe de erratas al artículo DOF 31-08-1931. Reformado DOF 23-12-1985

###### Artículo 109.- Cuando para ejercitar o continuar la acción penal sea necesaria una resolución previa

de autoridad jurisdiccional, la prescripción comenzará a correr desde que se dicte la sentencia irrevocable.

Artículo reformado DOF 23-12-1985

###### Artículo 110.- La prescripción de las acciones se interrumpirá por las actuaciones que se practiquen

en la investigación y de los imputados, aunque por ignorarse quienes sean estos no se practiquen las diligencias contra persona determinada.

Párrafo reformado DOF 17-06-2016

Si se dejare de actuar, la prescripción empezará a correr de nuevo desde el día siguiente al de la última diligencia.

La prescripción de las acciones se interrumpirá también por el requerimiento de auxilio en la investigación del delito o de quien lo haya cometido o participado en su comisión, por las diligencias que se practiquen para obtener la extradición internacional, y por el requerimiento de entrega del imputado que formalmente haga el Ministerio Público de una entidad federativa al de otra donde aquel se refugie, se localice o se encuentre detenido por el mismo o por otro delito. En el primer caso también causarán la interrupción las actuaciones que practique la autoridad requerida y en el segundo subsistirá la interrupción hasta en tanto la autoridad requerida niegue la entrega o en tanto desaparezca la situación legal del detenido, que dé motivo al aplazamiento de su entrega.

Párrafo adicionado DOF 10-01-1994. Reformado DOF 17-06-2016

La interrupción de la prescripción de la acción penal, sólo podrá ampliar hasta una mitad los plazos señalados en los artículos 105, 106 y 107 de este Código.

Párrafo adicionado DOF 10-01-1994

Fe de erratas al artículo DOF 31-08-1931. Reformado DOF 23-12-1985

###### Artículo 111.- Las prevenciones contenidas en los dos primeros párrafos y en el primer caso del

tercer párrafo del artículo anterior, no operarán cuando las actuaciones se practiquen después de que haya transcurrido la mitad del lapso necesario para la prescripción.

###### Artículo 112.- Si para deducir una acción penal exigiere la ley previa declaración o resolución de

alguna autoridad, las gestiones que con ese fin se practiquen, antes del término señalado en el artículo precedente, interrumpirán la prescripción.

Artículo reformado DOF 15-01-1951, 23-12-1985

###### Artículo 113.- Salvo que la ley disponga otra cosa, la pena privativa de libertad prescribirá en un

tiempo igual al fijado en la condena y una cuarta parte más, pero no podrá ser inferior a tres años; la pena de multa prescribirá en un año; las demás sanciones prescribirán en un plazo igual al que deberían durar y una cuarta parte más, sin que pueda ser inferior a dos años; las que no tengan temporalidad, prescribirán en dos años. Los plazos serán contados a partir de la fecha en que cause ejecutoria la resolución.

Artículo reformado DOF 23-12-1985

###### Artículo 114.- Cuando el sentenciado hubiere extinguido ya una parte de su sanción, se necesitará

para la prescripción tanto tiempo como el que falte de la condena y una cuarta parte más, pero no podrá ser menor de un año.

Artículo reformado DOF 23-12-1985, 17-06-2016

###### Artículo 115.- La prescripción de la sanción privativa de libertad solo se interrumpe aprehendiendo al

imputado aunque la aprehensión se ejecute por otro delito diverso, o por la formal solicitud de entrega que el Ministerio Público de una entidad federativa haga al de otra en que aquél se encuentre detenido, en cuyo caso subsistirá la interrupción hasta en tanto la autoridad requerida niegue dicha entrega o desaparezca la situación legal del detenido que motive aplazar el cumplimiento de lo solicitado. Párrafo reformado DOF 17-06-2016

La prescripción de las demás sanciones se interrumpirá por cualquier acto de autoridad competente para hacerlas efectivas. También se interrumpirá la prescripción de la pena de reparación del daño o de otras de carácter pecuniario, por las promociones que el ofendido o persona a cuyo favor se haya decretado dicha reparación haga ante la autoridad fiscal correspondiente y por las actuaciones que esa autoridad realice para ejecutarlas, así como por el inicio de juicio ejecutivo ante autoridad civil usando como título la sentencia condenatoria correspondiente.

Artículo reformado DOF 23-12-1985, 10-01-1994

##### CAPITULO VII

Cumplimiento de la Pena o Medida de Seguridad

Capítulo adicionado DOF 13-01-1984. Reubicado con denominación reformada DOF 23-12-1985

###### Artículo 116.- La pena y la medida de seguridad se extinguen, con todos sus efectos, por

cumplimiento de aquéllas o de las sanciones por las que hubiesen sido sustituidas o conmutadas. Asimismo, la sanción que se hubiese suspendido se extinguirá por el cumplimiento de los requisitos establecidos al otorgarla, en los términos y dentro de los plazos legalmente aplicables. Artículo reformado DOF 23-12-1985

##### CAPITULO VIII

Supresión del tipo penal

Capítulo adicionado DOF 23-12-1985. Denominación reformada DOF 17-06-2016

###### Artículo 117.

La ley que suprime el tipo penal o lo modifique, extingue, en su caso, la acción penal o la sanción correspondiente, conforme a lo dispuesto en el artículo 56.

##### CAPITULO IX

Existence of a Prior Sentence Issued in a Proceeding for the Same

Facts

Capítulo adicionado DOF 23-12-1985

###### Artículo 118.- No one may be tried twice for the same crime, whether in the trial they are

acquitted or convicted. When a sentence has been issued in a proceeding and it appears that another exists in relation to the same person and for the same facts considered in that one, the second proceeding shall conclude by means of a resolution that the authority hearing it shall issue ex officio. If there exist two sentences for the same facts, the effects of the one issued second shall be extinguished. Fe de erratas al artículo DOF 31-08-1931. Reformado DOF 23-12-1985

Extinction of Treatment Measures for Non-Culpable Persons

Capítulo adicionado DOF 23-12-1985

###### Artículo 118 Bis.- When the non-culpable person subject to a treatment measure is a fugitive and

subsequently is detained, the execution of the treatment measure shall be considered extinguished if it is proven that the personal conditions of the subject no longer correspond to those that would have given rise to its imposition.

Artículo adicionado DOF 13-01-1984

LIBRO SEGUNDO

#### TITULO PRIMERO

Crimes against the Security of the Nation

Título con denominación reformado DOF 29-07-1970

##### CAPITULO I

Treason against the Homeland

###### Artículo 123.- A penalty of imprisonment from five to forty years and a fine up to fifty

thousand pesos shall be imposed on the Mexican who commits treason against the homeland in any of the following forms: I.- Performs acts against the independence, sovereignty or integrity of the Mexican Nation with the purpose of subjecting it to a foreign person, group or government;

35 de 337

CÓDIGO PENAL FEDERAL

CÁMARA DE DIPUTADOS DEL H. CONGRESO DE LA UNIÓN Última Reforma DOF 08-05-2023

Secretaría General

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios

II.- Takes part in acts of hostility against the Nation, through war actions under the orders of a foreign State or cooperates with it in any manner that may harm Mexico.

When nationals serve as troops, a penalty of imprisonment from one to nine years and a fine up to ten thousand pesos shall be imposed;

The person who illegally deprives a person of their liberty in the national territory to deliver them to the authorities of another country or to transfer them outside Mexico with such purpose shall be considered under the scenario provided in the first paragraph of this section.

Párrafo adicionado DOF 17-07-1992

III.- Forms part of armed groups directed or advised by foreigners; organized within or outside the country, when they have the purpose of attacking the independence of the Republic, its sovereignty, its freedom or its territorial integrity or invading the national territory, even when there is no declaration of war;

IV.- Willfully destroys or removes the markers that mark the limits of the national territory, or causes them to be confused, provided that this originates a conflict for the Republic, or it is in a state of war;

V.- Recruits people to make war against Mexico, with the aid or under the protection of a foreign government;

VI.- Has, in times of peace or war, relations or intelligence with a foreign person, group or government or gives them instructions, information or advice, with the object of guiding a possible invasion of the national territory or of altering internal peace;

VII.- Willfully and without authorization provides, in times of peace or war, to a foreign person, group or government, documents, instructions or data of establishments or of possible military activities;

VIII.- Conceals or aids whoever commits acts of espionage, knowing that they perform them;

IX.- Provides to a foreign State or to armed groups directed by foreigners, the human or material elements to invade the national territory, or facilitates their entry to military posts or delivers or causes to be delivered to them combat units or food or war stores or prevents Mexican troops from receiving these supplies;

X.- Requests the intervention or establishment of a protectorate of a foreign State or requests that it make war against Mexico; if what is requested is not carried out, the imprisonment shall be from four to eight years and a fine up to ten thousand pesos;

XI.- Invites individuals of another State to take up arms against Mexico or invade the national territory, whatever the motive taken; if any of these acts is not carried out, a penalty of four to eight years of imprisonment and a fine up to ten thousand pesos shall be applied;

XII.- Attempts to alienate or encumber the national territory or contributes to its dismemberment; XIII.- Receives any benefit, or accepts a promise to receive it, with the purpose of carrying out any of the acts indicated in this article;

36 de 337

CÓDIGO PENAL FEDERAL

CÁMARA DE DIPUTADOS DEL H. CONGRESO DE LA UNIÓN Última Reforma DOF 08-05-2023

Secretaría General

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios

XIV.- Accepts from the invader an employment, position or commission and issues, agrees or votes provisions aimed at affirming the intrusive government and weakening the national one; and

XV.- Commits, once war is declared or hostilities are broken, sedition, mutiny, rebellion, terrorism, sabotage or conspiracy.

Artículo reformado DOF 29-07-1970

###### Artículo 124.- A penalty of imprisonment from five to twenty years and a fine up to twenty-five thousand

pesos shall be applied to the Mexican who:

I.- Without complying with constitutional provisions, celebrates or executes treaties or pacts of offensive alliance with any State, that produce or may produce war of Mexico with another, or admits foreign troops or war units in the country;

II.- In case of a foreign invasion, contributes to the establishment of a de facto government in places occupied by the enemy, whether by giving their vote, attending meetings, signing records or representations or by any other means;

III.- Accepts from the invader an employment, position or commission, or whoever, in the occupied place, having obtained it legitimately performs it in favor of the invader; and

IV.- With acts not authorized or approved by the government, provokes a foreign war with Mexico, or exposes Mexicans to suffer vexations or reprisals because of this.

Artículo reformado DOF 15-01-1951, 05-01-1955, 29-07-1970

###### Artículo 125.- A penalty of two to twelve years of imprisonment and a fine of one thousand to twenty thousand pesos shall

be applied to whoever incites the people to recognize the government imposed by the invader or to accept a foreign invasion or protectorate.

Artículo reformado DOF 15-01-1951, 29-07-1970

###### Artículo 126.- The same penalties shall be applied to foreigners who intervene in the commission of the

crimes referred to in this Chapter, with the exception of those provided in sections VI and VII of article

123.

Artículo reformado DOF 15-01-1951, 29-07-1970

##### CAPITULO II

Espionage

Capítulo reubicado DOF 29-07-1970


This excerpt sits under: CÓDIGO PENAL FEDERAL > Libro Primero.

###### Article 127.- A penalty of five to twenty years imprisonment and a fine of up to fifty thousand

pesos shall be imposed on the foreigner who in time of peace, with the purpose of guiding a possible invasion of the national territory or of disturbing the internal peace, has relations or intelligence with a foreign person, group or government or gives them instructions, information or advice.

The same penalty shall be imposed on the foreigner who in time of peace provides, without authorization to a foreign person, group or government, documents, instructions, or any data on establishments or possible military activities.

A penalty of five to forty years imprisonment and a fine of up to fifty thousand pesos shall be imposed on the foreigner who, upon declaration of war or outbreak of hostilities against Mexico, has relations or intelligence with the enemy or provides them with information, instructions or documents or any assistance that in any way harms or may harm the Mexican Nation.

Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 15-01-1951, 29-07-1970

###### Article 128.- A penalty of five to twenty years imprisonment and a fine of up to fifty thousand

pesos shall be imposed on the Mexican who, having in his possession confidential documents or information of a foreign government, reveals them to another government, if by doing so he harms the Mexican Nation. Article amended DOF 29-07-1970

###### Article 129.- A penalty of six months to five years imprisonment and a fine of up to five thousand

pesos shall be imposed on anyone who, having knowledge of the activities of a spy and of his identity, does not make it known to the authorities.

Article amended DOF 14-11-1941, 15-01-1951, 05-01-1955, 29-07-1970

##### CHAPTER III

Sedition

Chapter relocated with amended denomination DOF 29-07-1970

###### Article 130.- A penalty of six months to eight years imprisonment and a fine of up to ten thousand

pesos shall be imposed on those who in a tumultuous manner without use of arms, resist or attack the authority to prevent the free exercise of its functions with any of the purposes referred to in Article 132. Those who direct, organize, incite, compel or financially sponsor others to commit the crime of sedition shall be subject to a penalty of five to fifteen years imprisonment and a fine of up to twenty thousand pesos.

Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 15-01-1951, 29-07-1970

##### CHAPTER IV

Riot

Chapter added DOF 29-07-1970

###### Article 131.- A penalty of six months to seven years imprisonment and a fine of up to five thousand

pesos shall be imposed on those who, in order to exercise a right or under the pretext of its exercise or to avoid compliance with a law, assemble tumultuously and disturb public order through the use of violence against persons or property, or threaten the authority to intimidate it or force it to take some determination.

Those who direct, organize, incite, compel or financially sponsor others to commit the crime of riot shall be subject to a penalty of two to ten years imprisonment and a fine of up to fifteen thousand pesos. Article amended DOF 29-07-1970

##### CHAPTER V

Rebellion

Chapter added DOF 29-07-1970

###### Article 132.- A penalty of two to twenty years imprisonment and a fine of five thousand to fifty thousand

pesos shall be imposed on those who, not being military personnel in active service, through violence and use of arms attempt to: I.- Abolish or reform the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States;

II.- Reform, destroy or prevent the integration of the constitutional institutions of the Federation, or their free exercise; and

III.- Remove or prevent from performing their duties any of the high officials of the Federation mentioned in Article 2 of the Law on Responsibilities of Officials and Employees of the Federation, the Federal District and High Officials of the States. Subsection amended DOF 23-12-1974

Article amended DOF 15-01-1951, 29-07-1970

38 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Amendment DOF 08-05-2023 General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

###### Article 133.- The penalties specified in the preceding article shall be imposed on anyone who, residing in territory

occupied by the Federal Government, and without physical or moral coercion, provides the rebels with weapons, ammunition, money, provisions, means of transportation or communication or prevents the Government troops from receiving such assistance. If he resides in territory occupied by the rebels, the imprisonment shall be from six months to five years.

The public official or employee of the Federal or State Governments, or of the Municipalities, of decentralized public agencies, of state-owned enterprises, or of public services, federal or local, who by reason of his position has documents or reports of strategic interest and provides them to the rebels, shall be subject to a penalty of five to forty years imprisonment and a fine of five thousand to fifty thousand pesos.

Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 29-07-1970

###### Article 134.- A penalty of two to twenty years imprisonment and a fine of five thousand to fifty thousand

pesos shall be imposed on those who, not being military personnel in active service, through violence and use of arms, attack the Government of any of the States of the Federation, against its constitutional institutions or to achieve the removal from office of any of the high officials of the State, when the Powers of the Union intervene in the manner prescribed by Article 122 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States, and the rebels do not lay down their arms.

Article amended DOF 15-01-1951, 29-07-1970

###### Article 135.- A penalty of one to twenty years imprisonment and a fine of up to fifty thousand

pesos shall be imposed on anyone who:

I.- In any form or by any means invites to a rebellion;

II.- Residing in territory occupied by the Government:

- a) Conceals or assists the spies or scouts of the rebels, knowing that they are;
- b) Maintains relations with the rebels, to provide them with news concerning military operations or others that are useful to them.

III.- Voluntarily serves an employment, position or commission in a place occupied by the rebels, unless he acts under coercion or for humanitarian reasons.

Article amended DOF 15-01-1951, 29-07-1970

###### Article 136.- Government officials or agents and rebels who after combat

directly or by means of orders cause the death of prisoners, shall be subject to a penalty of fifteen to thirty years imprisonment and a fine of ten thousand to twenty thousand pesos.

Article amended DOF 29-07-1970

###### Article 137.

When during a rebellion the crimes of homicide, robbery, kidnapping, dispossession, arson, looting or other crimes are committed, the rules of concurrence shall be applied.

The rebels shall not be liable for homicides or injuries inflicted in the act of combat, but for those caused outside of it, both the person who orders them and the person who permits them and those who immediately execute them shall be liable.

Article amended DOF 15-01-1951, 29-07-1970

###### Article 138.- No penalty shall be imposed on those who lay down their arms before being taken prisoner, if

they have not committed any of the crimes mentioned in the preceding article. Article amended DOF 29-07-1970

39 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Amendment DOF 08-05-2023 General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

##### CHAPTER VI

Terrorism

Chapter added DOF 29-07-1970

###### Artículo 139.- A penalty of imprisonment of fifteen to forty years and a fine of four hundred to one

thousand two hundred days shall be imposed, without prejudice to the penalties corresponding to other resulting offenses:

- I. On whoever, using toxic substances, chemical, biological or similar weapons, radioactive material, nuclear material, nuclear fuel, radioactive mineral, radiation source or instruments that emit radiation, explosives, or firearms, or by fire, flood or any other violent means, intentionally performs acts against goods or services, whether public or private, or against the physical or emotional integrity, or the life of persons, which produce alarm, fear or terror in the population or in a group or sector thereof, to attack national security or to pressure the authority or a private individual, or to compel the latter to make a determination.
- II. On whoever agrees upon or prepares a terrorist act that is intended to be committed, is being committed or has been committed in national territory.

The penalties referred to in the first paragraph of this article shall be increased by one half, when in addition:

- I. The offense is committed against real property of public access;
- II. Damage or detriment is generated to the national economy, or
- III. In the commission of the offense a person is held hostage.

Artículo reformado DOF 29-07-1970, 28-06-2007, 14-03-2014

###### Artículo 139

Bis.- A penalty of one to nine years of imprisonment and a fine of one hundred to three hundred days shall be imposed on whoever harbors a terrorist, having knowledge of his activities or his identity. Artículo adicionado DOF 28-06-2007

###### Artículo 139 Ter.- A penalty of five to fifteen years of imprisonment and a fine of two hundred to six hundred

days shall be imposed on whoever threatens to commit the offense of terrorism referred to in the first paragraph of

###### artículo 139.

Artículo adicionado DOF 28-06-2007

##### CAPÍTULO VI BIS

Del Financiamiento al Terrorismo Capítulo adicionado DOF 14-03-2014

###### Artículo 139 Quáter.- The same penalty provided in article 139 of this Code shall be imposed, without

prejudice to the penalties corresponding to other resulting offenses, on whoever by any means whatsoever, whether directly or indirectly, contributes or collects economic funds or resources of any nature, with knowledge that they will be used to finance or support activities of terrorist individuals or organizations, or to be used, or are intended to be used, directly or indirectly, totally or partially, for the commission, in national territory or abroad, of any of the offenses provided in the following legal regulations:

### I. Del Código Penal Federal, los siguientes:

1) Terrorismo, previstos en los artículos 139, 139 Bis y 139 Ter;

2) Sabotaje, previsto en el artículo 140;

Quáter of this Code.

##### CAPITULO VII

Sabotaje

Capítulo adicionado DOF 29-07-1970

###### Artículo 140.

A penalty of two to twenty years of imprisonment and a fine of one thousand to fifty thousand pesos shall be imposed on whoever damages, destroys, harms or unlawfully obstructs communication routes, public services, functions of State agencies, decentralized public bodies, State-owned enterprises, autonomous constitutional bodies or their facilities; steel, electric or basic industries plants; centers of production or distribution of necessary consumer goods, weapons, ammunition or war implements, with the purpose of disrupting the economic life of the country or affecting its defense capacity.

Párrafo reformado DOF 14-07-2014

A penalty of six months to five years of imprisonment and a fine up to five thousand pesos shall be imposed on whoever, having knowledge of the activities of a saboteur and his identity, does not report it to the authorities.

The penalties referred to in the first paragraph of this article shall be increased by up to one half, when the acts of sabotage are carried out on the pipelines, equipment, facilities or assets of assignees, contractors, permit holders or distributors referred to in the Federal Law to Prevent and Punish Offenses Committed in Hydrocarbon Matters.

Párrafo adicionado DOF 12-01-2016

Artículo reformado DOF 29-07-1970

##### CAPITULO VIII

Conspiración

Capítulo adicionado DOF 29-07-1970

###### Artículo 141.- A penalty of one to nine years of imprisonment and a fine up to ten thousand pesos shall be imposed on

those who jointly resolve to commit one or more of the offenses of this Title and agree upon the means to carry out their determination.

Artículo reformado DOF 29-07-1970

##### CAPITULO IX

Disposiciones comunes para los capítulos de este Título

Capítulo adicionado DOF 29-07-1970

###### Artículo 142.- Whoever instigates, incites or invites the execution of the offenses provided in this Title shall be

subject to the same penalty provided for the offense in question, with the exception of what is established in the second paragraph of article 130, in the second paragraph of article 131 and in section I of article 135, which preserve their specific penalty.

Whoever instigates, incites or invites military personnel in service, to the execution of the offenses referred to in this Title, shall be subject to a penalty of five to forty years of imprisonment, with the exception of the offense of terrorism, the penalty for which shall be eight to forty years of imprisonment and a fine of five hundred to one thousand one hundred and fifty days. Párrafo reformado DOF 28-06-2007

Artículo reformado DOF 15-01-1951, 29-07-1970

###### Artículo 143.- When other offenses result from the commission of the offenses referred to in this Title,

the rules of concurrence shall apply.

In addition to the penalties provided in this Title, those responsible, if they are Mexican, shall be subject to suspension of their political rights for a period of up to ten years, which shall be computed from the fulfillment of their sentence. In the offenses included in chapters I and II of this Title, suspension of such rights shall be imposed for up to forty years.

Fe de erratas al artículo DOF 31-08-1931. Reformado DOF 29-07-1970

###### Artículo 144.- The offenses of rebellion, sedition, riot and conspiracy to commit them are considered offenses of

political character.

Artículo reformado DOF 15-01-1951, 29-07-1970

###### Artículo 145.- A penalty of five to forty years of imprisonment and one hundred twenty to one thousand one hundred

fifty days' fine shall be applied to an official or employee of the Federal or State Governments, or of the Municipalities, of decentralized public agencies, of state-owned enterprises or public services, federal or local, or of autonomous constitutional bodies, who commits any of the crimes provided for in this Title, with the exception of the crime of terrorism, for which the penalty shall be nine to forty-five years of imprisonment and five hundred to one thousand one hundred fifty days' fine.

###### Artículo 145-Bis.- (Repealed).

Article added DOF 14-11-1941. Repealed (by comprehensive reform of the First Title of the Second Book) DOF 29-07-1970 Note: By Decree DOF 29-07-1970, the then Second Title "Crimes against the internal security of the Nation" was repealed and deleted from the Second Book, with its Chapters I "Rebellion", II "Sedition and other public disorders" and III "Social dissolution crimes" (Chapter III previously added DOF 14-11-1941). Consequently, the subsequent Third to Twenty-fourth Titles were renumbered to become the current Second to Twenty-third Titles of the Second Book.

TITULO SEGUNDO

### CRIMES AGAINST INTERNATIONAL LAW

Title renumbered (formerly Title Third) DOF 29-07-1970

##### CAPITULO I

Piracy

###### Artículo 146.- The following shall be considered pirates:

42 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023 General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

I.- Those who, belonging to the crew of a Mexican merchant vessel, of another nation, or without nationality, seize by armed force any vessel, or commit depredations on it, or commit violence against persons on board;

II.- Those who, going aboard a vessel, seize it and voluntarily deliver it to a pirate, and

III.- Repealed.

Fraction repealed DOF 21-06-2018

###### Artículo 147.- Fifteen to thirty years of imprisonment and confiscation of the vessel shall be imposed on those who

belong to a pirate crew.

##### CAPITULO II

Violation of immunity and neutrality

###### Artículo 148.- Imprisonment of three days to two years and a fine of one hundred to two thousand pesos shall be applied for:

I.- The violation of any diplomatic immunity, real or personal, of a foreign sovereign, or of the representative of another nation, whether they reside in the Republic or are passing through it;

II.- The violation of the duties of neutrality that correspond to the Mexican nation, when done consciously;

III.- The violation of the immunity of a parliamentarian, or that granted by a safe-conduct, and

IV.- Any attack or violence of any kind against the shields, emblems or flags of another

foreign Nation.

Fraction reformed DOF 06-01-2023

In the case of fraction III, and if the circumstances warrant it, judges may impose up to six years of imprisonment.

Note: By Decree DOF 20-01-1967, the then Chapter III "Violations of the rights of humanity in prisoners, hostages, wounded or hospitals" was suppressed, due to the addition of a Fourth Title to the Second Book.

##### CAPITULO III

INTERNATIONAL TERRORISM

Chapter added DOF 28-06-2007

###### Artículo 148 Bis.- A penalty of imprisonment of fifteen to forty years and four hundred to one thousand

two hundred days' fine shall be imposed, without prejudice to the penalties corresponding to other crimes that may result:

I. On whoever, using toxic substances, chemical, biological or similar weapons, radioactive material, nuclear material, nuclear fuel, radioactive mineral, radiation source or instruments that emit radiation, explosives or firearms, or by fire, flood or by any other violent means, carries out in Mexican territory, acts against property, persons or services, of a foreign State, or of any international organism or organization, that produce alarm, fear or terror in the population or in a group or sector thereof, to pressure the authority of that foreign State, or to compel it or an international organism or organization to take a determination;

43 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

II. On whoever commits the crime of homicide or any act against the freedom of an internationally protected person;

III. On whoever carries out, in Mexican territory, any violent act against official premises, private residences or means of transport of an internationally protected person, that threatens their life or freedom, or

IV. On whoever agrees or prepares in Mexican territory a terrorist act that is intended to be committed, is being committed or has been committed abroad.

For the purposes of this article, an internationally protected person shall be understood as a head of State including each of the members of a collegiate body when, in accordance with the respective constitution, they fulfill the functions of head of State, a head of government or a minister of foreign affairs, as well as the members of their family who accompany them and, in addition, any representative, official or official personality of a State or any official, official personality or other agent of an intergovernmental organization who, at the time and place where a crime is committed against them, the members of their family who live with them, their official premises, their private residence or their means of transport, is entitled to special protection under international law.

Article added DOF 28-06-2007. Reformed DOF 14-03-2014

###### Artículo 148 Ter.- A penalty of five to ten years of imprisonment and one hundred to three hundred days'

fine shall be imposed on whoever conceals a terrorist, having knowledge of their identity or that they carry out any of the activities provided for in this chapter.

Article added DOF 28-06-2007

###### Artículo 148 Quáter.- A penalty of six to twelve years of imprisonment and two hundred to six hundred

days' fine shall be applied to whoever threatens to commit the crime of terrorism referred to in fractions I to III of

###### artículo 148 Bis.

Article added DOF 28-06-2007. Reformed DOF 14-03-2014

TITULO TERCERO

### Crimes against humanity

Title added DOF 20-01-1967. Renumbered (formerly Title Fourth) DOF 29-07-1970

##### CAPITULO I

Violation of the duties of humanity Chapter added DOF 20-01-1967

###### Artículo 149.- Whoever violates the duties of humanity in prisoners and hostages of war, in the

wounded, or in field hospitals, shall be subject for that fact alone to: imprisonment of three to six years, except as provided, for special cases, in military laws.

##### CAPITULO II

Genocide

Chapter added DOF 20-01-1967

###### Article 149-Bis.- Commits the crime of genocide whoever with the purpose of destroying, totally or

partially one or more national groups or of an ethnic, racial or religious character, perpetrates by any means, crimes against the life of members thereof, or imposes mass sterilization with the purpose of preventing the reproduction of the group.

44 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

For such crime, twenty to forty years of prison and a fine of fifteen thousand to twenty thousand pesos shall be imposed.

If with identical purpose attacks are carried out on the bodily integrity or health of the members of said communities or minors under eighteen years of age are transferred from them to other groups, using physical or moral violence for this purpose, the penalty shall be five to twenty years of prison and a fine of two thousand to seven thousand pesos.

Paragraph reformed DOF 09-03-2018

The same penalties indicated in the preceding paragraph shall be applied to whoever with the same purpose intentionally subjects the group to conditions of existence that are to bring about its physical destruction, total or partial.

In the event that those responsible for said crimes were rulers, officials or public employees and they committed them in the exercise of their functions or by reason thereof, in addition to the penalties established in this article, the penalties indicated in article 15 of the Law of Responsibilities of Officials and Employees of the Federation shall be applied to them.

Article added DOF 20-01-1967

Title Third Bis

Crimes against the Dignity of Persons

Title added DOF 14-06-2012

##### Sole Chapter

Discrimination

Chapter added DOF 14-06-2012

###### Article 149

Ter. A penalty of one to three years of prison or one hundred fifty to three hundred days of work for the benefit of the community and up to two hundred days fine shall be applied to whoever for reasons of ethnic or national origin or belonging, race, skin color, language, gender, sex, sexual preference, age, marital status, national or social origin, social or economic condition, health condition, pregnancy, political opinions or of any other nature attacks human dignity or annuls or diminishes the rights and freedoms of persons by carrying out any of the following conducts:

- I. Denies a person a service or benefit to which they are entitled;
- II. Denies or restricts labor rights, mainly by reason of gender or pregnancy; or limits a health service, mainly to women in relation to pregnancy; or
- III. Denies or restricts educational rights.

For the public servant who, for the reasons provided in the first paragraph of this article, denies or delays to a person a procedure, service or benefit to which they are entitled, the penalty provided in the first paragraph of this article shall be increased by one half, and in addition, dismissal and disqualification from holding any public position, employment or commission shall be imposed, for the same period of the deprivation of liberty imposed.

All those measures tending to the protection of socially disadvantaged groups shall not be considered discriminatory.

When the conducts referred to in this article are committed by a person with whom the victim has a relationship of labor subordination, the penalty shall be increased by one half.

45 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

Likewise, the penalty shall be increased when the discriminatory acts limit access to the legal guarantees indispensable for the protection of all human rights.

This crime shall be prosecuted by complaint.

Article added DOF 14-06-2012

TITLE FOURTH

Crimes against public security

Title moved DOF 20-01-1967. Moved (formerly Title Fifth) DOF 29-07-1970

##### CHAPTER I

Evasion of prisoners

###### Article 150.- Six months to nine years of prison shall be applied to whoever favors the evasion of

any detained, prosecuted or convicted person. If the detained or prosecuted person were charged with crime or crimes against health, the person who favors their evasion shall be imposed seven to fifteen years of prison, or, in the case of the evasion of a convicted person, they shall be increased to up to twenty years of prison.

If the person who facilitates the evasion were a public servant, the penalty shall be increased by one third of the penalties indicated in this article, as applicable. In addition, they shall be dismissed from their employment and shall be disqualified from obtaining another for a period of eight to twelve years.

Article reformed DOF 17-11-1986

###### Article 151.- Repealed.

Article repealed DOF 27-03-2017

###### Article 152.- Whoever favors at the same time, or in a single act, the evasion of several persons

deprived of liberty by the competent authority, shall be imposed up to one half more of the penalties of deprivation of liberty indicated in article 150, as applicable.

Article reformed DOF 17-11-1986

###### Article 153.

If the reapprehension of the fugitive is achieved through efforts of the person responsible for the evasion, ten to one hundred eighty days of work for the benefit of the community shall be applied to them, according to the seriousness of the crime charged to the prisoner or detainee, except as provided by article 150, second paragraph. Article reformed DOF 10-01-1994, 27-03-2017

###### Article 154.- The person deprived of their liberty who flees shall be imposed six months to three

years of prison, this penalty shall be increased by one third when the person acts in concert with another or other persons deprived of their liberty and any of them flees or exercises violence against persons. Article reformed DOF 27-03-2017

##### CHAPTER II

Breach of penalty

###### Article 155.- For the offender who flees while under any of the penalties of deprivation of liberty, or in

detention or preventive prison, the time spent outside the place where they must serve it shall not be counted, nor shall the good conduct they had before the escape be taken into account.

###### Article 156.- (Repealed).

Article repealed DOF 25-05-2011

46 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

###### Article 157.- The person sentenced to confinement who leaves the place that has been fixed as the place of

their residence before extinguishing it, shall be applied prison for the time remaining to extinguish the confinement.

###### Article 158.- Fifteen to ninety days of work for the benefit of the community shall be imposed:

Paragraph reformed DOF 10-01-1994

I.- On the offender subject to police surveillance who does not provide to it the reports requested about their conduct, and

II.- On the person who has been prohibited from going to a certain place or residing in it, if they violate the prohibition.

If the convicted person were so for a serious crime thus classified by law, the aforementioned penalty shall be one to four years of prison.

Paragraph added DOF 13-05-1996

###### Article 159.- The offender suspended in their profession or trade, or disqualified from exercising them, who breaches

their sentence, shall pay a fine of twenty to one thousand pesos. In case of recidivism, the fine shall be doubled and prison of one to six years shall be applied.

Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931

##### CHAPTER III

Prohibited weapons

###### Artículo 160.

Whoever carries, manufactures, imports, sells or stockpiles without a lawful purpose or with the intention to attack, instruments that can be used for attack or defense, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to six years and from five hundred to one thousand times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update, as well as confiscation.

Párrafo reformado DOF 30-12-1991, 19-02-2021

Public servants may carry the weapons necessary for the exercise of their office, subject to the regulations of the respective laws.

These crimes, whose jurisdiction belongs to the common jurisdiction, shall be punished without prejudice to the provisions of the Federal Law on Firearms and Explosives, of federal application with respect to these objects.

Artículo reformado DOF 15-01-1951, 13-01-1984

###### Artículo 161.

A special license is required for the carrying or sale of pistols or revolvers.

###### Artículo 162.- A penalty of six months to three years of imprisonment or 180 to 360 days fine and confiscation shall be applied:

Párrafo reformado DOF 15-01-1951, 30-12-1991

I.- Repealed.

Fracción derogada DOF 19-02-2021

II.- To whoever offers pistols or revolvers for sale, lacking the necessary permit;

III.- Repealed.

Fracción derogada DOF 19-02-2021

IV.- To whoever, without a lawful purpose or without the corresponding permit, stockpiles weapons, and 47 de 337

CÓDIGO PENAL FEDERAL

CÁMARA DE DIPUTADOS DEL H. CONGRESO DE LA UNIÓN Última Reforma DOF 08-05-2023

Secretaría General

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios

V.- To whoever, without a license, carries any weapon mentioned in article 161.

In all cases included in this article, in addition to the penalties indicated, the weapons shall be confiscated.

Officials and agents of the authority may carry the weapons necessary for the exercise of their office.

Disposición derogatoria DOF 10-11-1939. Artículo restablecido por disposición de vigencia DOF 27-12-1941

###### Artículo 163.- The granting of licenses referred to in article 161 shall be made by the Executive of the

Union through the Department or Secretariat that it designates, subject to the provisions of the respective regulatory law, and to the following:

I.- The sale of weapons included in article 161 may only be made by commercial establishments provided with a license and never by private individuals, and

II.- Whoever applies for a license to carry weapons must comply with the following requirements: Fe de erratas al párrafo DOF 31-08-1931 a). Provide a bond for the amount set by the authority, and b). Prove the need for carrying weapons and their record of honor and prudence, with the testimony of five persons well known to the authority.

##### CAPITULO IV

Criminal associations

###### Artículo 164.- Whoever forms part of an association or gang of three or more persons with the purpose of

committing crimes, shall be punished with imprisonment from five to ten years and from one hundred to three hundred days fine. Párrafo reformado DOF 08-02-1999

When the member of the association is or has been a public servant of a police corporation, the penalty referred to in the preceding paragraph shall be increased by one half and, in addition, dismissal from public employment, office or commission and disqualification from one to five years to hold another shall be imposed. If the member of the association belongs to the Mexican Armed Forces in retirement, reserve or active status, likewise the penalty shall be increased by one half and, in addition, definitive discharge from the Armed Force to which they belong shall be imposed and they shall be disqualified from one to five years to hold public office or commission.

Párrafo reformado DOF 10-01-1994

Fe de erratas al artículo DOF 31-08-1931. Reformado DOF 03-01-1989

###### Artículo 164 Bis.- When a crime is committed by a gang, those who participate in

its commission shall be subject to up to one half more of the penalties that correspond to them for the crime or crimes committed.

For the purposes of this provision, a gang is understood to be the habitual, occasional or temporary gathering of three or more persons who, without being organized for criminal purposes, commit a crime in common.

When the member of the gang is or has been a public servant of a police corporation, the penalty shall be increased up to two thirds of the penalties that correspond to them for the crime or crimes committed and, in addition, dismissal from public employment, office or commission and disqualification from one to five years to hold another shall be imposed.

Artículo adicionado DOF 08-03-1968. Reformado DOF 03-01-1989

TITULO QUINTO

Crimes regarding means of communication and correspondence

Fe de erratas a la denominación DOF 31-08-1931. Denominación reformada DOF 15-01-1951. Título recorrido DOF 20-01-1967. Recorrido (antes Título Sexto) DOF 29-07-1970

##### CAPITULO I

Attacks on means of communication and violation of correspondence

Fe de erratas a la denominación del Capítulo DOF 31-08-1931. Denominación reformada DOF 15-01-1951

###### Artículo 165.- Public roads are transit routes habitually intended for public

use, whoever the owner may be, and whatever the means of locomotion permitted and the dimensions it may have; excluding the sections that are within the limits of populated areas.

###### Artículo 166.- Whoever removes, cuts or destroys the moorings that hold a vessel or other

vehicle, or removes the obstacle that prevents or moderates its movement, shall be punished with imprisonment from fifteen days to two years if no damage results; if damage is caused, the corresponding penalty for the resulting crime shall also be applied.

Artículo reformado DOF 15-01-1951

###### Artículo 166 Bis.- Persons who by reason of their position or employment in

telecommunications companies, unlawfully provide information about persons who make use of those means of communication, shall be punished with a penalty of three months to three years of imprisonment and shall be dismissed from their position.

In the aforementioned case, the penalty shall be increased up to one half when the crime is committed by a public servant in the exercise of their duties. Likewise, in addition to the penalties indicated, dismissal from employment shall be imposed and they shall be disqualified from one to ten years to hold public office or commission.

Artículo adicionado DOF 14-07-2014

###### Artículo 167.- A penalty of one to five years imprisonment and one hundred to ten thousand days' fine shall be imposed:

Párrafo reformado DOF 15-01-1951. Fe de erratas DOF 31-05-1956. Reformado DOF 11-01-1982, 17-05-1999 I.- For the mere act of removing or modifying without due authorization: one or more ties, rails, nails, screws, plates and other similar objects that secure them, or a railroad switch of public use;

II.- To whoever destroys or removes one or more posts, insulators, wires, machines or devices, used in telegraph service; any of the components of the telecommunications network, used in telephone service, switching or radiocommunication, or any component of a magnetic or electromagnetic energy production installation or its means of transmission;

Fracción reformada DOF 17-05-1999, 14-07-2014

III.- To whoever, in order to stop vehicles on a public road, or prevent the passage of a locomotive, or derail it or the cars, removes or destroys the objects mentioned in section I, places any hindrance, or any suitable obstacle;

IV.- For the burning of a car, or any other vehicle intended for cargo transport and that is not part of a train in which any person is present;

V.- To whoever floods in whole or in part, a public road or pours water on it in a manner that causes damage;

49 de 337

CÓDIGO PENAL FEDERAL

CÁMARA DE DIPUTADOS DEL H. CONGRESO DE LA UNIÓN Última Reforma DOF 08-05-2023

Secretaría General

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios

VI.- To whoever fraudulently or for profit, interrupts or interferes with communications, wired, wireless or fiber optic, whether telegraphic, telephonic or satellite, through which audio, video or data signals are transferred;

Fracción reformada DOF 17-05-1999

VII. To whoever destroys in whole or in part, or paralyzes by another means specified in the preceding sections, a machine used on a railroad, or a vessel, or destroys or damages a bridge, a dike, a causeway or road, or a track;

Fracción reformada DOF 28-06-2007

VIII. To whoever with the purpose of harming or hindering communications, modifies or alters the mechanism of a vehicle causing it to lose power, speed or safety, and

Fracción adicionada DOF 15-01-1951. Reformada DOF 28-06-2007

IX. To whoever disseminates or transmits false information that in any way harms or may harm the safety of an aircraft, a vessel or another type of federal public service vehicle.

Fracción adicionada DOF 11-01-1982. Derogada DOF 07-11-1996. Adicionada DOF 28-06-2007

###### Artículo 168.- To whoever, for the execution of the acts referred to in the preceding articles, uses

explosives, imprisonment of fifteen to twenty years shall be applied.

###### Artículo 168

bis.- A penalty of six months to two years imprisonment and three hundred to three thousand days' fine shall be imposed on whoever without right:

- I. Deciphers or decodes telecommunications signals other than satellite signals carrying programs;

Fracción reformada DOF 01-07-2020

II. Transfers the ownership, use or enjoyment of devices, instruments or information that allow deciphering or decoding telecommunications signals other than satellite signals carrying programs, or

Fracción reformada DOF 01-07-2020

III. Receives or distributes an encrypted satellite signal carrying originally encoded programs, without authorization from the legal distributor of the signal.

Fracción adicionada DOF 01-07-2020

Artículo adicionado DOF 17-05-1999

###### Artículo 168

ter. A penalty of twelve to fifteen years imprisonment shall be imposed on whoever manufactures, markets, acquires, installs, carries, uses or operates equipment that blocks, cancels or nullifies cellular telephone signals, radiocommunication or data or image transmission signals, with the exception of what is established in the second paragraph of article 190 Bis of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law.

The equipment referred to in the first paragraph of article 190 Bis of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law, shall be secured in terms of what is established in the National Code of Criminal Procedures and subsequently must be completely destroyed.

If the crime referred to in the first paragraph of this article, were committed by public servants, and without express written authorization duly certified by their immediate superior, a penalty of fifteen to eighteen years imprisonment shall be imposed.

Artículo adicionado DOF 24-01-2020

Secretaría General

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios

###### Artículo 169.- To whoever sets in motion a locomotive, car, truck or similar vehicle and abandons it or,

in any other way, makes control of its speed impossible and may cause damage, a penalty of one to six years imprisonment shall be imposed.

###### Artículo 170.- To whoever using explosives or incendiary materials, or by any other means totally or

partially destroys air or maritime navigation installations or services or airports that provide services to civil aviation, any fixed platform, or a vessel, aircraft, or other vehicle of federal or local public service, or that provides services to the public, if they were occupied by one or more persons, a penalty of twenty to thirty years imprisonment shall be applied.

If no person is present in the vehicle, installation or platform in question, imprisonment of five to twenty years shall be applied.

Likewise, a penalty of three to twenty years imprisonment and one hundred to four hundred days' fine shall be imposed, without prejudice to the penalty corresponding to other crimes committed, on whoever through violence, threats or deceit, seizes or exercises control of a fixed platform, air or maritime navigation installations or services or airports that provide services to civil aviation; as well as a vessel, aircraft, railway machine or train, buses or any other means of collective, interstate or international public transport, or causes them to deviate from their route or destination.

When committed by a public servant of any police corporation, any of the crimes contemplated in this article and article 168, in addition to the penalties indicated in these articles, dismissal from employment shall be imposed and they shall be disqualified for one to ten years from holding public office or commission. If whoever commits the aforementioned crimes were a member of the Mexican Armed Forces in retirement, reserve or active status, they shall also be imposed definitive discharge from the Armed Force to which they belong and shall be disqualified for one to ten years from holding public office or commission.

For purposes of this article, fixed platform shall be understood as an artificial island, installation or structure permanently attached to the seabed or continental shelf for purposes of exploration or exploitation of resources or other economic purposes.

Artículo reformado DOF 05-01-1955, 24-12-1968, 10-01-1994, 14-03-2014

###### Artículo 171.- Imprisonment of up to six months, fine of up to one hundred pesos and suspension or

loss of the right to use a driver's license shall be imposed:

I.- (Se deroga)

Fracción derogada DOF 30-12-1991

II.- To whoever in a state of inebriation or under the influence of narcotic drugs commits any violation of traffic and circulation regulations while driving motor vehicles, independently of the sanction corresponding if they cause damage to persons or property.

Artículo reformado DOF 15-01-1951

###### Article 172.

When any damage is caused by means of any vehicle, motor or machinery, in addition to applying the sanctions for the resulting crime, the offender shall be disqualified from operating such devices for a period of not less than one month nor more than one year. In case of recidivism, the disqualification shall be definitive.

###### Article 172 Bis.- Any person who, for the purpose of carrying out criminal activities, uses or permits the use of

aerodromes, airports, heliports, landing strips or any other installation intended for air traffic that is his property or under his charge and care, shall be subject to imprisonment of two to six years and from one hundred to three hundred days' fine and confiscation of the instruments, objects or proceeds of the crime, whatever their nature. If such installations are clandestine, the penalty shall be increased by up to one-half.

The same penalties shall be imposed on those who carry out clandestine flights, or provide the means to facilitate the landing or takeoff of aircraft, or provide refueling or maintenance to aircraft used in such activities.

If the criminal activities referred to in the first paragraph relate to crimes against health, the penalties of imprisonment and fine shall be doubled.

Any person who constructs, installs, conditions or puts into operation the properties and installations referred to in the first paragraph, without having observed the concession, notice or permit rules contained in the respective legislation, shall be subject to three to eight years' imprisonment and from one hundred fifty to four hundred days' fine.

The sanctions provided for in this article shall be imposed without prejudice to the measures provided for by the General Roads of Communication Law and the sanctions that may apply, as the case may be, for other crimes committed.

Artículo adicionado DOF 10-01-1986. Reformado DOF 10-01-1994

##### CHAPTER II

Violation of correspondence

###### Article 173.- Three to one hundred eighty days of work in favor of the community shall be applied to:

Párrafo reformado DOF 30-12-1991, 10-01-1994

I.- Any person who improperly opens a written communication that is not addressed to him, and

II.- Any person who improperly intercepts a written communication that is not addressed to him, even if he keeps it sealed and does not learn of its contents.

The crimes provided for in this article shall be prosecuted by complaint.

Párrafo adicionado DOF 30-12-1991

###### Article 174.- Parents who open or intercept written communications addressed to their minor children, and guardians with respect to persons under their dependence, and spouses between themselves, shall not be considered to be acting criminally.

###### Article 175.- The provision of article 173 does not include correspondence circulated by the post office, with respect to which the provisions of postal legislation shall be observed.

###### Article 176.- An employee of a telegraph, telephone station or wireless station who

consciously fails to transmit a message delivered to him for that purpose, or to communicate to the addressee a message received from another office, if he causes damage, shall be subject to imprisonment of fifteen days to one year or from 30 to 180 days' fine.

TITLE SIXTH

Crimes Against Authority

##### CHAPTER I

Disobedience and resistance of individuals

###### Article 178.- Any person who, without legitimate cause, refuses to provide a service of public interest to which the

Law obliges him, or disobeys a legitimate order of the authority, shall be subject to fifteen to two hundred days of work in favor of the community.

Any person who disobeys the order of house arrest or the prohibition to leave a geographical demarcation, issued by a competent judicial authority, shall be subject to six months to two years' imprisonment and from ten to two hundred days' fine.

Párrafo adicionado DOF 08-02-1999

Artículo reformado DOF 30-12-1991, 10-01-1994

###### Article 178 Bis.- A natural person or, as the case may be, the representative of a legal person who is

required by the Public Prosecutor's Office or by the competent authority to collaborate or provide information for the geographical location, in real time, of communication devices in terms of the provisions of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law, that are related to investigations on organized crime, crimes against health, kidnapping, extortion, threats or any of those provided for in chapter II of Title Ninth of the Federal Penal Code and who willfully refuses to do so, shall be subject to a penalty of imprisonment of 3 to 8 years and from five thousand to ten thousand days' fine.

The same penalties shall apply to the natural person, or as the case may be to the representative of the legal person who willfully obstructs, delays without just cause or refuses to collaborate in the interception of private communications, or to provide information to which they are obligated, in the terms of the applicable legislation.

The same penalties shall apply to the natural person, or as the case may be to the representative of the legal person who is required by the competent authorities to collaborate or provide information for the geographical location, in real time, of communication devices in terms of the provisions of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law and who willfully refuses to do so. Artículo adicionado DOF 17-04-2012. Reformado DOF 14-07-2014

###### Article 179.

Any person who without legal excuse refuses to appear before the authority to give his statement, when legally required, shall not be considered guilty of the crime provided for in the preceding article, except when he insists on his disobedience after having been compelled by the judicial authority or warned by the administrative authority, as the case may be, to appear to testify.

###### Article 180.

Imprisonment of one to two years and a fine of ten to one thousand pesos shall be applied to: any person who, using force, threat or menace, opposes the exercise by the public authority or its agents of any of its functions or resists compliance with a legitimate order executed in legal form.
