##### CAPITULO I

Production, possession, trafficking, proselytism and other acts regarding narcotics

Capítulo adicionado DOF 14-02-1940. Capítulo declarado Ley de Emergencia DOF 12-05-1945. Declaratoria terminada DOF 28-12-1945.

Denominación reformada DOF 14-11-1947, 08-03-1968, 31-12-1974, 10-01-1994

###### Artículo 193.- Se consideran narcóticos a los estupefacientes, psicotrópicos y demás sustancias o

plants determined by the General Health Law, international conventions and treaties of mandatory observance in Mexico and those indicated by other applicable legal provisions on the matter.

For the purposes of this chapter, punishable are the conducts related to narcotics, psychotropics and other substances provided for in articles 237, 245, sections I, II, and III and 248 of the General Health Law, which constitute a serious problem for public health.

The judge, when individualizing the penalty or security measure to be imposed for the commission of any crime provided for in this chapter, shall take into account, in addition to that established in articles 51 and 52, the quantity and type of narcotic in question, as well as the lesser or greater injury or endangerment of public health and the personal conditions of the author or participant of the act or recidivism as the case may be.

The narcotics used in the commission of the crimes referred to in this chapter shall be placed at the disposal of the federal health authority, which shall proceed in accordance with the provisions or laws on the matter to their lawful use or destruction.

In the case of instruments and vehicles used to commit the crimes considered in this chapter, as well as objects and proceeds of those crimes, whatever the nature of said assets, the provisions of articles 40 and 41 shall apply. For that purpose, the Public Prosecutor shall order during the preliminary investigation the corresponding seizure and the appropriate destination in support of the administration of justice, or shall request it in the process, and shall promote confiscation so that the assets in question or their proceeds are destined to the administration of justice, or shall promote as the case may be, the 55 de 337

Artículo reformado DOF 14-11-1947, 08-03-1968, 31-12-1974, 14-01-1985, 10-01-1994

###### Article 194.- Imprisonment of ten to twenty-five years and a fine of one hundred to five hundred days shall be imposed on whoever:

I.- Produces, transports, traffics, trades, supplies even gratuitously or prescribes any of the narcotics indicated in the previous article, without the corresponding authorization referred to in the General Health Law;

For the purposes of this subsection, producing means: manufacturing, making, elaborating, preparing or conditioning any narcotic, and trading means: selling, buying, acquiring or alienating any narcotic.

Supply means the material transmission in a direct or indirect manner, for any reason, of the possession of narcotics.

Paragraph added DOF 20-08-2009

The trade and supply of narcotics may be investigated, prosecuted and, where applicable, sanctioned by the authorities of common jurisdiction in the terms of the General Health Law, when the assumptions of article 474 of said regulation are met.

Paragraph added DOF 20-08-2009

II.- Introduces or extracts from the country any of the narcotics included in the previous article, even if momentarily or in transit.

If the introduction or extraction referred to in this subsection is not consummated, but from the acts performed it clearly follows that this was the agent's purpose, the applicable penalty shall be up to two-thirds of that provided in this article.

III.- Provides economic resources or of any kind, or collaborates in any manner in the financing, supervision or promotion to enable the execution of any of the crimes referred to in this chapter; and

IV.- Carries out acts of advertising or propaganda, so that any of the substances included in the previous article are consumed.

The same penalties provided in this article and, in addition, deprivation of office or commission and disqualification to hold another for up to five years, shall be imposed on the public servant who, in the exercise of their functions or taking advantage of their position, permits, authorizes or tolerates any of the conducts indicated in this article.

Article amended DOF 14-11-1947, 08-03-1968, 31-12-1974, 08-12-1978, 10-01-1986, 30-12-1991, 10-01-1994

###### Article 195.- Five to fifteen years of imprisonment and one hundred to three hundred fifty days

fine shall be imposed on whoever possesses any of the narcotics indicated in article 193, without the corresponding authorization referred to in the General Health Law, provided that such possession is for the purpose of carrying out any of the conducts provided in article 194, both of this code. The possession of narcotics may be investigated, prosecuted and, where applicable, sanctioned by the authorities of common jurisdiction in the terms of the General Health Law, when the assumptions of article 474 of said regulation are met.

When the accused possesses any of the narcotics indicated in the table provided in article 479 of the General Health Law, in an amount equal to or greater than that resulting from multiplying by one thousand those referred to therein, it is presumed that the possession has as its object to commit any of the conducts provided in

###### article 194 of this code.

Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 08-03-1968, 31-12-1974, 08-12-1978, 03-01-1989, 10-01-1994, 20-08-2009

###### Article 195 bis.- When due to the circumstances of the fact the possession of any of the narcotics indicated in article 193, without the authorization referred to in the General Health Law, cannot be considered destined to carry out any of the conducts referred to in article 194, a penalty of four to seven years six months of imprisonment and fifty to one hundred fifty days fine shall be applied. The Federal Public Prosecutor's Office shall not proceed criminally for this crime against the person who possesses:

- I. Medications containing narcotics, whose sale to the public is subject to special acquisition requirements, when by their nature and quantity such medications are those necessary for the treatment of the person who possesses them or of other persons subject to the custody or care of whoever has them in their power.
- II. Peyote or hallucinogenic mushrooms, when by the quantity and circumstances of the case it may be presumed that they will be used in the ceremonies, uses and customs of indigenous peoples and communities, thus recognized by their own authorities.

For the purposes of this chapter, possession means: the material holding of narcotics or when these are within the radius of action and availability of the person.

The possession of narcotics may be investigated, prosecuted and, where applicable, sanctioned by the authorities of common jurisdiction in the terms of the General Health Law, when the assumptions of article 474 of said regulation are met.

Article added DOF 10-01-1994. Amended DOF 20-08-2009

###### Article 196.- The penalties that may be applicable for the crimes provided in article 194

shall be increased by one half, when:

I.- It is committed by public servants in charge of preventing, reporting, investigating or judging the commission of crimes against health or by a member of the Mexican Armed Forces in a retired, reserve or active status. In this case, such public servants shall additionally be imposed suspension to hold office or commission in the public service, for up to five years, or removal, and disqualification for up to a time equal to the imprisonment penalty imposed. If it is a member of the Mexican Armed Forces in any of the mentioned situations, they shall additionally be imposed definitive discharge from the Armed Force to which they belong, and shall be disqualified for up to a time equal to the imprisonment penalty imposed, to hold public office or commission where applicable;

II.- The victim is a minor or incapacitated to understand the relevance of the conduct or to resist the agent;

III.- Minors or incapacitated persons are used to commit any of those crimes; IV.- It is committed in educational, welfare, police or detention centers, or in their vicinity with those who attend them;

V.- The conduct is carried out by professionals, technicians, assistants or personnel related to the health disciplines in any of their branches and they take advantage of that situation to commit them. In this case, in addition, suspension of rights or functions for the professional practice or trade for up to five years and disqualification for up to a time equivalent to the imprisonment imposed shall be imposed;

VI.- The agent induces another person to commit any crime provided in article 194, taking advantage of the family or moral influence or the authority or hierarchy they have over them; and VII.- It involves the owner possessor, lessee or usufructuary of an establishment of any nature and uses it to carry out any of the crimes provided in this chapter or allows its commission by third parties. ###### Artículo 196

Ter.- A penalty of five to fifteen years of imprisonment and one hundred to three hundred days fine, as well as confiscation of the instruments, objects and products of the crime, shall be imposed on whoever diverts or by any means contributes to the diversion of chemical precursors, essential chemical products or machines, to the cultivation, extraction, production, preparation or conditioning of narcotics in any form prohibited by law.

###### Artículo 197.- Whoever, without a prescription from a legally authorized physician, administers to another

person, whether by injection, inhalation, ingestion or by any other means, any narcotic referred to in article 193, shall be imposed three to nine years of imprisonment and sixty to one hundred eighty days fine, whatever the amount administered. The penalties shall be increased by up to one half more if the victim is a minor or incapable of understanding the relevance of the conduct or of resisting the agent. Whoever improperly supplies free of charge or prescribes to a third party, of legal age, any narcotic mentioned in article 193, for their personal and immediate use, shall be imposed two to six years of imprisonment and forty to one hundred twenty days fine. If the person who acquires it is a minor or incapable, the penalties shall be increased by up to one half.

The same penalties of the preceding paragraph shall be imposed on whoever induces or assists another to consume any of the narcotics indicated in article 193.

Artículo reformado DOF 14-11-1947, 08-03-1968, 31-12-1974, 08-12-1978, 03-01-1989, 10-01-1994

###### Artículo 198.

Whoever, engaging as a principal activity in work typical of the countryside, sows, cultivates or harvests marijuana plants, poppy, hallucinogenic mushrooms, peyote or any other plant that produces similar effects, on their own account, or with financing from third parties, when they have scant education and extreme economic need, shall be imposed imprisonment of one to six years. 58 de 337

The same penalty shall be imposed on whoever, on land of their ownership, tenure or possession, consents to the sowing, cultivation or harvest of said plants in circumstances similar to the preceding hypothesis. If in the conducts described in the two preceding paragraphs the circumstances specified in them do not concur, the penalty shall be up to two thirds of that provided in article 194, provided that the sowing, cultivation or harvest is done with the purpose of carrying out any of the conducts provided in sections I and II of said article. If that purpose is lacking, the penalty shall be two to eight years of imprisonment.

If the crime were committed by a public servant of some police corporation, they shall also be imposed dismissal from public employment, position or commission and shall be disqualified from one to five years from holding another, and if the crime were committed by a member of the Mexican Armed Forces in retirement, reserve or active status, they shall be imposed, in addition to the penalty of imprisonment indicated, permanent discharge from the Armed Force to which they belong and shall be disqualified from one to five years from holding public position or commission.

The sowing, cultivation or harvest of marijuana plants shall not be punishable when these activities are carried out for medical and scientific purposes under the terms and conditions of the authorization issued for such purpose by the Federal Executive.

###### Artículo 199.- The Public Prosecutor's Office or the judicial authority with jurisdiction, as soon as it learns that

a person related to any proceeding for the crimes provided in articles 195 or 195 bis, is drug-dependent, shall immediately inform and, as appropriate, involve the competent health authorities, for purposes of the corresponding treatment.

In every detention center, rehabilitation services shall be provided to the drug-dependent person. For the granting of conditional sentence or the benefit of preparatory release, when they apply, the drug dependency shall not be considered as an antecedent of bad conduct, but in all cases it shall be required that the sentenced person submit to the corresponding medical treatment for their rehabilitation, under supervision of the executing authority.

Del peligro de contagio

###### Artículo 199 Bis.- Whoever, knowing that they are sick with a venereal disease or other

serious disease in an infectious period, endangers the health of another by contagion, through sexual relations or other transmissible means, shall be sanctioned with three days to three years of imprisonment and up to forty days fine. If the disease suffered were incurable, the penalty of six months to five years of imprisonment shall be imposed. When it involves spouses, cohabiting partners, legal action may only be taken upon complaint of the offended party.

###### Artículo 199

Ter. Whoever commits the crime provided in article 466 of the General Health Law with violence, shall be imposed five to fourteen years of imprisonment and up to one hundred twenty days fine. Artículo adicionado DOF 14-06-2012

###### Artículo 199

Quáter. Whoever implants in a woman a fertilized ovum, when they have used for this purpose another's ovum or sperm from an unauthorized donor, without the express consent of the patient, of the donor or with the consent of a minor or of a person incapable of understanding the meaning of the act or of resisting it, shall be sanctioned with four to seven years of imprisonment and up to seventy days fine.

If the crime is committed with violence or if a pregnancy results from it, the applicable penalty shall be five to fourteen years and up to one hundred twenty days fine.

In addition to the penalties provided, suspension from exercising the profession or, in the case of public servants, disqualification from holding public employment, position or commission, for a time equal to that of the penalty of imprisonment imposed, as well as dismissal, shall be imposed.

When between the active and passive party there exists a relationship of marriage, cohabitation or partnership relationship, the crimes provided in the preceding articles shall be prosecuted upon complaint.

If children result as a consequence of the commission of any of the crimes provided in the preceding articles, the reparation of damages shall also include payment of support for them and for the mother, under the terms established by civil legislation.

Artículo adicionado DOF 14-06-2012

###### Artículo 199

Quintus. Commits the crime of induced sterility whoever without the consent of a person performs on them surgical, chemical or any other type of procedures to make them sterile.

The person responsible for induced sterility shall be sentenced to four to seven years of imprisonment and up to seventy days fine, as well as full payment for reparation of damages and harm caused, which may include the corresponding surgical procedure to reverse the sterility.

In addition to the penalties indicated in the preceding paragraph, the responsible party shall be imposed suspension from employment or profession for a period equal to that of the imprisonment penalty imposed up to definitive disqualification, provided that by virtue of its exercise harm has resulted for the victim; or, in the event that the responsible party is a public servant, they shall be deprived of the public employment, position or commission they have been performing, provided that by virtue of its exercise they have committed said typical conduct. Article added DOF 14-06-2012

###### Artículo 199

Sextus. The crimes provided for in this chapter shall be prosecutable ex officio, except for those indicated by complaint of the offended party.

Article added DOF 14-06-2012

TÍTULO SÉPTIMO BIS

### CRIMES AGAINST THE INDEMNITY OF PRIVACY OF SEXUAL INFORMATION

Title added DOF 15-06-2018

##### CAPÍTULO I

Communication of Sexual Content with Persons Under Eighteen Years of Age or Persons who do not have the Capacity to understand the Meaning of the Act or Persons who do not have the Capacity to Resist it

Chapter added DOF 15-06-2018

60 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023 General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

###### Artículo 199 Septies.- Four to eight years of imprisonment and a fine of four hundred to one thousand

days fine shall be imposed on whoever, making use of broadcasting, telecommunications, computer or any other means of data transmission, contacts a person under eighteen years of age, one who does not have the capacity to understand the meaning of the act or a person who does not have the capacity to resist it and requests from them images, audio or video of explicit sexual activities, acts of sexual connotation, or solicits a sexual encounter from them.

Article added DOF 15-06-2018

##### CAPÍTULO II

Violation of Sexual Intimacy Chapter added DOF 01-06-2021

###### Artículo 199 Octies.- Commits the crime of violation of sexual intimacy, that person who

discloses, shares, distributes or publishes images, videos or audios of intimate sexual content of a person of legal age, without their consent, their approval or their authorization. As well as whoever video records, audio records, photographs, prints or produces, images, audios or videos with intimate sexual content of a person without their consent, without their approval, or without their authorization. These conducts shall be sanctioned with a penalty of three to six years of imprisonment and a fine of five hundred to one thousand Units of Measure and Update.

Article added DOF 01-06-2021

###### Artículo 199 Nonies.- The same sanctions provided for in the preceding article shall be imposed when the

images, videos or audios of intimate sexual content that are disclosed, shared, distributed or published do not correspond with the person who is indicated or identified in them. Article added DOF 01-06-2021

###### Artículo 199 Decies.- The minimum and maximum of the penalty shall be increased up to one half:

I.- When the crime is committed by the spouse, partner or cohabitant, or by any person with whom the victim has or has had a romantic, affective or trust relationship;

II.- When the crime is committed by a public servant in the exercise of their functions; III.- When it is committed against a person who cannot understand the meaning of the act or does not have the capacity to resist it;

IV.- When some type of non-profit benefit is obtained;

V.- When it is done for profit purposes, or

VI.- When as a consequence of the effects or impacts of the crime, the victim attempts against their integrity or against their own life.

Article added DOF 01-06-2021

TITULO OCTAVO

### CRIMES AGAINST THE FREE DEVELOPMENT OF PERSONALITY.

Denomination reformed DOF 14-01-1966. Title moved DOF 20-01-1967. Moved (formerly Title Ninth) DOF 29-07-1970.

Denomination reformed DOF 27-03-2007

##### CAPÍTULO I

61 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023 General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

Corruption of Persons Under Eighteen Years of Age or Persons who do not have the Capacity to understand the Meaning of the Act or Persons who do not have the Capacity to Resist it.

###### Artículo 200.

Whoever trades, distributes, exhibits, circulates or offers, to persons under eighteen years of age, books, writings, recordings, films, photographs, printed advertisements, images or objects, of a pornographic nature, real or simulated, whether physically, or through any means, shall be sentenced to six months to five years of imprisonment and three hundred to five hundred days fine.

That which signifies or has as its purpose scientific, artistic or technical dissemination, or as the case may be, sexual education, education on reproductive function, prevention of sexually transmitted diseases and adolescent pregnancy, shall not be understood as pornographic or harmful material, provided that they are approved by the competent authority.

Article reformed DOF 14-02-1940, 14-01-1966, 21-01-1991, 27-03-2007

###### Artículo 201.

Commits the crime of corruption of minors, whoever forces, induces, facilitates or procures one or more persons under 18 years of age or one or more persons who do not have the capacity to understand the meaning of the act or one or more persons who do not have the capacity to resist it to perform any of the following acts:

Paragraph reformed DOF 18-07-2016

a) Habitual consumption of alcoholic beverages;

b) Consumption of toxic substances or the consumption of any of the narcotics referred to in

###### first paragraph of article 193 of this Code or to drug dependence;

c) Begging for purposes of exploitation;

d) Commission of a crime;

e) Being part of a criminal association; or

Subsection reformed DOF 18-07-2016

f) Performing acts of bodily or sexual exhibitionism, simulated or not, with lascivious or sexual purpose. Whoever commits this crime shall be subject to: in the case of subsection a) or b) imprisonment of five to ten years and a fine of five hundred to one thousand days; in the case of subsection c) imprisonment of four to nine years and four hundred to nine hundred days fine; in the case of subsection d) the provisions of article 52, of Chapter I, of Title Three, of this Code shall apply; in the case of subsection e) or f) imprisonment of seven to twelve years and a fine of eight hundred to two thousand five hundred days.

When it concerns begging due to a situation of poverty or abandonment, it shall be addressed by social assistance.

Corruption shall not be understood as the preventive, educational or any other programs that public, private or social institutions design and provide that have as their purpose sex education, education on reproductive function, the prevention of sexually transmitted diseases and teenage pregnancy, provided they are approved by the competent authority; photographs, video recordings, audio recordings or still or moving images, printed, recorded or contained or reproduced in magnetic, electronic or other media and that constitute family memories.

62 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

In case of doubt, the judge shall request expert opinions to evaluate the conduct in question. When it is not possible to determine with precision the age of the offended person or persons, the judge shall request the corresponding expert opinions.

Article reformed DOF 14-01-1966, 08-03-1968, 31-12-1974, 03-01-1989, 10-01-1994, 04-01-2000, 27-03-2007

###### Article 201 BIS.- It is prohibited to employ persons under eighteen years of age or

persons who do not have capacity to understand the meaning of the act, in canteens, taverns, bars, nightclubs, vice centers or any other place where their healthy physical, mental or emotional development is negatively affected.

The violation of this provision shall be punished with imprisonment of one to three years and three hundred to seven hundred days fine, in case of recidivism, the definitive closure of the establishment shall be ordered. The same penalty shall be imposed on mothers, fathers, guardians or curators who accept or promote that their daughters or sons under eighteen years of age or persons under eighteen years of age or persons who are under their care, custody or guardianship, be employed in the aforementioned establishments.

For the purposes of this provision, a person under eighteen years of age who, for a salary, for food alone, for commission of any kind or for any other stipend or emolument, or gratuitously, provides their services in such place shall be considered as employed in the canteen, tavern, bar or vice center.

Article added DOF 04-01-2000. Reformed DOF 27-03-2007

###### Article 201 bis 1. Repealed

Article added DOF 04-01-2000. Repealed DOF 27-03-2007

###### Article 201 bis 2. Repealed

Article added DOF 04-01-2000. Repealed DOF 27-03-2007

###### Article 201 bis 3. Repealed

Article added DOF 04-01-2000. Repealed DOF 27-03-2007

##### CHAPTER II

Pornography of Persons Under Eighteen Years of Age or of Persons who do not have Capacity to Understand the Meaning of the Act or of Persons who do not have Capacity to Resist it.

Denomination reformed DOF 03-01-1989, 04-01-2000. Chapter relocated with denomination reformed DOF 27-03-2007

###### Article 202.- Commits the crime of pornography of persons under eighteen years of age or of

persons who do not have capacity to understand the meaning of the act or of persons who do not have capacity to resist it, whoever procures, compels, facilitates or induces, by any means, one or more of these persons to perform sexual acts or bodily exhibitionism with lascivious or sexual purposes, real or simulated, with the purpose of video recording them, photographing them, filming them, exhibiting them or describing them through printed advertisements, transmission of data files on public or private telecommunications networks, computer systems, electronic or substitutes. The perpetrator of this crime shall be imposed a penalty of seven to twelve years of imprisonment and eight hundred to two thousand days fine.

Whoever fixes, prints, video records, photographs, films or describes acts of bodily or lascivious or sexual exhibitionism, real or simulated, in which one or more persons under eighteen years of age or one or more persons who do not have capacity to understand the meaning of the act or one or more persons who do not have capacity to resist it participate, shall be imposed the penalty of seven to twelve 63 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

years of imprisonment and eight hundred to two thousand days fine, as well as the forfeiture of the objects, instruments and products of the crime.

The same penalty shall be imposed on whoever reproduces, stores, distributes, sells, purchases, leases, exhibits, publicizes, transmits, imports or exports the material referred to in the preceding paragraphs. Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Reformed DOF 14-01-1966, 27-03-2007

###### Article 202 BIS.- Whoever stores, purchases, leases, the material referred to in the preceding paragraphs, without purposes of commercialization or distribution shall be imposed one to five years of imprisonment and one hundred to five hundred days fine. Likewise, they shall be subject to specialized psychiatric treatment. Article added DOF 27-03-2007

##### CHAPTER III

Sexual Tourism against Persons Under Eighteen Years of Age or of Persons who do not have Capacity to Understand the Meaning of the Act or of Persons who do not have Capacity to Resist it.

Denomination reformed DOF 13-01-1984. Chapter relocated with denomination reformed DOF 27-03-2007

###### Article 203. Commits the crime of sexual tourism whoever promotes, publicizes, invites, facilitates or manages by any means that one or more persons travel inside or outside the national territory with the purpose of performing any type of real or simulated sexual acts with one or more persons under eighteen years of age, or with one or more persons who do not have capacity to understand the meaning of the act or with one or more persons who do not have capacity to resist it. The perpetrator of this crime shall be imposed a penalty of seven to twelve years of imprisonment and eight hundred to two thousand days fine.

Article reformed DOF 15-01-1951, 30-12-1997, 04-01-2000, 27-03-2007

###### Article 203 BIS.- Whoever performs any type of real or simulated sexual acts with one or more persons under eighteen years of age, or with one or more persons who do not have capacity to understand the meaning of the act or with one or more persons who do not have capacity to resist it, by virtue of sexual tourism, shall be imposed a penalty of twelve to sixteen years of imprisonment and two thousand to three thousand days fine, likewise, they shall be subject to specialized psychiatric treatment.

Article added DOF 27-03-2007

##### CHAPTER IV

Pimping of Persons Under Eighteen Years of Age or of Persons who do not have Capacity to Understand the Meaning of the Act or of Persons who do not have Capacity to Resist it.

Chapter relocated with denomination reformed DOF 27-03-2007

###### Artículo 204.- Commits the crime of pandering of persons under eighteen years of age or of

persons who lack the capacity to understand the meaning of the act or of persons who lack the capacity to resist it:

I.- Any person who exploits the body of the aforementioned persons, through carnal commerce or obtains any profit therefrom;

II.- Whoever induces or solicits any of the aforementioned persons, to sexually commerce with their body or facilitates the means for them to engage in prostitution, and

64 de 337

CÓDIGO PENAL FEDERAL

CÁMARA DE DIPUTADOS DEL H. CONGRESO DE LA UNIÓN Última Reforma DOF 08-05-2023

Secretaría General

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios

III.- Whoever manages, administers or maintains directly or indirectly, brothels, houses of assignation or places of assembly dedicated to exploiting the prostitution of persons under eighteen years of age or of persons who lack the capacity to understand the meaning of the act or of persons who lack the capacity to resist it, or obtains any benefit from their proceeds.

The person responsible for this crime shall be sentenced to imprisonment of eight to fifteen years and one thousand to two thousand five hundred days' fine, as well as definitive closure of the establishments described in section III. Artículo reformado DOF 27-03-2007

##### CAPÍTULO V

Trafficking of Persons Under Eighteen Years of Age or of Persons who Lack the Capacity to Understand the Meaning of the Act or of Persons who Lack the Capacity to Resist It.

Capítulo adicionado DOF 27-03-2007

###### Artículo 205. Derogado.

Artículo derogado DOF 14-01-1966. Adicionado DOF 13-01-1984. Reformado DOF 03-01-1989, 04-01-2000, 27-03-2007. Derogado DOF

###### Artículo 205

Bis. The sanctions indicated in articles 200, 201, 204 and 209 Quáter shall be imprescriptible. Likewise, the sanctions indicated in said articles shall be increased to double the corresponding amount when the perpetrator has, with respect to the victim, any of the following relationships: Párrafo reformado DOF 27-11-2007, 14-06-2012, 25-04-2023

a) Those who exercise parental authority, custody or care;

b) Ascendants or descendants without limit of degree;

c) Relatives in the collateral line up to the fourth degree;

d) Guardians or curators;

e) Whoever exercises authority over the victim by virtue of an employment, teaching, domestic, medical or any other relationship that implies subordination of the victim;

f) Whoever uses public office to commit the crime;

g) Whoever lives in the same domicile as the victim;

h) A minister of a religious denomination;

i) When the perpetrator employs physical, psychological or moral violence against the victim; and

j) Whoever is linked to the victim by an affective or friendship bond, of gratitude, or any other that may influence obtaining the victim's trust.

In the cases of subsections a), b), c) and d) in addition to the sanctions indicated, the perpetrators of the crime shall lose parental authority, guardianship or curatorship, as the case may be, with respect to all their descendants, the right to support that might correspond to them by their relationship with the victim and the right they might have with respect to the victim's property.

65 de 337

CÓDIGO PENAL FEDERAL

CÁMARA DE DIPUTADOS DEL H. CONGRESO DE LA UNIÓN Última Reforma DOF 08-05-2023

Secretaría General

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios

In the cases of subsections e), f) and h) in addition to the sanctions indicated, punishment shall be imposed of removal and disqualification from holding the position or commission or any other of a public or similar character, for up to a time equal to the imposed sentence.

In all cases the judge shall order the pertinent measures so that the offender is permanently prohibited from having any type of contact or relationship with the victim.

Artículo adicionado DOF 27-03-2007

##### CAPITULO VI

Pandering and Trafficking of Persons. Capítulo adicionado DOF 27-03-2007

###### Artículo 206.- Pandering shall be punished with imprisonment of two to nine years and fifty to five hundred

days' fine.

Artículo reformado DOF 03-01-1989, 27-03-2007

###### Artículo 206 BIS.- Commits the crime of pandering:

I.- Any person who exploits the body of another through carnal commerce, maintains themselves from this commerce or obtains any profit therefrom;

II.- Whoever induces or solicits a person to, with another, sexually commerce with their body or facilitates the means for them to engage in prostitution, and

III.- Whoever manages, administers or maintains directly or indirectly, brothels, houses of assignation or places of assembly expressly dedicated to exploiting prostitution, or obtains any benefit from their proceeds.

Artículo adicionado DOF 27-03-2007

###### Artículo 207. Derogado.

Artículo reformado DOF 14-02-1940, 27-03-2007. Derogado DOF 27-11-2007

##### CAPÍTULO VII

Provocation of a Crime and Apology for It or for Some Vice and Omission to Prevent a Crime that Attacks the Free Development of Personality, Human Dignity or Physical or Mental Integrity.

Capítulo adicionado DOF 27-03-2007

###### Artículo 208.- Whoever publicly provokes the commission of a crime, or makes an apology for it or for

some vice, shall be subject to ten to one hundred eighty days of community service, if the crime is not executed; otherwise the sanction that corresponds to the provoker for their participation in the crime committed shall be applied.

Artículo reformado DOF 14-01-1966, 23-12-1985, 04-01-2000, 27-03-2007

###### Artículo 209.- Whoever, being able to do so with their immediate intervention and without risk to themselves or others, does not

prevent the commission of one of the crimes contemplated in Title VIII, Book Second, of this Code or in the Federal Law to Prevent and Punish Trafficking in Persons shall be subject to a sentence of six months to three years imprisonment and fifty to two hundred days' fine.

Párrafo reformado DOF 19-08-2010

The same penalties shall be imposed on whoever, being able to do so, does not go to the authority or its agents so that they prevent a crime of those contemplated in the preceding paragraph and of whose imminent commission they have knowledge.

66 de 337

CÓDIGO PENAL FEDERAL

CÁMARA DE DIPUTADOS DEL H. CONGRESO DE LA UNIÓN Última Reforma DOF 08-05-2023

Secretaría General

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios

Said penalties shall be imposed on persons related to or assigned to any institution, association, organization or group of a religious, cultural, sports, educational, recreational or any other nature and who have knowledge of the commission of the crimes referred to in the first paragraph of this article, when they do not inform the competent authority or protect the person who commits it, whether by hiding them, transferring them to another location or in any other way providing them protection.

Párrafo adicionado DOF 19-08-2010

Artículo reformado DOF 10-01-1994, 27-03-2007

##### CAPÍTULO VIII

Pederasty

Capítulo adicionado DOF 19-08-2010

###### Artículo 209 Bis.- A penalty of nine to eighteen years of imprisonment and seven hundred fifty to two

thousand two hundred fifty days' fine shall be imposed on anyone who takes advantage of the trust, subordination or superiority they have over a person under eighteen years of age, derived from their kinship of any degree, guardianship, curatorship, custody, teaching, religious, employment, medical, cultural, domestic relationship or of any nature, and executes, compels, induces or convinces to execute any sexual act, with or without their consent.

The same penalty shall apply to anyone who commits the conduct described in the preceding paragraph against a person who does not have the capacity to understand the meaning of the act or to resist it.

If the agent uses physical violence, the penalties shall be increased by one-half.

The perpetrator of the crime may be subject to comprehensive medical treatment for the time required, which may not exceed the duration of the prison sentence imposed.

In addition to the foregoing penalties, the perpetrator of the crime shall lose, as applicable, parental authority, guardianship, curatorship, adoption, the right to support and any right they may have with respect to the victim's property, pursuant to civil legislation.

When the crime is committed by a public servant or a professional in the exercise of their functions or by reason thereof, in addition to the aforementioned prison sentence, they shall be disqualified, dismissed or suspended from their public employment or profession for a term equal to the sentence imposed. Article added DOF 19-08-2010

###### Artículo 209 Ter.- For the purpose of determining the harm caused to the free development of the victim's

personality, the necessary expert reports shall be requested to ascertain their harm. In case of non-compliance with this provision by the Public Prosecutor's Office, it shall be sanctioned pursuant to this Code and applicable legislation.

In cases where the convicted person refuses or cannot guarantee medical, psychological or specialized care as required, the State shall provide those services to the victim. Article added DOF 19-08-2010

##### CAPÍTULO IX

Forced Cohabitation of Persons Under Eighteen Years of Age or Persons who do not have the Capacity to Understand the Meaning of the Act or Persons who do not have the Capacity to Resist It.

Chapter added DOF 25-04-2023

###### Artículo 209

Quáter. Anyone who compels, coerces, induces, solicits, arranges or offers one or more of these persons to join informally or by common law, with or without their consent, with someone of the same condition or with a person over eighteen years of age, for the purpose of cohabiting constantly and in a manner equivalent to a marriage, commits the crime of forced cohabitation of persons under eighteen years of age or persons who do not have the capacity to understand the meaning of the act or

67 de 337

CÓDIGO PENAL FEDERAL

CÁMARA DE DIPUTADOS DEL H. CONGRESO DE LA UNIÓN Última Reforma DOF 08-05-2023

Secretaría General

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios persons who do not have the capacity to resist it.

The person responsible for this crime shall be subject to a penalty of eight to fifteen years of imprisonment and one thousand to two thousand five hundred days' fine.

The penalty provided in the preceding paragraph shall be increased by up to one-half, at its minimum and maximum, if the victim belongs to any indigenous or Afro-Mexican people or community.

Article added DOF 25-04-2023

TITULO NOVENO

Revelation of Secrets and Illegal Access to Computer Systems and Equipment

Title renumbered DOF 20-01-1967. Renumbered (formerly Title Tenth) DOF 29-07-1970. Title amended DOF 17-05-1999

##### CAPITULO I

Revelation of Secrets

Chapter added DOF 17-05-1999

###### Artículo 210.- Thirty to two hundred days of community service shall be imposed on anyone who,

without just cause, to the detriment of someone and without the consent of the person who may be harmed, reveals any secret or confidential communication that they know or have received by reason of their employment, office or position.

Erratum to article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 10-01-1994

###### Artículo 211.- The sanction shall be one to five years, a fine of fifty to five hundred pesos and

suspension from profession as applicable, from two months to one year, when the punishable revelation is made by a person providing professional or technical services or by a public official or employee or when the secret revealed or published is of an industrial nature.

###### Artículo 211 Bis.- Anyone who improperly reveals, discloses or uses, or to the detriment of another,

information or images obtained in an interception of private communication, shall be subject to penalties of six to twelve years of imprisonment and three hundred to six hundred days' fine.

Article added DOF 07-11-1996

##### CAPÍTULO II

Illegal Access to Computer Systems and Equipment

Chapter added DOF 17-05-1999

###### Artículo 211

bis 1.- Anyone who without authorization modifies, destroys or causes loss of information contained in computer systems or equipment protected by a security mechanism shall be subject to six months to two years of imprisonment and one hundred to three hundred days' fine.

Anyone who without authorization accesses or copies information contained in computer systems or equipment protected by a security mechanism shall be subject to three months to one year of imprisonment and fifty to one hundred fifty days' fine.

Article added DOF 17-05-1999

###### Artículo 211

bis 2.- Anyone who without authorization modifies, destroys or causes loss of information contained in computer systems or equipment of the State, protected by a security mechanism, shall be subject to one to four years of imprisonment and two hundred to six hundred days' fine. 68 de 337

CÓDIGO PENAL FEDERAL

CÁMARA DE DIPUTADOS DEL H. CONGRESO DE LA UNIÓN Última Reforma DOF 08-05-2023

Secretaría General

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios

Anyone who without authorization accesses or copies information contained in computer systems or equipment of the State, protected by a security mechanism, shall be subject to six months to two years of imprisonment and one hundred to three hundred days' fine.

Anyone who without authorization accesses, obtains, copies or uses information contained in any system, equipment or means of computer storage relating to public security, protected by any security means, shall be subject to a penalty of four to ten years of imprisonment and a fine of five hundred to one thousand days of the general minimum wage in force in the Federal District. If the responsible party is or has been a public servant in a public security institution, they shall also be subject to dismissal and disqualification for four to ten years from serving in any other employment, position, office or public commission.

Paragraph added DOF 24-06-2009

The foregoing sanctions shall be doubled when the conduct obstructs, hinders, impedes, limits or makes impossible the prosecution or administration of justice, or relates to records associated with a criminal proceeding safeguarded by the competent authorities.

Paragraph added DOF 17-06-2016

Article added DOF 17-05-1999


This excerpt sits under: CÓDIGO PENAL FEDERAL > Libro Primero. > DELITOS CONTRA EL LIBRE DESARROLLO DE LA PERSONALIDAD. > CAPÍTULO VIII

###### Artículo 211

bis 3.- Whoever, being authorized to access State computer systems and equipment, improperly modifies, destroys or causes loss of information contained therein, shall be punished with two to eight years of imprisonment and a fine of three hundred to nine hundred days.

Whoever, being authorized to access State computer systems and equipment, improperly copies information contained therein, shall be punished with one to four years of imprisonment and a fine of one hundred fifty to four hundred fifty days.

Whoever, being authorized to access computer systems, equipment or storage media in matters of public security, improperly obtains, copies or uses information contained therein, shall be punished with a penalty of four to ten years of imprisonment and a fine of five hundred to one thousand days of the general minimum wage in force in the Federal District. If the responsible party is or had been a public servant in a public security institution, up to one-half more of the imposed penalty shall be imposed in addition, as well as removal from office and disqualification for a period equal to that of the resulting penalty to serve in another employment, post, position or public commission.

Párrafo adicionado DOF 24-06-2009

Artículo adicionado DOF 17-05-1999

###### Artículo 211

bis 4.- Whoever without authorization modifies, destroys or causes loss of information contained in computer systems or equipment of the institutions that comprise the financial system, protected by any security mechanism, shall be punished with six months to four years of imprisonment and a fine of one hundred to six hundred days.

Whoever without authorization accesses or copies information contained in computer systems or equipment of the institutions that comprise the financial system, protected by any security mechanism, shall be punished with three months to two years of imprisonment and a fine of fifty to three hundred days. Artículo adicionado DOF 17-05-1999

###### Artículo 211

bis 5.- Whoever, being authorized to access computer systems and equipment of the institutions that comprise the financial system, improperly modifies, destroys or causes loss of information contained therein, shall be punished with six months to four years of imprisonment and a fine of one hundred to six hundred days.

Whoever, being authorized to access computer systems and equipment of the institutions that comprise the financial system, improperly copies information contained therein, shall be punished with three months to two years of imprisonment and a fine of fifty to three hundred days.

69 de 337

CÓDIGO PENAL FEDERAL

CÁMARA DE DIPUTADOS DEL H. CONGRESO DE LA UNIÓN Última Reforma DOF 08-05-2023

Secretaría General

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios

The penalties provided in this article shall be increased by one-half when the conduct is committed by officials or employees of the institutions that comprise the financial system. Artículo adicionado DOF 17-05-1999

###### Artículo 211

bis 6.- For the purposes of articles 211 Bis 4 and 211 Bis 5 above, institutions that comprise the financial system are understood to be those indicated in article 400 Bis of this Code. Artículo adicionado DOF 17-05-1999

###### Artículo 211

bis 7.- The penalties provided in this chapter shall be increased by up to one-half when the information obtained is used for one's own or another's benefit.

Artículo adicionado DOF 17-05-1999

TITULO DECIMO

Crimes due to acts of corruption

Título recorrido DOF 20-01-1967. Recorrido (antes Título Decimoprimero) DOF 29-07-1970. Denominación reformada DOF 05-01-1983,

##### CAPITULO I

Denominación del Capítulo suprimida DOF 05-01-1983

###### Artículo 212.- For the purposes of this Title and the subsequent one, a public servant is any person who

holds an employment, position or commission of any nature in the centralized Federal Public Administration or in that of the Federal District, decentralized agencies, state-owned enterprises with majority state participation, organizations and societies assimilated thereto, public trusts, state productive enterprises, in autonomous constitutional bodies, in the Congress of the Union, or in the Federal Judicial Power, or who handles federal economic resources. The provisions contained in this Title are applicable to the Governors of the States, to the Deputies, to the Local Legislatures and to the Magistrates of the Local Courts of Justice, for the commission of the crimes provided in this Title, in federal matters.

Párrafo reformado DOF 14-07-2014, 18-07-2016

The same sanctions provided for the crime in question shall be imposed on any person who participates in the perpetration of any of the crimes provided in this Title or the subsequent one. In addition to such sanctions, the penalties of removal from office and disqualification to hold employment, position or public commission, as well as to participate in acquisitions, leases, services or public works, concessions for the provision of public service or for exploitation, use and enjoyment of property of the domain of the Federation for a period of one to twenty years shall be imposed on those responsible for their commission, according to the following criteria:

I.- It shall be for a period of one to ten years when there is no damage or injury or when the amount of the affectation or benefit obtained from the commission of the crime does not exceed two hundred times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update, and

II.- It shall be for a period of ten to twenty years if said amount exceeds the limit indicated in the preceding section.

Párrafo con fracciones adicionado DOF 18-07-2016

For the purposes of the foregoing, the judge must consider, in the event that the responsible party has the character of public servant, in addition to that provided in article 213 of this Code, the elements of the employment, position or commission that he held when he incurred the crime.

Párrafo adicionado DOF 18-07-2016

70 de 337

CÓDIGO PENAL FEDERAL

CÁMARA DE DIPUTADOS DEL H. CONGRESO DE LA UNIÓN Última Reforma DOF 08-05-2023

Secretaría General

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios

When the responsible party has the character of a private individual, the judge must impose the sanction of disqualification to hold a public position, as well as to participate in acquisitions, leases, concessions, services or public works, considering, as applicable, the following: I.- The patrimonial damages and injuries caused by the acts or omissions;

II.- The socioeconomic circumstances of the responsible party;

III.- The external conditions and means of execution, and

IV.- The amount of benefit that the responsible party has obtained.

Párrafo con fracciones adicionado DOF 18-07-2016

Without prejudice to the foregoing, the category of trusted official or employee shall be a circumstance that may give rise to an aggravation of the penalty.

Párrafo adicionado DOF 18-07-2016

When the crimes referred to in articles 214, 217, 221, 222, 223 and 224 of this Code are committed by public servants elected by popular vote or whose appointment is subject to ratification by either of the Chambers of the Congress of the Union, the penalties provided shall be increased by up to one-third.

Párrafo adicionado DOF 18-07-2016

Fe de erratas al artículo DOF 31-08-1931. Reformado DOF 05-01-1983

###### Artículo 213.- For the individualization of the sanctions provided in this Title, the judge shall take into

account, as applicable, the hierarchical level of the public servant and the degree of responsibility of the position, his seniority in employment, his service record, his compensation, his level of education, the need to repair the damages and injuries caused by the unlawful conduct and the special circumstances of the facts constituting the crime. Without prejudice to the foregoing, the category of trusted official or employee shall be a circumstance that may give rise to an aggravation of the penalty. Artículo reformado DOF 05-01-1983, 18-07-2016

###### Artículo 213-Bis.- When the crimes referred to in articles 215, 219 and 222 of this

Code, are committed by public servants who are members of any police, customs or immigration corporation, the penalties provided shall be increased by up to one half.

Article added DOF 03-01-1989. Reformed DOF 18-07-2016

##### CAPITULO II

Illicit exercise of public service

Chapter relocated with reformed denomination DOF 05-01-1983. Denomination reformed DOF 18-07-2016

###### Artículo 214.- Commits the crime of illicit exercise of public service, the public servant who:

Paragraph reformed DOF 18-07-2016

I.- Exercises the functions of an employment, position or commission, without having taken legitimate possession, or without satisfying all legal requirements.

II.- Continues exercising the functions of an employment, position or commission after knowing that his appointment has been revoked or that he has been suspended or dismissed.

III.- Having knowledge by reason of his employment, position or commission that the patrimony or interests of any dependency or entity of the centralized federal public administration, decentralized agencies, majority state-owned enterprises, associations and societies assimilated to these and public trusts

71 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services public, of productive state enterprises, of autonomous constitutional bodies, of the

Congress of the Union or of the Judicial Power, may be seriously affected by any act or omission and does not report in writing to his hierarchical superior or prevent it if it is within his powers.

Fraction reformed DOF 14-07-2014, 18-07-2016

IV.- By himself or through an intermediary, unlawfully removes, destroys, conceals, uses, or renders useless information or documentation that is under his custody or to which he has access, or of which he has knowledge by virtue of his employment, position or commission.

V.- By himself or through an intermediary, when legally required, provides reports in which he states false facts or circumstances or denies the truth in whole or in part about them, and

Fraction added DOF 30-06-2006

VI.- Having the obligation by reason of employment, position or commission, to guard, watch over, protect or provide security to persons, places, facilities or objects, breaching his duty, in any way facilitates harm to persons, or to places, facilities or objects, or loss or removal of objects that are under his care.

Whoever commits any of the crimes referred to in fractions I and II of this article, shall be punished with one to three years of imprisonment and thirty to one hundred days fine.

Paragraph reformed DOF 18-07-2016

The violator of fractions III, IV, V and VI shall be punished with two to seven years of imprisonment and thirty to one hundred fifty days fine.

Paragraph reformed DOF 17-11-1986, 30-06-2006, 18-07-2016

Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Reformed DOF 05-01-1983

##### CAPITULO III

Abuse of authority

Denomination of the Chapter reformed DOF 05-01-1983

###### Artículo 215.- Public servants who engage in any

of the following conducts commit the crime of abuse of authority:

Paragraph amended DOF 03-01-1989

I.- When, in order to prevent the execution of a law, decree or regulation, the collection of a tax or the compliance with a judicial ruling, they request assistance from the public force or employ it for that purpose;

II. Repealed.

Clause repealed DOF 26-06-2017

III.- When they unduly delay or deny to individuals the protection or service they are obligated to provide them or prevent the submission or processing of a request;

IV.- When, being in charge of administering justice, under any pretext, even that of obscurity or silence of the law, they unjustifiably refuse to dispatch a matter pending before them, within the time periods established by law;

V. When the person in charge or member of a public force, legally required by a competent authority to provide assistance, refuses to provide it or unjustifiably delays it. The same provision shall apply in the case of expert witnesses.

Clause amended DOF 23-01-2009

72 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Amendment DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

VI.- When, being in charge of any establishment intended for the execution of custodial sanctions, social reintegration institutions or custody and rehabilitation of minors and preventive or administrative detention centers, or arraigo centers, without the legal requirements, receives a person as imprisoned, detained, arrested, arraigada or committed, or keeps them deprived of their liberty, without notifying the corresponding authority of the fact; denies that they are detained, if they were; or fails to comply with the release order issued by the competent authority;

Clause amended DOF 18-07-2016

VII.- When, having knowledge of an illegal deprivation of liberty, they do not report it immediately to the competent authority or do not cause it to cease, also immediately, if this is within their powers;

VIII.- When they cause funds, securities or other thing that has not been entrusted to them to be delivered to them and they appropriate them or dispose of them improperly.

IX.- They obtain, demand or request without any right or legitimate cause, for themselves or for any other person, part of the salary or remuneration of one or more of their subordinates, gifts or other goods or services;

Clause amended DOF 18-07-2016

X.- When in the exercise of their functions or by reason thereof, they grant public employment, position or commission, or contracts for the provision of professional or commercial services or of any other nature, that are remunerated, knowing that the service for which they were appointed will not be rendered, or the granted contract will not be fulfilled;

XI.- When they authorize or hire someone who is disqualified by final resolution of a competent authority to hold an employment, position or commission in the public service, or to participate in acquisitions, leases, services or public works, provided they do so with knowledge of such situation;

Clause amended DOF 06-06-2006, 18-07-2016

XII.- When they issue any identification that accredits as a public servant any person who does not actually hold the employment, position or commission referred to in said identification;

Clause amended DOF 06-06-2006

XIII. Repealed.

XIV.- Compelling the persons mentioned in article 243 Bis of the Federal Code of Criminal Procedures to testify about information obtained in the performance of their activity.

Clause added DOF 06-06-2006

XV. Failing to make the immediate registration of the corresponding detention, falsifying the corresponding Administrative Detention Report, failing to update it properly or unjustifiably delaying placing the detainee under the custody of the corresponding authority; and

Clause added DOF 23-01-2009. Amended DOF 30-11-2010, 26-06-2017

XVI. Failing to comply with the obligation to prevent the execution of conducts of deprivation of liberty.

Clause added DOF 30-11-2010

73 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Amendment DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

Whoever commits the crime of abuse of authority under the terms provided in clauses I through V and X through XII, shall be sentenced to one to eight years of imprisonment and fifty to one hundred days of fine. The same sanction shall be imposed on persons who accept the appointments, contracts or identifications referred to in clauses X through XII.

Paragraph amended DOF 03-01-1989, 18-07-2016

Whoever commits the crime of abuse of authority under the terms provided in clauses VI through IX, XIV, XV and XVI, shall be sentenced to two to nine years of imprisonment, seventy to four hundred days of fine and dismissal and disqualification for two to nine years from holding another public employment, position or commission.

Paragraph amended DOF 03-01-1989, 06-06-2006, 23-01-2009, 30-11-2010, 18-07-2016, 26-06-2017

Article amended DOF 05-01-1983
