##### CAPITULO IV

Coalition of public servants

Chapter relocated with amended denomination DOF 05-01-1983

###### Artículo 216.- Those who, having such character, form a coalition

to take measures contrary to a law, regulation or other provisions of a general nature, prevent their execution or to resign from their positions in order to prevent or suspend the public administration in any of its branches, commit the crime of coalition of public servants. Workers who form a coalition in the exercise of their constitutional rights or who make use of the right to strike do not commit this crime. Whoever commits the crime of coalition of public servants shall be sentenced to two to seven years of imprisonment and a fine of thirty to three hundred times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time of the commission of the crime.

Article amended DOF 05-01-1983, 18-07-2016

##### CAPITULO V

Illicit use of powers and authorities

Chapter relocated with amended denomination DOF 05-01-1983. Denomination amended DOF 18-07-2016

###### Article 217.- The following commit the crime of illicit use of attributions and powers:

Paragraph amended DOF 18-07-2016

I.- The public servant who illicitly:

Paragraph amended DOF 18-07-2016

74 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

A) Grants concessions for the provision of public service or for exploitation, use and benefit of property owned by the Federation;

B) Grants permits, licenses, awards or authorizations of economic content;

Subsection amended DOF 18-07-2016

C) Grants franchises, exemptions, deductions or subsidies on taxes, duties, products, benefits or contributions and social security fees, generally on fiscal revenues, and on prices and tariffs of goods and services produced or provided in the Federal Public Administration;

Subsection amended DOF 18-07-2016

D) Grants, carries out or contracts public works, acquisitions, leases, disposals of goods or services, with public resources;

Subsection amended DOF 18-07-2016

E) Contracts debt or makes placements of funds and securities with public resources.

Subsection added DOF 18-07-2016

I. bis.- The public servant who knowingly of the illegality of the act, and to the detriment of the patrimony or the public service or another person:

- A) Denies the granting or contracting of the operations referred to in this section, despite all the requirements established in the applicable regulations for their granting being met, or
- B) Being responsible for directly administering and verifying compliance with the terms of a concession, permit, assignment or contract, has failed to comply with said obligation.

Section with subsections added DOF 18-07-2016

II.- Any person who requests or promotes the improper carrying out, granting or contracting of the operations referred to in the preceding section or is a party thereto, and

III.- The public servant who having public funds under their charge, gives them an application different from that for which they were intended or makes an illegal payment.

Section amended DOF 18-07-2016

The same sanctions provided shall be imposed on any person who knowingly of the illegality of the act, and to the detriment of the patrimony or the public service or another person participates in, requests or promotes the perpetration of any of the crimes provided for in this article.

Paragraph added DOF 18-07-2016

To whoever commits the crime referred to in this article, a penalty of six months to twelve years of imprisonment and thirty to one hundred fifty days' fine shall be imposed.

Paragraph amended DOF 28-05-2009, 18-07-2016

Reform DOF 28-05-2009: Repealed the then third and fourth paragraphs of the article

Article amended DOF 05-01-1955, 05-01-1983

###### Article 217 Bis.- To the private individual who, in their capacity as contractor, permit holder, assignee, holder of a concession for the provision of a public service of exploitation, use or benefit of property owned by the Federation, with the purpose of obtaining a benefit for themselves or for a third party: I.- Generates and uses false or altered information, regarding the yields or benefits they obtain, and

75 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

II.- When being legally obligated to deliver to an authority information about the yields or benefits they obtain, conceals it.

To whoever commits the crime referred to in this article, a penalty of three months to nine years of imprisonment and thirty to one hundred days' fine shall be imposed.

Article added DOF 18-07-2016

##### CHAPTER V BIS

On the improper payment and receipt of remuneration of public servants

Chapter added DOF 05-11-2018

###### Article 217 Ter. In addition to administrative and political responsibilities, the following incur the crime of illicit remuneration:

- I. The public servant who approves or countersigns the payment, or who signs the voucher, check, payroll or payment order, for a remuneration, retribution, retirement, pension, retirement pay, severance for services rendered, loan or credit, not authorized in accordance with the provisions of the Federal Law on Remuneration of Public Servants;
- II. Whoever receives an improper payment under the terms of the preceding section without making the report within the deadline indicated in article 5 of the Federal Law on Remuneration of Public Servants, having knowledge of the illegality of the act, except when they are part of the permanent and supernumerary personnel of public entities who do not hold middle or upper management positions, as well as troop and enlisted personnel of the armed forces, or in cases considered by the same article 5 of the aforementioned Law as a non-serious administrative offense.

Article added DOF 05-11-2018. Article and number amended (formerly article 217 Bis) DOF 12-04-2019

###### Article 217 Quáter. For the commission of the crime indicated in the preceding article the following penalties shall be imposed:

- I. If the benefit improperly granted does not exceed the equivalent of five hundred times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time of committing the crime, a penalty of three months to two years of imprisonment and a fine of thirty to three hundred times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time of committing the crime shall be imposed;
- II. If the benefit improperly granted exceeds the equivalent of five hundred times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time of committing the crime, but is not greater than the equivalent of two thousand times said unit, a penalty of six months to three years of imprisonment and a fine of thirty to three hundred times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time of committing the crime shall be imposed;
- III. If the benefit improperly granted exceeds the equivalent of two thousand times, but is not greater than the equivalent of five thousand times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time of committing the crime, a penalty of three to five years of imprisonment and a fine of three hundred to one thousand times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time of committing the crime shall be imposed, and
- IV. If the benefit improperly granted exceeds the equivalent of five thousand times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time of committing the crime, a penalty of five to twelve years of imprisonment and a fine of five hundred to three thousand times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time of committing the crime shall be imposed.

76 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

Dismissal and disqualification from holding another public office, employment or commission from six months to fourteen years shall also be imposed.

Article added DOF 05-11-2018. Article and number amended (formerly article 217 Ter) DOF 12-04-2019

##### CHAPTER VI

Extortion

Chapter added DOF 05-01-1983

###### Artículo 218.- Comete el delito de concusión el servidor público que con el carácter de tal y a título de

impuesto o contribución, recargo, renta, rédito, salario o emolumento, exija, por sí o por medio de otro, dinero, valores, servicios o cualquiera otra cosa que sepa no ser debida, o en mayor cantidad que la señalada por la Ley.

Al que cometa el delito de concusión se le impondrán las siguientes sanciones:

Cuando la cantidad o el valor de lo exigido indebidamente no exceda del equivalente de quinientos días de Unidades de Medida y Actualización en el momento de cometerse el delito, o no sea valuable, se impondrán de tres meses a dos años de prisión y de treinta a cien días multa.

Párrafo reformado DOF 18-07-2016

Cuando la cantidad o el valor de lo exigido indebidamente exceda de quinientos días de Unidades de Medida y Actualización en el momento de cometerse el delito, se impondrán de dos años a doce años de prisión y multa de cien a ciento cincuenta días multa.

Párrafo reformado DOF 18-07-2016

Fe de erratas al artículo DOF 31-08-1931. Reformado DOF 05-01-1983

###### Article 218.- The public servant who, in such capacity and under the title of

tax or contribution, surcharge, rent, interest, salary or emolument, demands, by himself or through another, money, securities, services or any other thing that he knows is not due, or in a greater amount than that established by Law, commits the crime of extortion.

The following sanctions shall be imposed on whoever commits the crime of extortion:

When the amount or value of what is unlawfully demanded does not exceed the equivalent of five hundred days of Units of Measurement and Update at the time the crime is committed, or is not valuable, imprisonment of three months to two years and a fine of thirty to one hundred days shall be imposed.

Paragraph amended DOF 18-07-2016

When the amount or value of what is unlawfully demanded exceeds five hundred days of Units of Measurement and Update at the time the crime is committed, imprisonment of two years to twelve years and a fine of one hundred to one hundred fifty days shall be imposed.

Paragraph amended DOF 18-07-2016

Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 05-01-1983

##### CAPITULO VII

Intimidación

Capítulo adicionado DOF 05-01-1983

##### CHAPTER VII

Intimidation

Chapter added DOF 05-01-1983

###### Artículo 219.- Comete el delito de intimidación:

I.- El servidor público que por sí, o por interpósita persona, utilizando la violencia física o moral, inhiba o intimide a cualquier persona para evitar que ésta o un tercero denuncie, formule querella o aporte información relativa a la presunta comisión de una conducta sancionada por la Legislación Penal o por la Ley General de Responsabilidades Administrativas, y

Fracción reformada DOF 18-07-2016

II.- El servidor público que con motivo de la querella, denuncia o información a que hace referencia la fracción anterior realice una conducta ilícita u omita una lícita debida que lesione los intereses de las personas que las presenten o aporten, o de algún tercero con quien dichas personas guarden algún vínculo familiar, de negocios o afectivo.

Al que cometa el delito de intimidación se le impondrán de dos años a nueve años de prisión y de treinta a cien días multa.

Párrafo reformado DOF 18-07-2016

Artículo reformado DOF 03-01-1980, 05-01-1983

###### Article 219.- The following commit the crime of intimidation:

I.- The public servant who, by himself or through an intermediary, using physical or moral violence, inhibits or intimidates any person to prevent such person or a third party from reporting, filing a complaint or providing information related to the alleged commission of conduct sanctioned by Criminal Legislation or by the General Law of Administrative Responsibilities, and

Subsection amended DOF 18-07-2016

II.- The public servant who, by reason of the complaint, report or information referred to in the preceding subsection, performs an unlawful conduct or omits a lawful one that is due and that harms the interests of the persons who present or provide them, or of any third party with whom such persons have a family, business or emotional relationship.

Whoever commits the crime of intimidation shall be subject to imprisonment of two years to nine years and a fine of thirty to one hundred days.

Paragraph amended DOF 18-07-2016

Article amended DOF 03-01-1980, 05-01-1983

##### CAPITULO VIII

Ejercicio abusivo de funciones Capítulo adicionado DOF 05-01-1983

##### CHAPTER VIII

Abusive Exercise of Functions Chapter added DOF 05-01-1983

###### Artículo 220.- Comete el delito de ejercicio abusivo de funciones:

77 de 337

CÓDIGO PENAL FEDERAL

CÁMARA DE DIPUTADOS DEL H. CONGRESO DE LA UNIÓN Última Reforma DOF 08-05-2023

Secretaría General

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios

I.- El servidor público que en el desempeño de su empleo, cargo o comisión, ilícitamente otorgue por sí o por interpósita persona, contratos, concesiones, permisos, licencias, autorizaciones, franquicias, exenciones o efectúe compras o ventas o realice cualquier acto jurídico que produzca beneficios económicos al propio servidor público, a su cónyuge, descendiente o ascendiente, parientes por consanguinidad o afinidad hasta el cuarto grado, a cualquier tercero con el que tenga vínculos afectivos, económicos o de dependencia administrativa directa, socios o sociedades de las que el servidor público o las personas antes referidas formen parte;

Fracción reformada DOF 18-07-2016

II.- El servidor público que valiéndose de la información que posea por razón de su empleo, cargo o comisión, sea o no materia de sus funciones, y que no sea del conocimiento público, haga por sí, o por interpósita persona, inversiones, enajenaciones o adquisiciones, o cualquier otro acto que le produzca algún beneficio económico indebido al servidor público o a alguna de las personas mencionadas en la primera fracción.

Al que cometa el delito de ejercicio abusivo de funciones se le impondrán las siguientes sanciones: Cuando la cuantía a que asciendan las operaciones a que hace referencia este artículo no exceda del equivalente a quinientas veces el valor diario de la Unidad de Medida y Actualización en el momento de cometerse el delito, se impondrán de tres meses a dos años de prisión y de treinta a cien días multa. Párrafo reformado DOF 18-07-2016

Cuando la cuantía a que asciendan las operaciones a que hace referencia este artículo exceda de quinientas veces el valor diario de la Unidad de Medida y Actualización en el momento de cometerse el delito, se impondrán de dos años a doce años de prisión y de cien a ciento cincuenta días multa. Párrafo reformado DOF 18-07-2016

Artículo reformado DOF 24-03-1944, 09-03-1946. Fe de erratas DOF 16-07-1946. Reformado DOF 03-01-1980, 05-01-1983

###### Article 220.- The following commit the crime of abusive exercise of functions:

77 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Amendment DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

I.- The public servant who, in the performance of his employment, position or commission, unlawfully grants, by himself or through an intermediary, contracts, concessions, permits, licenses, authorizations, franchises, exemptions or makes purchases or sales or performs any legal act that produces economic benefits to the public servant himself, to his spouse, descendant or ascendant, relatives by consanguinity or affinity up to the fourth degree, to any third party with whom he has emotional, economic or direct administrative dependency ties, partners or companies of which the public servant or the aforementioned persons are part;

Subsection amended DOF 18-07-2016

II.- The public servant who, taking advantage of the information he possesses by reason of his employment, position or commission, whether or not it is the subject of his functions, and that is not public knowledge, makes, by himself or through an intermediary, investments, disposals or acquisitions, or any other act that produces an undue economic benefit to the public servant or to any of the persons mentioned in the first subsection.

The following sanctions shall be imposed on whoever commits the crime of abusive exercise of functions: When the amount to which the operations referred to in this article ascend does not exceed the equivalent of five hundred times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time the crime is committed, imprisonment of three months to two years and a fine of thirty to one hundred days shall be imposed. Paragraph amended DOF 18-07-2016

When the amount to which the operations referred to in this article ascend exceeds five hundred times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time the crime is committed, imprisonment of two years to twelve years and a fine of one hundred to one hundred fifty days shall be imposed. Paragraph amended DOF 18-07-2016

Article amended DOF 24-03-1944, 09-03-1946. Errata DOF 16-07-1946. Amended DOF 03-01-1980, 05-01-1983

##### CAPITULO IX

Tráfico de Influencia

Capítulo adicionado DOF 05-01-1983

##### CHAPTER IX

Influence Trafficking

Chapter added DOF 05-01-1983

###### Artículo 221.- Comete el delito de tráfico de influencia:

I.- El servidor público que por sí o por interpósita persona promueva o gestione la tramitación o resolución ilícita de negocios públicos ajenos a las responsabilidades inherentes a su empleo, cargo o comisión, y

II.- Cualquier persona que promueve la conducta ilícita del servidor público o se preste a la promoción o gestión a que hace referencia la fracción anterior.

III.- El servidor público que por sí, o por interpósita persona indebidamente, solicite o promueva cualquier resolución o la realización de cualquier acto materia del empleo, cargo o comisión de otro servidor público, que produzca beneficios económicos para sí o para cualquiera de las personas a que hace referencia la primera fracción del Artículo 220 de este Código.

IV.- Al particular que, sin estar autorizado legalmente para intervenir en un negocio público, afirme tener influencia ante los servidores públicos facultados para tomar decisiones dentro de dichos negocios, e intervenga ante ellos para promover la resolución ilícita de los mismos, a cambio de obtener un beneficio para sí o para otro.

Fracción adicionada DOF 18-07-2016

Al que cometa el delito de tráfico de influencia, se le impondrán de dos años a seis años de prisión y de treinta a cien días multa.

78 de 337

CÓDIGO PENAL FEDERAL

CÁMARA DE DIPUTADOS DEL H. CONGRESO DE LA UNIÓN Última Reforma DOF 08-05-2023

Secretaría General

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios

Párrafo reformado DOF 18-07-2016

Artículo derogado DOF 03-01-1980. Adicionado DOF 05-01-1983

###### Article 221.- The following commit the crime of influence trafficking:

I.- The public servant who, by himself or through an intermediary, promotes or manages the processing or unlawful resolution of public business unrelated to the responsibilities inherent to his employment, position or commission, and

II.- Any person who promotes the unlawful conduct of the public servant or lends himself to the promotion or management referred to in the preceding subsection.

III.- The public servant who, by himself or through an intermediary, unduly requests or promotes any resolution or the performance of any act that is the subject of the employment, position or commission of another public servant, which produces economic benefits for himself or for any of the persons referred to in the first subsection of Article 220 of this Code.

IV.- The private individual who, without being legally authorized to intervene in a public business, claims to have influence with the public servants empowered to make decisions within such business, and intervenes before them to promote the unlawful resolution thereof, in exchange for obtaining a benefit for himself or for another.

Subsection added DOF 18-07-2016

Whoever commits the crime of influence trafficking shall be subject to imprisonment of two years to six years and a fine of thirty to one hundred days.

78 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Amendment DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

Paragraph amended DOF 18-07-2016

##### CAPITULO X

Cohecho

Capítulo adicionado DOF 05-01-1983

##### CHAPTER X

Bribery

Chapter added DOF 05-01-1983

###### Article 222. The following commit the crime of bribery:

I.- The public servant who, by himself or through an intermediary, unlawfully solicits or receives for himself or for another, money or any benefit, or accepts a promise, to perform or refrain from performing an act proper to his functions inherent to his employment, position or commission;

Fraction reformed DOF 12-03-2015, 18-07-2016

II.- Whoever gives, promises or delivers any benefit to any of the persons mentioned in article 212 of this Code, so that he performs or omits an act related to his functions, to his employment, position or commission, and

Fraction reformed DOF 12-03-2015, 18-07-2016

III.- The federal legislator who, in the exercise of his functions or attributions, and within the framework of the approval process of the respective expenditure budget, manages or requests:

- a) The allocation of resources in favor of a public entity, demanding or obtaining, for himself or for a third party, a commission, gift or consideration, in money or in kind, different from that which corresponds to him for the exercise of his position;
- b) The granting of public works or service contracts in favor of certain natural or legal persons.

Fraction with subsections added DOF 18-07-2016

The same penalty shall be applied to any person who manages, requests in the name or on behalf of the federal legislator the allocations of resources or granting of contracts referred to in subsections a) and b) of this article.

Paragraph added DOF 18-07-2016

Whoever commits the crime of bribery shall be imposed the following sanctions:

When the amount or value of the gift, the goods or the promise does not exceed the equivalent of five hundred times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time the crime is committed, or is not valuable, a sentence of three months to two years of imprisonment and thirty to one hundred days fine shall be imposed.

Paragraph reformed DOF 23-08-2005, 18-07-2016

When the amount or value of the gift, goods, promise or benefit exceeds five hundred times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time the crime is committed, a sentence of two to fourteen years of imprisonment and one hundred to one hundred and fifty days fine shall be imposed. Paragraph reformed DOF 23-08-2005, 18-07-2016

In no case shall the money or gifts delivered be returned to those responsible for the crime of bribery, they shall be applied for the benefit of the State.

Article reformed DOF 05-01-1983

##### CHAPTER XI

Bribery of foreign public servants

Chapter added DOF 17-05-1999

79 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

###### Article 222

bis.- The penalties provided in the preceding article shall be imposed on whoever with the purpose of obtaining or retaining for himself or for another person undue advantages in the development or conduct of international commercial transactions, offers, promises or gives, by himself or through an intermediary, money or any other gift, whether in goods or services:

- I. To a foreign public servant, for his benefit or that of a third party, so that said public servant manages or refrains from managing the processing or resolution of matters related to the functions inherent to his employment, position or commission;

Fraction reformed DOF 23-08-2005, 12-03-2015

II. To a foreign public servant, for his benefit or that of a third party, so that said public servant manages the processing or resolution of any matter that is outside the scope of the functions inherent to his employment, position or commission, or

Fraction reformed DOF 23-08-2005, 12-03-2015

III. To any person so that he appears before a foreign public servant and requires or proposes to him to carry out the processing or resolution of any matter related to the functions inherent to the employment, position or commission of the latter.

For the purposes of this article, foreign public servant is understood to be any person who holds an employment, position or commission in the legislative, executive or judicial branch or in an autonomous public body at any order or level of government of a foreign State, whether appointed or elected; any person exercising a function for an authority, agency or public company or state-owned company of a foreign country; and any official or agent of an international public organization or body.

Paragraph reformed DOF 23-08-2005

When any of the crimes included in this article is committed in the cases referred to in article 11 of this Code, the judge shall impose on the legal person up to one thousand days fine and may decree its suspension or dissolution, taking into consideration the degree of knowledge of the management bodies regarding the bribery in the international transaction and the damage caused or the benefit obtained by the legal person.

Paragraph reformed DOF 23-08-2005

Article added DOF 17-05-1999

##### CHAPTER XII

Embezzlement

Chapter added DOF 05-01-1983

###### Article 223.- The following commits the crime of embezzlement:

I.- Any public servant who for his benefit or that of a third natural or legal person, diverts from its purpose money, securities, properties or any other thing belonging to the State or to a private party, if by reason of his position he had received them in administration, in deposit, in possession or for any other reason;

Fraction reformed DOF 18-07-2016

II.- The public servant who unlawfully uses public funds or grants any of the acts referred to in the article on unlawful use of attributions and powers with the purpose of promoting the political or social image of his person, that of his hierarchical superior or that of a third party, or in order to denigrate any person;

Fraction reformed DOF 18-07-2016

80 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

III.- Any person who requests or accepts to carry out the promotions or denigrations referred to in the preceding fraction, in exchange for public funds or the enjoyment of benefits derived from the acts referred to in the article on unlawful use of attributions and powers, and

Fraction reformed DOF 18-07-2016

IV.- Any person who without having the character of federal public servant and being legally obligated to the custody, administration or application of federal public resources, diverts them from their purpose for own or others' uses or gives them an application different from that for which they were intended.

Whoever commits the crime of embezzlement shall be imposed the following sanctions:

When the amount of what was diverted or of the funds used improperly does not exceed the equivalent of five hundred times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time the crime is committed, or is not valuable, a sentence of three months to two years of imprisonment and thirty to one hundred days fine shall be imposed.

Paragraph reformed DOF 18-07-2016

When the amount of what was diverted or of the funds used improperly exceeds five hundred times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update at the time the crime is committed, a sentence of two years to fourteen years of imprisonment and one hundred to one hundred and fifty days fine shall be imposed. Paragraph reformed DOF 18-07-2016

When the resources subject of the embezzlement are federal contributions for public security purposes, up to one third more of the penalties indicated in the preceding paragraphs shall be applied. Paragraph added DOF 24-06-2009

Article reformed DOF 05-01-1983

##### CHAPTER XIII

Illicit Enrichment

Chapter added DOF 05-01-1983

###### Article 224.- Whoever has incurred illicit enrichment by reason of their employment, position or commission in the public service shall be punished. Illicit enrichment exists when the public servant cannot prove the legitimate increase of their patrimony or the legitimate origin of the assets in their name or those with respect to which they conduct themselves as owner.

Paragraph amended DOF 18-07-2016

For purposes of the preceding paragraph, there shall be computed among the assets acquired by public servants or with respect to which they conduct themselves as owners, those received or disposed of by their spouse and their direct economic dependents, unless the public servant proves that they obtained them on their own.

Paragraph amended DOF 18-07-2016

It shall not be illicit enrichment in case the increase of patrimony is the product of conduct that fits another hypothesis of this Title. In this case the corresponding hypothesis and sanction shall be applied, without giving rise to concurrence of crimes.

Paragraph added DOF 18-07-2016

Whoever commits the crime of illicit enrichment shall be imposed the following sanctions:

Confiscation in benefit of the State of those assets whose origin cannot be proven.

Paragraph amended DOF 18-07-2016

81 of 337

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Amendment DOF 08-05-2023

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

When the amount to which the illicit enrichment ascends does not exceed the equivalent of five thousand times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update, imprisonment of three months to two years and a fine of thirty to one hundred days shall be imposed.

Paragraph amended DOF 18-07-2016

When the amount to which the illicit enrichment ascends exceeds the equivalent of five thousand times the daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update, imprisonment of two years to fourteen years and a fine of one hundred to one hundred fifty days shall be imposed.

Paragraph amended DOF 18-07-2016

TITLE ELEVENTH

Crimes committed against the administration of justice

Title moved DOF 20-01-1967. Moved (formerly Title Twelfth) DOF 29-07-1970. Name amended DOF 13-01-1984

##### CHAPTER I

Crimes committed by public servants

Chapter added DOF 13-01-1984

###### Article 225.- The following are crimes against the administration of justice, committed by public servants:

I.- Hearing matters for which they have a legal impediment or abstaining from hearing those that correspond to them, without having a legal impediment to do so;

II.- Performing any other official employment or a particular position or office that the law prohibits them; III.- Litigating for themselves or through an intermediary, when the law prohibits them from practicing their profession; IV.- Directing or advising persons who litigate before them;

V.- Not complying with a provision legally communicated to them by their competent superior, without justified cause for doing so;

VI.- Knowingly issuing a substantive resolution or a final judgment that is unlawful for violating a specific provision of law, or being contrary to the proceedings followed in the trial, or omitting to issue a procedural resolution, a substantive resolution or a lawful final judgment, within the terms established by law;

Fracción reformada DOF 18-07-2016

VII.- Executing acts or incurring omissions that produce damage or grant someone an undue advantage;

VIII.- Maliciously or negligently delaying or hindering the administration of justice;

IX. Unjustifiably abstaining from exercising the criminal action that corresponds to a person who is detained at their disposal as accused of a crime, when such action is appropriate in accordance with the Constitution and the laws on the matter, in cases where the law imposes that obligation on them; or exercising the criminal action when no complaint, accusation or charge is appropriate;

Fracción reformada DOF 10-01-1994, 17-06-2016

X.- Detaining an individual outside the cases established by law, or holding them for longer than the time established in the Constitution;

Improper Exercise of One's Own Right Chapter added DOF 13-01-1984

###### Article 226.

Anyone who, in order to enforce a right or claimed right that they must exercise, employs violence, shall be subject to imprisonment of three months to one year or 30 to 90 days fine. In these cases, proceedings shall only be initiated upon complaint by the offended party.

public administration, when in the exercise of their duties they commit the acts or incur the omissions stated in the aforementioned articles.

General Provisions

###### Article 228.- Professionals, artists or technicians and their assistants shall be responsible for the

crimes they commit in the exercise of their profession, under the following terms and without prejudice to the provisions contained in the General Health Law or in other norms on professional practice, as applicable:

Paragraph reformed DOF 13-01-1984, 14-01-1985

I.- In addition to the sanctions established for consummated crimes, whether intentional or negligent, they shall be subject to suspension of one month to two years in the exercise of the profession or permanent in case of recidivism; and

Section reformed DOF 13-01-1984

###### Article 229.- The preceding article shall apply to physicians who, having granted a letter of responsibility to

take charge of the care of an injured or sick person, abandon them in their treatment without just cause, and without giving immediate notice to the corresponding authority.

###### Article 230.- Imprisonment of three months to two years, up to one hundred days fine and suspension of

three months to one year at the discretion of the judge, shall be imposed on directors, managers or administrators of any health center, when they incur in any of the following cases:

I.- Preventing the discharge of a patient, when the patient or their family members request it, alleging debts of any kind;

II.- Unnecessarily retaining a newborn, for the reasons referred to in the final part of the preceding section;

III.- Delaying or denying for any reason the delivery of a corpse, except when an order from competent authority is required.

The same sanction shall be imposed on managers or administrators of funeral agencies who improperly delay or deny the delivery of a corpse, and likewise on managers, employees or staff of a pharmacy, who when filling a prescription substitute the medicine, specifically prescribed for another that causes harm or is evidently inappropriate for the condition for which it was prescribed.

Crimes of lawyers, advocates and litigants

suspension and disqualification for a term equal to that of the penalty previously indicated for practicing the profession, shall be imposed on lawyers, advocates, or litigants who are not ostensibly represented by lawyers, when they commit any of the following crimes:

III.- Knowingly and based on false or worthless documents or on false witnesses, bring an action or raise defenses against another, before judicial or administrative authorities; and

IV.- Simulate a legal act or a judicial act or writing, or alter elements of evidence and present them in trial, with the purpose of obtaining a judgment, resolution or administrative act contrary to law.

imprisonment may be imposed.

TITLE THIRTEENTH

Falsity

Title renumbered DOF 20-01-1967. Renumbered (formerly Title Fourteenth) DOF 29-07-1970

##### CHAPTER I

Counterfeiting, alteration and destruction of currency Chapter heading reformed DOF 11-06-1992

###### Article 234.- Anyone who commits the crime of counterfeiting currency shall be subject to five to twelve

years of imprisonment and up to five hundred days fine.

For the purposes of this Chapter, currency is understood to mean bills and metallic pieces, national or foreign, that have legal tender in the issuing country.

Anyone who produces, stores, distributes or introduces into the national territory any document or piece containing images or other elements used in circulating currency, and which therefore are suitable to deceive the public, by being confusable with legally issued currency, commits the crime of counterfeiting currency. Anyone who commits this crime in the degree of attempt shall be subject to four to eight years of imprisonment and up to three hundred days fine.

The penalty indicated in the first paragraph of this article shall also be imposed on anyone who knowingly makes use of counterfeit currency.

Article reformed DOF 11-06-1992

###### Article 235.- One to five years of imprisonment and up to five hundred days fine shall be imposed:

I.- On anyone who produces, stores or distributes pieces of paper with a size similar to or the same as that of bills, when such pieces present some of the images or elements contained in those, resulting in pieces with the appearance of bills;

II.- On anyone who marks currency with inscriptions, stamps, dies or in any other form, that are not erasable to disseminate messages directed to the public.

III.- On anyone who permits the use or carries out the transfer, by any means and title, of machines, instruments or tools that can only serve for the manufacture of currency, to persons not legally authorized to do so.

Article reformed DOF 11-06-1992

87 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023 General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services


This excerpt sits under: FEDERAL PENAL CODE > Book First. > CRIMES AGAINST THE FREE DEVELOPMENT OF PERSONALITY.

###### Artículo 236.- A prison sentence of five to twelve years and up to five hundred days' fine shall be imposed on anyone who alters

currency. The same penalty shall be imposed on anyone who knowingly circulates altered currency.

For the purposes of this article, it is understood that a person alters a bill who forms pieces by joining two or more fractions from different bills, and that a person alters a metallic coin who diminishes the content of gold, silver, platinum or palladium that compose the monetary pieces of legal tender, by means of filings, clippings, dissolution in acids or by employing any other means. Artículo reformado DOF 11-06-1992

###### Artículo 237.- A punishment of five to twelve years' imprisonment and up to five hundred days' fine shall be imposed on anyone who

provides their services or performs a position or commission in the mint or in any company that manufactures blanks, and who by any means, causes the coins of gold, silver, platinum or palladium to contain metal different from that specified by law, or to have less weight than the legal weight or an inferior alloy grade.

Artículo reformado DOF 11-06-1992

###### Artículo 238.- A prison sentence of three to seven years and up to five hundred days' fine shall be imposed on anyone who

unlawfully takes advantage of the metallic content by destroying coins in circulation through their melting or any other procedure.

Artículo reformado DOF 09-03-1946, 11-06-1992

##### CAPITULO II

Falsification and improper use of bearer titles, public credit documents and documents relating to credit

Capítulo reubicado con denominación reformada DOF 11-06-1992. Denominación reformada DOF 08-02-1999

###### Artículo 239.- Anyone who commits the crime of falsification of bearer titles and public credit documents

shall be sentenced to four to ten years' imprisonment and a fine of two hundred fifty to three thousand pesos.

The crime referred to in the preceding paragraph is committed by anyone who falsifies:

I.- Obligations or other public credit documents of the treasury, or the interest or dividend coupons of those titles.

II.- The obligations of the public debt of another nation, interest or dividend coupons of these titles.

III.- The obligations and other titles legally issued by companies or enterprises or by the public administrations of the Federation, the States or any Municipality, and the interest or dividend coupons of the mentioned documents.

Artículo reformado DOF 09-03-1946

###### Artículo 240.- Anyone who introduces into the Republic or puts into circulation in it the falsified documents

referred to in the preceding article shall be subject to the penalty indicated therein.

Artículo reformado DOF 09-03-1946, 11-06-1992

##### CAPITULO III

Falsification of seals, keys, dies or stamps, marks, weights and measures 88 de 337

CÓDIGO PENAL FEDERAL

CÁMARA DE DIPUTADOS DEL H. CONGRESO DE LA UNIÓN Última Reforma DOF 08-05-2023 Secretaría General

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios

###### Artículo 241.- A prison sentence of four to nine years and a fine of four hundred to two thousand

pesos shall be imposed:

I.- On anyone who falsifies official seals or marks;

II.- On anyone who falsifies the punches for marking the fineness of gold or silver;

III.- On anyone who falsifies the dies or stamps intended for manufacturing currency or the seal, mark or countersign that any authority uses to identify any object or to ensure the payment of any tax;

IV.- On anyone who falsifies the punches, matrices, plates or any other object that serves for the manufacture of shares, bonds, coupons or bills referred to in artículo 239, and

V.- On anyone who falsifies the inspection marks of weights and measures.

The penalty corresponding to the crime provided for in fracción I shall be increased by up to one half when seals of assignees, contractors, permittees or distributors referred to in the Ley Federal para Prevenir y Sancionar los Delitos Cometidos en Materia de Hidrocarburos are falsified. Párrafo adicionado DOF 12-01-2016

###### Artículo 242.- A prison sentence of three months to three years and a fine of twenty to one thousand pesos shall be imposed:

I.- On anyone who falsifies keys, the seal of a private individual, a seal, mark, stamp or countersign of a commercial house, a bank or an industrial establishment; or a ticket or token of a public spectacle;

II.- On anyone who falsifies in the Republic the seals, punches or marks of a foreign nation;

III.- On anyone who transfers a false seal, punch or mark, concealing this defect;

IV.- On anyone who, in order to defraud another, alters legitimate weights and measures or removes the true marks from them and transfers them to false weights or measures, or makes use of the latter.

V.- On anyone who falsifies national or foreign adhesive seals;

VI.- On anyone who causes any of the seals referred to in the preceding fracción or the indicator mark showing that it has already been used to disappear;

VII.- On anyone who, procuring the true seals, punches, marks, etc., makes improper use of them; and

VIII.- On anyone who knowingly makes use of the seals or any other false objects referred to in the preceding artículo and fracciones I, II, V y VI of this one.

Fe de erratas al artículo DOF 31-08-1931. Reformado DOF 09-03-1946

###### Artículo 242 Bis.- A prison sentence of one to five years and a fine of two hundred to two thousand pesos shall be imposed on

anyone who in any way alters the signals, blood marks or branding marks that are used to distinguish livestock, without authorization from the person who has them legally registered with the competent authority.

Artículo adicionado DOF 20-01-1967

Secretaría General

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios

Falsification of documents in general

###### Artículo 243.- The crime of falsification shall be punished, in the case of public documents, with imprisonment

of four to eight years and from two hundred to three hundred sixty days' fine. In the case of private documents, with imprisonment of six months to five years and from one hundred eighty to three hundred sixty days' fine. If the person who carries out the falsification is a servidor público, the applicable penalty shall be increased by up to one half more.

The penalty corresponding to the crime provided for in the first paragraph shall be increased by up to one half when documents of assignees, contractors, permittees or distributors referred to in the Ley Federal para Prevenir y Sancionar los Delitos Cometidos en Materia de Hidrocarburos are falsified. Párrafo adicionado DOF 12-01-2016

Artículo reformado DOF 30-12-1991, 13-05-1996

###### Article 244.- The crime of document falsification is committed by any of the following means:

I.- By placing a false signature or rubric, even if imaginary, or altering a true one;

II.- By improperly taking advantage of another's signature or rubric left in blank, drafting an obligation, release or any other document that may compromise the property, honor, person or reputation of another, or cause harm to society, the State or a third party;

III.- By altering the content of a true document, after it has been completed and signed, if this changes its meaning regarding any circumstance or substantial point, whether done by adding, amending or erasing, in whole or in part, one or more words or clauses, or by varying the punctuation;

IV.- By varying the date or any other circumstance relating to the time of execution of the act expressed in the document;

V.- By the person drafting the document attributing to himself or herself, or attributing to the person on whose behalf he or she acts: a name or an investiture, quality or circumstance that he or she does not have and that is necessary for the validity of the act;

VI.- By drafting a document in terms that change the agreement entered into a different one in which the declaration or provision of the grantor, the obligations that he or she intended to undertake, or the rights that he or she should have acquired are varied;

Errata to the section DOF 31-08-1931

VII.- By adding or altering clauses or declarations, or recording false facts as true, or as confessed those that are not, if the document in which they are recorded was drafted to record them and as proof of them;

Errata to the section DOF 31-08-1931

VIII.- By issuing a supposed testimony of documents that do not exist; issuing it for another existing one that lacks the legal requirements, falsely assuming it has them; or for another that does not lack them, but adding or suppressing something in the copy that constitutes a substantial variation, and

IX.- By an expert translator or paleographer altering the content of a document when translating or deciphering it.

90 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

X.- By producing plates, badges, insignia, documents or any other official identification, without having the authorization of the corresponding authority.

Section added DOF 23-12-1985

###### Article 245.- For the crime of document falsification to be punishable as such, it is necessary

that the following requirements concur:

I.- That the falsifier intends to obtain some benefit for himself or herself or for another, or to cause harm to society, the State or a third party;

II.- That harm results or may result to society, the State or an individual, whether to his or her property or to his or her person, honor or reputation, and

Errata to the section DOF 31-08-1931

III.- That the falsifier makes the falsification without the consent of the person to whom harm results or may result or without the consent of the person in whose name the document was made.

###### Article 246.- The penalty indicated in article 243 shall also be incurred by:

I.- The official or employee who, by deceit or surprise, causes someone to sign a public document that he or she would not have signed knowing its content;

II.- The Notary and any other public official who, in the exercise of his or her functions, issues a certification of facts that are not true, or attests to what does not appear in proceedings, registries, protocols or documents;

Errata to the section DOF 31-08-1931

III.- The person who, in order to be exempt from a legally due service, or from an obligation imposed by law, fabricates a certification of illness or impediment that he or she does not have as issued by a physician surgeon, whether the person to whom he or she attributes it actually exists, whether this person is imaginary or whether he or she takes the name of a real person, falsely attributing to them the quality of physician or surgeon;

IV.- The physician who falsely certifies that a person has an illness or other impediment sufficient to exempt him or her from providing a service required by law, or from fulfilling an obligation that it imposes, or to acquire any right;

Errata to the section DOF 31-08-1931

V.- The person who makes use of a true certification issued for another, as if it had been issued in his or her favor, or alters the one that was issued to him or her;

VI.- Those in charge of telegraphic, telephone or radio service who fabricate or falsify a dispatch of that kind;

Section reformed DOF 07-04-2016

VII.- The provider of certification services who carries out activities without having the respective accreditation, under the terms established by the Commercial Code and other applicable provisions, and

Section added DOF 07-04-2016

VIII.- The person who knowingly makes use of a false document or of a copy, transcription or testimony thereof, whether public or private.

Section reformed DOF 15-01-1951. Renumbered DOF 07-04-2016

##### CHAPTER V

Falsehood in judicial declarations and in reports given to an authority

###### Article 247.- Four to eight years of imprisonment and one hundred to three hundred days fine shall be imposed:

Paragraph reformed DOF 30-12-1991, 10-01-1994. Errata DOF 01-02-1994. Reformed DOF 20-06-2008, 23-01-2009

I.- On the person who, when questioned by any public authority other than the judicial authority in the exercise of its functions or on account of them, fails to tell the truth.

II. Repealed.

III.- On the person who bribes a witness, an expert or an interpreter, so that he or she testifies falsely in trial or obliges or compels them to do so by intimidating them or otherwise;

IV.- On the person who, in accordance with law, in any capacity except that of witness, is examined and fails to tell the truth to the detriment of another, denying that the signature with which he or she signed the document is his or her own or affirming a false fact or altering or denying a true one, or its substantial circumstances.

Paragraph reformed DOF 10-01-1994

The provisions of this section do not include cases in which the party is examined about the amount at which he or she values a thing or when he or she has the status of accused;

Reform DOF 05-01-1955: Deleted from the section the then third paragraph, whose content was moved to article 248

V.- On the person who in amparo proceedings submits reports as the responsible authority, in which he or she affirms a falsehood or denies the truth in whole or in part.

Section added DOF 05-01-1955

Article reformed DOF 09-03-1946

###### Article 247 Bis.- Five to twelve years of imprisonment and three hundred to five hundred days

fine shall be imposed:

On the person who, when examined by the judicial authority as a witness or expert, fails to tell the truth about the fact that is being investigated, or aspects, quantities, qualities or other circumstances that are relevant to establish the meaning of an opinion or expert opinion, whether by affirming, denying or maliciously concealing the existence of any data that may serve as proof of the truth or falsehood of the main fact, or that increases or decreases its gravity, or that serves to establish the nature or particularities of a technical or scientific nature that are important for the authority to issue a resolution on the matter questioned in the matter where the testimony or expert opinion is given.

When the convicted person is imposed a penalty of more than twenty years of imprisonment for false testimony or expert report, the sanction shall be eight to fifteen years of imprisonment and five hundred to eight hundred days fine.

Article added DOF 23-01-2009

###### Artículo 248.- El testigo, perito o intérprete que retracte espontáneamente sus falsas declaraciones

rendidas ante cualquier autoridad administrativa o ante la judicial antes de que se pronuncie sentencia en la instancia en que las diere, sólo pagará de 30 a 180 días multa, pero si faltare a la verdad al retractar sus declaraciones, se le aplicará la sanción que corresponda con arreglo a lo prevenido en este capítulo, considerándolo como reincidente.

Artículo reformado DOF 05-01-1955, 30-12-1991

##### CAPITULO VI

Variación del nombre o del domicilio

###### Artículo 249.- Se impondrán de diez a ciento ochenta jornadas de trabajo en favor de la comunidad:

Párrafo reformado DOF 30-12-1991, 10-01-1994

I.- Al que oculte su nombre o apellido y tome otro imaginario o el de otra persona, al declarar ante la autoridad judicial;

II.- Al que para eludir la práctica de una diligencia judicial o una notificación de cualquiera clase o citación de una autoridad, oculte su domicilio, o designe otro distinto o niegue de cualquier modo el verdadero, y

III.- Al funcionario o empleado público que, en los actos propios de su cargo, atribuyere a una persona título o nombre a sabiendas de que no le pertenece.

##### CAPITULO VII

Usurpación de funciones públicas o de profesión y uso indebido de condecoraciones, uniformes, grados jerárquicos, divisas, insignias y siglas

Denominación del Capítulo reformada DOF 21-01-1985

###### Artículo 250.- Se sancionará con prisión de uno a seis años y multa de cien a trescientos días a

quien:

Párrafo reformado DOF 10-01-1994

I.- Al que, sin ser funcionario público, se atribuya ese carácter y ejerza alguna de las funciones de tal;

II.- Al que sin tener título profesional o autorización para ejercer alguna profesión reglamentada, expedidas por autoridades u organismos legalmente capacitados para ello, conforme a las disposiciones reglamentarias del artículo 5 constitucional.

Párrafo reformado DOF 10-01-1994 a).- Se atribuya el carácter del profesionista. b).- Realice actos propios de una actividad profesional, con excepción de lo previsto en el 3er. párrafo del artículo 26 de la Ley Reglamentaria de los artículos 4o. y 5o. Constitucionales. c).- Ofrezca públicamente sus servicios como profesionista. d).- Use un título o autorización para ejercer alguna actividad profesional sin tener derecho a ello. e).- Con objeto de lucrar, se una a profesionistas legalmente autorizados con fines de ejercicio profesional o administre alguna asociación profesional.

III.- Al extranjero que ejerza una profesión reglamentada sin tener autorización de autoridad competente o después de vencido el plazo que aquella le hubiere concedido.

Fracción reformada DOF 21-01-1985, 10-01-1994

Artículo reformado DOF 30-01-1947

###### Artículo 250

bis.- Al que cometa el delito de falsificación de uniformes y divisas de las fuerzas armadas o de cualquier institución de seguridad pública, se le impondrá de cinco a doce años de prisión y hasta quinientos días multa.

Comete el delito de falsificación de uniformes y divisas de las fuerzas armadas o de cualquier institución de seguridad pública, el que sin autorización de la institución correspondiente fabrique, confeccione, produzca, imprima o pinte, cualquiera de los uniformes, insignias, credenciales de identificación, medallas, divisas, gafetes, escudos, documentos, adheribles, distintivos o piezas que contengan imágenes, siglas u otros elementos utilizados en dichas instituciones.

Se entiende por uniformes, divisas o insignias para los efectos de este artículo, los señalados en las disposiciones aplicables de las fuerzas armadas o de cualquier institución de seguridad pública. Artículo adicionado DOF 24-06-2009

###### Artículo 250

bis 1.- Se impondrá de uno a seis años de prisión y de cien a trescientos días multa a quien:

I.- Almacene, distribuya, posea o introduzca al territorio nacional uniformes o divisas de las fuerzas armadas o de cualquier institución de seguridad pública falsificadas;

II.- Adquiera, enajene o use por cualquier medio o título, uniformes, divisas, balizaje, credenciales de identificación o insignias de las fuerzas armadas o cualquier institución de seguridad pública, falsificadas;

III.- Obtenga, conserve, facilite o enajene sin autorización los verdaderos uniformes o divisas de las fuerzas armadas o de cualquier institución de seguridad pública;

IV.- Cuando se utilicen vehículos con balizaje, colores, equipamiento, originales, falsificados o con apariencia tal que se asemejen a los vehículos utilizados por las fuerzas armadas o instituciones de seguridad pública, y

V.- Al que utilice uniformes, balizaje, insignias, credenciales de identificación y divisas con tamaño similar o igual al reglamentario de las fuerzas armadas o de instituciones de seguridad pública, cuando dichas piezas, sin ser copia del original, presenten algunas de las imágenes o elementos de los contenidos en aquellos, resultando con ello objetos o piezas con apariencia similar, confundibles con los emitidos legalmente, con el objeto de hacerse pasar por servidor público.

Se impondrá de cinco a doce años de prisión y de doscientos a seiscientos días multa a quien realice alguna de las conductas previstas en este artículo con el propósito de cometer algún delito o bien cuando el sujeto activo sea o haya sido servidor público de las fuerzas armadas o de cualquier institución de seguridad pública.

Para los efectos de este artículo, se entiende por uniformes, insignias, credenciales de identificación, medallas, divisas, balizaje, gafetes, escudos, documentos, adheribles, distintivos o piezas que contengan 94 de 337

TITULO DECIMOCUARTO

Delitos contra la economía pública

Título recorrido DOF 20-01-1967. Recorrido (antes Título Decimoquinto) DOF 29-07-1970

##### CHAPTER I

Crimes against Consumption and National Wealth Chapter Title amended DOF 05-01-1955

###### Article 253.- The following are acts or omissions that seriously affect national consumption and shall be punished

with imprisonment from three to ten years and with a fine of two hundred to one thousand days:

Paragraph amended DOF 13-05-1996

I.- Those related to articles of necessary or widespread consumption or with the raw materials necessary to produce them, as well as with the raw materials essential for the activity of the national industry, consisting of: a).- Hoarding, concealment or unjustified refusal to sell, with the purpose of obtaining a price increase or affecting the supply to consumers. b).- Any act or procedure that avoids or hinders, or intends to avoid or hinder free competition in production or commerce. c).- The limitation of production or the handling thereof, with the purpose of maintaining merchandise at an unfair price.

d) (Repealed)

Subparagraph repealed DOF 10-05-2011 e).- The suspension of the production, processing, distribution, offer or sale of merchandise or the provision of services, carried out by industrialists, merchants, producers, businessmen or service providers, with the purpose of obtaining a price increase or affecting the supply to consumers.

If the unlawful conduct is abandoned within two business days following the moment when the competent administrative authority so requires, the applicable penalty shall be six months to three years of imprisonment, or one hundred to five hundred days fine;

Paragraph amended DOF 13-05-1996

95 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Amendment DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services f).- Exportation, without permission from the competent authority when such permission is necessary according to applicable legal provisions. g).- Sale with immoderate profit, by producers, distributors or merchants in general. In cases where the undue profit is less than the equivalent of sixty days of the general minimum wage in force in the region and at the time where the crime is consummated, it shall be punished with imprisonment from two to six years and from sixty to three hundred days fine;

Section amended DOF 13-05-1996 h).- Diverting, for different uses, merchandise that has been supplied for a specific purpose, by a public entity or by its distributors, when the price at which the merchandise was delivered is lower than what it would have if destined for other uses. i).- Preventing or attempting to prevent the generation, conduction, transformation, distribution or sale of public service electrical energy.

Subparagraph added DOF 13-05-1996

j) Repealed.

Subparagraph added DOF 17-05-1999. Repealed DOF 12-01-2016

II.- Bottling or packaging merchandise intended for sale, in quantities less than that indicated as net content and outside the respective tolerance or without indicating on the bottles or packages the official maximum retail price, when there is an obligation to do so.

III.- Delivering intentionally and repeatedly, when measurement is made at the time of the transaction, merchandise in quantities less than those agreed upon.

IV.- Altering or reducing by any means the properties that merchandise or products should have.

V. Reselling to a public agency, at guaranteed minimum prices or at prices authorized by the Secretariat of Economy, agricultural, maritime, river and lake products acquired at a lower price. The same penalty shall apply to the employee or official of the public agency who purchases them with knowledge of that situation or causes the producer to be forced to sell at lower prices to third parties.

Section amended DOF 09-04-2012

In any of the cases mentioned in the preceding sections, the judge may also order the suspension for up to one year or the dissolution of the company of which the offender is a member or representative, if the circumstances mentioned in article 11 of this Code concur.

In the cases of subparagraphs a), f) and h), of section I and of section IV of this article, the authority having knowledge of the facts shall immediately proceed to deposit the articles of necessary or widespread consumption, the raw materials to produce them or the raw materials essential for national industrial activity. The deposit shall be made in a general warehouse that is a national auxiliary credit organization and the goods shall be generically designated, in the terms of article 281 of the General Law of Negotiable Instruments and Credit Operations; when dealing with goods whose special nature does not allow generic deposit, a specific one shall be established, also indicating the term and conditions under which their sale or destruction must be carried out in accordance with the provisions of article 282 of the same Law. The deposit certificate to be issued shall be non-negotiable and shall be sent to the Public Prosecutor's Office or, as the case may be, to the Judge hearing the case, for appropriate purposes.

###### Artículo 254 bis. Imprisonment of five to ten years and one thousand to ten thousand days of fine shall be imposed on whoever enters into, orders or executes contracts, agreements, arrangements or combinations among economic agents competing with each other, whose object or effect is any of the following:

- I. To fix, raise, concert or manipulate the sale or purchase price of goods or services at which they are offered or demanded in the markets;
- II. To establish the obligation not to produce, process, distribute, commercialize or acquire but only a restricted or limited quantity of goods or the provision or transaction of a restricted or limited number, volume or frequency of services;
- III. To divide, distribute, assign or impose portions or segments of a current or potential market of goods and services, through clientele, suppliers, determined or determinable times or spaces;
- IV. To establish, concert or coordinate bids or abstention in tenders, contests, auctions or public sales, and
- V. To exchange information with any of the objects or effects referred to in the preceding sections.

The crime provided in this article shall be prosecuted by complaint of the Federal Economic Competition Commission or the Federal Telecommunications Institute, as appropriate, which may only be filed with the opinion of probable responsibility, in the terms provided in the Federal Economic Competition Law.

There shall be no criminal responsibility for economic agents who avail themselves of the benefit referred to in article 103 of the Federal Economic Competition Law, subject to a resolution of the Federal Economic Competition Commission or the Federal Telecommunications Institute determining that they comply with the terms established in said provision and other applicable ones.

Proceedings for this crime may be dismissed at the request of the Plenary of the Federal Economic Competition Commission or the Federal Telecommunications Institute, when the accused comply with the administrative sanctions imposed and, in addition, the requirements provided in the technical criteria issued by the Federal Economic Competition Commission or the Federal Telecommunications Institute are met.

The criminal action shall prescribe in a period equal to the arithmetic mean of the custodial sentence referred to in the first paragraph of this article.

Article added DOF 30-12-1991. Repealed DOF 13-12-1996. Added DOF 10-05-2011. Amended DOF 23-05-2014

TITULO DECIMOQUINTO

Crimes against Freedom and Normal Psychosexual Development

Title renumbered DOF 20-01-1967. Renumbered (formerly Title Sixteenth) DOF 29-07-1970. Denomination amended DOF 21-01-1991

##### Capítulo I

Sexual Harassment, Sexual Abuse, Statutory Rape and Rape Denomination of the Chapter amended DOF 21-01-1991

###### Article 259

Bis.- Whoever with lascivious purposes repeatedly harasses a person of any sex, taking advantage of their hierarchical position derived from their labor, teaching, domestic or any other relationship that implies subordination, shall be subject to a sanction of up to eight hundred days' fine. If the harasser were a public servant and used the means or circumstances that the position provides, in addition to the penalties indicated, they shall be removed from office and may be disqualified from holding any other public office for up to one year.

Paragraph amended DOF 15-06-2018

Sexual harassment shall only be punishable when harm or damage is caused.

Proceedings against the harasser shall only be initiated at the request of the offended party.

Article added DOF 21-01-1991

###### Article 260.

The crime of sexual abuse is committed by whoever performs on a person, without their consent, or forces them to perform on themselves or on another person, sexual acts without the purpose of reaching copulation. 99 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

Whoever commits this crime shall be subject to a penalty of six to ten years in prison and up to two hundred days' fine.

For purposes of this article, sexual acts are understood as obscene bodily touching or fondling, or those that represent explicitly sexual acts or force the victim to perform them. It is also considered sexual abuse when the victim is forced to observe a sexual act, or to exhibit their body without their consent.

If physical or psychological violence is used, the penalty shall be increased by one-half more in its minimum and maximum.

Article amended DOF 03-01-1989, 21-01-1991, 30-12-1997, 14-06-2012

###### Article 261. Whoever commits the crime of sexual abuse on a person under fifteen years of

age or on a person who does not have the capacity to understand the meaning of the act, even with their consent, or who for any reason cannot resist it or is forced to perform it on themselves or on another person, shall be subject to a penalty of six to thirteen years in prison and up to five hundred days' fine. If violence is used, the penalty shall be increased by one-half more in its minimum and maximum. Article amended DOF 03-01-1989, 21-01-1991, 30-12-1997, 14-06-2012

###### Article 262.

Whoever has copulation with a person over fifteen years and under eighteen, obtaining their consent by means of deceit, shall be subject to three months to four years in prison.

Article amended DOF 14-01-1985, 21-01-1991, 14-06-2012

###### Article 263.- In the case of the previous article, proceedings shall not be initiated against the active subject, except by complaint

of the offended party or their representatives. Article amended DOF 21-01-1991

###### Article 264.- (Repealed).

Article repealed DOF 13-01-1984

###### Article 265. The crime of rape is committed by whoever through physical or moral violence performs

copulation with a person of any sex, shall be subject to imprisonment of eight to twenty years.

Paragraph amended DOF 14-06-2012

For the purposes of this article, copulation is understood as the introduction of the male member into the body of the victim through vaginal, anal or oral means, regardless of their sex.

It shall also be considered rape and shall be punished with imprisonment of eight to twenty years for whoever introduces through vaginal or anal means any element or instrument other than the male member, by means of physical or moral violence, whatever the sex of the offended party.

Paragraph amended DOF 14-06-2012

Article amended DOF 20-01-1967, 13-01-1984, 03-01-1989, 21-01-1991, 30-12-1997

###### Article 265

bis.- If the victim of the rape were the wife or concubine, the penalty provided in the previous article shall be imposed.

This crime shall be prosecuted by complaint of the offended party.

Article added DOF 30-12-1997

I. Whoever without violence performs copulation with a person under fifteen years of age;

Section amended DOF 14-06-2012

II.- Whoever without violence performs copulation with a person who does not have the capacity to understand the meaning of the act or for any reason cannot resist it; and

III. Whoever without violence and with lascivious purposes introduces through anal or vaginal means any element or instrument other than the male member into a person under fifteen years of age or person who does not have the capacity to understand the meaning of the act, or for any reason cannot resist it, whatever the sex of the victim.

Section amended DOF 14-06-2012

If physical or moral violence is used, the minimum and maximum of the penalty shall be increased by up to one-half.

Article amended DOF 20-01-1967, 03-01-1989, 21-01-1991, 30-12-1997

###### Article 266 Bis.- The penalties provided for sexual abuse and rape shall be increased up to

one-half in their minimum and maximum, when:

I.- The crime is committed with direct or immediate intervention of two or more persons;

II.- The crime is committed by an ascendant against their descendant, the latter against the former, a sibling against their collateral, the guardian against their ward, or by the stepfather or lover of the offended party's mother against the stepchild. In addition to the prison sentence, the guilty party shall lose parental authority or guardianship, in cases where they exercise it over the victim;

III.- The crime is committed by someone who holds a public position or employment or exercises their profession, using the means or circumstances that these provide. In addition to the prison sentence, the convicted person shall be removed from the position or employment or suspended for a term of five years in the exercise of said profession;

IV.- The crime is committed by the person who has the offended party under their custody, care or education or takes advantage of the trust placed in them.

V. The crime is committed following the administration of narcotics or psychotropic substances to the victim, against their will or without their knowledge.

Section added DOF 15-06-2018

Article added DOF 20-01-1967. Amended DOF 03-01-1989, 21-01-1991

Incest

###### Article 272. The crime of incest shall be punished with a penalty of one to six years in prison, when

ascendants have sexual relations with their descendants, provided that the latter are of legal age.
