Chapter added DOF 13-01-1984

###### Artículo 276-Bis.- When as a consequence of the commission of any of the crimes provided for in this

Title children result, the reparation of damages shall include the payment of support for them and for the mother, in the terms established by civil legislation for cases of divorce.

Artículo adicionado DOF 13-01-1984

TITULO DECIMOSEXTO

Crimes Against Civil Status and Bigamy

Título recorrido DOF 20-01-1967. Recorrido (antes Título Decimoséptimo) DOF 29-07-1970

###### Artículo 277.- From one to six years of imprisonment and a fine of one hundred to one thousand pesos shall be imposed on those who, with the purpose of altering civil status, commit any of the following infractions:

I.- Attributing a newborn child to a woman who is not really his mother;

II.- Having a birth that has not occurred registered in the civil registry offices;

102 de 337

CÓDIGO PENAL FEDERAL

CÁMARA DE DIPUTADOS DEL H. CONGRESO DE LA UNIÓN Última Reforma DOF 08-05-2023

Secretaría General

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios

III.- Parents who do not present their child to the Registry with the purpose of causing him to lose his civil status, or who falsely declare his death, or present him concealing his names or supposing that the parents are other persons;

IV.- Those who substitute one child for another, or commit concealment of an infant, and

V.- Whoever usurps the civil status of another with the purpose of acquiring family rights that do not correspond to him.

###### Artículo 278.- Whoever commits any of the crimes expressed in the preceding article shall lose the

right to inherit that he may have with respect to the persons whom he harms in their family rights by the commission of the crime.

###### Artículo 279.- Up to five years of imprisonment or from 180 to 360 days fine shall be imposed on whoever, being united with a person in marriage not dissolved nor declared null, contracts another marriage with the legal formalities.

Artículo reformado DOF 30-12-1991

TITULO DECIMOSEPTIMO

Crimes in Matters of Burials and Exhumations

Denominación reformada DOF 15-01-1951. Título recorrido DOF 20-01-1967. Recorrido (antes Título Decimoctavo) DOF 29-07-1970

##### CAPITULO UNICO

Violation of Laws on Burials and Exhumations

Capítulo adicionado DOF 15-01-1951

###### Artículo 280.- Imprisonment from three days to two years or from 30 to 90 days fine shall be imposed:

Párrafo reformado DOF 30-12-1991

I.- On whoever conceals, destroys or buries a corpse or a human fetus, without the order of the authority that must give it or without the requirements demanded by the Civil and Sanitary Codes or special laws; II.- On whoever conceals, destroys, or without the corresponding license buries the corpse of a person, provided that the death has been as a consequence of blows, wounds or other injuries, if the offender knew that circumstance.

In this case no sanction shall be applied to the ascendants or descendants, spouse or siblings of the person responsible for the homicide, and

III.- On whoever exhumes a corpse without the legal requirements or with violation of rights.

Fe de erratas al artículo DOF 31-08-1931. Reformado DOF 15-01-1951

###### Artículo 280

Bis.- A penalty of five to eight years of imprisonment and from five hundred to one thousand days fine shall be imposed on whoever incinerates, buries, disintegrates or totally or partially destroys the corpse or human remains of an unidentified person, without authorization from the competent authorities in the matter. Artículo adicionado DOF 17-11-2017

###### Artículo 281.- From one to five years of imprisonment shall be imposed:

I.- On whoever violates a burial mound, a sepulcher, a grave or coffin, and

103 de 337

CÓDIGO PENAL FEDERAL

CÁMARA DE DIPUTADOS DEL H. CONGRESO DE LA UNIÓN Última Reforma DOF 08-05-2023

Secretaría General

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios

II.- On whoever desecrates a corpse or human remains with acts of vilification, mutilation, brutality or necrophilia. If the acts of necrophilia consist in the performance of coitus, the penalty of imprisonment shall be from four to eight years.

Artículo reformado DOF 13-01-1984

TITULO DECIMOCTAVO

Crimes Against the Peace and Security of Persons

Título recorrido DOF 20-01-1967. Recorrido (antes Título Decimonoveno) DOF 29-07-1970

##### CAPITULO I

Threats and Illegal Extrajudicial Collection

Denominación del Capítulo reformada DOF 22-06-2017

###### Artículo 282.- A sanction of three days to one year of imprisonment or from 180 to 360 days fine shall be applied:

Párrafo reformado DOF 30-12-1991

I.- On whoever in any way threatens another with causing him harm to his person, to his property, to his honor or to his rights, or to the person, honor, property or rights of someone with whom he is linked by some bond, and

II.- On whoever by means of threats of any kind tries to prevent another from doing what he has the right to do.

If the offended party were any of the relatives or persons referred to in articles 343 bis and 343 ter, in this latter case provided they reside in the same domicile, the corresponding penalty shall be increased up to one third in its minimum and maximum.

Párrafo adicionado DOF 30-12-1997

If the person offended by the threat were a victim or offended party or witness in a criminal proceeding, the penalty shall be from four to eight years of imprisonment and from one hundred to three hundred days fine.

Párrafo adicionado DOF 23-01-2009

The crimes provided for in this article shall be prosecuted by complaint, with the exception of that established in the preceding paragraph which shall be prosecuted ex officio.

Párrafo adicionado DOF 30-12-1991. Reformado DOF 23-01-2009

###### Artículo 283.- Surety not to offend shall be required:

I.- If the damages with which one threatens are minor or avoidable;

II.- If the threats are by means of emblems or signs, hieroglyphics or phrases of double meaning, and III.- If the threat has as a condition that the threatened person not perform an act unlawful in itself. In this case surety shall also be required from the threatened person, if the judge deems it necessary.

On whoever does not provide the surety not to offend, imprisonment from three days to six months shall be imposed.

###### Artículo 284.- If the threatener carries out his threat, the sanction for it and that of the resulting crime shall be accumulated.

If the threatener demanded that the threatened person commit a crime, to the sanction for the threat shall be accumulated that which corresponds to him for his participation in the resulting crime. Párrafo reformado DOF 10-01-1994

104 de 337

CÓDIGO PENAL FEDERAL

CÁMARA DE DIPUTADOS DEL H. CONGRESO DE LA UNIÓN Última Reforma DOF 08-05-2023 Secretaría General

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios

###### Artículo 284

Bis. From one to four years of imprisonment and a fine from fifty thousand to three hundred thousand pesos shall be imposed on whoever carries out the activity of illegal extrajudicial collection.

If he also uses false documents or seals, the penalty and the economic sanction shall be increased by one half. If he incurs in usurpation of functions or profession, the rules of the concurrence of crimes indicated in the Federal Penal Code shall be applied.

Illegal extrajudicial collection is understood as the use of unlawful violence or intimidation, whether personally or through any means, to demand payment of a debt derived from activities regulated in federal laws, including credits or financing that have been originally granted by persons habitually and professionally engaged in this activity, regardless of the holder of the collection rights at the time of carrying out the collection. It shall not be considered as unlawful intimidation to inform of those possible and legally valid consequences of non-payment or the ability to initiate legal actions against the debtor, guarantor, joint obligor or any third party related to them when these are legally possible.

Artículo adicionado DOF 22-06-2017

##### CAPITULO II

Trespass of Dwelling

###### Artículo 285.- Se impondrán de un mes a dos años de prisión y multa de diez a cien pesos, al que, sin

motivo justificado, sin orden de autoridad competente y fuera de los casos en que la ley lo permita, se introduzca, furtivamente o con engaño o violencia, o sin permiso de la persona autorizada para darlo, a un departamento, vivienda, aposento o dependencias de una casa habitada.

###### Article 285.- A penalty of one month to two years of imprisonment and a fine of ten to one hundred pesos shall be imposed on anyone who, without

justified cause, without order from competent authority and outside of cases in which the law permits it, enters, furtively or with deceit or violence, or without permission from the person authorized to give it, into a dwelling, housing, room or dependencies of an inhabited house.

###### Artículo 286.- Al que en despoblado o en paraje solitario haga uso de violencia sobre una persona

con el propósito de causar un mal, obtener un lucro o de exigir su asentimiento para cualquier fin y cualesquiera que sean los medios y el grado de violencia que se emplee, e independientemente de cualquier hecho delictuoso que resulte cometido, se le castigará con prisión de uno a cinco años. Fe de erratas al párrafo DOF 31-08-1931

La pena será de diez a treinta años de prisión para el que en vías generales de comunicación tales como caminos, carreteras, puentes o vías férreas, haga uso de la violencia en contra de los ocupantes de un vehículo de transporte público o privado.

Párrafo adicionado DOF 10-01-1994. Reformado DOF 18-06-2010

###### Article 286.- Anyone who in an unpopulated area or in a solitary place uses violence against a person

with the purpose of causing harm, obtaining profit or demanding their consent for any purpose and whatever the means and degree of violence employed may be, and independently of any criminal act that may be committed, shall be punished with imprisonment of one to five years. Erratum to the paragraph DOF 31-08-1931

The penalty shall be ten to thirty years of imprisonment for anyone who on general communication routes such as roads, highways, bridges or railways, uses violence against the occupants of a public or private transport vehicle.

Paragraph added DOF 10-01-1994. Amended DOF 18-06-2010

###### Artículo 287.- Si los salteadores atacaren una población, se aplicarán de veinte a treinta años de

prisión a los cabecillas o jefes, y de quince a veinte años a los demás.

###### Article 287.- If highway robbers attack a town, a penalty of twenty to thirty years of

imprisonment shall apply to the ringleaders or chiefs, and fifteen to twenty years to the others.

TITULO DECIMONOVENO

Delitos contra la vida y la integridad corporal

Título recorrido DOF 20-01-1967. Recorrido (antes Título Vigésimo) DOF 29-07-1970

TITLE NINETEENTH

Crimes against life and bodily integrity

Title renumbered DOF 20-01-1967. Renumbered (formerly Title Twentieth) DOF 29-07-1970

##### CAPITULO I

Lesiones

##### CHAPTER I

Injuries

###### Artículo 288.

Bajo el nombre de lesión, se comprende no solamente las heridas, escoriaciones, contusiones, fracturas, dislocaciones, quemaduras, sino toda alteración en la salud y cualquier otro daño que deja huella material en el cuerpo humano, si esos efectos son producidos por una causa externa.

###### Article 288.

Under the name of injury, it includes not only wounds, abrasions, contusions, fractures, dislocations, burns, but all alterations in health and any other damage that leaves material trace on the human body, if those effects are produced by an external cause.

###### Artículo 289.- Al que infiera una lesión que no ponga en peligro la vida del ofendido y tarde en sanar

menos de quince días, se le impondrán de tres a ocho meses de prisión, o de treinta a cincuenta días 105 de 337

CÓDIGO PENAL FEDERAL

CÁMARA DE DIPUTADOS DEL H. CONGRESO DE LA UNIÓN Última Reforma DOF 08-05-2023

Secretaría General

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios multa, o ambas sanciones a juicio del juez. Si tardare en sanar más de quince días, se le impondrán de cuatro meses a dos años de prisión y de sesenta a doscientos setenta días multa.

En estos casos, el delito se perseguirá por querella, salvo en el que contempla el artículo 295, en cuyo caso se perseguirá de oficio.

Artículo reformado DOF 13-01-1984, 30-12-1991, 13-05-1996

###### Article 289.- Anyone who inflicts an injury that does not endanger the life of the victim and takes to heal

less than fifteen days, shall be imposed three to eight months of imprisonment, or thirty to fifty days 105 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Amendment DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services fine, or both sanctions at the judge's discretion. If it takes more than fifteen days to heal, a penalty of four months to two years of imprisonment and sixty to two hundred seventy days fine shall be imposed.

In these cases, the crime shall be prosecuted by complaint, except in the one contemplated in article 295, in which case it shall be prosecuted ex officio.

Article amended DOF 13-01-1984, 30-12-1991, 13-05-1996

###### Artículo 290.- Se impondrán de dos a cinco años de prisión y multa de cien a trescientos pesos, al

que infiera una lesión que deje al ofendido cicatriz en la cara, perpetuamente notable.

###### Article 290.- A penalty of two to five years of imprisonment and a fine of one hundred to three hundred pesos shall be imposed on anyone

who inflicts an injury that leaves the victim with a permanently noticeable scar on the face.

###### Artículo 291.

Se impondrán de tres a cinco años de prisión y multa de trescientos a quinientos pesos, al que infiera una lesión que perturbe para siempre la vista, o disminuya la facultad de oír, entorpezca o debilite permanentemente una mano, un pie, un brazo, una pierna, o cualquier otro órgano, el uso de la palabra o alguna de las facultades mentales.

###### Article 291.

A penalty of three to five years of imprisonment and a fine of three hundred to five hundred pesos shall be imposed on anyone who inflicts an injury that permanently disturbs vision, or diminishes the faculty of hearing, permanently impairs or weakens a hand, a foot, an arm, a leg, or any other organ, the use of speech or any of the mental faculties.

###### Artículo 292.- Se impondrán de cinco a ocho años de prisión al que infiera una lesión de la que

resulte una enfermedad segura o probablemente incurable, la inutilización completa o la pérdida de un ojo, de un brazo, de una mano, de una pierna o de un pié, o de cualquier otro órgano; cuando quede perjudicada para siempre, cualquiera función orgánica o cuando el ofendido quede sordo, impotente o con una deformidad incorregible.

Se impondrán de seis a diez años de prisión, al que infiera una lesión a consecuencia de la cual resulte incapacidad permanente para trabajar, enajenación mental, la pérdida de la vista o del habla o de las funciones sexuales.

###### Article 292.- A penalty of five to eight years of imprisonment shall be imposed on anyone who inflicts an injury from which

results a certain or probably incurable disease, the complete incapacitation or loss of an eye, an arm, a hand, a leg or a foot, or any other organ; when any organic function is permanently impaired or when the victim becomes deaf, impotent or with an incorrigible deformity.

A penalty of six to ten years of imprisonment shall be imposed on anyone who inflicts an injury as a consequence of which results permanent incapacity to work, mental alienation, loss of sight or speech or sexual functions.

###### Artículo 293.- Al que infiera lesiones que pongan en peligro la vida, se le impondrán de tres a seis

años de prisión, sin perjuicio de las sanciones que le correspondan conforme a los artículos anteriores.

###### Article 293.- Anyone who inflicts injuries that endanger life shall be imposed three to six

years of imprisonment, without prejudice to the sanctions that correspond according to the previous articles.

###### Artículo 294.- (Se deroga).

Fe de erratas al artículo DOF 31-08-1931. Derogado DOF 13-01-1984

###### Article 294.- (Repealed).

Erratum to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Repealed DOF 13-01-1984

###### Artículo 295.- Al que ejerciendo la patria potestad o la tutela infiera lesiones a los menores o pupilos

bajo su guarda, el juez podrá imponerle, además de la pena correspondiente a las lesiones, suspensión o privación en el ejercicio de aquellos derechos.

Artículo reformado DOF 13-01-1984

###### Article 295.- Anyone who exercising parental authority or guardianship inflicts injuries on minors or wards

under their custody, the judge may impose, in addition to the penalty corresponding to the injuries, suspension or deprivation in the exercise of those rights.

Article amended DOF 13-01-1984

###### Artículo 296.- (Se deroga).

Fe de erratas al artículo DOF 31-08-1931. Derogado DOF 23-12-1985

Erratum to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Repealed DOF 23-12-1985

###### Artículo 297.- Si las lesiones fueren inferidas en riña o en duelo, las sanciones señaladas en los

artículos que anteceden podrán disminuirse hasta la mitad o hasta los cinco sextos, según que se trate del provocado o del provocador, y teniendo en cuenta la mayor o menor importancia de la provocación y lo dispuesto en los artículos 51 y 52.

Artículo reformado DOF 15-01-1951

###### Article 297.- If the injuries were inflicted in a brawl or in a duel, the sanctions indicated in the

preceding articles may be reduced by up to half or up to five-sixths, depending on whether it concerns the provoked or the provoker, and taking into account the greater or lesser importance of the provocation and the provisions of articles 51 and 52.

Article amended DOF 15-01-1951

###### Artículo 298.- Al responsable de una lesión calificada se le aumentará la sanción hasta el doble de la

que corresponda por la lesión simple causada.

Fe de erratas al artículo DOF 31-08-1931. Reformado DOF 17-05-1999

###### Article 298.- The person responsible for an aggravated injury shall have the sanction increased up to double that

which corresponds for the simple injury caused.

Erratum to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 17-05-1999

###### Artículo 299.- Derogado.

Artículo derogado DOF 10-01-1994

###### Article 299.- Repealed.

Article repealed DOF 10-01-1994

###### Artículo 300.- Si la víctima fuere alguno de los parientes o personas a que se refieren los artículos

343 bis y 343 ter, en este último caso siempre y cuando habiten en el mismo domicilio, se aumentará la 106 de 337

343 bis and 343 ter, in this latter case provided they reside in the same domicile, the 106 of 337

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services penalty that corresponds shall be increased by up to one-third in its minimum and maximum, in accordance with the preceding articles, unless the crime of family violence is also typified. Article amended DOF 30-12-1997

###### Artículo 301.- De las lesiones que a una persona cause algún animal bravío, será responsable el que

con esa intención lo azuce, o lo suelte o haga esto último por descuido.

###### Article 301.- For injuries caused to a person by a wild animal, the person who

with such intention incites it, or releases it or does the latter through negligence shall be responsible.

##### CAPITULO II

Homicidio

##### CHAPTER II

Homicide

###### Artículo 302.

Comete el delito de homicidio: el que priva de la vida a otro.

###### Article 302.

Commits the crime of homicide: anyone who deprives another of life.

###### Artículo 303.

Para la aplicación de las sanciones que correspondan al que infrinja el artículo anterior, no se tendrá como mortal una lesión, sino cuando se verifiquen las tres circunstancias siguientes: I.- Que la muerte se deba a las alteraciones causadas por la lesión en el órgano u órganos interesados, alguna de sus consecuencias inmediatas o alguna complicación determinada por la misma lesión y que no pudo combatirse, ya sea por ser incurable, ya por no tenerse al alcance los recursos necesarios;

Fe de erratas a la fracción DOF 31-08-1931

II.- Se deroga.

Fracción derogada DOF 10-01-1994

III.- Que si se encuentra el cadáver del occiso, declaren dos peritos después de hacer la autopsia, cuando ésta sea necesaria, que la lesión fue mortal, sujetándose para ello a las reglas contenidas en este artículo, en los dos siguientes y en el Código de Procedimientos Penales. Cuando el cadáver no se encuentre, o por otro motivo no se haga la autopsia, bastará que los peritos, en vista de los datos que obren en la causa, declaren que la muerte fue resultado de las lesiones inferidas.

###### Article 303.

For the application of sanctions that correspond to anyone who violates the previous article, an injury shall not be considered mortal, except when the following three circumstances are verified: I.- That the death is due to the alterations caused by the injury in the organ or organs involved, any of its immediate consequences or any complication determined by the same injury and which could not be combated, either because it is incurable, or because the necessary resources are not available;

Erratum to the section DOF 31-08-1931

II.- Repealed.

Section repealed DOF 10-01-1994

III.- That if the corpse of the deceased is found, two experts declare after performing the autopsy, when this is necessary, that the injury was mortal, subjecting themselves for this purpose to the rules contained in this article, in the following two and in the Code of Criminal Procedure. When the corpse is not found, or for another reason the autopsy is not performed, it shall suffice that the experts, in view of the data in the case, declare that the death was the result of the injuries inflicted.

###### Artículo 304.- Siempre que se verifiquen las tres circunstancias del artículo anterior, se tendrá como

mortal una lesión, aunque se pruebe:

I.- Que se habría evitado la muerte con auxilios oportunos;

II.- Que la lesión no habría sido mortal en otra persona, y

III.- Que fue a causa de la constitución física de la víctima, o de las circunstancias en que recibió la lesión.

###### Article 304.- Whenever the three circumstances of the previous article are verified, it shall be considered

mortal an injury, even if it is proven:

I.- That death would have been avoided with timely assistance;

II.- That the injury would not have been mortal in another person, and

III.- That it was due to the physical constitution of the victim, or the circumstances in which the injury was received.

###### Artículo 305.- A lesion shall not be considered mortal, although the person who received it dies: when death

is the result of a cause prior to the lesion and upon which the lesion has not had an influence, or when the lesion has been aggravated by subsequent causes, such as the application of positively harmful medications, unfortunate surgical operations, excesses or imprudences of the patient or those around him.

###### Artículo 306.- (Repealed).

Errata to article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 08-03-1968. Repealed DOF 30-12-1991

###### Artículo 307.- The person responsible for any simple intentional homicide that does not have a special

sanction provided in this Code shall be imposed from twelve to twenty-four years of imprisonment. Article amended DOF 05-01-1955, 17-05-1999

107 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023 General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

###### Artículo 308.- If the homicide is committed in a brawl, four to twelve years of imprisonment shall be

applied to its author.

If the homicide is committed in a duel, two to eight years of imprisonment shall be applied to its author. In addition to that provided in articles 51 and 52 for the setting of penalties within the minimum and maximum previously indicated, who was the provoked and who was the provoker shall be taken into account, as well as the greater or lesser importance of the provocation.

Errata to article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 15-01-1951

###### Artículo 309.- (Repealed).

Article amended DOF 08-03-1968. Repealed DOF 23-12-1985

##### CAPITULO III

Common rules for lesions and homicide

###### Artículo 310. (Repealed)

Errata to article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 10-01-1994. Repealed DOF 14-06-2012

###### Artículo 311.- Repealed.

Article amended DOF 18-02-1969. Repealed DOF 10-01-1994

###### Artículo 312.- Whoever provides assistance to or induces another to commit suicide shall be punished with the penalty

of one to five years of imprisonment; if he provides such assistance to the point of himself executing the death, the imprisonment shall be from four to twelve years.

###### Artículo 313.- If the deceased or suicide victim is a minor or suffers from any form of mental

derangement, the sanctions indicated for qualified homicide or qualified lesions shall be applied to the killer or instigator.

###### Artículo 314.- For all criminal purposes, a brawl is understood to be: a fight of deeds and not of

words, between two or more persons.

###### Artículo 315.- Lesions and homicide are understood to be qualified when they are committed with

premeditation, with advantage, with treachery or with treason.

Errata to paragraph DOF 31-08-1931

There is premeditation: whenever the offender intentionally causes a lesion, after having reflected on the crime he is about to commit.

Errata to paragraph DOF 31-08-1931

Premeditation shall be presumed when the lesions or homicide are committed by flooding, fire, mines, bombs or explosives; by means of poisons or any other substance harmful to health, venereal contagion, asphyxiation or narcotics or by remuneration given or promised; by torture, depraved motives or brutal ferocity.

###### Artículo 315 Bis.- The penalty of article 320 of this Code shall be imposed when the homicide is

committed intentionally, on the occasion of a rape or robbery by the active subject thereof, against his victim or victims.

The penalty referred to in Article 320 of this Code shall also be applied when the homicide is intentionally committed in a dwelling-house, having penetrated the same in a furtive manner, with deceit or violence, or without permission from the person authorized to give it.

Article added DOF 03-01-1989

108 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

###### Artículo 316.- It is understood that there is advantage:

I.- When the delinquent is superior in physical force to the offended and the latter is not armed; II.- When he is superior by the weapons he employs, by his greater skill in handling them or by the number of those who accompany him;

III. When he avails himself of some means that weakens the defense of the offended;

Subsection amended DOF 14-06-2012

IV. When the latter is unarmed or fallen and the former armed or standing;

Subsection amended DOF 14-06-2012

V. The active party is a man superior in physical force and the passive party a woman or person under eighteen years of age;

Subsection added DOF 14-06-2012

VI. The homicide and lesions occur in situations of family violence; and

Subsection added DOF 14-06-2012

VII. There exists a situation of vulnerability motivated by physical or mental condition or by discrimination.

Subsection added DOF 14-06-2012

The advantage shall not be taken into consideration in the first three cases, if the one who has it acted in legitimate defense, nor in the fourth, if the one who is armed or standing was the assaulted party, and, furthermore, his life would have been in danger by not taking advantage of that circumstance.

Errata to paragraph DOF 31-08-1931

###### Artículo 317.- The advantage shall only be considered as a qualifying circumstance of the crimes referred to in the

previous chapters of this title: when it is such that the delinquent runs no risk of being killed or wounded by the offended and the former does not act in legitimate defense.

###### Artículo 318.- Treachery consists: in intentionally surprising someone suddenly, or

employing ambush or another means that does not give him a place to defend himself or avoid the harm intended for him.

###### Artículo 319.- It is said that one acts with treason: he who not only employs treachery but also

perfidy, violating the faith or security that he had expressly promised to his victim, or the tacit one that the victim should have expected from him by their relations of kinship, gratitude, friendship or any other that inspires confidence.

Errata to article DOF 31-08-1931

###### Artículo 320.- The person responsible for a qualified homicide shall be imposed from thirty to sixty years of

imprisonment.

Article amended DOF 15-01-1951, 05-01-1955, 03-01-1989, 17-05-1999

###### Artículo 321.- Repealed.

Errata to article DOF 31-08-1931. Repealed DOF 13-05-1996

###### Artículo 321 Bis.- No proceedings shall be brought against one who culpably causes lesions or homicide to

the detriment of an ascendant or descendant related by blood in a direct line, sibling, spouse, concubine, adopter or adoptee, unless the author is under the effect of intoxicating beverages, narcotics or psychotropic substances, without medical prescription, or does not aid the victim. 109 of 337

###### Artículo 322.

In addition to the sanctions indicated in the two previous chapters, judges may, if they deem it convenient:

I.- Declare the offenders subject to police surveillance, and

II.- Prohibit them from going to a certain place, Municipality, District or State, or residing in it.

##### CAPITULO IV

Homicide by reason of kinship or relationship Denomination of Chapter amended DOF 10-01-1994

###### Artículo 323.

Whoever deprives of life his ascendant or descendant related by blood in a direct line, sibling, spouse, cohabitant, civil partner, concubine, adopter or adoptee, with knowledge of that relationship shall be imposed imprisonment from thirty to sixty years. If such knowledge is lacking, the punishability provided in article 307 shall apply, without prejudice to observing any circumstance that aggravates or attenuates the sanction referred to in chapters II and III above.

Article amended DOF 10-01-1994, 14-06-2012

###### Artículo 324.- Repealed.

Article amended DOF 05-01-1955, 03-01-1989. Repealed DOF 10-01-1994

##### Capítulo V

Femicide

Chapter title reformed DOF 14-06-2012

###### Artículo 325. Whoever deprives a woman of life for a gender-related reason commits the crime of

femicide.

Paragraph reformed DOF 25-04-2023

A gender-related reason is considered to exist when any of the following circumstances occur:

Paragraph heading added DOF 25-04-2023

I. The victim shows signs of sexual violence of any type;

II. The victim has been inflicted with defamatory or degrading injuries or mutilations, prior or subsequent to deprivation of life or acts of necrophilia;

III. There exists a history or evidence of any type of violence in the family, labor, community, political or school sphere, by the active subject against the victim;

Subsection reformed DOF 25-04-2023

IV. There has existed between the active subject and the victim kinship by consanguinity or affinity or a sentimental, affective, labor, teaching, trust relationship or any de facto relationship between the parties;

Subsection reformed DOF 25-04-2023

V. There exists evidence that establishes there were direct or indirect threats related to the criminal act, harassment or injuries by the active subject against the victim;

Subsection reformed DOF 25-04-2023

110 de 337

CÓDIGO PENAL FEDERAL

CÁMARA DE DIPUTADOS DEL H. CONGRESO DE LA UNIÓN Última Reforma DOF 08-05-2023

Secretaría General

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios

VI. The victim has been held incommunicado, whatever the time prior to the deprivation of life;

VII. The victim's body is exposed, thrown, deposited or exhibited in a public place, or Subsection reformed DOF 25-04-2023

VIII. The active subject has forced the victim to perform an activity or work or has exercised over her any form of exploitation.

Subsection added DOF 25-04-2023

Whoever commits the crime of femicide shall be sentenced to forty to sixty years of imprisonment and five hundred to one thousand days fine.

The penalty shall be aggravated by up to one third when the victim is a minor woman, pregnant, elderly or with a disability, as well as when the active subject is a public servant and has committed the conduct taking advantage of this condition.

Paragraph added DOF 25-04-2023

In addition to the sanctions described in this article, the active subject shall lose all rights in relation to the victim, including those of a succession nature. Where applicable, he shall also lose all rights in relation to the victim's children, guaranteeing the best interest of children in terms of that provided by the General Law on the Rights of Girls, Boys and Adolescents.

Paragraph reformed DOF 25-04-2023

The public servant who delays or hinders maliciously or through negligence the procurement or administration of justice shall be sentenced to imprisonment of three to eight years and five hundred to one thousand five hundred days fine, and shall also be dismissed and disqualified from three to ten years to hold another public employment, position or commission.

Reform DOF 25-04-2023: Repealed the then fourth paragraph from the article

##### CAPITULO VI

Abortion

###### Artículo 329.

Abortion is the death of the product of conception at any moment of pregnancy.

###### Artículo 330.- Whoever causes a woman to abort shall be sentenced to one to three years of imprisonment,

whatever the means employed, provided it is done with her consent. When consent is lacking, the imprisonment shall be three to six years and if physical or moral violence is involved, the offender shall be sentenced to six to eight years of imprisonment.

###### Artículo 331.

If the abortion is caused by a physician, surgeon, midwife or birth attendant, in addition to the sanctions corresponding to them under the previous article, they shall be suspended from two to five years in the exercise of their profession.

111 de 337

CÓDIGO PENAL FEDERAL

CÁMARA DE DIPUTADOS DEL H. CONGRESO DE LA UNIÓN Última Reforma DOF 08-05-2023

Secretaría General

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios

###### Artículo 332.- Six months to one year of imprisonment shall be imposed on the mother who voluntarily

procures her abortion or consents to another causing her to abort, if these three circumstances occur: I.- That she does not have a bad reputation;

II.- That she has managed to conceal her pregnancy, and

III.- That it is the fruit of an illegitimate union.

If any of the mentioned circumstances is lacking, one to five years of imprisonment shall be applied.

###### Artículo 333.- Abortion caused only by imprudence of the pregnant woman, or when the pregnancy is

the result of rape, is not punishable.

###### Artículo 334.- No sanction shall be applied: when if the abortion is not induced, the pregnant woman is

in danger of death, in the judgment of the physician attending her, hearing the opinion of another physician, provided this is possible and the delay is not dangerous.

Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931

##### CAPITULO VII

Abandonment of persons

###### Artículo 335.

Whoever abandons a child incapable of caring for himself or a sick person, having an obligation to care for them, shall be sentenced to one month to four years of imprisonment, if no harm results, also depriving him of parental authority or guardianship, if the offender is an ascendant or guardian of the offended party.

###### Artículo 336.- Whoever without justified reason abandons his children or spouse, without resources to

attend to their subsistence needs, shall be sentenced to one month to five years of imprisonment, or 180 to 360 days fine; deprivation of family rights, and payment, as reparation of the damage, of the amounts not timely provided by the accused.

Article reformed DOF 26-12-1977, 30-12-1991

###### Artículo 336 Bis.- Whoever fraudulently places himself in a state of insolvency with the purpose of

evading compliance with the alimony obligations determined by law, shall be sentenced to imprisonment of six months to three years. The judge shall order the application of the product of work performed by the agent to the satisfaction of his alimony obligations.

Article added DOF 13-01-1984. Reformed DOF 30-12-1991, 10-01-1994

###### Artículo 337.

The crime of abandonment of spouse shall be prosecuted at the request of the aggrieved party. The crime of abandonment of children shall be prosecuted ex officio and, when appropriate, the Public Ministry shall promote the designation of a special guardian to represent the victims of the crime, before the Judge of the case, who shall have powers to designate him. In the case of the crime of abandonment of children, the criminal action shall be declared extinguished, the judicial authority having previously heard the representative of the minors, when the accused covers the overdue alimony, and provides sufficient guarantee in the Judge's judgment for the subsistence of the children.

Article reformed DOF 26-12-1977

###### Artículo 338.- For the pardon granted by the offended spouse to produce the freedom of the accused,

the latter must pay all amounts that he has failed to provide as alimony and post bond or other security that he will henceforth pay the amount corresponding to him. Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931

112 de 337

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios

###### Artículo 339.- If from the abandonment referred to in the previous articles any injury or death results,

these shall be presumed as premeditated for purposes of applying the sanctions corresponding to these crimes.

###### Artículo 340.- To anyone who finds abandoned in any place a minor incapable of caring for himself

or an injured, invalid person or threatened by any danger, ten to sixty days of work in favor of the community shall be imposed if they do not immediately notify the authority or fail to provide the necessary assistance when able to do so without personal risk.

Article amended DOF 10-01-1994

###### Artículo 341.- To anyone who, having run over a person, culpably or fortuitously, does not provide assistance

or does not request the assistance required, when able to do so, fifteen to sixty days of work in favor of the community shall be imposed, independently of the penalty that may proceed for the crime committed by the running over.

Erratum to article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 30-12-1991, 10-01-1994

###### Artículo 342.- To anyone who exposes in a foundling home a child under seven years of age that had been

entrusted to them, or delivers them to another charitable establishment or to any other person, without the consent of the person who entrusted them or of the authority in their absence, one to four months of imprisonment and a fine of five to twenty pesos shall be imposed.

Erratum to article DOF 31-08-1931

###### Artículo 343.- Ascendants or guardians who deliver to a foundling home a child who is

under their authority, shall lose by that sole fact the rights they have over the person and property of the foundling.

CAPÍTULO OCTAVO

Family violence

Chapter added DOF 30-12-1997

###### Artículo 343

Bis. Commits the crime of family violence whoever carries out acts or conduct of domination, control or physical, psychological, patrimonial or economic aggression, against any person with whom they are or have been united by matrimonial bond, kinship by consanguinity, affinity or civil, concubinage, or a relationship of a couple inside or outside the family home.

To whoever commits the crime of family violence, six months to four years of imprisonment shall be imposed and they will lose the right to alimony. Likewise, they shall be subject to specialized psychological treatment.

Article added DOF 30-12-1997. Amended DOF 14-06-2012

###### Artículo 343

Ter. It is equivalent to family violence and shall be punished with six months to four years of imprisonment to whoever carries out any of the acts indicated in the previous article against the person who is subject to the custody, guard, protection, education, instruction or care of said person. Article added DOF 30-12-1997. Amended DOF 14-06-2012

###### Artículo 343

quáter.- In all cases provided in the two preceding articles, the Public Prosecutor

shall exhort the probable responsible party to refrain from any conduct that could be offensive to the victim and shall agree on the necessary preventive measures to safeguard the physical or psychological integrity of the same. The administrative authority shall monitor compliance with these measures. In all cases the Public Prosecutor must request the precautionary measures they consider pertinent.

Article added DOF 30-12-1997

TITULO VIGESIMO

###### Artículo 364.- Six months to three years of imprisonment and twenty-five to one hundred days fine shall be imposed:

Paragraph amended DOF 29-07-1970, 13-05-1996

I.- To the private individual who deprives another of their liberty. If the deprivation of liberty exceeds twenty-four hours, the prison sentence shall be increased by one more month for each day.

Paragraph amended DOF 19-05-2006

The prison sentence shall be increased by up to one half, when the deprivation of liberty is carried out with violence, when the victim is under sixteen or over sixty years of age, or when due to any circumstance, the victim is in a situation of physical or mental inferiority with respect to the person who executes it.

###### Artículo 366

Bis. Repealed.

Article added DOF 13-05-1996. Amended DOF 12-06-2000. Repealed DOF 30-11-2010

###### Article 366 Ter.- Commits the crime of trafficking of minors, whoever transports a minor under sixteen years of age or delivers them to a third party, unlawfully, outside the national territory, with the purpose of obtaining an undue economic benefit from the transport or delivery of the minor.

Commit the crime referred to in the preceding paragraph:

- I. Those who exercise parental authority or custody over the minor, even if it has not been declared, when they materially carry out the transport or delivery or for having granted their consent thereto;
- II. Ascendants without limit of degree, collateral relatives and relatives by affinity up to the fourth degree, as well as any third party who does not have kinship with the minor.

It shall be understood that the persons referred to in the preceding paragraph act unlawfully when they have knowledge that:

- a) Those who exercise parental authority or custody of the minor have not granted their express consent for the transport or delivery, or
- b) Those who exercise parental authority or custody of the minor will obtain an undue economic benefit from the transport or delivery.
  - III. The person or persons who receive the minor.

To those who commit the crime referred to in this article, a penalty of three to ten years of imprisonment and four hundred to one thousand days' fine shall be imposed.

In addition to the sanctions indicated in the preceding paragraph, the rights of parental authority, guardianship or custody shall be deprived from those who, as the case may be, having the exercise thereof commit the crime referred to in this article.

Up to two-thirds of the penalties referred to in this article shall be applied when the transport or delivery of the minor takes place in national territory.

Article added DOF 13-01-1984. Moved (formerly article 366 bis) DOF 13-05-1996. Amended DOF 12-06-2000

###### Article 366 quáter.- The penalties referred to in the preceding article shall be reduced by one half when:

- I. The transport or delivery of the minor is carried out without the purpose of obtaining an undue economic benefit, or
- II. The person who receives the minor has the purpose of incorporating them into their family nucleus.

The penalties referred to in this article shall be imposed on the father or mother of a minor under sixteen years who unlawfully or without the consent of whoever exercises parental authority or custody of the minor, without the purpose of obtaining undue profit, transports them outside the national territory with the purpose of changing their habitual residence or preventing the mother or father, as the case may be, from living with the minor or visiting them.

TITLE TWENTY-SECOND

Crimes against persons in their patrimony

Title moved DOF 20-01-1967. Moved (formerly Title Twenty-Third) DOF 29-07-1970

##### CHAPTER I

Robbery

###### Article 367.- Commits the crime of robbery: whoever takes possession of another's movable property, without right and without consent of the person who may dispose of it according to law.

###### Article 368.- Are equated to robbery and shall be punished as such:

I.- The willful taking or destruction of one's own movable property, if it is in the possession of another person by any legitimate title and there is no consent; and Section amended DOF 10-01-1994. Errata DOF 01-02-1994

II.- The use or exploitation of electrical, magnetic, electromagnetic energy, of any fluid, or of any means of transmission, without right and without consent of the person who may legally dispose of them.

Section amended DOF 17-05-1999

III.- Repealed.

Section added DOF 13-05-1996. Repealed DOF 17-05-1999

###### Article 368 Bis.- Shall be punished with a penalty of three to ten years of imprisonment and up to one thousand days' fine, whoever after the execution of the robbery and without having participated in it, possesses, alienates or traffics in any manner, acquires or receives, the instruments, objects or products of the robbery, knowing of this circumstance and the intrinsic value thereof is greater than five hundred times the salary. Article added DOF 13-05-1996

###### Article 368 Ter.- Whoever habitually trades in stolen objects, knowing of this circumstance and the intrinsic value thereof is greater than five hundred times the salary, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment of six to thirteen years and one hundred to one thousand days' fine.

Article added DOF 13-05-1996

###### Article 368 Quáter. Repealed.

Article added DOF 17-05-1999. Amended DOF 24-10-2011. Repealed DOF 12-01-2016

###### Article 368 Quinquies.- Whoever commits the crime of robbery of radioactive material, nuclear material, nuclear fuel, radioactive mineral or radiation source, shall be imposed twelve to twenty years of imprisonment and twelve thousand to twenty thousand days' fine.

Article added DOF 14-03-2014

###### Article 369.- For the application of the sanction, the robbery shall be deemed consummated from the moment the thief has the stolen thing in their possession; even when they abandon it or are dispossessed of it. As for the determination of the value of what was stolen, as well as the fine imposed, the salary at the time of the execution of the crime shall be taken into consideration.

Article amended DOF 29-12-1981

117 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

###### Article 369 Bis.- To establish the amount corresponding to the crimes provided for in this Title, the general minimum salary in force at the time and place where the crime was committed shall be taken into consideration.

Article added DOF 29-12-1981. Amended DOF 14-01-1985

###### Article 370.- When the value of what was stolen does not exceed one hundred times the salary, up to two years of imprisonment and fine up to one hundred times the salary shall be imposed.

When it exceeds one hundred times the salary, but not five hundred, the sanction shall be two to four years of imprisonment and fine of one hundred to one hundred eighty times the salary.

Errata to the paragraph DOF 13-01-1982

When it exceeds five hundred times the salary, the sanction shall be four to ten years of imprisonment and fine of one hundred eighty to five hundred times the salary.

Errata to the paragraph DOF 13-01-1982

Article amended DOF 09-03-1946, 15-01-1951, 30-12-1975, 29-12-1981

###### Article 371.- To estimate the amount of the robbery, only the intrinsic value of the object of the taking shall be considered, but if for any circumstance it is not estimable in money or if by its nature it is not possible to fix its value, imprisonment of three days to five years shall be applied.

In cases of attempted robbery, when it is not possible to determine its amount, three days to two years of imprisonment shall be applied.

When the robbery is committed by two or more subjects, regardless of the amount of what was stolen, through violence, stalking or any other circumstance that diminishes the possibilities of defense of the victim or places them in conditions of disadvantage, the applicable penalty shall be five to fifteen years of imprisonment and up to one thousand days' fine. Also, the prohibition to go to a determined place or surveillance by the authority may be applied, up to a term equal to that of the custodial sanction imposed. Paragraph added DOF 13-05-1996

Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 15-01-1951


This excerpt sits under: FEDERAL PENAL CODE > Book First. > CRIMES AGAINST THE FREE DEVELOPMENT OF THE PERSONALITY. > SINGLE CHAPTER

###### Article 372.- If the robbery is executed with violence, to the penalty corresponding to simple robbery shall be

added from six months to five years of imprisonment. If the violence constitutes another crime, the rules of accumulation shall apply.

Artículo reformado DOF 03-01-1989

###### Article 373.- Violence against persons is distinguished as physical and moral.

Physical violence in robbery is understood as: the material force applied to a person to commit it.

There is moral violence: when the thief threatens or menaces a person with a serious, present or imminent harm, capable of intimidating them.

###### Article 374.- For the imposition of the sanction, robbery shall also be considered as committed with violence:

I.- When it is committed against a person other than the one robbed, who is in their company, and II.- When the thief exercises it after the robbery is consummated, to secure their escape or defend what was stolen.

118 de 337

CÓDIGO PENAL FEDERAL

CÁMARA DE DIPUTADOS DEL H. CONGRESO DE LA UNIÓN Última Reforma DOF 08-05-2023 Secretaría General

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios

###### Article 375.- When the value of what was stolen does not exceed ten times the salary, is spontaneously

returned by the offender and they pay all damages and losses, before the Authority becomes aware of the crime, no sanction shall be imposed, if the robbery has not been executed by means of violence.

Artículo reformado DOF 30-12-1975, 29-12-1981

###### Article 376.- In any case of robbery, if the judge deems it just, they may suspend the offender for one

month to six years, in the rights of parental authority, guardianship, curatorship, expert, depositary or judicial receiver, trustee or receiver in compositions or bankruptcies, advisor and representative of absent persons, and in the exercise of any profession that requires a degree.

###### Article 376

bis.- When the stolen object is a land motor vehicle that is subject to registration according to the law of the matter, with the exception of motorcycles, the penalty shall be seven to fifteen years of imprisonment and one thousand five hundred to two thousand days' fine.

The penalty provided in the preceding paragraph shall be increased by one half, when a public servant who has functions of prevention, prosecution or sanction of the crime or execution of sentences participates in the robbery and, in addition, dismissal and disqualification from holding any public employment, position or commission for a time equal to the prison sentence imposed shall be applied. Artículo adicionado DOF 17-05-1999

###### Article 376 Ter.- Whoever commits the crime of robbery against persons who provide or use, by themselves

or through a third party, federal freight, passenger, tourism or private transportation services, shall be imposed a penalty of 6 to 12 years of imprisonment, when the object of the robbery is the merchandise and 2 to 7 years of imprisonment, when it involves only luggage or valuables of tourists or passengers, at any place during the course of the trip, regardless of the value of what was stolen.

When the object of the robbery is the motor vehicle, the provisions of articles 376 Bis and 377 of this Code shall apply, without prejudice to the accumulation of penalties corresponding to other unlawful conduct that concur in the commission of the crime, including those provided in the first paragraph of this article.

When the conduct referred to in this article is committed by a criminal association, gang or band, it shall be sanctioned in accordance with articles 164 or 164 Bis, as applicable.

Artículo adicionado DOF 21-02-2018

###### Article 376 Quáter.- In addition to the penalty corresponding to them under the first paragraph of the

preceding article, those provided in this article shall apply in the following cases:

I.- The prison sentence shall be increased by one third when there is seizure of the trailer or semi-trailer and it is used to commit another unlawful act, and

II.- The prison sentence shall be increased by one half when, knowingly of their functions, the public servant commits or participates in the robbery and they have or perform functions of prevention, investigation, prosecution of the crime or execution of sentences, regardless of the criminal sanction, they shall be disqualified from holding any public employment, position or commission for a time equal to the prison sentence imposed.

Artículo adicionado DOF 21-02-2018

###### Article 377.- A penalty of five to fifteen years of imprisonment and up to one thousand days' fine shall be

imposed on whoever knowingly and regardless of the penalties corresponding to them for the commission of other crimes:

I. Dismantles any stolen vehicle or vehicles or commercializes jointly or separately their parts;

119 de 337

CÓDIGO PENAL FEDERAL

CÁMARA DE DIPUTADOS DEL H. CONGRESO DE LA UNIÓN Última Reforma DOF 08-05-2023

Secretaría General

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios

II. Alienates or traffics in any manner with stolen vehicle or vehicles;

III. Holds, possesses, keeps, alters or modifies in any way the documentation that proves ownership or identification of a stolen vehicle;

IV. Transfers the stolen vehicle or vehicles to another federative entity or abroad, and

V. Uses the stolen vehicle or vehicles in the commission of another or other crimes.

Whoever provides economic or any other resources for the execution of the activities described in the preceding sections shall be considered an accomplice in the terms of article 13 of this Code.

If any public servant who has functions of prevention, prosecution or sanction of the crime or of execution of sentences participates in the mentioned acts, in addition to the sanctions referred to in this article, the prison sentence shall be increased by up to one half more and they shall be disqualified from holding any public employment, position or commission for a period equal to the prison sentence imposed.

Fe de erratas al artículo DOF 31-08-1931. Derogado DOF 13-01-1984. Adicionado DOF 13-05-1996

###### Article 378.- Whoever manufactures or alters without permission from the competent authority a license

plate, the sticker, the circulation card or other official documents issued to identify motor vehicles or trailers shall be imposed four to eight years of imprisonment and three hundred to one thousand days' fine.

The same penalties shall be imposed on whoever possesses, uses, acquires or alienates any of the objects referred to in the preceding paragraph, knowing that they are stolen, falsified or were obtained improperly.

The same penalties shall likewise be imposed on whoever, knowingly, uses for a stolen vehicle or one that is illegally in the country, the license plates, the sticker or other official documents issued to identify another vehicle.

Artículo derogado DOF 13-01-1984. Adicionado DOF 17-05-1999

###### Article 379.- Whoever, without using deceit or violent means, seizes once the objects strictly indispensable

to satisfy their personal or family needs of the moment shall not be punished.

###### Article 380.- Whoever is charged with having taken someone else's property without the consent of the

owner or lawful possessor and proves having taken it temporarily and not to appropriate it or sell it, shall be imposed one to six months of imprisonment or 30 to 90 days' fine, provided they prove not having refused to return it, if required to do so. In addition, they shall pay the offended party, as reparation for the damage, double the rent, lease or interest of the used thing.

Artículo reformado DOF 30-12-1991

###### Article 381.- In addition to the penalty corresponding according to articles 370, 371 and the first

paragraph of article 376 Ter, the penalties provided in this article shall be applied to the offender, in the following cases:

Paragraph reformed DOF 13-01-1984, 03-01-1989, 17-05-1999, 21-02-2018

I.- When the crime is committed in an enclosed place.

III.- When committed by a guest or diner or a member of their family or of the servants accompanying them, in the house where they receive hospitality, gift or entertainment;

IV.- When committed by the owner or a member of their family in the former's house, against their employees or domestic servants or against any other person;

VI.- When committed by workers, artisans, apprentices or pupils, in the house, workshop or school where they habitually work or learn or in the room, office, warehouse or other place to which they have free entry by virtue of the indicated character.

###### Article 381 Bis.- Without prejudice to the sanctions that must be imposed in accordance with articles 370, 371 and 372

, three days to ten years of imprisonment shall be applied to anyone who commits robbery in buildings, dwellings, rooms or chambers that are inhabited or intended for habitation, this denomination including not only those fixed to the ground, but also movable ones, whatever the material they are constructed of, as well as in those places or establishments intended for commercial activities.

Article added DOF 05-01-1955. Reformed DOF 20-01-1967, 13-01-1984, 17-05-1999, 23-06-2017

###### Article 381 Ter.- Commits the crime of cattle rustling, whoever by themselves or through an intermediary person takes possession of

one or more head of livestock, without the consent of whoever may legally dispose of them. For the purposes of this crime, livestock shall be considered the species: bovine, equine, asinine, mule, ovine, caprine, porcine or one or more colonies of bees in an apiary; as well as domesticated, wild, hoofed, large livestock or small livestock, regardless of the typical activity of the animal. For such crime, two to ten years of imprisonment shall be imposed.

The slaughter of livestock without the consent of whoever may legally grant it shall be equated to the crime of cattle rustling and shall be sanctioned with the same penalty.

Article added DOF 23-06-2017

###### Article 381 Quáter.- The crime of cattle rustling is considered aggravated and the penalty shall be increased up to

one half, when committed by someone who has an employment relationship, or relationship by consanguinity or affinity up to the fourth degree with the owner of the livestock.

Similarly, the penalty established in the preceding paragraph shall be imposed, when the crime is executed through physical or moral violence, or when committed by a public servant.

When the conduct referred to in this article is committed by a criminal association, gang or band, it shall be sanctioned pursuant to articles 164 or 164 Bis, as applicable. Article added DOF 23-06-2017

##### CHAPTER II

Breach of trust

###### Article 382.

Whoever, to the detriment of someone, disposes for themselves or for another, of any movable property of another, of which possession but not ownership has been transferred to them, shall be sanctioned with imprisonment up to 122 de 337

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services of 1 year and a fine up to 100 times the salary, when the amount of the breach does not exceed 200 times the salary.

If it exceeds this amount, but not 2000, the imprisonment shall be from 1 to 6 years and the fine from 100 up to 180 times the salary.

Erratum to the paragraph DOF 13-01-1982

If the amount is greater than 2,000 times the salary, the imprisonment shall be from 6 to 12 years and the fine 120 times the salary.

Erratum to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Reformed DOF 09-03-1946, 15-01-1951, 30-12-1975, 29-12-1981

###### Artículo 383.- Se considera como abuso de confianza para los efectos de la pena:

I.- El hecho de disponer o sustraer una cosa, su dueño, si le ha sido embargada y la tiene en su poder con el carácter de depositario judicial, o bien si la hubiere dado en prenda y la conserva en su poder como depositario a virtud de un contrato celebrado con alguna Institución de Crédito, en perjuicio de ésta.

Fracción reformada DOF 03-01-1980

II.- El hecho de disponer de la cosa depositada, o sustraerla el depositario judicial o el designado por o ante las autoridades, administrativas o del trabajo.

Fracción reformada DOF 24-03-1944

III.- El hecho de que una persona haga aparecer como suyo un depósito que garantice la libertad caucional de un procesado y del cual no le corresponda la propiedad.

###### Artículo 384.- Se reputa como abuso de confianza la ilegítima posesión de la cosa retenida si el

tenedor o poseedor de ella no la devuelve a pesar de ser requerido formalmente por quien tenga derecho, o no la entrega a la autoridad, para que ésta disponga de la misma conforme a la ley. Artículo reformado DOF 09-03-1946

###### Artículo 385.- Se considera como abuso de confianza y se sancionará con seis meses a seis años de

prisión y multa hasta de cien veces el salario a quien disponga indebidamente o se niegue sin justificación a entregar un vehículo recibido en depósito de autoridad competente, relacionado con delitos por tránsito de vehículos, habiendo sido requerido por la autoridad que conozca o siga conociendo del caso. Fe de erratas al artículo DOF 31-08-1931. Derogado DOF 13-01-1984. Adicionado DOF 19-11-1986

##### CAPITULO III

Fraude

###### Artículo 386.- Comete el delito de fraude el que engañando a uno o aprovechándose del error en que

éste se halla se hace ilícitamente de alguna cosa o alcanza un lucro indebido.

El delito de fraude se castigará con las penas siguientes:

I.- Con prisión de 3 días a 6 meses o de 30 a 180 días multa, cuando el valor de lo defraudado no exceda de diez veces el salario;

Fracción reformada DOF 30-12-1991

II.- Con Prisión de 6 meses a 3 años y multa de 10 a 100 veces el salario, cuando el valor de lo defraudado excediera de 10, pero no de 500 veces el salario;

Fe de erratas a la fracción DOF 13-01-1982, 15-01-1982

Secretaría General

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios

III.- Con prisión de tres a doce años y multa hasta de ciento veinte veces el salario, si el valor de lo defraudado fuere mayor de quinientas veces el salario.

Fe de erratas al artículo DOF 31-08-1931. Reformado DOF 09-03-1946. Fe de erratas DOF 16-07-1946. Reformado DOF 30-12-1975,

###### Artículo 387.- The same penalties indicated in the previous article shall be imposed:

I.- On whoever obtains money, securities or any other thing by offering to take charge of the defense of an accused person or a convict, or of the direction or sponsorship in a civil or administrative matter, if they do not carry out the former or do not perform the latter, whether because they do not legally take charge of it, or because they resign or abandon the business or cause without justified reason;

II.- On whoever for valuable consideration alienates something knowing that they have no right to dispose of it, or rents it, mortgages it, pledges it or encumbers it in any other way, if they have received the price, the rent, the amount for which they encumbered it, part of them or an equivalent profit;

III.- On whoever obtains from another a sum of money or any other profit, by granting or endorsing to them in their own name or another's, a nominative document, to order or to bearer against a supposed person or whom the grantor knows will not pay them;

IV.- On whoever has something served or accepts a service in any commercial establishment and does not pay the amount;

V.- On whoever purchases a movable thing offering to pay its price in cash and refuses after receiving it, to make the payment or return the thing, if the seller demands the former within fifteen days of having received the thing from the buyer;

VI.- On whoever has sold a movable thing and received its price, if they do not deliver it within fifteen days of the agreed term or do not return its amount in the same term, in the event that the latter is demanded of them.

VII.- On whoever sells to two persons the same thing, whether movable or real property and receives the price of the first or second alienation, of both or part of it, or any other profit with prejudice to the first or second buyer.

VIII.- On whoever taking advantage of the ignorance or poor economic conditions of a person, obtains usurious advantages from them by means of contracts or agreements in which interest or profits higher than those usual in the market are stipulated.

IX.- On whoever in order to obtain an undue profit, puts into circulation tokens, cards, small plates or other objects of any material as conventional signs in substitution of legal currency;

X.- On whoever simulates a contract, an act or judicial writing, with prejudice to another or to obtain any undue benefit.

Reform DOF 10-01-1994: Repealed from the section the then second paragraph

XI.- On whoever through drawings, raffles, lotteries, promises of sale or by any other means, keeps in whole or in part the amounts received, without delivering the merchandise or object offered.

XII.- On the manufacturer, entrepreneur, contractor, or builder of any work, who uses in its construction, materials in quantity or quality inferior to that agreed upon or labor inferior to that stipulated, provided they have received the price or part of it;

XIII.- On the seller of construction materials or any kind, who having received the price thereof, does not deliver them in their agreed totality or quality;

XIV.- On whoever sells or transfers a business without authorization of its creditors, or without the new acquirer committing to be responsible for the credits, provided that the latter remain unpaid. When the alienation is made by a legal person, those who authorize it and the directors, administrators or agents who carry it out shall be criminally liable;

Section reformed DOF 05-01-1955

XV.- On whoever exploits the worries, superstition or ignorance of the people, by means of supposed evocation of spirits, divinations or healings.

XVI.- (Repealed).

XVII.- On whoever taking advantage of the ignorance or poor economic conditions of a worker in their service, pays them amounts less than those legally due to them for the work they perform or makes them grant receipts or proof of payment of any kind that cover sums of money higher than those actually delivered.

Section added DOF 05-01-1955

XVIII.- On whoever having received merchandise with subsidy or franchise to give them a determined destination, diverts them from this destination or in any way distorts the purposes pursued with the subsidy or franchise.

Section added DOF 05-01-1955

XIX.- On intermediaries in operations of transfer of ownership of real property or of real encumbrances on them, who obtain money, titles or securities for the amount of their price, on account of it or to constitute that encumbrance, if they do not destine them, in whole or in part, to the object of the agreed operation, by their disposition for their own benefit or another's.

For the purposes of this crime it shall be understood that an intermediary has not given its destination, or has disposed, in whole or in part, of the money, titles or securities obtained for the amount of the price or on account of the real property object of the transfer of ownership or of the real encumbrance, if they do not make a deposit in Nacional Financiera, S. A. or in any Deposit Institution, within 30 days following its reception in favor of its owner or possessor, unless they had delivered it, within that term, to the seller or to the debtor of the real encumbrance, or returned it to the buyer or to the creditor of the same encumbrance.

The same sanctions shall be imposed on managers, directors, agents with powers of ownership or administration, administrators of legal persons who do not comply with or enforce the obligation referred to in the preceding paragraph.

The deposit shall be delivered by Nacional Financiera, S. A. or the Deposit Institution in question, to its owner or to the buyer.

When the active subject of the crime returns to the interested parties the amounts of money obtained with their action, before conclusions are formulated in the respective process, the penalty that shall be applied to them shall be three days to six months of imprisonment.

Section added DOF 08-03-1968

###### Article 388.- Anyone who, for any reason, being in charge of the administration or care of

property belonging to others, with intent to profit, harms the owner thereof by altering accounts or contract terms, making non-existent or exaggerated operations or expenses appear, concealing or retaining assets or using them improperly, or knowingly performs operations detrimental to the owner's patrimony for their own benefit or that of a third party, shall be subject to the penalties provided for the crime of fraud.

Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Repealed DOF 30-12-1975. Added DOF 13-01-1984. Amended DOF 10-01-1994

###### Article 388 Bis.- Anyone who places themselves in a state of insolvency, with the purpose of evading their obligations

with respect to their creditors, shall be subject to a penalty of six months to four years imprisonment and fifty to three hundred days' fine.

In case of bankruptcy, the provisions of the special law shall apply.

Article added DOF 10-01-1994

###### Article 389.- It is equivalent to the crime of fraud and shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to ten years and

a fine of four hundred to four thousand pesos, to use the position one holds in the government, in a decentralized or state-owned enterprise, or in any union organization, or one's relations with officials or leaders of said organizations, to obtain money, securities, gifts, presents or any other benefit, in exchange for promising or providing a job, a promotion or salary increase in such organizations.

Article repealed DOF 09-03-1946. Added DOF 05-01-1955. Amended DOF 30-12-1975, 07-01-1980

###### Article 389 Bis.- Anyone who, by themselves or through an intermediary, causes public or private harm

by subdividing and transferring or promising to transfer ownership, possession or any other right over urban or rural land, their own or belonging to others, with or without constructions, without prior permission 126 of 337

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services from competent administrative authorities, or when such permission exists but the requirements specified therein have not been met, commits the crime of fraud. This crime shall be punished even in the case of total or partial lack of payment. For criminal purposes, subdividing is understood as the division of land into lots.

##### CHAPTER III BIS

Extortion

Chapter added DOF 13-01-1984

###### Article 390.

Anyone who, without right, compels another to give, do, refrain from doing or tolerate something, obtaining a profit for themselves or another or causing someone patrimonial harm, shall be subject to two to eight years imprisonment and forty to one hundred sixty days' fine.

The penalties shall be increased by up to an additional half if the coercion is carried out by a criminal association, or by a public servant or former public servant, or by a member or former member of any police force or the Mexican Armed Forces. In this case, the public servant or former public servant and the member or former member of any police force shall also be subject to removal from employment, position or commission and disqualification for one to five years from holding public office or commission, and if it concerns a member of the Mexican Armed Forces in retired, reserve or active status, definitive discharge from the Armed Force to which they belong and disqualification for one to five years from holding public office or commission.

Article amended DOF 13-01-1984, 10-01-1994

##### CHAPTER V

Dispossession of immovable property or water

127 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Amendment DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

###### Article 395.- A penalty of three months to five years imprisonment and a fine of fifty to

five hundred pesos shall be imposed:

I.- On anyone who, on their own authority and using violence or stealth, or employing threat or deceit, occupies another's immovable property or makes use of it, or of a real right that does not belong to them; II.- On anyone who, on their own authority and using the means indicated in the preceding fraction, occupies immovable property of their own ownership, in cases where the law does not permit it because it is in the possession of another person, or exercises acts of ownership that harm the legitimate rights of the occupant, and

III.- On anyone who, under the terms of the preceding fractions, commits dispossession of water.

The penalty shall be applicable even when the right to possession of the usurped thing is doubtful or in dispute. When the dispossession is carried out by a group or groups that together are more than five persons, in addition to the penalty specified in this article, a penalty of one to six years imprisonment shall be imposed on the intellectual authors and those who direct the invasion.

On those who repeatedly engage in promoting the dispossession of urban immovable property in the Federal District, a penalty of two to nine years imprisonment shall be imposed. Those are considered to repeatedly engage in promoting the dispossession of urban immovable property who have been previously convicted for this form of participation in dispossession, or have had an order of formal imprisonment issued against them on more than two occasions for this same crime, except when in the corresponding proceeding the clearing of evidence, dismissal or acquittal of the accused has been resolved. Paragraph added DOF 14-01-1985

Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931. Amended DOF 09-03-1946

###### Article 396.- To the penalties specified in the preceding article shall be added those corresponding to

the violence or threat.

##### CHAPTER VI

Damage to property belonging to others

###### Article 397.- Five to ten years imprisonment and a fine of one hundred to five thousand pesos shall be imposed on

those who cause fire, flood or explosion with damage or danger to:

I.- A building, dwelling or room where any person is present;

II.- Clothing, furniture or objects in such manner that they may cause serious personal harm; III.- Public or notarial archives;

IV.- Libraries, museums, temples, schools or public buildings and monuments, and V.- Mountains, forests, jungles, pastures, crops or cultivations of any kind.

###### Article 398.- If in addition to direct damages any other crime is consummated, the rules of

accumulation shall apply.

###### Article 399.- When damage, destruction or deterioration of property belonging to others, or of one's own property to

the detriment of a third party, is caused by any means, the penalties for simple theft shall apply. 128 of 337

###### Article 399 Bis.- The crimes provided for in this title shall be prosecuted upon complaint by the offended party

when committed by an ascendant, descendant, spouse, relatives by consanguinity up to the second degree, concubine or cohabitant, adopter or adoptee, and relatives by affinity likewise up to the second degree. A complaint shall also be required for the prosecution of third parties who have participated in the execution of the crime with the persons referred to in the preceding paragraph. If any other act that by itself constitutes a crime is committed, the sanction that the law establishes for it shall be applied.

The crimes provided for in articles 380 and 382 to 399 shall be prosecuted upon complaint, except for article 390 and the cases referred to in the last two paragraphs of article 395.

Paragraph reformed DOF 30-12-1991

TITLE TWENTY-THIRD

Concealment and Operations with Resources of Illicit Origin

Title renumbered DOF 20-01-1967. Renumbered (formerly Title Twenty-Fourth) DOF 29-07-1970. Denomination reformed DOF 13-05-1996

##### CHAPTER I

Concealment

Chapter added DOF 13-05-1996

###### Article 400.- Imprisonment of three months to three years and from fifteen to sixty days' fine shall be imposed on whoever:

I.- With intent to profit, after the execution of the crime and without having participated in it, acquires, receives or conceals the product thereof with knowledge of this circumstance.

If the person who received the thing in sale, pledge or under any other concept, had no knowledge of the illicit origin thereof, for not having taken the indispensable precautions to ensure that the person from whom he received it had the right to dispose of it, the penalty shall be reduced by up to one half;

Reform DOF 30-12-1991: Repealed from the section the then third paragraph (previously added DOF 23-12-1985) II.- Provides assistance or cooperation of any kind to the author of a crime, with knowledge of this circumstance, by agreement subsequent to the execution of said crime;

III.- Conceals or favors the concealment of the person responsible for a crime, the effects, objects or instruments thereof or prevents investigation;

Section reformed DOF 23-12-1985

IV. When required by the authorities, does not provide assistance for the investigation of crimes or for the prosecution of criminals;

Section reformed DOF 23-01-2009

V. Does not seek, by the lawful means at his disposal and without risk to his person, to prevent the consummation of crimes that he knows are going to be committed or are being committed, unless he has an obligation to face the risk, in which case the provisions of this article or other applicable regulations shall apply;

Section reformed DOF 23-01-2009

VI. Unlawfully alters, modifies or disturbs the place, traces or vestiges of the criminal act, and Section added DOF 23-01-2009

VII. Diverts or obstructs the investigation of the criminal act in question or favors the accused in evading the action of justice.
