Section added DOF 23-01-2009

The penalty provided for in this article shall not be applied in the cases of sections III, regarding the concealment of the offender, and IV, when it concerns:

- a) Ascendants and descendants by consanguinity or affinity;
- b) The spouse, concubine, cohabitant and collateral relatives by consanguinity up to the fourth degree, and by affinity up to the second; and
- c) Those who are bound to the criminal by love, respect, gratitude or close friendship derived from noble motives.

Paragraph with subsections added DOF 23-12-1985

The provisions contained in the preceding paragraph and the absolute excuses provided for in subsections

a) to c) shall not be applicable when the offender who is concealed is responsible for the crime of femicide or homicide.

Paragraph added DOF 25-04-2023

The judge, taking into account the nature of the action, the personal circumstances of the accused and the others indicated in article 52, may impose in cases of concealment referred to in sections I, first paragraph and II to IV of this article, instead of the sanctions indicated, up to two-thirds of those that would correspond to the author of the crime; and must state in the sentence the reasons on which he bases the application of the sanction authorized by this paragraph.

Paragraph added DOF 13-05-1996

Article reformed DOF 09-03-1946, 20-01-1967, 14-01-1985

##### CHAPTER II

Operations with Resources of Illicit Origin

Chapter added DOF 13-05-1996

###### Article 400

Bis. Five to fifteen years of imprisonment and from one thousand to five thousand days' fine shall be imposed on whoever, by himself or through an intermediary, carries out any of the following conducts:

- I. Acquires, alienates, administers, holds in custody, possesses, exchanges, converts, deposits, withdraws, gives or receives for any reason, invests, transfers, transports or conveys, within the national territory, from it to abroad or vice versa, resources, rights or property of any nature, when he has knowledge that they proceed from or represent the product of an illicit activity, or
- II. Conceals, covers up or attempts to conceal or cover up the nature, origin, location, destination, movement, ownership or title of resources, rights or property, when he has knowledge that they proceed from or represent the product of an illicit activity.

For purposes of this Chapter, resources, rights or property of any nature shall be understood to be the product of an illicit activity when there are founded indications or certainty that they come directly or indirectly from, or represent the profits derived from the commission of some crime and their legitimate origin cannot be proven.

In the case of conducts provided for in this Chapter, in which services of institutions that make up the financial system are used, to proceed criminally the prior complaint of the Ministry of Finance and Public Credit shall be required.

130 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

When the Ministry of Finance and Public Credit, in the exercise of its oversight powers, finds elements that allow presuming the commission of any of the crimes referred to in this Chapter, it must exercise with respect to them the verification powers conferred by law and report the facts that may probably constitute said illicit acts.

Article added DOF 09-03-1946. Reformed DOF 20-01-1967, 14-01-1985, 13-05-1996, 14-03-2014

###### Artículo 400

Bis 1. The penalties provided in this Chapter shall be increased from one third up to one half, when the person who carries out any of the conducts provided in article 400 Bis of this Code has the character of advisor, administrator, official, employee, attorney-in-fact or service provider of any person subject to the regime for prevention of operations with resources of illicit origin, or carries them out within two years following separation from any of said positions. Furthermore, they shall be subject to disqualification from holding employment, position or commission in legal entities subject to the prevention regime for a period equal to that of the prison sentence imposed. The disqualification shall begin to run from the time the prison sentence has been served.

The penalties provided in this Chapter shall be doubled, if the conduct is committed by public servants in charge of preventing, detecting, reporting, investigating or judging the commission of crimes or executing criminal sanctions, as well as to former public servants in charge of such functions who commit said conduct within two years after their termination. Furthermore, they shall be subject to disqualification from holding employment, position or commission for a period equal to that of the prison sentence imposed. The disqualification shall begin to run from the time the prison sentence has been served.

Likewise, the penalties provided in this Chapter shall be increased up to one half if the person who carries out any of the conducts provided in article 400 Bis, sections I and II, uses persons under eighteen years of age or persons who lack the capacity to understand the meaning of the act or who lack the capacity to resist it.

Article added DOF 14-03-2014

TITLE TWENTY-FOURTH

Electoral Crimes and regarding the National Registry of Citizens

Title added DOF 15-08-1990

##### SOLE CHAPTER

Chapter added DOF 15-08-1990

###### Article 401.- For the purposes of this Chapter, the following is understood as:

I. Public Servants, the persons who are within the assumptions established by article 212 of this Code.

State and Municipal Public Administration officials and employees shall also be understood as Public Servants;

II. Electoral officials, those who in the terms of federal electoral legislation comprise the bodies that fulfill electoral functions;

III. Party officials, the leaders of national political parties and political groups, and their representatives before electoral bodies, in the terms of federal electoral legislation;

IV. Candidates, citizens formally registered as such by the competent authority; 131 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

V. Electoral public documents, the records of the electoral day, those relating to the scrutiny and count of each of the elections, electoral packages and polling station files, the detailed records of the counting sessions of the local and district councils, and those of the counts of the plurinominal constituency and, in general all documents and records issued in the exercise of their functions by the bodies of the Federal Electoral Institute; and

VI. Electoral materials, the physical elements, such as ballot boxes, booths or modular elements for casting votes, credential markers, indelible liquid, office supplies and other equipment authorized for use in polling stations during the electoral day.

Article added DOF 15-08-1990. Reformed DOF 22-11-1996

###### Article 402.- For the commission of any of the crimes included in this Chapter

the disqualification from one to five years may be imposed in addition to the penalty indicated, and where appropriate, removal from office.

Article added DOF 15-08-1990. Reformed DOF 25-03-1994

###### Article 403.- From ten to one hundred days' fine and imprisonment from six months to three years shall be imposed on whoever:

I. Votes knowing that they do not meet the requirements of the law;

II. Votes more than once in the same election;

III. Engages in proselytism or objectively pressures voters on the day of the electoral day inside the polling stations or in the place where voters are lined up, in order to direct the meaning of their vote;

IV. Willfully obstructs or interferes with the normal development of voting, scrutiny and counting, the transfer and delivery of electoral packages and documentation, or the adequate exercise of the tasks of electoral officials;

V. Collects at any time, without cause provided by law, voting credentials from citizens;

VI. Solicits votes for pay, gift, promise of money or other reward during electoral campaigns or the electoral day;

VII. On the day of the electoral day violates, in any manner, the right of the citizen to cast their vote in secret;

VIII. Votes or attempts to vote with a voting credential of which they are not the holder;

IX. On the day of the electoral day carries out the transportation of voters, restricting or attempting to restrict their freedom to cast their vote;

X. Illicitly introduces into or removes from the ballot boxes one or more electoral ballots, or seizes, destroys or alters ballots, documents or electoral materials, or prevents in any way their transfer or delivery to the competent bodies;

XI. Obtains or requests a signed declaration from the voter regarding their intention or the meaning of their vote, or that, through threat or promise of pay or gift, commits their vote in favor of a particular political party or candidate;

132 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

XII. Violently prevents the installation of a polling station, or willfully assumes any conduct that has the purpose of preventing the normal installation of the polling station; or

XIII. During the eight days prior to the election and until the official closing time of the polling stations that are in the most western time zone areas of the national territory, publishes or disseminates by any means the results of surveys or opinion polls that reveal the preferences of citizens.

Article added DOF 15-08-1990. Reformed DOF 25-03-1994, 22-11-1996

###### Article 404.- Up to 500 days' fine shall be imposed on ministers of religious worship who, in the

performance of public acts proper to their ministry, expressly induce the electorate to vote in favor of or against a candidate or political party, or to abstain from exercising the right to vote. Article added DOF 15-08-1990. Reformed DOF 25-03-1994, 22-11-1996

###### Artículo 405.- A penalty of fifty to two hundred days' fine and imprisonment of two to six years shall be imposed on the

electoral official who:

Paragraph amended DOF 25-03-1994

I.- Alters in any way, substitutes, destroys or makes improper use of documents related to the Federal Register of Voters;

II. Fails to comply, without justified cause, with the obligations of their position, to the detriment of the electoral process;

Subsection amended DOF 22-11-1996

III.- Obstructs the normal conduct of voting without justified cause;

IV. Alters electoral results, removes or destroys ballots, documents or electoral materials;

Subsection amended DOF 25-03-1994, 22-11-1996

V. Fails to deliver or prevents the timely delivery of documents or electoral materials, without justified cause;

Subsection amended DOF 22-11-1996

VI. In the exercise of their duties exerts pressure on voters and objectively induces them to vote for a particular candidate or party, inside the polling station or in the place where the voters themselves are lined up;

Subsection amended DOF 22-11-1996

VII.- One who fraudulently installs, opens or closes a polling station outside the times and forms provided by the law on the matter, installs it in a place other than that legally designated, or prevents its installation; Subsection amended DOF 25-03-1994

VIII. Without cause provided by law expels or orders the removal from the polling station of representatives of a political party or restricts the rights granted to them by law;

Subsection amended DOF 25-03-1994, 22-11-1996

IX.- Repealed.

Subsection added DOF 25-03-1994. Repealed DOF 22-11-1996

X. Permits or tolerates a citizen casting their vote knowing that they do not meet the legal requirements or that one or more ballots are illicitly introduced into the ballot boxes; or Subsection added DOF 25-03-1994. Amended DOF 22-11-1996

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

XI. Disseminates, in a public and fraudulent manner, false news regarding the conduct of election day or regarding its results.

Subsection added DOF 25-03-1994. Amended DOF 22-11-1996

Article added DOF 15-08-1990

###### Artículo 406.- A penalty of one hundred to two hundred days' fine and imprisonment of one to six years shall be imposed on the

party official or candidate who:

Paragraph amended DOF 25-03-1994, 22-11-1996

I. Exerts pressure on voters and induces them to abstain or to vote for a particular candidate or party inside the polling station or in the place where the voters themselves are lined up;

Subsection amended DOF 22-11-1996

II.- Conducts electoral propaganda while performing their duties during election day;

III. Removes, destroys, alters or makes improper use of documents or electoral materials; Subsection amended DOF 22-11-1996

IV. Obstructs the normal conduct of voting or of the acts subsequent to it without justified cause, or for that purpose threatens or exercises physical violence against electoral officials;

Subsection amended DOF 22-11-1996

V. Disseminates, in a public and fraudulent manner, false news regarding the conduct of election day or regarding its results;

Subsection amended DOF 25-03-1994, 22-11-1996

VI. Prevents by violence the installation, opening or closing of a polling station; or

Subsection amended DOF 22-11-1996

VII. Obtains and knowingly uses in their capacity as candidate, funds from illicit activities for their electoral campaign.

Subsection added DOF 22-11-1996

Article added DOF 15-08-1990

###### Artículo 407.

A penalty of two hundred to four hundred days' fine and imprisonment of one to nine years shall be imposed on the public servant who:

- I. Compels their subordinates, expressly and making use of their authority or hierarchy, to cast their votes in favor of a political party or candidate;

Subsection amended DOF 22-11-1996

II. Conditions the provision of a public service, the fulfillment of programs or the execution of public works, within the scope of their competence, on the casting of votes in favor of a political party or candidate;

Subsection amended DOF 22-11-1996

III. Allocates, illegally, funds, goods or services at their disposal by virtue of their position such as vehicles, real estate and equipment, to the support of a political party or a candidate, without prejudice to the penalties that may apply for the crime of embezzlement; or Subsection amended DOF 22-11-1996

IV. Provides support or renders any service to political parties or their candidates, through their subordinates, using the time corresponding to their duties, illegally. Subsection added DOF 22-11-1996

###### Artículo 408.- A penalty of suspension of political rights for up to six years shall be imposed on

those who, having been elected deputies or senators, do not appear, without justified cause in the judgment of the respective Chamber, to perform the position within the period indicated in the first paragraph of article 63 of the Constitution.

Article added DOF 15-08-1990

###### Artículo 409.- A penalty of twenty to one hundred days' fine and imprisonment of three months to five years shall be imposed on whoever:

Paragraph amended DOF 25-03-1994

I.- Provides false documents or information to the National Register of Citizens to obtain the document that accredits citizenship; and

II.- Alters in any way, substitutes, destroys or makes improper use of the document that accredits citizenship, which under the terms of the law on the matter, is issued by the National Register of Citizens.

###### Artículo 410.- The penalty referred to in the preceding article may be increased by one quarter if

the conducts are committed by personnel of the body in charge of the service of the National Register of Citizens in accordance with the law on the matter, or if the person is of foreign nationality. Article added DOF 15-08-1990

###### Artículo 411.- A penalty of seventy to two hundred days' fine and imprisonment of three to seven years shall be imposed on whoever

by any means alters or participates in the alteration of the Federal Register of Voters, of the nominal lists or in the illicit issuance of credentials to Vote.

Article added DOF 25-03-1994. Amended DOF 22-11-1996

###### Artículo 412.- Imprisonment of two to nine years shall be imposed on the party official or the

organizers of campaign events who, knowingly, illicitly takes advantage of funds, goods or services under the terms of subsection III of article 407 of this Code. In the commission of this crime there shall be no benefit of provisional release.

TITLE TWENTY-FIVE

Crimes Against the Environment and Environmental Management

Title added DOF 13-12-1996. Denomination added DOF 06-02-2002

##### CHAPTER ONE

On technological and hazardous activities

Chapter added DOF 13-12-1996. Chapter renumbered and denomination amended DOF 06-02-2002 (formerly Single Chapter)

###### Article 414.- A penalty of one to nine years in prison and three hundred to three thousand days' fine shall be imposed

on whoever unlawfully, or without applying prevention or security measures, carries out activities of production, storage, traffic, importation or exportation, transportation, abandonment, disposal, discharge, or carries out any other activity with substances considered hazardous due to their corrosive, reactive, explosive, toxic, flammable, radioactive or other analogous characteristics, or 135 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform Official Gazette 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services orders or authorizes it, that causes damage to natural resources, to flora, to fauna, to ecosystems, to water quality, to soil, to subsoil or to the environment.

The same penalty shall apply to whoever unlawfully carries out the conducts with the substances set forth in the preceding paragraph, or with ozone-depleting substances and causes a risk of damage to natural resources, to flora, to fauna, to ecosystems, to water quality or to the environment. In the event that the activities referred to in the preceding paragraphs are carried out in a protected natural area, the prison sentence shall be increased by up to three years and the economic penalty by up to one thousand days' fine, with the exception of activities carried out with ozone-depleting substances.

When the conducts referred to in the first and second paragraphs of this article are carried out in urban areas with spent oils or ozone-depleting substances in quantities not exceeding 200 liters, or with waste considered hazardous due to its biological-infectious characteristics, up to half of the penalty provided for in this article shall be applied, unless they are repeated conducts with amounts less than those indicated when they exceed such amount.

Article added Official Gazette 13-12-1996. Amended Official Gazette 06-02-2002

###### Article 415.- A penalty of one to nine years in prison and three hundred to three thousand days'

fine shall be imposed on whoever without applying prevention or security measures:

- I. Emits, releases, discharges into the atmosphere, authorizes or orders it, gases, smoke, dust or pollutants that cause damage to natural resources, to fauna, to flora, to ecosystems or to the environment, provided that such emissions come from stationary sources of federal jurisdiction, in accordance with the provisions of the General Law of Ecological Balance and Environmental Protection, or
- II. Generates emissions of noise, vibrations, thermal or light energy, coming from emitting sources of federal jurisdiction, in accordance with the regulation indicated in the preceding section, that cause damage to natural resources, to flora, to fauna, to ecosystems or to the environment.

The same penalties shall apply to whoever unlawfully carries out the activities described in the preceding sections, that cause a risk to natural resources, to flora, to fauna, to ecosystems or to the environment.

In the event that the activities referred to in this article are carried out in a protected natural area, the prison sentence shall be increased by up to three years and the economic penalty by up to one thousand days' fine.

Article added Official Gazette 13-12-1996. Amended Official Gazette 06-02-2002

###### Article 416.- A penalty of one to nine years in prison and three hundred to three thousand days'

fine shall be imposed on whoever unlawfully discharges, deposits, or infiltrates, authorizes or orders it, wastewater, chemical or biochemical liquids, waste or pollutants in soils, subsoils, marine waters, rivers, basins, vessels or other deposits or watercourses of federal jurisdiction, that causes a risk of damage or damages natural resources, flora, fauna, water quality, ecosystems or the environment.

When the waters are deposited, flow in or toward a protected natural area, the prison sentence shall be increased by up to three years and the economic penalty by up to one thousand days' fine. Article added Official Gazette 13-12-1996. Amended Official Gazette 06-02-2002

CHAPTER TWO

On biodiversity

Chapter added Official Gazette 06-02-2002

###### Article 417.- A penalty of one to nine years in prison and three hundred to three thousand days'

fine shall be imposed on whoever introduces into the national territory, or traffics with forest resources, wild flora or fauna, living or dead, their products or derivatives, that carry, suffer or have suffered, as applicable, any contagious disease, that causes or may cause its dissemination or propagation or contagion to flora, to fauna, to forest resources or to ecosystems.

Article added Official Gazette 13-12-1996. Amended Official Gazette 06-02-2002

###### Article 418.- A penalty of six months to nine years in prison and a fine of one hundred to three thousand

times the current daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update shall be imposed, provided that such activities are not carried out in urban areas, on whoever without having the prior authorization of the competent authority: Paragraph amended Official Gazette 08-05-2023

I. Clears or destroys forest vegetation;

Section amended Official Gazette 08-05-2023

II. Cuts, uproots, fells or logs any tree or trees, or

III. Changes land use on forest land without authorization issued by the competent authority.

Section amended Official Gazette 08-05-2023

The prison sentence shall be increased by up to four additional years and the fine by up to five thousand times the current daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update in the event that the conducts referred to in the sections of the first paragraph of this article affect a protected natural area. Paragraph amended Official Gazette 08-05-2023

When the conducts referred to in this article are carried out using firearms or by any other violent means against persons or to obtain profit or economic benefit, a penalty of three to twelve years in prison and a fine of five hundred to five thousand times the current daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update shall be imposed.

Paragraph added Official Gazette 08-05-2023

Article added Official Gazette 13-12-1996. Amended Official Gazette 06-02-2002

###### Article 419.- On whoever, without an administrative act authorizing it or does not have

documentation proving lawful origin, transports, trades, alienates, distributes, supplies, gathers, purchases, receives, acquires, stores, safeguards, possesses or transforms forest raw materials or timber forest products, the following penalties shall be imposed:

- I. When the volume does not exceed two cubic meters, a penalty of two to five years in prison and a fine of five hundred to three thousand times the current daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update shall be imposed.
- II. If the volume exceeds two cubic meters, a penalty of six to twelve years in prison and a fine of one thousand to five thousand times the value of the Unit of Measurement and Update shall be imposed.

The custodial sentences referred to in the preceding sections shall be increased by up to four years in prison and a fine of up to five thousand times the current daily value of the Unit of Measurement and Update, when the forest raw materials or timber forest products come from a protected natural area.

Article added Official Gazette 13-12-1996. Amended Official Gazette 24-12-1996, 06-02-2002, 08-05-2023

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform Official Gazette 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

###### Artículo 419 Bis.- A penalty of six months to five years of imprisonment and the equivalent of

two hundred to two thousand days fine shall be imposed on whoever:

- I. Breeds or trains a dog with the purpose of making it participate in any exhibition, show or activity involving a fight between two or more dogs for recreational, entertainment or any other purposes;
- II. Possesses, transports, purchases or sells dogs with the purpose of involving them in any exhibition, show or activity involving a fight between two or more dogs;
- III. Organizes, promotes, advertises, sponsors or sells tickets to attend shows involving dog fights;
- IV. Owns or manages a property where dog fights take place with knowledge of such activity;
- V. Causes minors to attend or witness any exhibition, show or activity involving a fight between two or more dogs, or
- VI. Carries out with or without profit any act with the objective of involving dogs in any exhibition, show or activity involving a fight between two or more dogs.

The sanction mentioned in the preceding paragraph shall be increased by one half when it concerns public servants.

Likewise, criminal liability is incurred by whoever attends as a spectator any exhibition, show or activity involving a fight between two or more dogs, with knowledge of this circumstance. In such cases, one third of the penalty provided in this article shall be imposed. Article added DOF 22-06-2017

###### Artículo 420.- A penalty of one to nine years of imprisonment and the equivalent of three hundred to

three thousand days fine shall be imposed on whoever unlawfully:

- I. Captures, harms or deprives of life any specimen of turtle or marine mammal, or collects or stores in any way their products or by-products;
- II. Captures, transforms, gathers, transports or harms specimens of aquatic species declared under closed season;

II Bis. Willfully captures, transforms, gathers, transports, destroys or trades with the aquatic species known as abalone, shrimp, sea cucumber and lobster, within or outside the closed season periods, without the corresponding authorization, in quantity exceeding 10 kilograms of weight.

Fraction added DOF 08-02-2006. Amended DOF 07-04-2017

III. Carries out hunting, fishing or capture activities with a non-permitted means, of any specimen of a wildlife species, or puts at risk the biological viability of a wild population or species;

IV. Carries out any activity for purposes of trafficking, or captures, possesses, transports, gathers, introduces into the country or extracts from it, any specimen, its products or by-products and other genetic resources, of a species of wild flora or fauna, terrestrial or aquatic under closed season, 138 of 337

Secretaría de Servicios Parlamentarios considered endemic, threatened, in danger of extinction, subject to special protection, or regulated by any international treaty to which Mexico is a party, or

V. Harms any specimen of the wild flora or fauna species, terrestrial or aquatic indicated in the preceding fraction.

An additional penalty of up to three more years of imprisonment and up to one thousand additional days fine shall be applied when the conducts described in this article are carried out in or affect a protected natural area, or when they are carried out for commercial purposes.

In the cases provided for in fraction IV of this article and fraction X of article 2o. of the Federal Law against Organized Crime, a penalty of five to fifteen years of imprisonment and the equivalent of three thousand to six thousand days fine shall be imposed when it concerns any specimen, parts, derivatives, products or by-products of the species totoaba macdonaldi.

Paragraph added DOF 19-02-2021

Article added DOF 13-12-1996. Amended DOF 06-02-2002

###### Artículo 420 Bis.- A penalty of two to ten years of imprisonment and the equivalent of three hundred

to three thousand days fine shall be imposed on whoever unlawfully:

- I. Damages, drains or fills wetlands, mangroves, lagoons, estuaries or swamps;
- II. Damages reefs;
- III. Introduces or releases into the natural environment any specimen of exotic flora or fauna that harms an ecosystem, or that hinders, alters or affects native or migratory species in the natural cycles of their reproduction or migration, or
- IV. Causes a fire in a forest, jungle, natural vegetation or forestlands, that damages natural elements, flora, fauna, ecosystems or the environment.

An additional penalty of up to two years of imprisonment and up to one thousand additional days fine shall be applied when the conducts described in this article are carried out in or affect a protected natural area, or the author or participant of the crime provided for in fraction IV carries out the conduct to obtain a profit or economic benefit.

Article added DOF 06-02-2002

CAPÍTULO TERCERO

On biosafety

Chapter added DOF 06-02-2002

###### Artículo 420 Ter.- A penalty of one to nine years of imprisonment and three hundred to three thousand days

fine shall be imposed on whoever in contravention of what is established in the applicable regulations, introduces into the country, or extracts from it, trades, transports, stores or releases into the environment any genetically modified organism that alters or may negatively alter the components, structure or functioning of natural ecosystems.

For purposes of this article, genetically modified organism shall be understood as any organism that possesses a new combination of genetic material that has been obtained through the application of biotechnology, including those derived from genetic engineering techniques. Article added DOF 06-02-2002

Crimes against environmental management

Chapter added DOF 06-02-2002

###### Artículo 420 Quáter.- A penalty of one to four years of imprisonment and three hundred to three thousand

days fine shall be imposed on whoever:

- I. Transports or consents, authorizes or orders the transport of any waste considered dangerous due to its corrosive, reactive, explosive, toxic, flammable, biologically infectious or radioactive characteristics, to a destination for which there is no authorization to receive it, store it, dispose of it or abandon it;
- II. Enters false data in the records, logbooks or any other document used with the purpose of simulating compliance with obligations derived from federal environmental regulations;
- III. Destroys, alters or conceals information, records, reports or any other document required to be maintained or archived in accordance with federal environmental regulations;
- IV. Providing services as a technical auditor, specialist or expert or specialist in environmental impact, forestry, wildlife, fishing or other environmental matters, fails to tell the truth causing damage to natural resources, flora, fauna, ecosystems, water quality or the environment, or
- V. Does not carry out or comply with the technical, corrective or security measures necessary to avoid environmental damage or risk that the administrative or judicial authority orders or imposes.

The crimes provided for in this Chapter shall be prosecuted upon complaint by the Federal Attorney for Environmental Protection.

Article added DOF 06-02-2002

CAPÍTULO QUINTO

Common provisions for crimes against the environment

Chapter added DOF 06-02-2002

###### Artículo 421. In addition to what is established in the previous chapters of the Twenty-Fifth Title, the

following penalties and security measures shall be imposed:

Paragraph reformed DOF 07-06-2013

I. The repair and, where applicable, the compensation of damage to the environment, in accordance with the provisions of the Federal Environmental Liability Law;

Clause reformed DOF 07-06-2013

II. The suspension, modification or demolition of constructions, works or activities, as appropriate, that gave rise to the respective environmental crime;

III. The reincorporation of natural elements, specimens or species of wild flora and fauna, to the habitats from which they were removed, provided that their reincorporation does not constitute a danger to ecological balance or hinder the reproduction or migration of species of wild flora or fauna;

IV. The return of hazardous materials or waste or specimens of threatened or endangered wild flora and fauna, to the country of origin, considering the provisions of international treaties and conventions to which Mexico is a party, or

140 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

V. Disqualification, when the author or participant of the crime has the status of a public servant, for a period equal to that which would have been set as a custodial sentence, which must run from the moment the convicted person has served the prison sentence or it has been considered served.

Community service work referred to in Article 24 of this regulation shall consist of activities related to environmental protection or the restoration of natural resources. For the purposes referred to in this article, the judge must request from the competent federal agency or higher education or scientific research institutions, the issuance of the corresponding technical opinion.

The competent public administration agencies must provide the public prosecutor or the judge with the technical or expert opinions required as a result of complaints filed for the commission of crimes referred to in this Title.

The minimum and maximum parameters of prison sentences referred to in this Title shall be reduced by half, when the accused or defendant voluntarily repairs or compensates the damage to the environment before such obligation has been imposed by administrative resolution or judicial sentence. Such reduction shall also proceed when the repair or compensation of the damage is carried out or guaranteed in terms of the provisions of Title Two of the Federal Environmental Liability Law.

Paragraph reformed DOF 07-06-2013

Persons legitimized in terms of the provisions of the Federal Environmental Liability Law shall be considered victims with the right to request the repair or compensation of environmental damage and assist in the criminal process.

Paragraph added DOF 07-06-2013

Article added DOF 13-12-1996. Reformed DOF 06-02-2002

###### Artículo 422.- In the case of crimes against the environment, when the author or participant has the status

of guarantor with respect to the protected assets, the prison sentence shall be increased by up to three years.

Article added DOF 13-12-1996. Reformed DOF 06-02-2002

###### Artículo 423.- No penalty shall be applied to anyone who engages in the conduct indicated in the

first paragraph, second clause, of Article 418, nor to anyone who transports firewood or dead wood referred to in Article 419, when carrying out the activity for domestic use within the rural, indigenous or Afro-Mexican community to which they belong.

Article added DOF 13-12-1996. Reformed DOF 06-02-2002, 08-05-2023

TITLE TWENTY-SIXTH

Crimes Relating to Copyright

Title added DOF 24-12-1996

###### Artículo 424.- Prison of six months to six years and three hundred to three thousand days' fine shall be imposed:

I. On anyone who speculates in any way with free textbooks distributed by the Ministry of Public Education;

II. On the editor, producer or recorder who knowingly produces more copies of a work protected by the Federal Copyright Law than those authorized by the rights holder;

141 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

III. On anyone who uses fraudulently, for profit and without the corresponding authorization, works protected by the Federal Copyright Law.

Clause reformed DOF 19-05-1997, 17-05-1999

IV. Repealed.

Clause repealed DOF 17-05-1999

Article added DOF 24-12-1996

###### Artículo 424

bis.- Prison of three to ten years and two thousand to twenty thousand days' fine shall be imposed:

- I. On anyone who produces, reproduces, introduces into the country, stores, transports, distributes, sells or leases copies of works, phonograms, videograms or books, protected by the Federal Copyright Law, fraudulently, for the purpose of commercial speculation and without the authorization that must be granted by the holder of copyright or related rights under the terms of said Law.

The same penalty shall be imposed on those who, knowingly, contribute or provide in any way, raw materials or supplies intended for the production or reproduction of works, phonograms, videograms or books referred to in the preceding paragraph;

Paragraph reformed DOF 01-07-2020

II. On anyone who manufactures for profit a device or system whose purpose is to disable the electronic protection devices of a computer program, or

Clause reformed DOF 01-07-2020

III. On anyone who records, transmits or makes a total or partial copy of a protected cinematographic work, exhibited in a movie theater or places that serve the same purpose, without the authorization of the holder of copyright or related rights.

Clause added DOF 01-07-2020

Article added DOF 17-05-1999

###### Artículo 424

ter.- Prison of six months to six years and five thousand to thirty thousand days' fine shall be imposed on anyone who sells to any final consumer in public roads or places, fraudulently, for purposes of commercial speculation, copies of works, phonograms, videograms or books, referred to in clause I of the previous article.

If the sale is made in commercial establishments, or in an organized or permanent manner, the provisions of Article 424 Bis of this Code shall apply.

Article added DOF 17-05-1999

###### Artículo 425.- Prison of six months to two years or three hundred to three thousand days' fine shall be imposed on

anyone who knowingly and without right exploits for profit a performance or an execution.

Article added DOF 24-12-1996

###### Artículo 426.

Imprisonment of six months to four years and three hundred to three thousand days' fine shall be imposed in the following cases:

- I. To whoever manufactures, modifies, imports, distributes, sells or leases a device or system to decrypt an encrypted satellite signal carrying programs, without authorization from the legitimate distributor of said signal;

Section amended DOF 01-07-2020

II. To whoever carries out for profit any act for the purpose of decrypting an encrypted satellite signal carrying programs, without authorization from the legitimate distributor of said signal;

142 de 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

Section amended DOF 01-07-2020

III. To whoever manufactures or distributes equipment intended for the reception of an encrypted cable signal carrying programs, without authorization from the legitimate distributor of said signal, or Section added DOF 01-07-2020

IV. To whoever receives or assists another to receive an encrypted cable signal carrying programs without authorization from the legitimate distributor of said signal.

Section added DOF 01-07-2020

Article added DOF 24-12-1996

###### Artículo 427.- Imprisonment of six months to six years and three hundred to three thousand days' fine shall be imposed on

whoever knowingly publishes a work substituting the name of the author with another name.

Article added DOF 24-12-1996

###### Artículo 427 Bis.- To whoever, knowingly and for profit, circumvents without authorization any

effective technological protection measure used by producers of phonograms, artists, performers or executants, as well as authors of any work protected by copyright or related rights, shall be punished with imprisonment of six months to six years and five hundred to one thousand days' fine. Article added DOF 01-07-2020

###### Artículo 427

Ter.- To whoever, for profit, manufactures, imports, distributes, rents or in any way commercializes devices, products or components intended to circumvent an effective technological protection measure used by producers of phonograms, artists, performers or executants, as well as authors of any work protected by copyright or related rights, shall be imposed six months to six years of imprisonment and five hundred to one thousand days' fine.

Article added DOF 01-07-2020

###### Artículo 427 Quáter.- To whoever, for profit, provides or offers services to the public intended

primarily to circumvent an effective technological protection measure used by producers of phonograms, artists, performers or executants, as well as authors of any work protected by copyright or related rights, shall be imposed six months to six years of imprisonment and five hundred to one thousand days' fine.

Article added DOF 01-07-2020

###### Artículo 427

Quinquies.- To whoever, knowingly, without authorization and for profit, removes or alters, by himself or through another, any rights management information, shall be imposed six months to six years of imprisonment and five hundred to one thousand days' fine.

The same penalty shall be imposed on whoever for profit:

- I. Distributes or imports for distribution rights management information, knowing that it has been removed or altered without authorization, or
- II. Distributes, imports for distribution, transmits, communicates or makes available to the public copies of works, performances, executions or phonograms, knowing that the rights management information has been removed or altered without authorization.

Article added DOF 01-07-2020

###### Artículo 428.- The pecuniary sanctions provided for in this title shall be applied without prejudice to the

reparation of damages, the amount of which may not be less than forty percent of the public sale price of each product or the provision of services that imply violation of any or some of the rights protected by the Federal Copyright Law.

Article added DOF 24-12-1996

143 de 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

###### Article 429. The crimes provided for in this Title shall be prosecuted ex officio, except as provided in

articles 424, section II, 424 Bis, section III and 427.

Article added DOF 24-12-1996. Amended DOF 28-06-2010, 01-07-2020

TRANSITORY ARTICLES:

1º.- This Code shall enter into force on September 17, 1931.

2º.- From that same date, the Penal Code of December 15, 1929 is hereby abrogated, as well as all other laws that are contrary to this one; but both that Code and the one of December 7, 1871 shall continue to apply to acts committed, respectively, during their validity, unless the accused manifest their will to avail themselves of the legal order they deem most favorable, between the present Code and the one in force at the time of the commission of the crime.

Errata to the article DOF 31-08-1931

3º.- The provisions of a penal nature contained in special laws remain in force in all that is not provided for in this Code.

Therefore, I order that it be printed, published, circulated and duly complied with.

Given at the Palace of the Federal Executive Power, in Mexico, on the thirteenth day of the month of August, nineteen hundred thirty-one.- P. Ortiz Rubio.- Signature.- The Undersecretary of the Interior, In Charge of

the Office, Octavio Mendoza González.- Signature.

Which I communicate to you for publication and other purposes.

Effective Suffrage. No Reelection.

Mexico, D.F., August 14, 1931.- The Undersecretary of the Interior. In Charge of the Office, Octavio Mendoza González.- Signature.

LAW creating the Guardianship Councils for Minor Offenders of the Federal District and Territories.

Published in the Diario Oficial of the Federation on August 2, 1974

TRANSITORY PROVISIONS

###### ARTICLE 1. Transitory.- This Law shall enter into force thirty days after its publication in

the "Diario Oficial", and as of the same date, articles 119 to 122 of the Penal Code for the Federal District and Territories in matters of Common Jurisdiction and for the entire Republic in matters of Federal Jurisdiction, of August 13, 1931, shall be repealed only with respect to the Federal District and Territories, the Organic Law and Rules of Procedure of the Juvenile Courts and their Auxiliary Institutions in the Federal District and Territories, of April 22, 1941 and all other provisions that conflict with this Act.

###### ARTICLE 2. Transitory.- The first rotation among the Councilors for purposes of instructing

the proceedings shall be established according to the chronological order of their appointment.

###### ARTICLE 3. Transitory.- The Councilors appointed upon the entry into force of this Law, pursuant

to article 5, shall serve in their position until December 31, 1976.

###### ARTICLE 4. Transitory.- Both proceedings that are being conducted at the commencement of its

validity and measures imposed prior thereto, which are in the process of execution, shall be subject to the provisions of this Law.

###### ARTICLE 5. Transitory.- Until the Auxiliary Councils are established, the Qualifying Judges shall

hear violations of police and good government regulations and the Guardianship Council shall hear the other cases indicated in article 48.

#### Title One

, of chapters III and VI, of Title Two; of chapters II, IV and V, of Title Three, and of chapters III and IV of Title Five, and of Book Two those of Chapter III, of Title Eight, and of

##### Chapter I, of Title Twelfth, of the Penal Code for the Federal District in matters of Common

Jurisdiction and for the entire Republic in matters of Federal Jurisdiction to read as follows: ARTICULO QUINTO.- Articles 58, 264, 294, 377, 378 and 385 of the Penal Code for the Federal District in matters of Common Jurisdiction and for the entire Republic in matters of Federal Jurisdiction are hereby repealed.

ARTICULO TERCERO.- For the imposition of fines under the system of fine-days referred to in

###### artículo 29

of the Penal Code, reformed under the terms of this Decree, the judge shall comply with the following rules:

194 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023 General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

I.- When a fine is imposed in pesos, the respective conversion shall be made taking into account the maximum fine set by law, with the corresponding amounts indicated below: when the maximum is five hundred pesos, for one day-fine; if it exceeds this amount, but not ten thousand pesos, between two and twenty day-fines; if it is greater than ten thousand pesos, but does not exceed one hundred thousand, from twenty-one to two hundred day-fines; and if it exceeds one hundred thousand pesos, between two hundred one and five hundred day-fines.

II.- When a fine is established on the basis of days of minimum wage, it shall be converted at the rate of one day of wage per one day-fine.

ARTICLE FOURTH.- With respect to the regime applicable to the unimputables referred to in

###### Artículo 15

section II of the Penal Code, reformed under the terms of this decree, the provisions for the mentally ill in the Federal Code of Criminal Procedures shall apply, the same regime that shall apply for infractions of common jurisdiction.

ARTICLE FIFTH.- The surveillance measures of the authority and in compliance with the substitutes for imprisonment referred to in the Penal Code, reformed under the terms of this decree, shall be the responsibility of the Federal Executive agency in charge of the execution of sanctions.
ARTICLE SIXTH.- For the purposes of recognition of the innocence of the subject referred to in

###### Artículo 96

of the Penal Code, reformed under the terms of this decree, the provisions for necessary pardon shall apply, both in the Federal Code of Criminal Procedures and in the Code of Criminal Procedures for the Federal District, as appropriate.

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

LAW for the treatment of Juvenile Offenders, for the Federal District in Common Matters

and for the entire Republic in Federal Matters.

Published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on December 24, 1991

TRANSITORY

FIRST.- This Law shall enter into force sixty days after its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.

SECOND.- The Law that creates the Guardianship Council for Juvenile Offenders of the Federal District, published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on August 2, 1974, is hereby abrogated.

THIRD.- Articles 119 to 122 of the Penal Code for the Federal District in Matters of Common Jurisdiction and for the entire Republic in Matters of Federal Jurisdiction; 73 to 78 of the Organic Law of the Judicial Power of the Federation, and 503 of the Federal Code of Criminal Procedures; as well as articles 673 and 674, sections II and X, of the Code of Criminal Procedures for the Federal District, solely with respect to juvenile offenders, are hereby repealed.

FOURTH.- Matters that are in process on the date on which this Law enters into force shall be transferred to the competent areas corresponding to hear them, in accordance with the new determination of competencies.

FIFTH.- The regulations of the diagnostic and treatment centers shall be issued within ninety days following the date of installation of the Council for Minors.

SIXTH.- The currently existing auxiliary councils shall hear administrative violations of police and good government regulations committed by minors, until the competent body is established. These councils may only apply the guidance and protection measures provided for in this Law.

SEVENTH.- Until the Council for Minors has integrated its expert services, it may seek assistance from the corresponding bodies of the Attorney General's Office of the Republic, the Attorney General's Office of Justice of the Federal District and the Superior Court of Justice of the Federal District.

###### ARTICLE FIRST.

Articles 7, section III, 64, second and third paragraphs, 65, 70, sections I, II and III, 185, 189, 243, 253 first paragraph and section I, in its subsections e) second paragraph and g), 289, 364 first paragraph and section I, 366 first paragraph with its sections, second and third paragraphs, and 400 bis are hereby amended; a third paragraph to article 63, a second and third paragraph to article 65, a final paragraph to article 70 and another to article 158, article 196 ter, a second paragraph to article 243, subsection i) to section I of article 253, sections VII and VIII to article 254, article 254 ter, a second and third paragraph to section I of article 364, a new article 366 bis, whereupon the current article 366 bis is renumbered to become article 366 ter, section III to article 368, articles 368 bis and 368 ter, a third paragraph to article 371, article 377 and a last paragraph to article 400 are hereby added; the title of the Twenty-Third Title of Book Two is modified and divided into two chapters; and article 321 of the Penal Code for the Federal District in Matters of Common Jurisdiction, and for the entire Republic in Matters of Federal Jurisdiction, is hereby repealed, to read as follows:

TRANSITORY

FIRST.- This decree shall enter into force on the day following its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.

SECOND.- Article 115 bis of the Fiscal Code of the Federation, in force until the entry into force of this decree, shall continue to apply to acts performed during its effectiveness. Likewise, said provision shall continue to apply to persons prosecuted or sentenced for offenses provided for and punished by the aforementioned article.

To proceed criminally in the cases referred to in article 115 bis of the Fiscal Code of the Federation, under the terms of the preceding paragraph, the complaint of the Secretariat of Finance and Public Credit shall continue to be required.

For purposes of the application of the respective penalties, the provisions of article 56 of the cited Penal Code shall govern, without this implying the extinction of the criminal offenses.

THIRD.- For the purposes, subjects and effects of the preceding article, the offenses provided for in

###### article 115

bis of the Fiscal Code of the Federation, shall continue to be classified as serious, under the terms of article 194 of the Federal Code of Criminal Procedures, for all applicable legal effects.

Federal Jurisdiction; the Statute of Government of the Federal District; and issuing the General Law of the System of Legal Remedies in Electoral Matters.

Published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on November 22, 1996

ARTICLE FIVE.- Articles 401; 403 and 404; fractions II, IV through VI, VIII, X and

XI of article 405; the first paragraph and fractions I, III through VI of article 406; fractions I through III of

###### article 407;

and article 411 ARE REFORMED. Fraction XIII is ADDED to article 403; fraction VII to article 406; and a fraction IV to article 407. Fraction IX of article 405 IS REPEALED; all of the Penal Code for the Federal District in Matters of Common Jurisdiction, and for the entire Republic in Matters of Federal Jurisdiction, to read in the following terms:

TRANSITORY PROVISION

Sole.- The reforms to the Penal Code for the Federal District in Matters of Common Jurisdiction and for the entire Republic in Matters of Federal Jurisdiction referred to in ARTICLE FIVE of this Decree, shall enter into force on the day of its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.

TRANSITORY ARTICLES OF THE DECREE REFORMING, ADDING AND

REPEALING VARIOUS PROVISIONS OF THE FEDERAL CODE OF ELECTORAL

INSTITUTIONS AND PROCEDURES; THE REGULATORY LAW OF FRACTIONS

I AND II OF ARTICLE 105 OF THE POLITICAL CONSTITUTION OF THE UNITED

MEXICAN STATES; THE ORGANIC LAW OF THE JUDICIAL POWER OF THE

FEDERATION; THE PENAL CODE FOR THE FEDERAL DISTRICT IN MATTERS OF

COMMON JURISDICTION AND FOR THE ENTIRE REPUBLIC IN MATTERS OF

FEDERAL JURISDICTION; THE STATUTE OF GOVERNMENT OF THE FEDERAL

DISTRICT; AND ISSUING THE GENERAL LAW OF THE SYSTEM OF LEGAL

REMEDIES IN ELECTORAL MATTERS

FIRST.- The reforms referred to in this Decree shall enter into force on the day of its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation, with the particularities established in the transitory provisions of each of the articles of this Decree.

SECOND.- All provisions that oppose this Decree are repealed.

THIRD.- On July 6, 1997, exclusively the Head of Government and the Deputies to the Legislative Assembly of the Federal District shall be elected for the Federal District.

All articles of the Statute of Government of the Federal District referring to the election of Citizen Councilors are repealed.

The rules that regulate the substantive functions of the current Citizen Councilors established in the regulations in force, shall continue to apply until the end of the period for which they were elected.

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform OGF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

Based on the new Statute of Government of the Federal District, the Legislative Assembly shall issue the provisions relating to citizen participation in the Federal District.

FOURTH.- The head of the Federal Executive Power is authorized so that through the Secretariat of Finance and Public Credit, the necessary budgetary transfers may be made, so that the corresponding authorities may comply with the obligations and carry out the new activities that the present reforms and additions impose on them.

FIFTH.- The jurisprudential criteria upheld by the Central Chamber and the Chamber of Second Instance of the Federal Electoral Tribunal, as appropriate, shall continue to be applicable as long as they do not oppose the reforms established in ARTICLES TWO, THREE and FOUR of this Decree.

For the jurisprudential criteria referred to in the preceding paragraph to be binding, the formal declaration of the Superior Chamber of the Electoral Tribunal shall be required. Once the declaration is made, the jurisprudence shall be immediately notified to the Regional Chambers, the Federal Electoral Institute and, where appropriate, to the local electoral authorities.

Published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on December 24, 1996

###### ARTICULO PRIMERO.- Se deroga la fracción XVI del artículo 387 del Código Penal para el Distrito

Federal en Materia de Fuero Común y para toda la República en Materia de Fuero Federal, para quedar como sigue:

ARTICULO SEGUNDO.- Se reforma el artículo 419 del Código Penal para el Distrito Federal en Materia de Fuero Común y para toda la República en Materia de Fuero Federal, para quedar como sigue: ARTICULO TERCERO.- Se adiciona un Título Vigésimo Sexto al Libro Segundo del Código Penal para el Distrito Federal en Materia de Fuero Común y para toda la República en Materia de Fuero Federal, para quedar como sigue:

TRANSITORIOS

PRIMERO.- El artículo Segundo del presente Decreto entrará en vigor al día siguiente de su publicación en el Diario Oficial de la Federación. Los artículos Primero y Tercero entrarán en vigor noventa días después de su publicación en el Diario Oficial de la Federación.

SEGUNDO.- A las personas que hubieren cometido delitos contemplados en la Ley Federal de Derechos de Autor publicada en el Diario Oficial de la Federación el 29 de diciembre de 1956, con anterioridad a la entrada en vigor del presente Decreto, les serán aplicables las sanciones vigentes al momento en que se hubieren realizado dichas conductas. Al efecto, los artículos 135 a 144 de dicha ley, seguirán vigentes y se aplicarán a la persecución, sanción y ejecución de sentencias por hechos ejecutados hasta antes de la entrada en vigor del Título Vigésimo Sexto que se adiciona al Código Penal para el Distrito Federal en materia de Fuero Común y para toda la República en materia de Fuero Federal por este Decreto.

SEGUNDO.- Mentions made in other provisions of federal character to the Penal Code for the Federal District in Matters of Common Jurisdiction and for all the Republic in Matters of Federal Jurisdiction, shall be understood as referring to the Federal Penal Code.

Mexico City, Federal District, April 29, 1999.- Sen. Héctor Ximénez González, President.- Rep. María Mercedes Maciel Ortiz, President.- Sen. Sonia Alcántara Magos, Secretary.- Rep. Leticia Villegas Nava, Secretary.- Signatures".

Code of Criminal Procedures and the Organic Law of the Judicial Power of the

Federation.

Published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on June 12, 2000

###### ARTICULO PRIMERO.

Articles 85, Section I, subsection f), 366, the last three paragraphs, 366 bis, first paragraph, 366 ter and 366 quáter are reformed, and article 366 is added with a Section III and a second paragraph, shifting the others in order, of the Federal Penal Code, to read as follows:

TRANSITORY

FIRST.- This Decree shall enter into force the day following its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.

SECOND.- Within a period not exceeding six months, the Federal Executive must create the necessary administrative structure in the Attorney General's Office of the Republic for the attention of the crimes provided for in articles 366, Section III; 366 ter and 366 quáter provided in this Decree.

For the purposes of the preceding paragraph, the Federal Executive is authorized to make the budgetary adjustments that may be necessary.

"THIRD.- The crimes provided for in articles 366 ter and 366 quáter of the Federal Penal Code in force prior to the effective date of this Decree shall continue to apply with respect to acts committed during their term."

FOURTH.- Provisions that conflict with this Decree are repealed.

Mexico City, April 29, 2000.- Sen. Dionisio Pérez Jácome, Acting Vice President.- Rep. Francisco José Paoli Bolio, President.- Sen. Raúl Juárez Valencia, Secretary.- Rep. Guadalupe Sánchez Martínez, Secretary.- Signatures".

Article Fourth.- Subparagraphs i) and j) of Section I of article 85 are amended; subparagraphs k) and l) are added to Section I of article 85, and article 240 Bis of the Federal Penal Code is repealed, to read as follows:

Transitories

First. This Decree shall enter into force on the day following its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.

Second. The offenses provided for in articles 112 Bis of the Law of Credit Institutions and 240 Bis of the Federal Penal Code in force until the entry into force of this decree, shall continue to apply to acts committed during their validity. Likewise, said provisions shall continue to apply to persons prosecuted or sentenced for the offenses provided for and punished by the same articles. The foregoing without prejudice to applying, when appropriate, the provisions of article 56 of the aforementioned Federal Penal Code.

Third. For the assumptions, subjects and effects of the preceding article, the offenses provided for in article 240 Bis of the Federal Penal Code shall continue to be classified as serious under the terms of article 194 of the Federal Code of Criminal Procedures, for all applicable legal effects.

Mexico City, Federal District, April 22, 2008.- Rep. Ruth Zavaleta Salgado, President.- Sen. Santiago Creel Miranda, President.- Rep. Esmeralda Cardenas Sanchez, Secretary.- Sen. Adrián Rivera Pérez, Secretary.- Signatures."

250 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform OGF 05-08-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

DECREE reforming, adding and repealing various provisions of the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector, the Law on Public Works

and Related Services, the Federal Law on

Administrative Responsibilities of Public Servants and the Federal Penal

Code.

Published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on May 28, 2009

Fourth Article.- The second paragraph of Article 217 is reformed and its third and fourth paragraphs are repealed, of the Federal Penal Code, to read as follows:

TRANSITORY ARTICLES

FIRST. This Decree shall enter into force thirty calendar days following its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation, except for the provisions that for their application require modifications to the CompraNet electronic system referred to in the Tenth transitory article of this Decree.

SECOND. All provisions that oppose the provisions of this

Decree are repealed.

THIRD. Until the corresponding reforms to the Regulations of the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector and to the Regulations of the Law on Public Works and Related Services are issued, as well as other administrative provisions derived from this Decree, the regulations of said laws and administrative provisions in force on the matter shall continue to apply, insofar as they do not oppose this Decree.

FOURTH. The Secretariat of Public Administration shall conduct an annual evaluation to determine the increase in coverage of participation of social witnesses referred to in articles 26 Ter of the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector and 27 Bis of the Law on Public Works and Related Services. Likewise, that Secretariat shall implement measures for the purpose of expanding said coverage to guarantee, within a period of ten years from the entry into force of this Law, the total coverage of social witnesses in public contracting.

FIFTH. For the adequate application of the criterion for evaluation of proposals through the mechanism of points and percentages referred to in articles 36 of the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector and 38 of the Law on Public Works and Related Services, the Secretariat of Public Administration shall issue within a period not exceeding ninety calendar days, counted from the entry into force of this Decree, the corresponding guidelines.

SIXTH. The guidelines referred to in the second paragraph of articles 48 of the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector and of the Law on Public Works and Related Services, must be issued within a period not exceeding six months, counted from the entry into force of the aforementioned legal provisions.

SEVENTH. Contracting procedures that are in process as of the date of entry into force of this Decree shall continue to be processed until their conclusion in accordance with the provisions of the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector and the Law on Public Works and Related Services, as applicable, in force at the time of their initiation.

251 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform OGF 05-08-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

EIGHTH. Contracts entered into before the entry into force of this Decree shall continue to be governed until their termination by the provisions of the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector and the Law on Public Works and Related Services, as applicable, in force at the time of their execution.

NINTH. Conciliation, objection and sanction procedures that are in process or pending resolution as of the date of entry into force of this Decree must be processed and concluded in accordance with the provisions in force at the time such procedures were initiated.

TENTH. The adjustments to the CompraNet government contracting electronic system that allow the application of the reforms made by this Decree to the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector and to the Law on Public Works and Related Services must be completed within a period not exceeding one year, counted from the date of entry into force of this Decree.

The following articles shall enter into force within said period as the modifications referred to in the preceding paragraph are made: article 37 fifth paragraph regarding notification of the award in CompraNet; article 48 second paragraph regarding the obligation of dependencies and entities to consider the performance records of suppliers in contracts for the purpose of determining the percentages of guarantees; article 50 last paragraph, 56 and 69 second paragraph, of the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector; article 39 penultimate paragraph regarding notification of the award in CompraNet; article 48 second paragraph regarding the obligation of dependencies and entities to consider the performance records of contractors in contracts for the purpose of determining the percentages of guarantees; article 51 last paragraph, 74 and 87 second paragraph, of the Law on Public Works and Related Services.

Until the provisions referred to in the preceding paragraph enter into force, the acts indicated therein shall continue to be carried out in accordance with the regulations in force.

Within a period of six months counted from the entry into force of this Decree, the information relating to the annual programs on acquisitions, leases and services and public works and related services of the dependencies and entities, registry of social witnesses, the register of sanctioned suppliers and contractors, and the testimonies of social witnesses, referred to in articles 56 of the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector and 74 of the Law on Public Works and Related Services, shall be available in CompraNet.

In the case of dependencies and entities that have a database on the performance of suppliers and contractors in the contracts they have entered into with them, they may apply upon the entry into force of this Decree, the provisions of the second paragraph of article 48 of the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector and the second paragraph of

###### article 48

of the Public Works and Related Services Law, in accordance with the guidelines issued by the Ministry of Public Administration.

ELEVENTH. The Ministry of Economy shall progressively increase the percentage of national content referred to in section I of article 28 of the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector, up to sixty-five percent, within a period of three years, counted from the entry into force of this Decree.

TWELFTH. The administrative unit of the Ministry of Public Administration referred to in articles 2 section II and 56 of the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector 252 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

and 2 section II and 74 of the Public Works and Related Services Law, shall not imply the creation of new organic and occupational structures, nor the creation of budgetary positions.

THIRTEENTH. The Ministry of Public Administration shall issue guidelines to promote the expediting of payments to suppliers, as regulated in article 51 of the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector, within a period not exceeding six months, counted from the entry into force of this Decree.

FOURTEENTH. Regardless of the exceptions to the bidding procedure provided for in article 42 of the Public Works and Related Services Law, from the day following the publication of this Decree in the Official Gazette of the Federation and until December 31, 2009, all contracting for construction, maintenance or repair of works shall also be excepted from the public bidding procedure, in which it is proven that there is intensive use of labor representing at least 50% of the total cost of the project.

FIFTEENTH. The sum of the amounts of contracts made during the year 2009, under article 43 of the Public Works and Related Services Law, may not exceed thirty-five percent of the authorized budget to carry out public works and related services; the amount allocated to each contractor may not exceed five percent of said budget.

SIXTEENTH. The Federal Executive shall inform the Budget and Public Accounts Commission of the Chamber of Deputies, where applicable, through the Permanent Commission of the Congress of the Union, on the progress in the savings generated as a result of the application of the measures relating to the rationalization of spending provided for in the Medium-Term Program, in accordance with the provisions of articles 45 and 61 of the Federal Budget and Fiscal Responsibility Law; in the second article of the Decree that reforms, adds and repeals various provisions of the Federal Budget and Fiscal Responsibility Law; Organic Law of the Federal Public Administration, Fiscal Coordination Law, Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector, and Public Works and Related Services Law, published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on October 1, 2007; as well as article 16 of the Expenditure Budget Decree of the Federation for fiscal year 2009.

The Budget and Public Accounts Commission of the Chamber of Deputies shall take into account the information provided by the Federal Executive, regarding the concepts indicated in article 16 of the Expenditure Budget Decree of the Federation for fiscal year 2009, for purposes of the approval process of the Expenditure Budget of the Federation for fiscal year 2010. SEVENTEENTH.- The Federal Executive, through the Ministry of Public Administration and the Ministry of Environment and Natural Resources, within a period of 30 calendar days counted from the entry into force of this Decree, shall issue guidelines for the proper application of the provisions in the fourth paragraph of article 26 of the Law on Acquisitions, Leases and Services of the Public Sector.

Mexico City, D.F., April 30, 2009.- Rep. Cesar Horacio Duarte Jaquez, President.- Sen. Gustavo Enrique Madero Muñoz, President.- Rep. Maria Eugenia Jimenez Valenzuela, Secretary.- Sen. Gabino Cue Monteagudo, Secretary.- Signatures."

Published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on August 20, 2009

Second Article. Articles 195, 195 bis and 199 are REFORMED; and third and fourth paragraphs are ADDED to section I of article 194, all of the Federal Penal Code, to read as follows:

TRANSITORY PROVISIONS

FIRST.- This Decree shall enter into force the day following its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.

For purposes of the provisions of article 474 of the General Health Law, the local legislatures and the Legislative Assembly of the Federal District shall have a period of one year from the entry into force of this Decree to make the appropriate adjustments to the corresponding legislation.

The Federation and the federative entities shall have a period of three years from the entry into force of this Decree, to carry out the necessary actions, as appropriate, in order to duly comply with the powers contained therein.

SECOND.- Criminal proceedings that are being processed at the entry into force of this Decree shall continue in accordance with the provisions in force at the time of commission of the acts.

THIRD.- Persons who have committed an offense contemplated in this Decree prior to its entry into force, including those prosecuted or sentenced, shall be subject to the provisions in force at the time it was committed.

FOURTH.- The competent authorities shall finance the actions derived from compliance with this Decree with the resources annually provided for in the Expenditure Budget of the Federation, without prejudice to the resources that the federative entities contribute for such purposes. FIFTH.- All provisions that oppose this Decree are repealed.
