General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

DECREE issuing the General Law to Prevent and Punish Crimes in

Matters of Kidnapping, Regulatory of section XXI of article 73 of the Political

Constitution of the United Mexican States; and reforming, adding and repealing various provisions of the Federal Code of Criminal Procedure, the Federal Penal Code, the Federal Law against Organized Crime, the Organic Law of the Judicial

Power of the Federation, the Federal Police Law, the Federal

Telecommunications Law and the General Law of the National Public Security System

Published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on November 30, 2010

ARTICLE THREE. Articles 55, second and fourth paragraphs; 64, first paragraph;

85, subparagraph f), of section I; article 215, in its section XIII and last paragraph and section XIV are reformed; numeral 19 is added to article 24, section IV to article 85, article 180 Bis and section XVI to

###### article 215;

and articles 366 and 366 Bis are repealed, all of the Federal Penal Code, to read as follows:

TRANSITORY ARTICLES

First. This Decree shall enter into force ninety days after its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.

Second. Criminal proceedings initiated before the entry into force of this Decree regarding crimes provided for therein shall continue to be processed until their conclusion in accordance with the provisions in force at the time of commission of the acts that gave rise to them. The same shall be observed with respect to the execution of the corresponding penalties.

Third. All legal provisions that oppose this Decree are repealed.

Fourth. The implementation of this Decree shall be charged to the respective budgets approved for the agencies of the three levels of government obligated to comply with the provisions established herein.

Fifth. The provisions relating to kidnapping crimes provided for both in the Federal Penal Code and in the local Penal Codes in force until the entry into force of this Decree shall continue to apply for acts committed during their validity. Likewise, said provisions shall continue to apply to persons prosecuted or sentenced for the crimes provided for and punished by the same articles.

Sixth. The Attorney General of the Republic and the Attorneys General of Justice of the States and of the Federal District shall have one year counted from the publication of this Decree in the Official Gazette of the Federation to issue the corresponding administrative provisions regarding protection of persons in the terms indicated by this Law, without prejudice to the protection measures they may grant previously.

Seventh. The National Public Security Council and the National Conference of Justice Prosecution shall prepare a National Program to prevent, prosecute and punish the conducts provided for in this ordinance, independently of the program of each particular entity, having a period of six months, counted from the publication of this Decree in the Official Gazette of the Federation.

260 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform OGF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

Eighth. The reform to section XIV of article 44 of the Federal Telecommunications Law shall enter into force the day after the publication of the Decree with respect to users of mobile telephony in any of its modalities acquired prior to the entry into force of this Decree and, with respect to new mobile telephony users, in terms of the fourth transitory article of the decree of reforms to said law, published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on February 9, 2009. Ninth. The Federal Institute of Public Defense of the Judicial Branch of the Federation, within the scope of its competence, shall establish specialized areas in defense of victims of kidnapping, in the terms provided in the law on the matter.

Tenth. For the establishment and organization of the specialized units against kidnapping referred to in this Law, the federative entities shall have the resources of the Fund for Support of Public Security that they have respectively received.

Eleventh. The H. Congress of the Union may empower the victims or offended parties by the crimes provided for in the General Law to Prevent and Punish Crimes in the Matter of Kidnapping, Regulatory of article 73 section XXI of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States, to exercise the right with respect to the exercise of criminal action before the judicial authority for the crime of kidnapping, in the law on the matter to be issued for that purpose.

Twelfth. Within a period of one hundred eighty days counted from the entry into force of this Law, the necessary adjustments shall be made to the applicable provisions so that the corresponding resources are allocated to the Fund referred to in article 38 of the General Law to Prevent and Punish Crimes in the Matter of Kidnapping, Regulatory of section XXI of article 73 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States.

Mexico City, as of October 7, 2010.- Sen. Manlio Fabio Beltrones Rivera, President.- Rep. Jorge Carlos Ramírez Marín, President.- Sen. Martha Leticia Sosa Govea, Secretary.- Rep. María de Jesús Aguirre Maldonado, Secretary.- Signatures."

In compliance with the provisions of section I of Article 89 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States, and for its due publication and observance, I issue this Decree at the Residence of the Federal Executive Power, in Mexico City, Federal District, as of November 29, 2010.- Felipe de Jesús Calderón Hinojosa.- Signature.- The Secretary of the Interior, José Francisco Blake Mora.- Signature.

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform OGF 08-05-2023

DECREE reforming, adding and repealing various provisions of the Federal Economic

Competition Law, the Federal Penal Code and the Fiscal Code of the

Federation.

Published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on May 10, 2011

ARTICLE SECOND. An article 254 bis is ADDED and article 253 section I, subsection d) is REPEALED, all of the Federal Penal Code, to read as follows:

TRANSITORY ARTICLES

###### ARTICLE FIRST. This decree shall enter into force on the day following its publication in the

Official Gazette of the Federation, except as provided in the following third, fourth and sixth transitory articles.

ARTICLE SECOND. Within a period not exceeding six months from the entry into force of this decree, the Plenary will publish the technical criteria referred to in Article 24, section XVIII bis, subsections a) through j) of the Federal Economic Competition Law.

ARTICLE THIRD. Article 28, first and second paragraphs, of the Federal Economic Competition Law shall enter into force once the current term of the President of the Commission concludes. ARTICLE FOURTH. Investigations, verification visits, proceedings and any other matter that are in process at the entry into force of this Decree shall be processed in accordance with the provisions in force at the time of their commencement.

Infractions and crimes committed prior to the date of entry into force of this Decree shall be sanctioned in accordance with the Law in force at the time of their commission.

ARTICLE FIFTH. The resources necessary for the implementation of this Decree shall be charged to the authorized budget of the Federal Competition Commission.

ARTICLE SIXTH. The amendment to Article 39 of the Federal Economic Competition Law shall enter into force once the specialized courts on economic competition matters are established by the Federal Judicial Branch and the procedural rules applicable to the ordinary administrative trial are issued in the corresponding legal provisions, within a period not exceeding 180 calendar days from the entry into force of this Decree.

ARTICLE SEVENTH. Provisions that oppose this Decree are hereby repealed.

Mexico City, D.F., April 28, 2011.- Rep. Jorge Carlos Ramirez Marin, President.- Sen. Manlio Fabio Beltrones Rivera, President.- Rep. Maria de Jesus Aguirre Maldonado, Secretary.- Sen. Martha Leticia Sosa Govea, Secretary.- Signatures."

TRANSITORY PROVISIONS OF THE DECREE ISSUING THE MIGRATION LAW AND

AMENDING, REPEALING AND ADDING VARIOUS PROVISIONS OF THE GENERAL

POPULATION LAW, THE FEDERAL PENAL CODE, THE FEDERAL CODE OF

FIRST. This Decree shall enter into force on the day following its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.

Article Fourth. Paragraphs three and four are added to article 51 of the Federal Penal Code, to read as follows:

Transitory Provisions

First.- This Decree shall enter into force the day following its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.

Second.- The Head of the Federal Executive Power shall have a term of 180 calendar days to issue the amendments to the Regulations of the Organic Law of the Office of the Attorney General of the Republic, in order to establish the administrative unit with jurisdiction over federal crimes committed against any journalist, person or facility that undermine the right to information or the freedoms of expression or press, as well as for those common jurisdiction crimes that are assumed in accordance with the provisions of articles 73, section XXI, second paragraph, of the Constitution and 10 of the Federal Code of Criminal Procedures.

Third.- Until the amendments to the Regulations of the Organic Law of the Office of the Attorney General of the Republic referred to in the preceding transitory article are issued, the Special Prosecutor's Office for Attention to Crimes Committed against Freedom of Expression of the Office of the Attorney General of the Republic shall exercise the powers established in this Decree.

Mexico City, Federal District, April 25, 2013.- Sen. Ernesto Cordero Arroyo, President.- Rep. Francisco Arroyo Vieyra, President.- Sen. Rosa Adriana Díaz Lizama, Secretary.- Rep. Angel Cedillo Hernández, Secretary.- Signatures."

Published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on June 7, 2013

ARTICLE SEVENTH.- The first paragraph, section I and the fifth paragraph of article 421 are hereby reformed; and a sixth paragraph is added to article 421 of the Federal Penal Code, to read as follows:

TRANSITORY PROVISIONS

FIRST.- This Decree shall enter into force thirty days following its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.

SECOND.- The Environmental Liability Fund must be established and its bases and operating rules must be prepared and approved within one hundred eighty days following the entry into force of this Decree.

THIRD.- The District Courts specialized in environmental matters must be established within a maximum term of two years counted from the entry into force of this Decree. Specialized jurisdiction in environmental matters may be granted to the District Courts in operation in each jurisdictional circuit or according to the provisions of the Federal Judiciary Council, without this implying the creation of new jurisdictional bodies. The personnel of each of said District Courts shall receive specialized training in environmental regulations. Mexico City, Federal District, April 25, 2013.- Sen. United Mexican States.

Published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on March 14, 2014

###### FIRST ARTICLE.- Articles 139; 148 Bis; 148 Quater; 170 and 400 Bis are reformed; and

CHAPTER VI BIS entitled "Terrorism Financing" is added to Title One of Book Two with articles 139 Quater and 139 Quinquies; article 368 Quinquies, and articles 400 Bis 1, within CHAPTER II, Title Twenty-Third, Book Two, which refers to "Operations with Resources of Illicit Proceeds", of the Federal Penal Code, to read as follows:

Transitory Provisions

FIRST. This Decree shall enter into force on the day following its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.

SECOND. The legal provisions that establish measures and procedures to prevent and detect acts, omissions or operations of those established in article 400 Bis, of the Federal Penal Code, as well as acts or omissions that could favor, provide aid, assistance or cooperation of any kind for the commission of the crimes provided for in articles 139 or 148 Bis of the same Code established in other laws, shall be understood henceforth also applicable as pertinent, for the prevention, identification and prosecution of the conducts provided for in article 139 Quater of the aforementioned Federal Penal Code.

THIRD. Those who have committed any of the crimes provided for in articles 139; 148 Bis; 148 Quater; 170 and 400 Bis, which are reformed by virtue of this Decree prior to its entry into force, including those who are within any stage of the procedures provided for in the Federal Code of Criminal Procedures, shall continue to be subject to the provisions in force at the time the crime was committed and those sentenced must serve their sentences and penalties under the terms in which they were imposed.

Mexico City, February 11, 2014.- Rep. Ricardo Anaya Cortés, President.- Sen. Raúl Cervantes Andrade, President.- Rep. Merilyn Gómez Pozos, Secretary.- Sen. Lilia Guadalupe Merodio Reza, Secretary.- Signatures."

276 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform OGF 05-08-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

DECREE issuing the Federal Economic Competition Law and reforming and adding various articles of the Federal Penal Code.

Published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on May 23, 2014

SECOND ARTICLE.- Article 254 bis is reformed and an article 254 bis 1 is added to the Federal Penal Code, to read as follows:

TRANSITORY PROVISIONS

First. This Decree shall enter into force forty-five calendar days following its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.

Second. The Federal Economic Competition Law published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on December 24, 1992 is repealed.

The proceedings that are in process upon the entry into force of this Decree, shall be processed according to the provisions in force at the time of their initiation, before the administrative units established by the organic statute issued pursuant to the following transitory provision. The resolutions issued in such proceedings may only be challenged through amparo proceedings, in accordance with the provisions of article 28 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States.

Third. For the case of the appointment of the first head as Investigating Authority, with regard to the requirement established in section VII of article 31 of this Law, it must be observed in the sense that during the three years prior to his appointment, he must not have held any employment, position or management function or have represented in any way the interests of any Economic Agent that has been subject to any of the proceedings provided for in the Federal Economic Competition Law published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on December 24, 1992, in force until before the entry into force of this Decree.

Fourth. The Plenary of the Commission must adapt its Organic Statute to the provisions of this Decree within a period that shall not exceed thirty days from its entry into force. While the adaptation is carried out, the Organic Statute in force at the time of entry into force of this Decree shall continue to apply, insofar as it does not conflict with it.

Fifth. Within thirty days following the entry into force of this Decree, the Chamber of Deputies of the Congress of the Union must integrate a technical working group, for the purpose of analyzing and formulating proposals for adjustments to the criminal legislation in force, in the matter subject of this Decree. The working group must present the corresponding proposals within sixty days following its installation.

Sixth. Within a period not exceeding six months from the entry into force of this Decree, the Plenary shall publish the Regulatory Provisions referred to in article 12, section XXII of the Federal Economic Competition Law.

Seventh. Within one year from the entry into force of this Decree, the Congress of the Union must make adaptations to the legal framework in order to harmonize it with the principles regarding competition and free competition provided for in article 28 of the Constitution. For this purpose, the Congress of the Union may request an opinion from the Federal Economic Competition Commission.

DECREE issuing the Federal Law of Telecommunications and Broadcasting, and the Law of the Public Broadcasting System of the Mexican State; and reforming, adding and repealing various provisions on telecommunications and broadcasting.

Published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on July 14, 2014

ARTICLE NINTH.- The first paragraph of article 140 is reformed; article 145; section II of


This excerpt sits under: FEDERAL PENAL CODE > First Book > single chapter

###### article 167; article 178 Bis; the first paragraph of article 212; section III of article 214; and an article 166 Bis is added to the Federal Penal Code, to read as follows:

TRANSITORY PROVISIONS

FIRST. This Decree shall enter into force thirty calendar days following its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation, without prejudice to the provisions set forth in the following transitory articles. SECOND. The Federal Telecommunications Law and the Federal Radio and

Television Law are repealed. Those provisions of the General Communications Routes Law that are contrary to the provisions of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law issued by virtue of this Decree are rendered without effect. THIRD. The regulatory and administrative provisions and Mexican official standards in force shall continue to apply until such time as new regulations are issued to replace them, except insofar as they are contrary to the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law issued by virtue of this Decree. FOURTH. The Federal Telecommunications Institute must adapt its organic statute to the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law within sixty calendar days following the entry into force of this Decree. FIFTH. The Federal Executive must issue, within one hundred eighty calendar days following the issuance of this Decree, the regulatory provisions and guidelines on content matters established in the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law issued by virtue of this Decree. Broadcasting and restricted television or audio concessionaires may not promote video games that have not been classified in accordance with applicable regulations, which must be issued by the Federal Executive within the period referred to in the preceding paragraph. SIXTH. The attention, processing and resolution of matters and proceedings that have commenced prior to the entry into force of this Decree shall be carried out under the terms established in the Seventh Transitory Article of the Decree reforming and adding various provisions of articles 6, 7, 27, 28, 73, 78, 94 and 105 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States on telecommunications matters, published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on June 11, 2013. The foregoing is without prejudice to the provisions of the Twentieth Transitory Article of this Decree. SEVENTH. Without prejudice to the provisions of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law issued by virtue of the Decree, in the law and in the regulations issued for this purpose by the Federal Telecommunications Institute, concessions and permits granted prior to the entry into force of this Decree shall be maintained under the terms and conditions set forth in the respective titles or 280 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services permits until their termination, unless authorization is obtained to provide services additional to those that are the object of their concession or they have transitioned to the single concession provided for in the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law, in which case, the terms and conditions established by the Federal Telecommunications Institute shall apply. In the case of radioelectric spectrum concessions, the term of the concession, the authorized coverage and the number of Megahertz granted may not be modified, nor may the conditions to do or not to do provided in the original concession title and that were determinant for the granting of the concession be modified. Requests for extension of sound broadcasting concessions submitted prior to the termination date of the original term established in the corresponding titles shall be resolved in accordance with the provisions of article 114 of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law, without the period provided for the extension request in question being applicable. Paragraph added DOF 15-06-2018

EIGHTH. Except as provided in the Tenth and Eleventh Transitory Articles of this Decree, current concessionaires may obtain authorization from the Federal Telecommunications Institute to provide services additional to those that are the object of their concession or to transition to the single concession, provided that they are in compliance with the obligations provided in the laws and in their concession titles. Concessionaires holding radioelectric spectrum concessions must pay the corresponding considerations under the terms of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law. Concessionaires holding multiple concession titles may, in addition to being able to transition to the single concession, consolidate their titles into a single concession. NINTH. As long as there is a preponderant economic agent in the telecommunications and broadcasting sectors, in order to promote competition and develop viable competitors in the long term, concentrations carried out between economic agents holding concessions, nor cessions of concession and changes of control deriving therefrom, that meet the following requirements, shall not require authorization from the Federal Telecommunications Institute:

- a. They generate a sectoral reduction of the Dominance Index "ID", provided that the Hirschman-Herfindahl index "IHH" does not increase by more than two hundred points;
- b. They result in the economic agent having a sectoral participation percentage of less than twenty percent;
- c. That the preponderant economic agent in the sector in which the concentration takes place does not participate in said concentration, and
- d. They do not have the effect of diminishing, damaging or impeding free competition and concurrence in the corresponding sector. The Hirschman-Herfindahl Index "IHH" means the sum of the squares of the participation of each economic agent (IHH=i qi2), in the corresponding sector, measured in the case of the telecommunications sector based on the indicator of number of subscribers and users of telecommunications services, and for the broadcasting sector based on audience. This index can take values between zero and ten thousand. To calculate the Dominance Index "ID", the percentage contribution hi of each economic agent to the IHH index defined in the preceding paragraph will first be determined (hi = 100xqi2/IHH). Then the 281 of 337

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services value of ID will be calculated by applying the Hirschman-Herfindahl formula, but now using the contributions hi instead of the participations qi (that is, ID=i hi2). This index also varies between zero and ten thousand. Economic agents must submit to the Federal Telecommunications Institute, within 10 days following the concentration, a written notice that will contain the information referred to in article 89 of the Federal Economic Competition Law referred to the corresponding sector as well as the elements of conviction demonstrating that the concentration complies with the preceding subsections. The Institute shall investigate such concentrations within a period not exceeding ninety calendar days and if it finds that there is substantial power in the telecommunications network market providing voice, data or video services or in the radio and television market according to the corresponding sector, it may impose the necessary measures to protect and promote free competition and concurrence in said market, in accordance with the provisions of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law and the Federal Economic Competition Law without prejudice to the concentrations referred to in this article. The measures imposed by the Institute shall be extinguished once the preponderant economic agents are authorized to provide additional services. TENTH. Preponderant economic agents and concessionaires whose concession titles contain any express prohibition or restriction to provide specific services, prior to initiating the procedure to obtain authorization to provide additional services, shall accredit before the Federal Telecommunications Institute and the latter shall supervise effective compliance with the obligations provided in the Decree reforming and adding various provisions of articles 6, 7, 27, 28, 73, 78, 94 and 105 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States, on telecommunications matters, published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on June 11, 2013, of the Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law, as well as of the Federal Economic Competition Law, their concession titles and applicable administrative provisions, in accordance with the following:

- I. Preponderant economic agents must prove to the Federal Telecommunications Institute that they are in effective compliance with the foregoing and with the measures issued by the Federal Telecommunications Institute itself referred to in sections III and IV of the Eighth Transitory Article of the aforementioned Decree. For this purpose, the Federal Telecommunications Institute shall establish the form and terms for submitting the respective information and documentation;
- II. The preponderant economic agent must be in effective compliance with the measures referred to in section I above for at least eighteen months continuously;
- III. After the period referred to in the preceding section has elapsed and provided that it continues in compliance with the provisions of section I above, the Federal Telecommunications Institute shall resolve and issue an opinion certifying that effective compliance with the referenced obligations was given, and
- IV. Once the concessionaire has obtained the compliance certification, it may request authorization from the Federal Telecommunications Institute for the additional service. The provisions of this article shall also apply in the event that the respective agents and concessionaires opt to transition to the single concession. 282 of 337

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

The provisions of this article shall not apply after five years have elapsed from the entry into force of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law, provided that the preponderant economic agent in the telecommunications sector is in compliance with the Eighth Transitory Article of this Decree, with the measures that have been imposed upon it pursuant to the provisions of sections III and IV of the Eighth Transitory Article of the Decree reforming and adding various provisions of articles 6, 7, 27, 28, 73, 78, 94 and 105 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States, on telecommunications matters, published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on June 11, 2013, and with those that have been imposed upon it by the Federal Telecommunications Institute under the terms of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law. ELEVENTH. The processing of the request referred to in the preceding article shall be subject to the following:

- I. Preponderant economic agents and concessionaires whose concession titles contain any express prohibition or restriction to provide specific services, must comply with the provisions of the guidelines of the Federal Telecommunications Institute under the terms of the Fourth Transitory Article of the Decree reforming and adding various provisions of articles 6, 7, 27, 28, 73, 78, 94 and 105 of the Constitution on telecommunications matters, published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on June 11, 2013;
- II. When submitting the request, said agents and concessionaires must attach the compliance opinion referred to in section III of the preceding article, submit the information determined by the Federal Telecommunications Institute regarding the services they intend to provide;
- III. The Federal Telecommunications Institute shall resolve on the merits of the request within sixty calendar days following its submission, based on the general guidelines it issues for this purpose and shall determine the considerations that apply. Once the period indicated in the preceding paragraph has elapsed without the Institute having resolved the corresponding request, it shall be deemed denied, and

IV. In processing the request, the Federal Telecommunications Institute must ensure that granting the authorization does not generate adverse effects on competition and free concurrence. It shall be understood that adverse effects on competition and free concurrence are generated, among other factors considered by the Federal Telecommunications Institute, when:

- a. Said authorization may have the effect of increasing the participation in the corresponding sector of the preponderant economic agent or of the economic interest group to which the concessionaires whose concession titles contain a prohibition or restriction to provide specific services belong, with respect to the participation determined by the Federal Telecommunications Institute in the resolution by which it was declared a preponderant economic agent in the corresponding sector.
- b. The authorization of additional services has the effect of conferring substantial power in the relevant market to any of the concessionaires or members of the preponderant economic agent or of the concessionaires whose concession titles contain a prohibition or restriction to provide specific services in the corresponding sector. 283 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

The provisions of this article shall apply in the event that the respective agents and concessionaires opt to transition to the single concession, and shall be independent of the economic sanctions that apply pursuant to the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law. TWELFTH. The preponderant economic agent in the telecommunications sector may opt at any time for the scheme provided in article 276 of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law or exercise the right established by this article. The preponderant economic agent in the telecommunications sector may submit to the Federal Telecommunications Institute a plan based on a real, concrete situation and regarding specific persons, that includes where applicable, structural separation, total or partial divestiture of assets, rights, corporate shares or shares or any combination of the foregoing options in order to reduce its national participation below fifty percent of the telecommunications sector referred to in section III of the Eighth Transitory Article of the Decree reforming and adding various provisions of articles 6, 7, 27, 28, 73, 78, 94 and 105 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States, on telecommunications matters, published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on June 11, 2013, in accordance with the variables and measurement parameters used by the Federal Telecommunications Institute in the corresponding preponderance declaration, and provided that the execution of said plan generates effective competition conditions in the markets that comprise said sector in accordance with the Federal Economic Competition Law. In the event that the preponderant economic agent exercises this option, the following shall apply:

- I. When submitting the plan referred to in the preceding paragraph, the preponderant economic agent must state in writing that it adheres to the provisions of this article and that it accepts its terms and conditions; likewise it must attach the necessary information and documentation that allows the Federal Telecommunications Institute to know and analyze the proposed plan;
- II. If the Federal Telecommunications Institute considers that the information submitted is insufficient, within 20 business days following submission of the plan, it shall notify the preponderant economic agent to submit the missing information within 20 business days. In the event that the preponderant economic agent does not respond to the notification within the indicated period or that in the Institute's judgment the documentation or information submitted is not sufficient or suitable for analyzing the proposed plan, a second notification may be made to it under the terms indicated above and in the event that it does not comply with this latter notification the plan shall be deemed not submitted, without prejudice to the economic agent being able to submit a new plan proposal under the terms of this article;
- III. Once the notification has been addressed in the terms formulated, the Federal Telecommunications Institute shall analyze, evaluate and, where appropriate, approve the proposed plan within one hundred twenty calendar days following. If the Institute deems it necessary it may extend said period up to two times and for up to ninety calendar days each. To approve said plan the Federal Telecommunications Institute must determine that it effectively reduces the national participation of the preponderant economic agent below fifty percent in the telecommunications sector referred to in section III of the Eighth Transitory Article of the aforementioned Decree, that it generates effective competition conditions in the markets that comprise said sector under the terms of the Federal Economic Competition Law and that it does not have the purpose or effect of affecting or reducing existing social coverage. The plan must result in the participation in the sector that the preponderant agent decreases being transferred to one or more other economic agents different from and independent of the preponderant economic agent. Upon approving the plan, the Federal Telecommunications Institute must 284 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services ensure the effective separation and independence of those agents and must establish the terms and conditions necessary for that situation to be duly safeguarded; IV. In the event that the Federal Telecommunications Institute approves the plan, the preponderant economic agent in the telecommunications sector shall have a period of up to ten business days to state that it accepts the plan and expressly consents to the rates derived from the application of subsections a) and b) of the second paragraph of article 131 of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law, and sections VI to VIII of this article. Once the plan is accepted by the preponderant economic agent, it may not be modified and must be executed in its terms, without said agent being able to exercise again the benefit granted by this article and without prejudice to being able to opt for the provisions of article 276 of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law; V. The plan must be executed during the 365 calendar days following its acceptance under the terms of section IV. The economic agents involved in the plan must report with the frequency established by the Federal Telecommunications Institute on the plan execution process. In the event that the preponderant economic agent proves that the failure to comply with the plan within the referred period is due to causes not attributable to it, it may request an extension from the Federal Telecommunications Institute, which may be granted for a period of up to 120 calendar days, only once and provided that said causes are duly justified; VI. From the date on which the preponderant economic agent in the telecommunications sector has accepted the plan and during the period referred to in the preceding section, the reciprocal traffic compensation agreements referred to in the first paragraph of article 131 of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law shall be applied provisionally between the preponderant economic agent in the telecommunications sector and the other concessionaires, and the rates derived from the application of subsections a) and b) of the second paragraph of said article shall be suspended between them; VII. The Federal Telecommunications Institute shall certify that the plan has been effectively executed within the period indicated in section V of this article. For this purpose, within 5 business days following the end of the execution period or, where applicable, at the end of the corresponding extension, the Federal Telecommunications Institute must initiate studies demonstrating that its execution generated effective competition conditions in the markets that comprise the telecommunications sector, in accordance with the Federal Economic Competition Law. Once the certification referred to in the preceding paragraph is granted, the traffic compensation agreements referred to in the first paragraph of article 131 of the cited Law shall apply generally to all concessionaires; VIII. In the event that the plan is not executed within the period referred to in section V or, where applicable, at the end of the corresponding extension, or the Federal Telecommunications Institute denies the certification referred to in the preceding section or determines that full compliance with said plan was not given in the approved terms, the reciprocal traffic compensation agreements and the suspension of rates referred to in subsections a) and b) of article 131 of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law, between the preponderant economic agent in the telecommunications sector and the other concessionaires, shall be rendered without effect, and their application shall be retroactive to the date on which the suspension began, said agent being required to restore to the other concessionaires the amounts corresponding to the application of the cited rates. In this case, the cited concessionaires may offset the amounts to be restored against other amounts owed by them to the preponderant economic agent; IX. The Federal Telecommunications Institute shall authorize the economic agent that proposed the plan and the resulting economic agents or that are part of said plan, to provide services 285 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services additional to those that are the object of their concession or their transition to the single concession model, from the time it certifies that the plan has been effectively executed and provided that the execution of said plan generates effective competition conditions in the markets that comprise the telecommunications sector in accordance with the Federal Economic Competition Law; X. Once the Federal Telecommunications Institute certifies that the approved plan has been effectively executed, it shall proceed to extinguish:

- a. The resolutions by which it has determined the economic agent as preponderant in the telecommunications sector as well as the asymmetric measures it has imposed upon it under the terms of the provisions of section III and IV of the Eighth Article of the aforementioned Decree, and
- b. The resolutions by which it has determined the economic agent with substantial power in any market, as well as the specific measures it has imposed upon it. THIRTEENTH. The Federal Executive through the Secretariat of Communications and Transportation shall carry out actions aimed at installing the shared public telecommunications network referred to in the Sixteenth transitory article of the Decree reforming and adding various provisions of articles 6, 7, 27, 28, 73, 78, 94 and 105 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States, on telecommunications matters, published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on June 11, 2013. In the event that the Federal Executive requires frequency bands of the spectrum freed by the transition to Digital Terrestrial Television (700 MHz band) to grow and strengthen the shared network indicated in the preceding paragraph, the Federal Telecommunications Institute shall grant them directly, provided that said network remains under the control of a public entity or agency or under a public-private partnership scheme. FOURTEENTH. The Federal Telecommunications Institute must implement a professional service system within one hundred eighty calendar days following the entry into force of this Decree, which must contain, among other aspects, recognition of the rights of workers of the Federal Telecommunications Commission who are certified as professional service workers. FIFTEENTH. The Federal Telecommunications Institute must install its Advisory Council within one hundred eighty calendar days following the entry into force of this Decree. SIXTEENTH. The Secretariat of Communications and Transportation must establish mechanisms to carry out the coordination provided in article 9, section V of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law, within one hundred eighty calendar days following the entry into force of this Decree. SEVENTEENTH. Broadcasting permits that are in force or in the renewal process upon the entry into force of this Decree must transition to the corresponding concession regime within one year following the entry into force of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law, under the terms established by the Institute. Permits that have been granted to the powers of the Union, of the states, the governing bodies of the Federal District, the municipalities, autonomous constitutional bodies and higher education institutions of a public nature must transition to the public use concession regime, while the rest of the permits granted must do so to the social use concession regime. 286 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

To transition to the corresponding concession regime, permittees must submit an application to the Federal Telecommunications Institute, which shall resolve accordingly within ninety business days. While the transition takes place, said permits shall be governed by the provisions of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law for public or social use concessions, as applicable. Failure to comply with this article shall result in the permits concluding their term. EIGHTEENTH. The Federal Telecommunications Institute must issue within one hundred eighty days following the entry into force of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law, the work program to reorganize the radioelectric spectrum for radio and television stations referred to in subsection b) of section V of the Seventeenth transitory article of the Decree reforming and adding various provisions of articles 6, 7, 27, 28, 73, 78, 94 and 105 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States, on telecommunications matters, published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on June 11, 2013. In determining the work program, the Institute shall seek the development of the relevant radio market, the migration of the largest possible number of concessionaire stations from the AM to FM band, the strengthening of competition conditions and continuity in the provision of services. NINETEENTH. The digital terrestrial transition shall culminate on December 31, 2015. The Federal Executive, through the Secretariat of Communications and Transportation, shall implement programs and actions linked to the digital terrestrial television transition policy, for the delivery or distribution of receiving equipment or decoders referred to in the third paragraph of the Fifth transitory article of the Decree reforming and adding various provisions of articles 6, 7, 27, 28, 73, 78, 94 and 105 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States, on telecommunications matters, published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on June 11, 2013. The Federal Telecommunications Institute must conclude the transmission of analog broadcast television signals throughout the country, no later than December 31, 2015, once a ninety percent penetration level of low-income households defined by the Secretariat of Social Development is reached, with receivers or decoders suitable for receiving digital broadcast television signals. For the foregoing, the Federal Telecommunications Institute must conclude analog broadcast television signals in advance of December 31, 2015, by coverage area of said signals, once the penetration level referred to in the preceding paragraph is reached in the corresponding area. The Secretariat of Communications and Transportation and the Federal Telecommunications Institute shall conduct dissemination campaigns for the delivery or distribution of equipment and for the conclusion of analog television signal transmission, respectively. Broadcast television concessionaires and permittees shall be required to make all necessary investments and installations to transition to digital terrestrial television no later than December 31, 2015. The Federal Telecommunications Institute shall monitor due compliance with the cited obligation. Those permittees or concessionaires of public or social use, including community and indigenous ones, that provide the broadcasting service who are not in a position to begin digital transmissions by December 31, 2015, must, prior to that date, give notice to the Federal Telecommunications Institute, under the terms provided in article 157 of the Federal

287 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law in order to be authorized temporary suspension of their transmissions or, where applicable, reduce their apparent radiated power so that the continuity program referred to in the following paragraph of this article applies to them. The periods authorized by the Institute shall in no case exceed December 31, 2016.Paragraph added DOF 18-12-2015

In the event that, by the dates of early conclusion of analog broadcast television signals by coverage area or as of December 31, 2015, current broadcast television stations with an apparent radiated power of less than or equal to 1 kW for VHF channels and 10 kW for UHF channels are not transmitting terrestrial digital television signals and/or the penetration level indicated in the third and fourth paragraphs of this article has not been reached, whether in a region, locality or throughout the country; the Federal Telecommunications Institute shall establish a program so that the population continues to receive this public service of general interest in the respective areas, while digital transmissions are initiated and/or the penetration levels indicated in this article are reached. The station holders must make the necessary investments and installations in accordance with the deadlines provided in the program. In no case shall the actions derived from this program exceed December 31, 2016. Paragraph amended DOF 18-12-2015

Legal, administrative or regulatory provisions that are contrary to this transitory provision are hereby repealed. TWENTIETH. The Federal Telecommunications Institute shall apply article 131 of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law and others that are applicable in matters of interconnection in terms thereof, and shall guarantee due compliance with the obligations established in said precepts, which shall be enforceable without prejudice to and independent of whether at the entry into force of the Law, it had already determined the existence of a preponderant economic agent and imposed necessary measures to prevent competition and free concurrence from being affected in accordance with section III of Transitory Article Eighth of the Decree reforming and adding various provisions of articles 6o., 7o., 27, 28, 73, 78, 94 and 105 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States, in matters of telecommunications, published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on June 11, 2013. For purposes of the provisions of subsection b) of article 131 of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law, and until the concessionaires referred to in that subsection agree on the corresponding interconnection tariffs or, as the case may be, the Institute resolves any dispute regarding said tariffs, those currently in effect shall remain in force, except in the case of the economic agent referred to in the second paragraph of article 131 of the aforementioned Law, to which subsection a) of the same article shall be applicable. TWENTY-FIRST. For the attention, promotion and supervision of user rights provided for in the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law, and in the Federal Consumer Protection Law, PROFECO must create a specialized area with a level no lower than Deputy Attorney General's Office, as well as the necessary structure for this purpose, in accordance with the budget approved by the Chamber of Deputies for such purpose. TWENTY-SECOND. The Federal Telecommunications Institute must issue the general administrative provisions referred to in Title Eight of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law, within a maximum period of ninety calendar days counted from the entry into force of this Decree. TWENTY-THIRD. The budgetary impact generated by the entry into force of this Decree in matters of personal services, as well as the establishment of new powers 288 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services and activities in charge of the Federal Telecommunications Institute, shall be covered by the budget annually approved by the Chamber of Deputies to said body. TWENTY-FOURTH. In accordance with the provisions of Transitory Articles Fifteenth, Sixteenth and Seventeenth of the Decree reforming and adding various provisions of articles 6o., 7o., 27, 28, 73, 78, 94 and 105 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States, in matters of telecommunications, published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on June 11, 2013, the last paragraph of article 14 of the Law of Income of the Federation for Fiscal Year 2014 is hereby repealed. TWENTY-FIFTH. The provisions of section V of article 118 of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law shall enter into force on January 1, 2015, so that from that date concessionaires of public telecommunications networks that provide fixed, mobile or both services may not make national long distance charges to their users for calls they make to any national destination. Without prejudice to the foregoing, concessionaires must consolidate all existing local service areas in the country in accordance with the guidelines issued by the Federal Telecommunications Institute for such purpose. Each concessionaire must assume the costs arising from said consolidation. Likewise, the Federal Telecommunications Institute, within one hundred eighty days following the entry into force of this Decree, must define the interconnection points to the public telecommunications network of the preponderant economic agent or with substantial power. Administrative resolutions that have been issued shall be without effect insofar as they are contrary to the provisions of this transitory provision. Concessionaires shall maintain the numbering that has been assigned to them in order to use it for intelligent network services in their modalities of reverse charging and other special services, such as 900 numbers. TWENTY-SIXTH. The Federal Executive must submit to the Senate of the Republic or, as the case may be, to the Permanent Commission, the proposal for designation of the President of the Public Broadcasting System of the Mexican State, within thirty calendar days following the entry into force of this Decree. The Senate or, as the case may be, the Permanent Commission, must designate the President of the System within thirty calendar days following the one in which it receives the proposal from the Federal Executive. TWENTY-SEVENTH. The representatives of the Secretariats of State who comprise the Board of

Governors of the Public System of the Mexican State must be designated within sixty calendar days following the entry into force of this Decree. TWENTY-EIGHTH. The designation of the members of the Citizen Council of the Public Broadcasting System of the Mexican State must be carried out within sixty calendar days following the entry into force of this Decree. TWENTY-NINTH. The President of the Public Broadcasting System of the Mexican State shall submit to the Board of Governors, for its approval, the draft Organic Statute, within ninety calendar days following his appointment. 289 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

THIRTIETH. From the entry into force of this Decree, the decentralized body called Audiovisual Media Promotion Body is transformed into the Public Broadcasting System of the Mexican State, which shall have the human, budgetary, financial and material resources of the aforementioned body. While the Organic Statute of the Public Broadcasting System of the Mexican State is issued, the Organic Statute of the Audiovisual Media Promotion Body shall continue to be applied, insofar as it does not oppose the Law of the Public Broadcasting System of the Mexican State. The labor rights of the personnel of the Audiovisual Media Promotion Body shall be respected in accordance with the law. THIRTY-FIRST. The human, budgetary, financial and material resources of the

Audiovisual Media Promotion Body shall become part of the Public Broadcasting System of the Mexican State once its President is appointed, without prejudice to the labor rights of its workers. THIRTY-SECOND. The Secretariat of the Interior must coordinate with the corresponding authorities for the exercise of the powers that the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law establishes in matters of monitoring. The Chamber of Deputies must allocate the necessary resources to guarantee the adequate exercise of the powers referred to in this transitory provision. THIRTY-THIRD. The Federal Telecommunications Institute shall issue the guidelines referred to in section III of article 158 of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law, within a period of no more than 180 calendar days counted from the day following the entry into force of this Decree. THIRTY-FOURTH. The Chamber of Deputies must allocate to the Public Broadcasting System of the Mexican State economic resources in accordance with its objectives and functions, for which it must consider:

- I. Its growth plans; - II. Its operating expenses, and
- III. Its financial balance. THIRTY-FIFTH. With the exception of the provisions of Transitory Article Twentieth, by which the Federal Telecommunications Institute is obligated to apply article 131 of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law issued by virtue of this Decree and others that are applicable in matters of interconnection in terms thereof, the administrative resolutions that the Federal Telecommunications Institute had issued prior to the entry into force of this Decree in matters of preponderance shall continue to have all their effects. THIRTY-SIXTH. The Federal Telecommunications Institute within 180 days after the entry into force of this Decree, must carry out the corresponding studies to analyze whether it is necessary to establish mechanisms that promote and encourage concessionaires to include a programming block aimed at children in which culture, sports, environmental conservation, respect for human rights, the best interests of children, gender equality and non-discrimination are promoted. 290 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

THIRTY-SEVENTH. For purposes of the law enforcement authorities referred to in section I of article 190 of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law, the provisions of the Federal Telecommunications Law in matters of real-time geographic location shall remain in force until the National Code of Criminal Procedures enters into force. THIRTY-EIGHTH. The Federal Telecommunications Institute must issue within sixty business days following the entry into force of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law, the necessary administrative rules that eliminate requirements that may delay or prevent number portability and, as the case may be, promote that it be done through electronic means. The rules referred to in the preceding paragraph must guarantee effective portability and that it be carried out within a period of no more than 24 hours from the request made by the holder of the respective number. To carry out said portability, only the identification of the holder and the manifestation of the user's will shall be necessary. In the case of legal entities, the procedure must be carried out by the legal representative or attorney-in-fact who proves his personality in terms of the applicable regulations. THIRTY-NINTH. For purposes of the provisions of article 264 of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law, the Federal Telecommunications Institute shall initiate, without prejudice to the provisions of Transitory Article Ninth of this Decree, within thirty calendar days after its entry into force, the corresponding investigation procedures in terms of the Federal Economic Competition Law, in order to determine the existence of economic agents with substantial power in any of the relevant markets of the telecommunications and broadcasting sectors, among which the national market for audio and video associated through public telecommunications networks must be included and, as the case may be, impose the corresponding measures. FORTIETH. The preponderant economic agent in the telecommunications sector or the agent with substantial power in the corresponding relevant market shall be obligated to comply with the provisions of articles 138, section VIII, 208 and sections V and VI of article 267 of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law, from its entry into force. FORTY-FIRST. Public higher education institutions that, as of the date of entry into force of this Decree, have broadcasting media referred to in articles 67 section II and 76 section II of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law, shall not receive additional budget for that purpose. FORTY-SECOND. To the concession to install, operate and exploit a public telecommunications network that, in the terms of Transitory Article Fifteenth of the Decree reforming and adding various provisions of articles 6o, 7o, 27, 28, 73, 78, 94 and 105 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States, in matters of telecommunications, must be transferred by the Federal Electricity Commission to Telecommunications of Mexico, the provisions of articles 140 and 144 of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law shall not be applicable, exclusively with respect to those contracts in force as of the date of publication of this Decree that have been entered into between the Federal Electricity Commission and those natural or legal persons who, in accordance with the same Law, are to be considered as end users. Said contracts shall be transferred by the Federal Electricity Commission to Telecommunications of Mexico, together with the corresponding concession title. Telecommunications of Mexico shall transfer the aforementioned contracts in favor of other concessionaires authorized to provide services to end users, within six months following the date on which they have been transferred to it. 291 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

In the event that there is a technical, legal or economic impediment for Telecommunications of

Mexico to transfer the aforementioned contracts, these shall remain in force at most until the date indicated therein for their termination, and they may not be renewed or extended for new periods. FORTY-THIRD. Within a period that shall not exceed 36 months from the entry into force of this Decree, the signals of commercial use concessionaires that transmit broadcast television and that cover more than fifty percent of the national territory must have Mexican sign language or closed captioning in the national language, in the programming they transmit from 06:00 to 24:00 hours, excluding advertising and other cases established by the Federal Telecommunications Institute, taking into account international best practices. Federal public entities that are public use concessionaires of broadcast television shall be subject to the same obligation. FORTY-FOURTH. In relation to the obligations established in matters of accessibility for persons with disabilities referred to in the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law for audience defenders, concessionaires shall have a period of up to ninety calendar days from the entry into force of this Decree to initiate the corresponding adjustments and mechanisms. FORTY-FIFTH. The restriction to access infrastructure sharing of the preponderant economic agent in broadcasting, provided for in section VII of article 266 of the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law, shall not be applicable to the concessionaire or concessionaires resulting from the tender of the new digital open television networks referred to in section II of Transitory Article Eighth of the Decree reforming and adding various provisions of articles 6o., 7o., 27, 28, 73, 78, 94 and 105 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States, in matters of telecommunications. Mexico City, D.F., as of July 08, 2014.- Sen. Raúl Cervantes Andrade, President.- Rep. José González Morfín, President.- Sen. María Elena Barrera Tapia, Secretary.- Rep. DECREE reforming the Nineteenth Transitory Article of the Decree issuing the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law, and the Law of the

Mexican State Public Broadcasting System; and reforming, adding and repealing various provisions on telecommunications and broadcasting, published on July 14, 2014.

Published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on December 18, 2015

###### SOLE ARTICLE.- A seventh paragraph is added, the subsequent ones are moved down, and the

current seventh paragraph of the Nineteenth Transitory Article of the "DECREE ISSUING THE

FEDERAL TELECOMMUNICATIONS AND BROADCASTING LAW, AND THE LAW OF THE PUBLIC

BROADCASTING SYSTEM OF THE MEXICAN STATE; AND VARIOUS PROVISIONS ON

TELECOMMUNICATIONS AND BROADCASTING ARE REFORMED, ADDED AND REPEALED",

PUBLISHED IN THE OFFICIAL GAZETTE OF THE FEDERATION ON JULY 14, 2014, is amended, to

read as follows:

Transitory Provisions

First. This Decree shall enter into force the day following its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.

Second. Upon the entry into force of this Decree, all legal, regulatory and administrative provisions that oppose it are repealed.

Third. From the start of pre-campaigns until election day, the Federal Government and the governments of the states and municipalities shall suspend the distribution or replacement of receiver equipment or decoders, as well as digital television delivery programs carried out in those states where electoral processes are held during 2016. The National Electoral Institute shall verify compliance with this provision and shall apply, as appropriate, the corresponding sanctions. The delivery, distribution or replacement of receiver equipment, decoders, or digital televisions in contravention of the provisions of this article shall be sanctioned in terms of the General Law on Electoral Institutions and Procedures.

Mexico City, December 9, 2015.- Sen. Roberto Gil Zuarth, President.- Rep. José de Jesús Zambrano Grijalva, President.- Sen. Rosa Adriana Díaz Lizama, Secretary.- Rep. Alejandra Noemí Reynoso Sánchez, Secretary.- Signatures."

DECREE issuing the Federal Law to Prevent and Punish Crimes Committed in Hydrocarbon

Matters; and reforming, adding and repealing various provisions of the Federal Code of

Criminal Procedures; the Federal Criminal Code; the Federal Law against Organized Crime;

the Federal Law on Asset Forfeiture, regulating Article 22 of the Political Constitution of the

United Mexican States; the Federal Fiscal Code and the National Code of Criminal

Procedures.

Published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on January 12, 2016

ARTICLE THREE. A third paragraph is added to article 140; a final paragraph is added to article 241 and a third paragraph is added to article 243, and subsection j) of section I of article 253; sections VII and VIII of article 254, and article 368 Quater of the Federal Criminal Code, are repealed, to read as follows:

TRANSITORY PROVISIONS

First.- This Decree shall enter into force the day following its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.

Second.- Criminal proceedings initiated before the entry into force of this Decree shall continue to be processed until their conclusion in accordance with the provisions in force at the time of the commission of the acts that gave rise to them.

Third.- As of the entry into force of this Decree, in the event that the Federal Law to Prevent and Punish Crimes Committed in Hydrocarbon Matters contemplates a legal description of a criminal conduct that in the former Federal Criminal Code or Federal Fiscal Code was contemplated as a crime and by virtue of the present reforms, is denominated, penalized or aggravated in a different manner, provided that the conducts and facts respond to the description now established, the following shall apply:

- I. In initiated proceedings in which accusatory conclusions have not yet been formulated, the Federal Public Prosecutor's Office shall formulate them in accordance with the resulting transposition of the type;
- II. In proceedings pending judgment in first and second instance, the judge or the Court, respectively, may effect the transposition of the type in accordance with the conduct that has been proven and its modalities; and
- III. The executing authority when applying any modality of benefit for the convicted person, shall consider the penalties that have been imposed, based on the transposition of the type, according to the corresponding modalities.

Fourth.- The pecuniary sanctions provided for in the Federal Law to Prevent and Punish Crimes Committed in Hydrocarbon Matters shall be adjusted, as appropriate, to the equivalent unit of measurement and update provided by law in the Mexican criminal system.

Fifth.- The expenditures generated as a result of the entry into force of this Decree for the agencies and entities of the Federal Public Administration, shall be covered with the resources approved by the Chamber of Deputies in the Expenditure Budget of the Federation for the fiscal year of 295 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform OGF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services in question, therefore no additional resources shall be authorized for the current fiscal year and subsequent ones.

Mexico City, December 15, 2015.- Rep. José de Jesús Zambrano Grijalva, President.- Sen. Roberto Gil Zuarth, President.- Rep. Ernestina Godoy Ramos, Secretary.- Sen. María Elena Barrera Tapia, Secretary.- Signatures."

DECREE issuing the National Law on Criminal Enforcement; adding sections XXXV, XXXVI and XXXVII and a fifth paragraph, and reforming the third paragraph of


This excerpt sits under: FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE > Book One > sole chapter

###### Article 225 of the Federal Penal Code.

Published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on June 16, 2016

###### Article First. The National Law of Criminal Execution is hereby issued.

TRANSITORY PROVISIONS

First. This Law shall enter into force on the day following its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.

For the purposes indicated in the third paragraph of the second transitory article of the Decree by which various provisions of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States are reformed and added, published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on June 18, 2008, it is declared that this legislation incorporates the accusatory criminal procedural system and shall enter into force in accordance with the following transitory articles.

Second. Sections III and X and the seventh paragraph of Article 10; Articles 26 and 27, section II of Article 28; section VII of Article 108; Articles 146, 147, 148, 149, 150 and 151 shall enter into force one year after the publication of this Law or on the day following the publication of the Declaration issued for such purpose by the Congress of the Union or the legislatures of the federal entities within the scope of their respective powers, without exceeding November 30, 2017.

Articles 31, 32, 33, 34, 35, 36, 59, 60, 61, 75, 77, 78, 80, 82, 83, 86, 91, 92, 93, 94, 95, 96, 97, 182, 183, 184, 185, 186, 187, 188, 189, 192, 193, 194, 195, 200, 201, 202, 203, 204, 205, 206 and 207 shall enter into force no later than two years after the publication of this Law or on the day following the publication of the Declaration issued for such purpose by the Congress of the Union or the legislatures of the federal entities within the scope of their respective powers, without exceeding November 30, At the Federal level, the Congress of the Union shall issue the Declaration, upon joint request of the Coordinating Council for the Implementation of the Criminal Justice System or the agency that, as the case may be, is in charge of coordinating the consolidation of the Criminal Justice System, and the National Conference of the Penitentiary System.

In the case of the federal entities, the corresponding legislative body shall issue the Declaration upon request of the authority responsible for the implementation of the Criminal Justice System in each of them.

In the federal entities where the new Criminal Justice System is in force, the corresponding legislative body must issue within the following ten days the annex to the Declaration for the commencement of validity of this Law.

Third. As of the entry into force of this Law, the Law Establishing Minimum Standards on Social Rehabilitation of Sentenced Persons and those regulating the execution of criminal sanctions in the federal entities shall be abrogated.

298 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform OGF 05-08-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

Proceedings that are in process at the entry into force of this ordinance shall continue with their processing in accordance with the legislation applicable at the commencement thereof, and shall apply the jurisdictional control mechanisms provided for in this Law, in accordance with the pro persona principle established in Article 1 of the Constitution.

As of the entry into force of this Law, all regulatory provisions that contravene it are hereby repealed.

Fourth. As of the entry into force of this Law, the provisions contained in the Federal Criminal Code and special federal laws relating to partial remission of sentence, preparatory release and substitution of sentence during execution are hereby repealed.

The federal entities must adapt their legislation in order to repeal the provisions relating to partial remission of sentence, preparatory release and substitution of sentence during execution, within the scope of their respective powers.

The federal entities must legislate in their criminal codes on the responsibilities of probation supervisors.

Fifth. Within a period not exceeding one hundred eighty calendar days after the publication of this Decree, the Federation and the federal entities must publish the amendments to their laws that are necessary for the implementation of this Law, as well as the provisions of Article 92, section V regarding social security.

Upon the entry into force of this Law, in those places where its commencement is determined, both at the federal and local levels, the corresponding general administrative provisions must be in place, and the harmonization of methodological, technical and procedural criteria may be provided for, for which purpose the authorities involved may coordinate.

Sixth. The expenditures generated by the entry into force of this Decree for the Judicial Branch of the Federation, the departments and entities of the Federal Public Administration, shall be covered by their budgets for the current fiscal year and subsequent ones. Likewise, the federal entities must make the necessary budgetary provisions and adjustments to comply with the obligations established in this Decree.

Seventh. The Council of the Federal Judiciary, the Federal Public Defender's Institute, the Attorney General's Office of the Republic, the Ministry of the Interior, the Ministry of Labor and Social Welfare, the Ministry of Economy, the Ministry of Public Education, the Ministry of Culture, the Ministry of Social Development, the Ministry of Health, the National Commission for Physical Culture and Sports, the Executive Commission for Victim Assistance and any department or entity of the Federal Public Administration and their equivalents in the federal entities to which direct or indirect responsibilities are conferred under this Law, must provide for its adequate and correct implementation in their programs, and must establish within the respective budget projects, the necessary items to address the execution of those programs, infrastructure works, hiring of personnel, training and all other requirements necessary to fulfill the objectives of this Law.

Eighth. The Coordinating Council for the Implementation of the Criminal Justice System shall establish a Committee for the Implementation, Evaluation and Monitoring of the Criminal Execution System that shall be chaired by the National Conference of the Penitentiary System, which shall render a semi-annual report to the Coordinating Council. The foregoing with the purpose of coordinating, contributing and supporting federal authorities and federal entities when so requested.

299 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform OGF 05-08-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

The Penitentiary Authority shall have a period of four years, from the publication of this Decree, to train, adapt penitentiary establishments and their installed capacity, equip, develop information and communication technologies, as well as adapt its organizational structure. All of this in accordance with the activity plans registered with the Committee referred to in the preceding paragraph.

The Coordinating Council shall present annually before the Chambers of the Congress of the Union, an annual report on the monitoring of the implementation of the Criminal Execution System.

Ninth. Within one hundred eighty calendar days following the entry into force of this Decree, the National Conference of the Penitentiary System must issue a General Agreement establishing a gradual regime by virtue of which the Penitentiary Authorities, within the scope of their powers, shall designate special spaces for detention, within penitentiary establishments, for persons sentenced for the crimes of organized crime and kidnapping, provided for in the General Law to Prevent and Punish Crimes in Matters of Kidnapping, as well as those persons deprived of liberty who require special security measures.

Tenth. As of the entry into force of this Decree, all persons who have been sentenced to custodial sentences for the commission of the following crimes may access, immediately and without having to satisfy the requirements established in sections IV and VII of Article 141 of this Law, the benefit of early release:

- I. The commission of the crime of theft where the value of the stolen property does not exceed 80 times the Unit of Measurement and Update, and when no type of violence was involved in the commission of the crime, or
- II. The commission of the crime of possession without purposes of trade or supply, of Cannabis Sativa, Indica or Marijuana, contemplated in Article 477 of the General Health Law, in any of its forms, derivatives or preparations, and when no type of violence was involved in the commission of the crime, nor the concurrence of additional crimes.

For such purpose, the jurisdictional authority shall require from the Penitentiary Authority the report on compliance with the requirements referred to in the preceding paragraph.

Eleventh. The prosecutors or attorneys general of the Federation and of the federal entities, within their respective scope of competence, may request before the competent jurisdictional authority, the application of the early release benefits referred to in the tenth transitory article. Likewise, the competent judicial authorities shall process the respective proceeding ex officio or at the request of the person to whom said benefit applies.

Twelfth. The Judicial Branch of the Federation and the judicial branches of the federal entities shall issue general agreements, to determine the territorial jurisdiction of exception of the execution courts with the purpose of hearing the various matters by reason of security and special measures, while the Law enters into force; for which purpose they may execute the corresponding agreements with the operating agencies of the Criminal Justice System.

Article Second.- Sections XXXV, XXXVI and XXXVII and a fifth paragraph are added, and the third paragraph of Article 225 of the Federal Criminal Code is reformed, to read as follows: Transitory
