###### Article 73

of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States, of the Amparo Law, Regulatory of articles 103 and 107 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States, of the Organic Law of the Judicial Power of the Federation, of the Federal Public Defender's Office Law, of the Federal Fiscal Code and of the Credit Institutions Law.

Published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on June 17, 2016

Artículo Segundo.- Articles 7o., fraction I of the third paragraph; 16; 25; 27, first and last paragraphs; 29, last paragraph; 34, first and last paragraphs; 35, fourth paragraph; 38; 40; 50 Bis, first paragraph; 55, first and third paragraphs; 56; 64; 65, second paragraph; 71, second paragraph; 74, first paragraph; 75; 76; 77; 87; the denomination of Title Fifth, Chapter I of Book First; 91; 93, fourth paragraph; 97, first paragraph; 99; 101, second and third paragraphs; 110, first and third paragraphs; 114; 115, first paragraph; the denomination of Chapter VIII of Title Fifth of Book First; 225, fractions IX, X, XII, XIV, XVI, XVII, XIX, XXI, XXVII, XXVIII, XXX, XXXI and XXXII are reformed; articles 11 Bis; a second paragraph to article 55, shifting the subsequent ones in their order; a fourth paragraph to article 211 Bis 2 are added; the fifth paragraph of article 35; the fourth and sixth paragraphs of article 55; the

###### article 90 Bis and fractions XI and XIII of article 225, of the Federal Penal Code are repealed, to read as

follows:

Transitory Provisions

First.- This Decree shall enter into force the day following its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation except as provided in the following article.

Second.- The reforms to the National Code of Criminal Procedures, to the Federal Penal Code, to the General Law to Prevent and Punish Crimes in Matter of Kidnapping, Regulatory of fraction XXI of article 73 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States, articles 2, 13, 44 and 49 of the Federal Law for the Protection of Persons Involved in Criminal Proceedings and articles 21 in its fraction X, 50 Bis and 158 of the Organic Law of the Judicial Power of the Federation, shall enter into force in terms of the provisions of Transitory Article Second of the Decree issuing the National Code of Criminal Procedures published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on March 5, 2014.

Procedures that are in process, related to the modifications of the legal precepts contemplated in this Decree, shall be resolved in accordance with the provisions that gave rise to them.

Third.- Within 180 calendar days of the entry into force of this Decree, the Federation and the federative entities within the scope of their respective competences, must have an Authority for Supervision of Precautionary Measures and of the Conditional Suspension of the Process. Likewise, within 30 days following the date of creation of the authorities for precautionary measures and conditional suspension of the process of the Federation and of the federative entities, the agreements and guidelines that regulate their organization and operation must be issued.

302 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

Fourth.- The provisions of this Decree relating to criminal execution shall enter into force once the legislation on the matter provided for in article 73, fraction XXI, subsection c) of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States enters into force.

Fifth.- In the case of those measures depriving personal liberty or preventive detention that have been decreed by order of judicial authority during proceedings initiated based on the criminal procedural legislation in force prior to the entry into force of the adversarial accusatory criminal justice system, the accused or defendant may request from the competent jurisdictional body the review of said measures, for the purpose that the judge of the case, in the terms of articles 153 to 171 of the National Code of Criminal Procedures, having given notice to the parties, so that the Public Prosecutor investigates and accredits what is appropriate, and having held the corresponding hearing, the jurisdictional body, taking into consideration the risk assessment, resolves on the imposition, review, substitution, modification or cessation, in terms of the rules of preventive detention of article 19 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States, as well as of the National Code of Criminal Procedures. In case of substituting the precautionary measure, the supervision thereof shall apply accordingly in terms of articles 176 to 182 of the cited Code.

Sixth.- The Attorney General's Office of the Republic shall propose to the National Public Security Council the achievement of the agreements it deems necessary between the authorities of the federative entities and the federation within the framework of the Federal Law for the Protection of Persons Involved in Criminal Proceedings.

Zambrano Grijalva, President.- Sen. Hilda Esthela Flores Escalera, Secretary.- Dip. Verónica Delgadillo García, Secretary.- Signatures."

of Title Tenth; the first paragraph and its fraction III, the second and third paragraphs of

###### article 214;

fractions VI, IX, XI, XIII and the second and third paragraphs of article 215; the first and second paragraphs of article 216; the denomination of Chapter V of Title Tenth; the first paragraph, fraction I and subsections B), C), D), fraction III and the second paragraph of article 217; the third and fourth paragraphs of article 218; fraction I and the second paragraph of article 219; fraction I and the third and fourth paragraphs of article 220; the second paragraph of article 221; fractions I, II and the current third and fourth paragraphs of article 222; fractions I, II, III and the third and fourth paragraphs of

###### article 223;

the current first, second, fourth, fifth and sixth paragraphs of article 224; sections

VI, X, XIII, XVII, XX, XXIV, XXVIII and XXXII of article 225; there are added a third paragraph with sections I, II, a fourth paragraph, a fifth paragraph with sections I, II, III and IV, a sixth and a seventh paragraph to article 212; a subsection E) to section I, a section I Bis with subsections A) and B) and a second paragraph to article 217; an article 217 Bis; a section IV to article 221; a section III with subsections a, b and a second paragraph with subsequent paragraphs renumbered to article 222; a third paragraph with subsequent paragraphs renumbered to article 224, and the fourth paragraph of article 225 of the Federal Penal Code is repealed, to read as follows:

Transitory Provisions

First.- This Decree shall enter into force as of the appointment by the Senate of the Republic of the Head of the Specialized Prosecutor's Office for crimes related to acts of corruption, created pursuant to the provisions of the second paragraph of Transitory Article Eighteenth of the Decree that reforms, adds and repeals various provisions of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States, on political-electoral matters, published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on February 10, 2014, through Agreement A/011/14 that creates the Specialized Prosecutor's Office for Crimes Related to Acts of Corruption and establishes its powers, published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on March 12, 2014.

Second.- As of the entry into force of this Decree, in the event that the reforms contained herein contemplate a legal description of a criminal conduct that in the reformed articles was contemplated as a crime and by virtue of the present reforms, is denominated, penalized or aggravated in a different manner, provided that the conducts and facts correspond to the description now established, the following shall apply:

- I. In cases of facts that constitute any of the crimes reformed by this Decree, when knowledge thereof is obtained, the Public Prosecutor's Office shall initiate the investigation in accordance with the resulting type translation;
- II. In investigations initiated, in which the criminal action has not yet been exercised, the Public Prosecutor's Office shall exercise it in accordance with the resulting type translation;
- III. In proceedings instituted, in which accusatory conclusions have not yet been formulated, the Public Prosecutor's Office shall formulate them in accordance with the resulting type translation;

IV. In proceedings pending judgment in first and second instance, the judge or the Court, respectively, may effect the type translation in accordance with the conduct that has been proven and its modalities, and

V. The executing authority, when applying any modality of benefit for the sentenced person, shall consider the penalties that have been imposed, based on the type translation, according to the corresponding modalities.

Third.- Once the General Law to Prevent, Investigate and Punish Torture and Other Cruel, Inhuman or Degrading Treatment or Punishment, established by subsection a) of section XXI of article 73, enters into force, section XIII of article 215 shall be repealed and proceedings initiated for acts that occur as of said entry into force shall be continued in accordance with the provisions thereof.

Proceedings initiated before the effective date of said law shall continue their processing in accordance with this Code.

Fourth.- Sentenced persons shall continue serving their sentence in accordance with the provisions of the legislation in force at the time it became final. Zambrano Grijalva, President.- Sen. César Octavio Pedroza Gaitán, Secretary.- Rep. Isaura Ivanova Pool Pech, Secretary.- Signatures."

Article Second.- A final paragraph is added to article 198 of the Federal Penal Code, to read as follows:

Transitory

First.- This Decree shall enter into force the day following its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.

Second.- As of the entry into force of this Decree, the Ministry of Health shall strengthen the programs and actions referred to in Chapter IV of the Eleventh Title of the General Health Law, with emphasis on the prevention, treatment, rehabilitation, social reintegration and control of the consumption of cannabis sativa, indica and americana or marijuana and its derivatives, by girls, boys and adolescents, as well as the treatment of persons addicted to said narcotics. Third.- The General Health Council, based on the results of national research, shall ascertain the therapeutic or medicinal value leading to the production of drugs derived from cannabis sativa, indica and americana or marijuana and its derivatives, to guarantee the health of patients.

Fourth.- The Ministry of Health shall have 180 days from the entry into force of this Decree to harmonize the regulations and standards for the therapeutic use of TETRAHYDROCANNABINOL of the following isomers: ∆6a (10a), ∆6a (7), ∆7, ∆8, ∆9, ∆10, ∆9 (11) and their stereochemical variants. ###### Single Article. Article 381 Bis is amended and articles 381 Ter and 381 Quater are added to the

Federal Penal Code, to read as follows:

Transitory

First.- This Decree shall enter into force the day following its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.

Second.- As of the entry into force of this Decree, with respect to the legal description of criminal conduct contained in articles 381 Ter and 381 Quater of the Federal Penal Code, which in the


This excerpt sits under: FEDERAL PENAL CODE > First Book > Chapter II

###### artículo 381

Bis was contemplated as a crime and which by virtue of the present reforms, is denominated, penalized or aggravated in a different manner, provided that the conducts and facts respond to the description now established, the following shall apply:

- I. In proceedings initiated in which accusatory conclusions have not yet been formulated, the Federal Public Prosecutor's Office shall formulate them in accordance with the resulting reclassification of the offense;
- II. In proceedings pending judgment in first and second instance, the judge or the Court, respectively, may effect the reclassification of the offense in accordance with the conduct that has been proven and its modalities, and
- III. The executing authority, when applying any modality of benefit for the sentenced person, shall consider the penalties that have been imposed, based on the reclassification of the offense, according to the corresponding modalities.

DECREE issuing the General Law to Prevent, Investigate and Sanction Torture and Other Cruel, Inhuman or Degrading Treatment or Punishment; and reforming, adding and repealing various provisions of the Federal Penal Code, the Law of the National Human Rights Commission, the Law of the National

Public Safety System and the International Extradition Law.

Published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on June 26, 2017

SECOND ARTICLE.- Section V is added to Article 85 and Section XV of


This excerpt sits under: CÓDIGO PENAL FEDERAL > Libro Primero > Capítulo II

Reproduce the Markdown structure exactly: every heading keeps its own level (the same number of leading # characters), and every list marker, number and clause marker is preserved. Do not add, remove, merge or reorder any heading, list item or paragraph. Do not wrap the output in a code fence.

###### article 215;

and sections II and XIII of Article 215, as well as section XII of article 225, of the Federal Penal Code are repealed, to read as follows:

Transitory Provisions

First. This Decree shall enter into force on the day following its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.

Second. The Federal Law to Prevent and Punish Torture, published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on December 27, 1991, is repealed.

Proceedings initiated for acts occurring after the entry into force of this Law shall be conducted in accordance with the provisions of the National Code of Criminal Proceedings and this Law.

Proceedings initiated before this law enters into force shall continue their processing in accordance with the legislation applicable at the time of their commencement.

Sentenced persons shall continue serving their sentence in accordance with the provisions of the legislation in force at the time it became final.

Those persons, sentenced or prosecuted, whose evidence presented against them lacks probative value, having been obtained directly through torture and any other violation of human or fundamental rights, as well as evidence obtained by legal means but derived from such acts, may file the corresponding appeals and incidental proceedings.

Third. Within a maximum period of one hundred eighty days from the entry into force of this Decree, the legislature of each federative entity must harmonize its legal framework in accordance therewith.

Fourth. The authorities of the three levels of government, within the scope of their powers, must adopt and publish the protocols and criteria referred to in the General Law to Prevent, Investigate and Punish Torture and Other Cruel, Inhuman or Degrading Treatment or Punishment, within one hundred eighty days following the publication of this Decree.

Fifth. The Attorney General's Office of the Republic shall have one hundred eighty days following the date on which this Decree enters into force to issue the National Program to Prevent and Punish Torture and Other Cruel, Inhuman or Degrading Treatment or Punishment and to have the necessary technological infrastructure to operate the National Registry of the Crime of Torture.

312 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

Within ninety days following the completion of the period indicated in the preceding paragraph, the attorney's offices and prosecutors' offices of the federative entities must launch their corresponding registries.

Sixth. The Federation and the federative entities shall have ninety days following the date on which this Decree enters into force to create and operate their Special Prosecutor's Offices for the investigation of the crime of torture, except in cases where due to lack of sufficient resources they must be exercised by the corresponding specialized administrative unit.

Seventh. The authorities of the three levels of government, within the scope of their powers, must initiate continuous training programs for their public servants in accordance with the provisions of the General Law to Prevent, Investigate and Punish Torture and Other Cruel, Inhuman or Degrading Treatment or Punishment, within one hundred eighty days following the publication of this Decree.

Eighth. The authorities of the three levels of government, within the scope of their powers and within a period not exceeding ninety days from the entry into force of this Decree, must carry out the necessary procedures and perform the legal and administrative acts necessary to provide the Justice Procurement Institutions with the organizational and staffing structure necessary for compliance with the Law.

Ninth. Within one hundred eighty days following the entry into force of this Decree, the National Human Rights Commission must formally establish the National Mechanism for the Prevention of Torture and Other Cruel, Inhuman or Degrading Treatment or Punishment, and issue the bases for its operation and functioning in the ordinary session immediately following the establishment.

Likewise, within ninety days following the completion of the period indicated in the preceding paragraph, they must issue the general guidelines that determine the modalities and procedures to be followed during visits.

The head of the National Prevention Mechanism shall appoint the Executive Director within ninety days following the publication of this Decree in the Official Gazette of the Federation.

The election of the members of the Technical Committee referred to in section II of article 73 of this Law shall be conducted on a single occasion, following the gradual approach below:

Of the four expert persons elected, two shall serve for two years and the other two shall serve for four years, a situation that shall be defined by the Senate according to majority vote; the foregoing so that there is staggered replacement in the composition of the Technical Committee, whereby once the two-year period of the members elected for such period concludes, those who replace them shall be elected under the terms of the law for four years.

The Head President of the Technical Committee of the National Prevention Mechanism shall serve in office for the duration of their term as President of the National Human Rights Commission. Tenth. The expenditures generated by the entry into force of this Decree for the agencies and entities of the Federal Public Administration shall be covered by their budgets for the current fiscal year and subsequent years. Likewise, the federative entities must make the necessary budgetary provisions and adjustments to comply with the obligations established in this Decree.

313 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

Eleventh. The expenditures generated by the operation of the National Mechanism for the Prevention of Torture for the National Human Rights Commission shall be covered by its approved budget for the current fiscal year and subsequent years.

Twelfth. The state legislatures and the legislative body of Mexico City, under the terms of applicable legislation, must allocate resources for compliance with the obligations incumbent upon them under the terms of this Decree.

Thirteenth. In the federative entities where there is no Victims Assistance Commission, the public institutions of the federative entity must provide assistance to Victims in accordance with the provisions of Title Six of the General Law to Prevent, Investigate and Punish Torture and Other Cruel, Inhuman or Degrading Treatment or Punishment.

Likewise, in accordance with article 79 of the General Victims Law, the Executive Victims Assistance Commission shall have jurisdiction to comply with the provisions of the preceding paragraph. In the case established in the first paragraph of this article, the request referred to in section I of article 91 of the Law must be signed by the Secretary of Government of the corresponding federative entity.

Fourteenth. Once, under the terms of the Fifth Transitory Article of this Decree, the Attorney General's Office of the Republic begins to operate the National Registry of the Crime of Torture, the Executive Commission and the Justice Procurement Institutions may enter into collaboration agreements for the transmission of information on Victims of the crime of torture to said Registry.

Fifteenth. Within a period not exceeding one hundred eighty days from the entry into force of this Decree, the Executive Victims Assistance Commission must carry out the necessary acts to make the organizational modifications that are essential for compliance with the provisions thereof.

Sixteenth. In order to comply with the powers established in the General Law to Prevent, Investigate and Punish Torture and Other Cruel, Inhuman or Degrading Treatment or Punishment, the Executive Victims Assistance Commission shall make adjustments to its Organic Statute and other internal regulations as necessary, as well as to the trust that administers the resources of the Fund for Aid, Assistance and Comprehensive Reparation, within one hundred eighty days from its entry into force.

Mexico City, April 26, 2017.- Rep. María Guadalupe Murguía Gutiérrez, President.- Sen. Pablo Escudero Morales, President.- Rep. María Eugenia Ocampo Bedolla, Secretary.- Sen. María Elena Barrera Tapia, Secretary.- Signatures."

In compliance with the provisions of section I of Article 89 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States, and for its due publication and observance, I issue this Decree at the Residence of the Federal Executive Power, in Mexico City, on the twenty-first of June, two thousand seventeen.- Enrique Peña Nieto.- Signature.- The Secretary of the Interior, Miguel Ángel Osorio Chong.- Signature.

DECREE issuing the General Law on Forced Disappearance of Persons, Disappearance Committed by Private Individuals and the National System for the Search of Persons, and reforming and repealing various provisions of the Federal Penal Code and the General Health Law.

Published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on November 17, 2017

ARTICLE SECOND.- The third paragraph of article 25 is reformed; Chapter III Bis of Title Ten consisting of articles 215-A to 215-D is repealed, and an article 280 Bis is added to Title Seventeen of the Federal Penal Code, to read as follows:

Transitory Provisions

First. This Decree shall enter into force sixty days after its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.

From the entry into force of this Decree and until the issuance of the instruments referred to in the Fourteenth Transitory Article, the Attorney General's Office and the Local Attorney's Offices and other authorities must comply with search obligations in accordance with the provisions that have been previously issued, provided they do not conflict with this Law.

The Attorney General's Office and the Local Attorney's Offices, in addition to the protocols provided for in this Law, shall continue to apply the existing protocols for the search of persons in vulnerable situations. Second. The Law of the National Registry of Data on Missing or Disappeared Persons is repealed.

Third. The Specialized Prosecutor's Offices and the National Search Commission shall commence operations within thirty days following the entry into force of this Decree. Within thirty days following the National Search Commission beginning its functions, it must issue the guiding protocols for its operation provided for in article 53, section VIII, of this Law.

Within one hundred eighty days following the National Search Commission commencing operations, it must issue the National Search Program.

The public servants who comprise the Specialized Prosecutor's Offices and the Search Commissions must be certified within one year following their creation.

For purposes of the provisions of the preceding paragraph, the National Search Commission shall issue the criteria provided for in article 53, section L, of this Law, within ninety days following its commencement of operations.

The National Search Commission and the local commissions may, from the time they commence operations, exercise the powers that this Law confers upon them in relation to pending search processes. The National Search Commission shall coordinate the search for disappeared persons related to searches in which, at the entry into force of this Law, federal authorities participate.

315 of 337

FEDERAL PENAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

Fourth. The Local Search Commissions must commence operations within ninety days following the entry into force of this Decree.

The National Search Commission must provide the necessary advice to the federal entities for the establishment of their Local Search Commissions.

Fifth. The Citizens' Council must be formed within ninety days following the entry into force of this Decree.

Within thirty days following its formation, the Citizens' Council must issue its operating rules.

Sixth. The National System for the Search of Persons must be established within one hundred eighty days following the publication of this Decree.

In the first ordinary session of the National Search System, the guidelines and models referred to in article 49, sections I, VIII, XV and XVI of this Law must be issued.

In the second ordinary session of the National Search System, to be held in accordance with the provisions of this Law, the certification and specialization criteria provided for in the

###### artículo 55.

Seventh. Within one hundred eighty days following the issuance of the guidelines provided for in the preceding transitory article, the National Search Commission must have the necessary technological infrastructure and begin to operate the National Registry of Disappeared and Not Located Persons.

Within ninety days following the commencement of operation of the National Registry of Disappeared and Not Located Persons, the Federal Entities must launch their registries of Disappeared and Not Located Persons.

Eighth. Until the registries of Disappeared and Not Located Persons begin to operate, the Local Attorney General's Offices must incorporate into a provisional registry, electronic or printed, the information of the Reports, Complaints or Notices received in accordance with the provisions of article 106 of this Law. The Federation and the Federal Entities must migrate the information contained in the provisional registries referred to in the preceding paragraph, within fifteen days following the commencement of operation of the registries of Disappeared and Not Located Persons.

Ninth. The Congress of the Union must legislate on the matter of Special Declaration of Absence within one hundred eighty days following the date on which this Decree enters into force. The Federal Entities must issue and, as applicable, harmonize the legislation corresponding to their scope of competence within one hundred eighty days following the date on which this Decree enters into force.

In those Federal Entities in which the harmonization provided for in Chapter Three of Title Four of this Law has not been carried out, within the period indicated in the preceding paragraph, the provisions of said Chapter shall be applicable notwithstanding the provisions of the applicable local legislation.

Tenth. As of the entry into force of this Decree, in the event that the provisions contained therein contemplate the legal description of conducts provided for in other norms as 316 of 337

FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE

CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services crimes and by virtue of this Law they are designated, classified, penalized or aggravated in a different manner, provided that the conduct and the facts correspond to the description now established, the following shall apply:

- I. In cases of acts that constitute any of the crimes of this Law, when knowledge thereof is obtained, the Public Prosecutor's Office shall initiate the investigation in accordance with this Law;
- II. In investigations initiated in which criminal action has not yet been exercised, the Public Prosecutor's Office shall exercise it in accordance with the translation of the type that is appropriate;
- III. In proceedings initiated under the mixed criminal system in which the Public Prosecutor's Office has not yet formulated accusatory conclusions, it shall proceed to their preparation and presentation in accordance with the translation of the criminal type that, as applicable, is appropriate;
- IV. In proceedings initiated under the adversarial accusatory system, in which the Public Prosecutor's Office has not yet presented an accusation, it shall proceed to its preparation and presentation according to the translation of the type that may be appropriate;
- V. In proceedings pending the issuance of a judgment in first and second instance, the judge or the corresponding Court, may effect the translation of the type in accordance with the conduct that has been proven, including its modalities, without exceeding the amount of the penalties indicated in the respective law in force at the time of the commission of the acts, and
- VI. The executing authority, upon applying any modality of benefit for the sentenced person, shall consider the penalties that have been imposed, according to the corresponding modalities.

Eleventh. The Federal Executive, within a period of one hundred eighty days from the entry into force of this Decree, must issue and harmonize the regulatory provisions that correspond in accordance with the provisions of this Decree.

Twelfth. Within thirty days following the creation of the National Search Commission, the Executive Secretariat of the National Public Security System shall transfer to it the technological tools and the information it has collected in compliance with the provisions of the Law of the National Registry of Data on Missing or Disappeared Persons.

Within ninety days following receipt of the information referred to in the preceding paragraph, the National Search Commission must transmit to the Specialized Prosecutor's Offices the information on Disappeared or Not Located Persons that correspond to the scope of their competence.

The Specialized Prosecutor's Offices must update the content of the National Registry, in accordance with the following:

- I. Within one hundred eighty days following receipt of the information, the corresponding Specialized Prosecutor's Office must collect information on the persons registered in the Registry provided for in the Law of the National Registry of Data on Missing or Disappeared Persons that correspond to its scope of competence, so that such information is in accordance with the provisions of article 106 and, as applicable, article 112 of this Law;
- II. Pursuant to the preceding section, the Specialized Prosecutor's Offices that are materially prevented from updating the information within the provided period, must publish a registry with the name of the Disappeared or Not Located Persons whose information has not been updated, so that, 317 of 337 FEDERAL CRIMINAL CODE CHAMBER OF DEPUTIES OF THE H. CONGRESS OF THE UNION Last Reform DOF 08-05-2023 General Secretariat Secretariat of Parliamentary Services within the following one hundred twenty days, the Family Members and civil society organizations provide the information that could be useful to carry out such update;
- III. Once the information is updated, the National Search Commission must enter it into the corresponding registry, except that the update reveals that the person was located, in which case, it shall be recorded in the National Registry of Disappeared Persons, and
- IV. Upon completion of the action provided for in section II of this article, if the registry has not been updated, the corresponding Specialized Prosecutor's Office will be materially unable to update it. In this case, the registry shall remain with the notation of pending update and shall be migrated, with that status, to the corresponding registry.

Thirteenth. The National Forensic Data Bank, the Federal forensic registries and those of the Federal Entities shall begin to operate within the year following the entry into force of this Decree.

Within three months following the commencement of operation of such registries, the authorities that possess forensic information must incorporate it into the corresponding registry.

Fourteenth. Within one hundred eighty days following the entry into force of this Decree, the National Conference on Justice Procurement must issue the Standardized Investigation Protocol referred to in article 99 of this Law.

Fifteenth. The authorities and institutions that collect the information referred to in article 103 must incorporate it within a period of one year from the entry into force of this Decree. Sixteenth. In the Federal Entities in which a Victims Assistance Commission does not exist, the competent public institutions of the Federal Entity must provide victim assistance in accordance with the provisions of Title Four of this Law.

Seventeenth. The expenditures generated by virtue of the entry into force of this Decree for the dependencies and parastatal entities of the Federal Public Administration, shall be covered with the resources approved by the Chamber of Deputies in the Expenditure Budget of the Federation for the fiscal year in question.

The Legislatures of the Federal Entities, pursuant to the applicable legislation, must allocate the resources for the fulfillment of the obligations incumbent upon them pursuant to this Decree.

Eighteenth. The guidelines for determining the techniques and procedures that must be applied for the preservation of cadavers or remains of persons referred to in article 130 of this Law must be published in the Official Gazette of the Federation within the period of one hundred eighty days from the entry into force of this Law.

Nineteenth. The Attorney General's Office of the Republic must issue the necessary technological guidelines to ensure that the registries and the National Forensic Data Bank have the appropriate technical characteristics and technological support, in accordance with the provisions of articles 131, section III and 132, within the period of one hundred eighty days from the entry into force of this Law. Within the period provided for in the preceding paragraph, the Attorney General's Office of the Republic shall issue the necessary guidelines so that the authorities of the different orders of government submit in a standardized manner the information that will be integrated into the National Registry of Deceased Persons Not Identified and Not Claimed and the National Forensic Data Bank provided for in the General Law on 318 of 337

Likewise, the Federal Entities must make the necessary budgetary provisions and adjustments to comply with the obligations established in this Decree.

General Secretariat

Secretariat of Parliamentary Services

DECREE adding an article 190 Bis to the Federal Telecommunications and Broadcasting Law, and an article 168 ter to the Federal Penal Code.

Published in the Official Gazette of the Federation on January 24, 2020

Article Second. An article 168 ter is added to the Federal Penal Code, to read as follows: Transitory Provisions

First. This Decree shall enter into force the day following its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.

Second. Private individuals who possess apparatus or equipment that serves to block, cancel or nullify cellular telephone, radiocommunication or data or image transmission signals, must deliver the same to the Secretariat of Security and Citizen Protection, destroy or, as the case may be, remove them from the country, within a period of 30 days from the entry into force of this Decree. Third. The Secretariat of Security and Citizen Protection shall coordinate and supervise the delivery-reception by private individuals of all apparatus whose purpose is to block, cancel or nullify cellular telephone, radiocommunication or data or image transmission signals and shall proceed to render them unusable under the methods it deems appropriate.

Fourth. The corresponding authorities shall have a term of no more than 180 calendar days to make modifications to Official Mexican Standard 220-SCFI-2017, in order to harmonize it with the contents of this Decree.

Fifth. The Secretariat of Security and Citizen Protection shall report annually on compliance with the provisions expressed in this Decree, to the Public Security Commissions of the Senate of the Republic and the Chamber of Federal Deputies.

Mexico City, December 11, 2019.- Rep. Laura Angélica Rojas Hernández, President.- Sen. Mónica Fernández Balboa, President.- Rep. Lizbeth Mata Lozano, Secretary.- Sen. Primo Dothé Mata, Secretary.- Signatures."

Sánchez Cordero Dávila.- Signature.

Artículo Sexto.- Article 160, first paragraph, is reformed; a third paragraph is added to Article 420, and Sections I and III of Article 162 of the Federal Penal Code are repealed, to read as follows:

Transitorios

Primero. This Decree shall enter into force on the day following its publication in the Official Gazette of the Federation.

Segundo. All provisions that oppose the content of this

Decree are hereby repealed.

Tercero. Criminal proceedings being processed at the entry into force of this

Decree shall continue in accordance with the provisions in force at the time the facts were committed.

Persons who have committed a crime contemplated in this Decree prior to its entry into force, shall be subject to the provisions in force at the time it was committed.

Cuarto. Sentenced persons shall continue serving their sentence in accordance with what is established in the legislation in force at the time it became final.

Quinto. The application of the norms in the criminal cases referred to in this Decree shall be made in accordance with the provisions of Article 14 of the Political Constitution of the United Mexican States.
